<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--ma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰म</span> <i>-ma</i> forme des a. ord. et des s. m. ou n.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--maana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰मान</span> <i>-māna</i> var. <i>-āna</i> forme des ppr. r. ou ps. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> forme des pfu. r. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> forme des ppft. r.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--man">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰मन्</span> <i>-man</i> forme des s. n. (qqf. m.).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--mara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰मर</span> <i>-mara</i> forme des a. signifiant «qui fait».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--mat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰मत्</span> <i>-mat</i> forme des a. (remplace <a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a> après th. en <i>i</i>, <i>ī</i>, <i>u</i>, <i>ū</i>, <i>ṛ</i>, <i>o</i>, <i>iṣ</i> ou <i>uṣ</i>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry--min">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">॰मिन्</span> <i>-min</i> cf. <a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृ</span> <i>mṛ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. md. <i>(mriyate)</i> pft. <i>(mamāra)</i> pft. md. <i>(mamre)</i> aor. md. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <i>(amṛta)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(amīmarat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mṛta</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(martum)</i> mourir — ca. <i>(mārayati)</i> faire mourir, tuer — dés. <i>(mumūrṣati)</i> être mourant, souhaiter mourir — int. <i>(marīmarti)</i> int. md. <i>(memrīyate)</i> agoniser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>morior</i>; ang. murder; all. Mord; fr. mourir.<br> <i>adyā</i> <i>mamāra</i> il est mort maintenant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_r_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृड्</span> <i>mṛḍ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(mṛḍati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(mṛḍayati)</i> pardonner, épargner, faire grâce à qqn. ⟨dat.⟩ de qqc. ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> traiter avec douceur; réjouir, amuser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_r_naala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृणाल</span> <i>mṛṇāla</i> <a href="#entry-m_r_naala" class="skt-link">n.</a> tige ou racine du lotus (symbole de fragilité).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_r_nmaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृण्मय</span> <i>mṛṇmaya</i> a. <a href="#entry-m_r_nmaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-m_r_nmaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-m_r_nmayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛṇmayī</i> cf. <a href="#entry-m_rnmaya" class="skt-link">mṛnmaya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_r_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृष्</span> <i>mṛṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(mṛṣyati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i><i>)</i> oublier, pardonner; supporter, tolérer, accepter, permettre — ca. <i>(marṣayati)</i> supporter; pardonner.<br> <i>na</i> <i>mṛṣ</i> molester.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_r_s_ta_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृष्टि</span> <i>mṛṣṭi_1</i> [act. <a href="#entry-m_rj" class="skt-link">mṛj</a>] <a href="#entry-m_r_s_ti_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> repas savoureux.
  </div>

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_r_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृषा</span> <i>mṛṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-m_r_s" class="skt-link">mṛṣ</a>] adv. en vain, inutilement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faussement, à tort — <a href="#entry-m_r_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> tromperie, mensonge, fausseté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mṛṣā, la Tromperie personnifiée, mère de <a href="#entry-Udambha" class="skt-link">Dambha</a> par <a href="#entry-Uadharma" class="skt-link">Adharma</a> — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(mṛṣāyate)</i> être dans l'erreur.<br> <i>mṛṣā</i> <i>kṛ</i> feindre.<br> <i>mṛṣā</i> <i>jñā</i> savoir comme faux.<br> <i>mṛṣā</i> <i>man</i> considérer comme faux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_r_saatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛṣātva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-m_r_saatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> fausseté, erreur; injustice.
  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rcchaka_tikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rd_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृद्</span> <i>mṛd_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(mṛdnāti)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(mardati)</i> pr. md. <i>(mardate)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> broyer, écraser; piétiner; frotter; détruire — ca. <i>(mardayati)</i> presser, broyer; détruire, tuer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>mordeo</i>.
  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृदा</span> <i>mṛdā</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rd_2" class="skt-link">mṛd_2</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rdaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> terre, argile.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-entry-content">

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdhas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृधस्</span> <i>mṛdhas</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rdh" class="skt-link">mṛdh</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rdhas" class="skt-link">n.</a> dédain, mépris, injure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdhra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृध्र</span> <i>mṛdhra</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rdh" class="skt-link">mṛdh</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rdhra" class="skt-link">n.</a> mépris, injure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdhravaac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛdhravāc</i> [<a href="#entry-vaac" class="skt-link">vāc</a>] a. <a href="#entry-m_rdhravaac" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdhravaac" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdhravaac" class="skt-link">f.</a> injurieux, insultant.<br> <a href="#entry-m_rdhra" class="skt-link">mṛdhra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृदु</span> <i>mṛdu</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rd_2" class="skt-link">mṛd_2</a><a href="#entry--u" class="skt-link">-u</a>] a. <a href="#entry-m_rdu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛdvī</i> mou; tendre, doux, délicat; gentil — <a href="#entry-m_rdu" class="skt-link">n.</a> douceur, délicatesse.<br> <i>mṛdū</i> <i>kṛ</i> amollir.<br> <i>mṛdū</i> <i>bhū</i> se ramollir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdubhaa_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛdubhāṣin</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_sin" class="skt-link">bhāṣin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-m_rdubhaa_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdubhaa_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdubhaa_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛdubhāṣiṇī</i> à la voix délicate <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui parle avec douceur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdubhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛdubhava</i> [<a href="#entry-bhava" class="skt-link">bhava</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rdubhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> douceur, tendrese.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृदुल</span> <i>mṛdula</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rdu" class="skt-link">mṛdu</a><a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] a. <a href="#entry-m_rdula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdula" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛdulā</i> mou; tendre, doux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdupuurva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛdupūrva</i> [<a href="#entry-puurva" class="skt-link">pūrva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-m_rdupuurva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdupuurva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdupuurvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛdupūrvā</i> [«qui commence doucement»] gentil, aimable, tendre (not. paroles).<br> <i>mṛdupūrvam</i> doucement; tendrement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdusparza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛdusparśa</i> [<a href="#entry-sparza" class="skt-link">sparśa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-m_rdusparza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdusparza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rdusparzaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛdusparśā</i> doux au contact.<br> <a href="#entry-m_rdu" class="skt-link">mṛdu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdutaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛdutā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rdutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> douceur; tendresse; faiblesse.<br> <i>mṛdutām</i> <i>gam</i> s'adoucir, se ramollir, s'affaiblir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rdutva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛdutva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rdutva" class="skt-link">n.</a> douceur; tendresse; faiblesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृग</span> <i>mṛga</i> [<a href="#entry-m_r" class="skt-link">mṛ</a>-<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rga" class="skt-link">m.</a> recherche, investigation; chasse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gibier, animal sauvage paisible; opp. <a href="#entry-vyaala" class="skt-link">vyāla</a> (fauve); opp. <a href="#entry-pazu" class="skt-link">paśu</a>, (animal domestique) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> not. antilope, gazelle, daim <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. la constellation [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>] d'Orion; cf. <a href="#entry-m_rgaziras" class="skt-link">mṛgaśiras</a> — <a href="#entry-m_rgii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛgī</i> animal sauvage femelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mṛgī, fille de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukrodhavazaa" class="skt-link">Krodhavaśā</a>; elle engendra la race des antilopes — f. cf. <a href="#entry-m_rgaa" class="skt-link">mṛgā</a> — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(mṛgayate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(mṛgayati)</i> ps. <i>(mṛgyate)</i> inf. <i>(mṛgayitum)</i> chercher, chasser, pourchasser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> poursuivre, viser à ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> demander, exiger (de ⟨abl. g.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> explorer.<br> <i>mṛgacapalā</i> f. (jeune fille) timide comme une gazelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृगा</span> <i>mṛgā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-m_rga" class="skt-link">mṛga</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> antilope femelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgaafka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgāṅka</i> [<a href="#entry-afka" class="skt-link">aṅka</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgaafka" class="skt-link">m.</a> Lune [«dont les taches ont la forme d'une antilope»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgaak_sii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgākṣī</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sa" class="skt-link">akṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgaak_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> jolie femme [«au regard de biche»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgaavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgāvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-m_rgaavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rgaavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rgaavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛgāvatī</i> qui a une antilope — <a href="#entry-m_rgaavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛgāvatī</i> lit. np. de la princesse du <a href="#entry-Uvideha" class="skt-link">Videha</a> Mṛgāvatī, elle épousa le roi <a href="#entry-Usahasraaniika" class="skt-link">Sahasrānīka</a>; enlevée par un oiseau qui l'abandonna dans les bois, elle fut recueillie par <a href="#entry-Ujamadagni" class="skt-link">Jamadagni</a>, et accoucha d'Udayana à son ermitage.<br> <a href="#entry-m_rgaa" class="skt-link">mṛgā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgacakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgacakra</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgacakra" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. écliptique; zodiaque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgad_rz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgadṛś</i> [<a href="#entry-d_rz_2" class="skt-link">dṛś_2</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgad_rz" class="skt-link">f.</a> jolie femme [«au regard de biche»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgadaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgadāva</i> [<a href="#entry-daava" class="skt-link">dāva</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgadaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. parc aux gazelles de <a href="#entry-Usaaranaatha" class="skt-link">Sāranātha</a>, où <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> fit son premier sermon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgamada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgamada</i> [<a href="#entry-mada" class="skt-link">mada</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgamada" class="skt-link">m.</a> musc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgamanda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgamanda</i> [<a href="#entry-manda" class="skt-link">manda</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgamanda" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. animal des marais — <a href="#entry-m_rgamandaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛgamandā</i> myth. np. de Mṛgamandā, fille de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukrodhavazaa" class="skt-link">Krodhavaśā</a>; elle engendra la race des yacks [<a href="#entry-camara" class="skt-link">camara</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgapati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgapati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgapati" class="skt-link">m.</a> lion [«maître des animaux»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgaraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgarāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgaraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> lion [«roi des animaux»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgat_r_s_naa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgatṛṣṇā</i> [<a href="#entry-t_r_s_naa" class="skt-link">tṛṣṇā</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgat_r_s_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«soif de gazelle»] mirage (du désert) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>mṛgatṛṣṇi</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgat_r_s_nikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgatṛṣṇikā</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgat_r_s_nikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-m_rgat_r_s_naa" class="skt-link">mṛgatṛṣṇā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgavyaadha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgavyādha</i> [<a href="#entry-vyaadha" class="skt-link">vyādha</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgavyaadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> chasseur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mṛgavyādha «le Chasseur», personnifiant l'étoile Sirius, symbolisant <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> comme l'archer divin qui frappa de sa flèche <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a> commettant l'inceste au matin du monde; par ext., épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> dans son rôle de vengeur de l'unité primordiale; il est l'un des 11 souffles divins [<a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>]; syn. <a href="#entry-Ulubdhaka" class="skt-link">Lubdhaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgayaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृगया</span> <i>mṛgayā</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rga" class="skt-link">mṛga</a>-<a href="#entry-ya_2" class="skt-link">ya_2</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> chasse.<br> <i>mṛgayāṃ</i> <i>dhāv</i> [«courir le gibier»] aller à la chasse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgayu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृगयु</span> <i>mṛgayu</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rga" class="skt-link">mṛga</a><a href="#entry--yu" class="skt-link">-yu</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgayu" class="skt-link">m.</a> chasseur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgaziras">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgaśiras</i> [<a href="#entry-ziras" class="skt-link">śiras</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgaziras" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. np. de Mṛgaśiras «Tête de l'antilope», 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. au Baudrier d'Orion (Trois Rois); <a href="#entry-Usoma" class="skt-link">Soma</a> y préside; son symbole est la tête de gazelle; il symbolise <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a>, la victime primordiale, percé par <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> après l'inceste de la Création; cf. <a href="#entry-Urohi_nii" class="skt-link">Rohiṇī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgendra</i> [<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgendra" class="skt-link">m.</a> lion [«roi des animaux»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rgendratantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛgendratantra</i> [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rgendratantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mṛgendratantra.<br> <a href="#entry-m_rgendra" class="skt-link">mṛgendra</a> <br> <a href="#entry-m_rga" class="skt-link">mṛga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृज्</span> <i>mṛj</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(mārṣṭi)</i> pr. md. <i>(mṛṣṭe)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(mārjati)</i> pr. md. <i>(mārjate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(mṛjati)</i> pr. md. <i>(mṛjate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(mārṣṭum)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>apa</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> essuyer, nettoyer, frotter; effacer; parer — ca. <i>(mārjayati)</i> effacer; frotter, polir; orner — int. md. <i>(marmṛjyate)</i> frotter avec vigueur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>mulgeo</i>; ang. to milk; all. melken.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rka_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृकण्ड</span> <i>mṛkaṇḍa</i> var. <i>mṛkaṇḍu</i> <a href="#entry-m_rka_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Mṛkaṇḍa ou Mṛkaṇḍu, père de <a href="#entry-Umaarka_n_deya" class="skt-link">Mārkaṇḍeya</a> par <a href="#entry-Umanasvinii" class="skt-link">Manasvinī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rnmaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛnmaya</i> var. <i>mṛṇmaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-m_rnmaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rnmaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rnmayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛnmayī</i> en terre.<br> <i>mṛnmaya</i> <i>gṛha</i> n. tombe.<br> <i>mṛnmayī</i> <i>pratimā</i> idole en terre.<br> <a href="#entry-m_rd_2" class="skt-link">mṛd_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृत</span> <i>mṛta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-m_r" class="skt-link">mṛ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-m_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛtā</i> mort, abattu; parti, disparu, consumé — <a href="#entry-m_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> mort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mendicité, moyen de subsistance convenable pour un brâhmane [<a href="#entry-_sa_tkarman" class="skt-link">ṣaṭkarman</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtaa_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛtāṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-a_n_da" class="skt-link">aṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rtaa_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> œuf mort ou qu'on croit mort — <a href="#entry-m_rtaa_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛtāṇḍā</i> femme dont la descendance est morte.<br> <a href="#entry-m_rta" class="skt-link">mṛta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛtapa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_2" class="skt-link">pa_2</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rtapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [«gardien des morts»] préposé aux bûchers funéraires, de caste impure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>mṛtapā</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtasa_njiivanii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛtasaṃjīvanī</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_njiivanii" class="skt-link">saṃjīvanī</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rtasa_mjiivanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. herbe de résurrection; cf. <a href="#entry-sa_njiivanii" class="skt-link">saṃjīvanī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtatithi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛtatithi</i> [<a href="#entry-tithi" class="skt-link">tithi</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rtatithi" class="skt-link">m.</a> anniversaire de décès.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtsna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृत्स्न</span> <i>mṛtsna</i> <a href="#entry-m_rtsna" class="skt-link">m.</a> poussière, poudre — <a href="#entry-m_rtsnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛtsnā</i> argile; bonne terre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rttikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृत्तिका</span> <i>mṛttikā</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rd_2" class="skt-link">mṛd_2</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rttikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> terre, sol; argile, glèbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rttikaagha_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛttikāghaṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-gha_ta" class="skt-link">ghaṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rttikaagha_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> pot en terre cuite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rttikaavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛttikāvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rttikaavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛttikāvatī</i> géo. np. de la ville de Mṛttikāvatī «Faite de boue».<br> <a href="#entry-m_rttikaa" class="skt-link">mṛttikā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtyu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृत्यु</span> <i>mṛtyu</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rta" class="skt-link">mṛta</a><a href="#entry--yu" class="skt-link">-yu</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rtyu" class="skt-link">m.</a> mort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mṛtyu la Mort, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>, premier humain, devenu à sa mort dieu de la mort; sa fille aînée est <a href="#entry-Usuniithaa" class="skt-link">Sunīthā</a>.<br> <i>mṛtyave</i> <i>tvā</i> <i>dadāmi</i> [KU.] Je te donne à la Mort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtyuhiina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛtyuhīna</i> [<a href="#entry-hiina" class="skt-link">hīna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-m_rtyuhiina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rtyuhiina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-m_rtyuhiinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mṛtyuhīnā</i> immortel.<br> <a href="#entry-m_rtyu" class="skt-link">mṛtyu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtyum">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृत्युम्</span> <i>mṛtyum</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-m_rtyu" class="skt-link">mṛtyu</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtyusa_mskaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛtyusaṃskāra</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mskaara" class="skt-link">saṃskāra</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rtyusa_mskaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. rite de cérémonie funéraire; cf. <a href="#entry-antye_s_ti" class="skt-link">antyeṣṭi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtyuva_ncana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛtyuvañcana</i> [<a href="#entry-va_ncana" class="skt-link">vañcana</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rtyuva_ncana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. tromperie de la mort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtyuva_ncanopadeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛtyuvañcanopadeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-upadeza" class="skt-link">upadeśa</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rtyuva_ncanopadeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Mṛtyuvañcanopadeśa «Traité sur comment tromper la mort», ouvrage bd. de <a href="#entry-Uvaagiizvarakiirti" class="skt-link">Vāgīśvarakīrti</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-m_rtyuva_ncana" class="skt-link">mṛtyuvañcana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtyuvijaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛtyuvijaya</i> [<a href="#entry-vijaya" class="skt-link">vijaya</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rtyuvijaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Udazakumaaracarita" class="skt-link">Daśakumāracarita</a>] np. de l'éléphant-bourreau Mṛtyuvijaya «qui vainc la mort»; supposé exécuter <a href="#entry-Upuur_nabhadra_1" class="skt-link">Pūrṇabhadra_1</a>, il fut mis en fuite par lui.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rtyu_njaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mṛtyuṃjaya</i> var. <i>mṛtyuñjaya</i> [<a href="#entry-jaya" class="skt-link">jaya</a>] <a href="#entry-m_rtyu_mjaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mṛtyuṃjaya, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «Qui vainc la mort» — <a href="#entry-m_rtyu_mjaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> nom d'un <a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">śloka</a> du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-traiyambakamantra" class="skt-link">traiyambakamantra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-m_rtyum" class="skt-link">mṛtyum</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-m_rz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृश्</span> <i>mṛś</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(mṛśati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mṛṣṭa</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>mṛṣya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>parā</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> passer la main sur, toucher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> réfléchir, examiner — ca. <i>(marśayati)</i> considérer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>mulceo</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_mh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मंह्</span> <i>maṃh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(maṃhate)</i> pft. <i>(māmahe)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mahita</i><i>)</i> donner avec largesse, accorder; offrir en récompense — ca. <i>(maṃhayati)</i> faire accorder.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मण्ड्</span> <i>maṇḍ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(maṇḍati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>maṇḍita</i><i>)</i> orner, parer, décorer — ca. <i>(maṇḍayati)</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मण्ड</span> <i>maṇḍa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ma_n_d" class="skt-link">maṇḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> écume (de cuisson du riz, du lait, etc.); crème; essence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_dabuddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇḍabuddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-buddhi" class="skt-link">buddhi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ma_n_dabuddhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dabuddhi" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dabuddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a l'esprit lent.<br> <i>adhyayanāt</i> <i>parājayate</i> <i>maṇḍabuddhiḥ</i> (L'élève) à l'esprit lent se lasse de l'étude.<br> <a href="#entry-ma_n_da" class="skt-link">maṇḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_daka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मण्डक</span> <i>maṇḍaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ma_n_d" class="skt-link">maṇḍ</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_n_daka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇḍikā</i> galette, crêpe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_dala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मण्डल</span> <i>maṇḍala</i> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dala" class="skt-link">n.</a> cercle, disque <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. diagramme rituel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> groupe, groupement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. l'une des 10 divisions du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a>, regroupé en hymnes [<a href="#entry-suukta" class="skt-link">sūkta</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_dalin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇḍalin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ma_n_dalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dalin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇḍalinī</i> circulaire — ifc. entourant.<br> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dala" class="skt-link">maṇḍala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_dalita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मण्डलित</span> <i>maṇḍalita</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_n_dala" class="skt-link">maṇḍala</a><a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ma_n_dalita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dalita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dalitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇḍalitā</i> enroulé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_dalitahasta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇḍalitahasta</i> [<a href="#entry-hasta" class="skt-link">hasta</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_n_dalitahasta" class="skt-link">m.</a> (éléphant) à la trompe enroulée.<br> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dalita" class="skt-link">maṇḍalita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_dana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मण्डन</span> <i>maṇḍana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ma_n_d" class="skt-link">maṇḍ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ma_n_dana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_danaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇḍanā</i> qui orne, qui décore ⟨g.⟩ — <a href="#entry-ma_n_dana" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Maṇḍana <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a> <a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>, philosophe de la langue né au <a href="#entry-Ubihaara" class="skt-link">Bihāra</a> (9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); cf. <a href="#entry-Usurezvara" class="skt-link">Sureśvara</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvidhiviveka" class="skt-link">Vidhiviveka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du grammairien jaïne Maṇḍana_1 <a href="#entry-Umantrii" class="skt-link">Mantrī</a>, premier ministre du roi Hośāṃga Ghorī du <a href="#entry-Umaalava" class="skt-link">Mālava</a> (15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); on lui doit l'<a href="#entry-Uupasargama_n_dana" class="skt-link">Upasargamaṇḍana</a>, supplément au <a href="#entry-Ukavikalpadruma" class="skt-link">Kavikalpadruma</a> — <a href="#entry-ma_n_dana" class="skt-link">n.</a> ornement, parure, décoration — <a href="#entry-ma_n_danaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇḍanā</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_dapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मण्डप</span> <i>maṇḍapa</i> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dapa" class="skt-link">n.</a> arch. hall ouvert dans un temple, pavillon, porche à piliers; tente; berceau de verdure.<br> <i>maṇḍapakṣetra</i> territoire consacré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_dapaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇḍapaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_n_dapaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> id — <a href="#entry-ma_n_dapikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇḍapikā</i> petit pavillon; abri, appentis.<br> <a href="#entry-ma_n_dapa" class="skt-link">maṇḍapa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_du">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मण्डु</span> <i>maṇḍu</i> <a href="#entry-ma_n_du" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Maṇḍu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_duuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मण्डूक</span> <i>maṇḍūka</i> <a href="#entry-ma_n_duuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_n_duukii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇḍūkī</i> natu. zoo. grenouille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_duukapar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇḍūkaparṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-par_na" class="skt-link">parṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_n_duukapar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Oroxylum indicum</i>, arbre bignone d'Inde, utilisé en médecine — <a href="#entry-ma_n_duukapar_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇḍūkaparṇī</i> méd. plante médicinale, utilisée pour soigner l'activité cérébrale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_duukapluti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇḍūkapluti</i> [<a href="#entry-pluti" class="skt-link">pluti</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_n_duukapluti" class="skt-link">f.</a> saut de grenouille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. discontinuité dans la portée d'une condition contextuelle dans l'<a href="#entry-anuv_rtti" class="skt-link">anuvṛtti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_duukaplutinyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇḍūkaplutinyāya</i> [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_n_duukaplutinyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. maxime du saut de grenouille, expliquant par analogie une discontinuité dans le contexte [<a href="#entry-anuv_rtti" class="skt-link">anuvṛtti</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-ma_n_duukapluti" class="skt-link">maṇḍūkapluti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_duukayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇḍūkayoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_n_duukayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> posture de <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a> d'immobilité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_n_duukazik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇḍūkaśikṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-zik_saa" class="skt-link">śikṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_n_duukazik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Maṇḍūkaśikṣā «Doctrine de la grenouille», recueil de textes de l'école véd. des <a href="#entry-Umaa_n_duukaas" class="skt-link">Māṇḍūkās</a><br> <a href="#entry-ma_n_duuka" class="skt-link">maṇḍūka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मणि</span> <i>maṇi</i> <a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> joyau, pierre précieuse, perle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gland du pénis; clitoris.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nibandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇibandha</i> [<a href="#entry-bandha" class="skt-link">bandha</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nibandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> port de bijoux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> poignet (où l'on porte des bijoux).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nibhadra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇibhadra</i> [<a href="#entry-bhadra" class="skt-link">bhadra</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nibhadra" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [DKC.] np. du roi <a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a> Maṇibhadra «Fortuné en joyaux»; <a href="#entry-Utaaraavalii" class="skt-link">Tārāvalī</a> est sa fille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nibhuumi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇibhūmi</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuumi" class="skt-link">bhūmi</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nibhuumi" class="skt-link">f.</a> pièce d'un palais pour le bain, au sol fait d'une mosaïque en pierres précieuses.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nibhuumikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇibhūmikā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuumikaa" class="skt-link">bhūmikā</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nibhuumikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> mosaïque en pierres précieuses.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nibhuumikaakarma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇibhūmikākarma</i> [<a href="#entry-karma" class="skt-link">karma</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nibhuumikaakarma" class="skt-link">n.</a> la confection de mosaïques en pierres précieuses, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-ma_nibhuumikaa" class="skt-link">maṇibhūmikā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nigriiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇigrīva</i> [<a href="#entry-griivaa" class="skt-link">grīvā</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nigriiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du génie [<a href="#entry-guhyaka" class="skt-link">guhyaka</a>] Maṇigrīva «Gorge précieuse», fils de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Unalakuubara" class="skt-link">Nalakūbara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मणिक</span> <i>maṇika</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">maṇi</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nika" class="skt-link">m.</a> joyau, gemme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nika_n_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇikaṇṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kaṇṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nika_n_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. geai bleu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Maṇikaṇṭha, épith. d'Ayyappā «portant au cou un joyau» (sur le collier donné à sa naissance par <a href="#entry-Umohinii" class="skt-link">Mohinī</a> et <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nika_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇikaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_na" class="skt-link">kaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nika_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Maṇikaṇa, ouvrage de <a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a> d'auteur inconnu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nikar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇikarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-kar_na" class="skt-link">karṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ma_nikar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nikar_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nikar_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇikarṇī</i> qui porte un ornement à l'oreille — a. <a href="#entry-ma_nikar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nikar_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nikar_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇikarṇī</i> myth. np. de Maṇikarṇī, déesse tutélaire [<a href="#entry-adhi_s_thaatrii" class="skt-link">adhiṣṭhātrī</a>] de Bénarès.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nikar_nikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇikarṇikā</i> [<a href="#entry-kar_nikaa" class="skt-link">karṇikā</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nikar_nikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de l'étang sacré Maṇikarṇikā à Bénarès; <a href="#entry-Uma_nikar_nii" class="skt-link">Maṇikarṇī</a> en est la déesse tutélaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [DKC.] np. de Maṇikarṇikā, fille du prince <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dagho_sa" class="skt-link">Caṇḍaghoṣa</a> et d'<a href="#entry-Uaacaaravatii" class="skt-link">Ācāravatī</a>; elle fut orpheline de naissance; le roi <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dasi_mha" class="skt-link">Caṇḍasiṃha</a> ordonna qu'elle soit élevée en secret dans un palais souterrain, qu'<a href="#entry-Uarthapaala" class="skt-link">Arthapāla</a> découvrit en creusant un tunnel; sa mère avait perdu un pari aux dés avec <a href="#entry-Ukaantimatii" class="skt-link">Kāntimatī</a> avant sa naissance, et de ce fait elle devait épouser son fils <a href="#entry-Uarthapaala" class="skt-link">Arthapāla</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nikar_nikaagha_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇikarṇikāghaṭṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-gha_t_ta" class="skt-link">ghaṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nikar_nikaagha_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du Maṇikarṇikāghaṭṭa, 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ lieu de crémation à Bénarès.<br> <a href="#entry-ma_nikar_nikaa" class="skt-link">maṇikarṇikā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ma_nimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇimatī</i> orné de joyaux — <a href="#entry-ma_nimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇimatī</i> myth. np. de Maṇimatī «Pleine de joyaux», riche capitale des <a href="#entry-nivaatakavacaas" class="skt-link">nivātakavacās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nipadma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇipadma</i> [<a href="#entry-padma" class="skt-link">padma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ma_nipadma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nipadma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nipadmaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇipadmā</i> portant le joyau et le lotus — <a href="#entry-ma_nipadmaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṇipadmā</i> bd. np. de Maṇipadmā «Celle qui porte le joyau et le lotus», épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uavalokitezvara" class="skt-link">Avalokiteśvara</a> sous sa forme féminine.<br> <i>oṃ</i> <i>maṇipadme</i> <i>hum</i> bd. «Ô Toi qui porte le joyau et le lotus», <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> bouddhiste évoquant <a href="#entry-Uma_nipadmaa" class="skt-link">Maṇipadmā</a>; parfois appelé <a href="#entry-vidyaa_sa_dak_sarii" class="skt-link">vidyāṣaḍakṣarī</a> ou formule en 6 syllabes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_niprabhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇiprabhā</i> [<a href="#entry-prabhaa_2" class="skt-link">prabhā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_niprabhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Maṇiprabhā, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Uyogasuutra" class="skt-link">Yogasūtra</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uraamaananda_2" class="skt-link">Rāmānanda_2</a> <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nipravaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇipravāla</i> [<a href="#entry-pravaala" class="skt-link">pravāla</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nipravaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> langue littéraire médiévale du <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>, mélange de sanskrit et de <i>malayālam</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nipuura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇipūra</i> [<a href="#entry-puura" class="skt-link">pūra</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nipuura" class="skt-link">m.</a> nombril — <a href="#entry-ma_nipuura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville (mod. Imphal au Manipur) et du royaume de Maṇipūra ou Maṇipura «Abondant en joyaux»; cf. <a href="#entry-Ucitravaahana" class="skt-link">Citravāhana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. ancienne ville du <a href="#entry-Ukalifga" class="skt-link">Kaliṅga</a>, d'emplacement inconnu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ centre d'énergie [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>], situé au niveau du plexus solaire; il est représenté par un lotus bleu à 10 pétales; au centre, un triangle rouge y symbolise l'élément feu, et porte le <a href="#entry-biijamantra" class="skt-link">bījamantra</a> ‘<i>raṃ</i>’; ce <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a> contrôle le sens de la vue; il est le centre psychique de la force vitale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_niraaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇirāga</i> [<a href="#entry-raaga" class="skt-link">rāga</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_niraaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> coloration d'un joyau — <a href="#entry-ma_niraaga" class="skt-link">n.</a> rubis; syn. <a href="#entry-maa_nikya" class="skt-link">māṇikya</a>, <a href="#entry-padmaraaga" class="skt-link">padmarāga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_niraagaakaraj_naana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇirāgākarajñāna</i> [<a href="#entry-aakara" class="skt-link">ākara</a>-<a href="#entry-j_naana" class="skt-link">jñāna</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_niraagaakaraj_naana" class="skt-link">n.</a> la connaissance des couleurs et lieux d'origine des gemmes, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-ma_niraaga" class="skt-link">maṇirāga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nistambha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṇistambha</i> [<a href="#entry-stambha" class="skt-link">stambha</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nistambha" class="skt-link">m.</a> poteau au centre d'un bassin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pilier de cristal.<br> <a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">maṇi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मठ</span> <i>maṭha</i> <a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> cellule d'ermite; cloître, ermitage, monastère, lieu de résidence de renonçants [<a href="#entry-sannyaasin" class="skt-link">sannyāsin</a>]; collège de jeunes prêtres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_thaadhipati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṭhādhipati</i> [<a href="#entry-adhipati" class="skt-link">adhipati</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_thaadhipati" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. supérieur de monastère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_thaamnaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṭhāmnāya</i> [<a href="#entry-aamnaaya" class="skt-link">āmnāya</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_thaamnaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Maṭhāmnāya, texte spécifiant les régulations d'un monastère [<a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a>], attribué à <a href="#entry-Uzafkaraacaarya" class="skt-link">Śaṅkarācārya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_ms">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मांस्</span> <i>māṃs</i> <a href="#entry-maa_ms" class="skt-link">n.</a> viande, chair.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_msa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मांस</span> <i>māṃsa</i> [<a href="#entry-maa_ms" class="skt-link">māṃs</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_msa" class="skt-link">n.</a> viande, chair <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pulpe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>māṃsāni</i> id. — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(māṃsīyati)</i> avoir envie de viande <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>membrum</i>; ang. member; fr. membre; ru. meso; lithuanian mesa.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_msaazana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṃsāśana</i> [<a href="#entry-azana_2" class="skt-link">aśana_2</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_msaazana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de manger de la viande; état de carnivore.<br> <a href="#entry-maa_msa" class="skt-link">māṃsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_msarasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṃsarasa</i> [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_msarasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> bouillon ou jus de viande <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sang.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_msatattva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṃsatattva</i> [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_msatattva" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. sujet de la consommation de la viande.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_msatattvaviveka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṃsatattvaviveka</i> [<a href="#entry-viveka" class="skt-link">viveka</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_msatattvaviveka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Māṃsatattvaviveka «Controverse au sujet de la viande», traité de coutume [<a href="#entry-sm_rti" class="skt-link">smṛti</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uvizvanaatha_3" class="skt-link">Viśvanātha_3</a>; il y justifie la consommation de viande (non bovine) par les brâhmanes du l'Inde du Nord, et fustige le végétarianisme des brâhmanes de l'Inde du Sud comme étant d'influence bouddhiste.<br> <a href="#entry-maa_msatattva" class="skt-link">māṃsatattva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_msii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मांसी</span> <i>māṃsī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-maa_msa" class="skt-link">māṃsa</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_msii" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-ja_taamaa_msii" class="skt-link">jaṭāmāṃsī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_n_dava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माण्डव</span> <i>māṇḍava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ma_n_da" class="skt-link">maṇḍa</a><a href="#entry--va" class="skt-link">-va</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_n_dava" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. [«lento»] se dit d'un hymne [<a href="#entry-saaman" class="skt-link">sāman</a>] à chanter lentement — <a href="#entry-maa_n_davii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māṇḍavī</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Māṇḍavī, fille aînée du roi <a href="#entry-Ukuzadhvaja_1" class="skt-link">Kuśadhvaja_1</a>; elle épousa <a href="#entry-Ubharata_1" class="skt-link">Bharata_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_n_davya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माण्डव्य</span> <i>māṇḍavya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ma_n_du" class="skt-link">maṇḍu</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_n_davya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de l'ascète Māṇḍavya «(de la lignée de) <a href="#entry-Uma_n_du" class="skt-link">Maṇḍu</a>»; il méditait à son ermitage quand des voleurs vinrent y cacher leur butin; ayant fait vœu de silence [<a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a>], il ne répondit pas aux questions des soldats du roi, fut accusé du vol, et empalé sur un trident; voyant qu'il ne mourait pas, et que sa colère était grande, le roi le fit détacher et s'excusa de l'injustice, mais on ne put retirer le pal qui l'embrochait; <a href="#entry-Umaa_n_davya" class="skt-link">Māṇḍavya</a> alla se plaindre de son sort à <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a>, qui lui dit qu'il l'avait mérité car, enfant, il avait empalé un papillon sur un brin d'herbe; <a href="#entry-Umaa_n_davya" class="skt-link">Māṇḍavya</a> décréta que nul n'est responsable de ses actes avant l'âge de 14 ans, et il maudit <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a> en le condamnant à s'incarner sur Terre comme fils d'une servante [<a href="#entry-zuudra" class="skt-link">śūdra</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Uvidura" class="skt-link">Vidura</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_n_duuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माण्डूक</span> <i>māṇḍūka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ma_n_duuka" class="skt-link">maṇḍūka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maa_n_duuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maa_n_duuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maa_n_duukii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māṇḍūkī</i> lit. concernant la <a href="#entry-ma_n_duukazik_saa" class="skt-link">maṇḍūkaśikṣā</a> — <a href="#entry-maa_n_duuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. soc. np. de l'école védique des Māṇḍūkās.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_n_duukaayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṇḍūkāyana</i> [<a href="#entry-ayana" class="skt-link">ayana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maa_n_duukaayana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maa_n_duukaayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. qui suit la doctrine de la grenouille [<a href="#entry-Uma_n_duukazik_saa" class="skt-link">Maṇḍūkaśikṣā</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. soc. école védique des <a href="#entry-Umaa_n_duukaas" class="skt-link">Māṇḍūkās</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_n_duukeya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṇḍūkeya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_n_duukeya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du maître Māṇḍūkeya — <a href="#entry-maa_n_duukeya" class="skt-link">n.</a> sa doctrine.<br> <a href="#entry-maa_n_duuka" class="skt-link">māṇḍūka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_n_duukya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माण्डूक्य</span> <i>māṇḍūkya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ma_n_duuka" class="skt-link">maṇḍūka</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] iic. phil. relatif à la doctrine de la grenouille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_n_duukyakaarikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṇḍūkyakārikā</i> [<a href="#entry-kaarikaa" class="skt-link">kārikā</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_n_duukyakaarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Māṇḍūkyakārikā, commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Umaa_n_duukyopani_sad" class="skt-link">Māṇḍūkyopaniṣad</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Ugau_dapaada" class="skt-link">Gauḍapāda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_n_duukyopani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṇḍūkyopaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_n_duukyopani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Māṇḍūkyopaniṣad, leçon [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] de l'<a href="#entry-Uatharvaveda" class="skt-link">Atharvaveda</a>; elle explique le sens de l'<a href="#entry-ofkaara" class="skt-link">oṃkāra</a> par la triade [<a href="#entry-avasthaatraya" class="skt-link">avasthātraya</a>] des trois stades de la conscience <a href="#entry-jaagrat" class="skt-link">jāgrat</a>, <a href="#entry-svapna" class="skt-link">svapna</a> et <a href="#entry-su_supti" class="skt-link">suṣupti</a>, et le silence qui suit comme l'état <a href="#entry-turiiya" class="skt-link">turīya</a> de libération.<br> <a href="#entry-maa_n_duukya" class="skt-link">māṇḍūkya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_nava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माणव</span> <i>māṇava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maa_nava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maa_nava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maa_navaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māṇavā</i> gamin, gars; garçon; not. jeune <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> péj. petit homme, nabot <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ornement de perles de 16 (ou 20) rangs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_navaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṇavaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_navaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> gamin, gars; écolier, étudiant brahmanique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> parure à 16 rangs de perles — <a href="#entry-maa_navikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māṇavikā</i> jeune fille, demoiselle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_naviina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṇavīna</i> [<a href="#entry--iina" class="skt-link">-īna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maa_naviina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maa_naviina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maa_naviinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māṇavīnā</i> bon pour les écoliers.<br> <a href="#entry-maa_nava" class="skt-link">māṇava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_nika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माणिक</span> <i>māṇika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ma_nika" class="skt-link">maṇika</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_nika" class="skt-link">m.</a> bijoutier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_nikya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माणिक्य</span> <i>māṇikya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ma_nika" class="skt-link">maṇika</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_nikya" class="skt-link">n.</a> rubis; syn. <a href="#entry-ma_niraaga" class="skt-link">maṇirāga</a>, <a href="#entry-padmaraaga" class="skt-link">padmarāga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माष</span> <i>māṣa</i> <a href="#entry-maa_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maa_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. bot. <i>Phaseolus radiatus</i>, fève <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mesure de poids d'or, valant 5 <a href="#entry-k_r_s_nala" class="skt-link">kṛṣṇala</a> (environ 2 g) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> valeur de ce poids d'or.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṣaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> fève, haricot — <a href="#entry-maa_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> mesure de poids d'or, valant 8 graines de <a href="#entry-gu_njaa" class="skt-link">guñjā</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-hema" class="skt-link">hema</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maa_sa" class="skt-link">māṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_thara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माठर</span> <i>māṭhara</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-maa_thara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. supérieur d'un monastère ou d'une école <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de l'<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a> Māṭhara; on lui doit un commentaire des <a href="#entry-Usaafkhyakaarikaas" class="skt-link">Sāṃkhyakārikās</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Umaa_tharav_rtti" class="skt-link">Māṭharavṛtti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_tharav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māṭharavṛtti</i> phil. np. de la Māṭharavṛtti, commentaire des <a href="#entry-Usaafkhyakaarikaas" class="skt-link">Sāṃkhyakārikās</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Umaa_thara" class="skt-link">Māṭhara</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maa_thara" class="skt-link">māṭhara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मा</span> <i>mā_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. md. <i>(mimīte)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(māti)</i> pft. <i>(mamau)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mita_1</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>meya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>ni</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> mesurer, délimiter, marquer; comparer avec ⟨i.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (<i>māti</i>) correspondre en mesure; être de la taille de ⟨g.⟩; tenir dans ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mesurer à, servir à ⟨dat.⟩; accorder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (av. <a href="#entry-na" class="skt-link">na</a>) ne pas admettre ⟨g.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. inférer, conclure — ps. <i>(mīyate)</i> être mesuré — ca. <i>(māpayati)</i> mesurer; ériger <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μετρον; lat. <i>mensura</i>, <i>metor</i>; fr. mesurer, mètre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मा</span> <i>mā_2</i> part. de négation prohibitive (av. inj. ou imp. ou opt. ou subj.) non, ne ... pas, afin que ... ne ... pas (qqf. suivie de <a href="#entry-sma" class="skt-link">sma</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μη; lat. <i>ne</i>.<br> <i>x</i> <i>mā</i> <i>y</i> y plutôt que x.<br> <i>mo</i> [<a href="#entry-u" class="skt-link">u</a>] adv. (<a href="#entry-prag_rhya" class="skt-link">pragṛhya</a>) et non.<br> <i>māstu</i> [<a href="#entry-astu" class="skt-link">astu</a>] n'en faites rien<br> <i>mā</i> <i>bhūḥ</i> ne sois pas<br> <i>tanmākārṣīḥ</i> ne fais pas ça!<br> <i>mākarot</i> qu'il ne le fasse pas<br> <i>maivam</i> certainement pas!<br> <i>maivaṃ</i> <i>vocas</i> ne parle pas ainsi!<br> <i>mābhaiṣīḥ</i> var. <i>mābhaiḥ</i> n'aies pas peur.<br> <i>mā</i> <i>nāma</i> <i>...</i> surtout pas ...<br> <i>mā</i> <i>nāma</i> <i>rakṣiṇaḥ</i> pourvu que ça ne soit pas les gardes.<br> <i>mā</i> <i>bhūt</i> <i>...</i> (technique) il n'en est rien, sinon ... <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> la règle ne vaut pas pour un cas tel que ...<br> <i>mā</i> <i>bhūd</i> <i>āgataḥ</i> ne devrait-il pas être là?<br> <i>mā</i> <i>jīvan</i> puisse-t-il ne pas vivre!
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_3">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मा</span> <i>mā_3</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] pr. <i>(mimāti)</i> mugir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_4">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मा</span> <i>mā_4</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(mayate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(mayati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ni</i><i>)</i> échanger, troquer (contre ⟨i.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maa_5">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मा</span> <i>mā_5</i> cl. pour <i>mām</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maaciram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माचिरम्</span> <i>māciram</i> [<a href="#entry-maa_2" class="skt-link">mā_2</a>-<a href="#entry-ciram" class="skt-link">ciram</a>] adv. bientôt; vite [«sans perdre un instant»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माद</span> <i>māda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mad_1" class="skt-link">mad_1</a>] <a href="#entry-maada" class="skt-link">m.</a> ivresse, intoxication; passion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> combat, guerre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मादन</span> <i>mādana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mad_1" class="skt-link">mad_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādanā</i> intoxicant, excitant, enivrant — <a href="#entry-maadana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mādana «l'Enivrant», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> — <a href="#entry-maadana" class="skt-link">n.</a> intoxication, ivresse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadayitnu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādayitnu</i> [<a href="#entry--yitnu" class="skt-link">-yitnu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadayitnu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadayitnu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadayitnu" class="skt-link">f.</a> intoxicant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>madayiṣṇu</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-maada" class="skt-link">māda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माधव</span> <i>mādhava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">madhu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhavī</i> printanier — <a href="#entry-maadhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> nom du second mois véd. du printemps; printemps <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. descendant du roi <a href="#entry-Umadhu" class="skt-link">Madhu</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-yaadava" class="skt-link">yādava</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. not. np. de Mādhava, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «descendant de <a href="#entry-Umadhu" class="skt-link">Madhu</a>» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. désigne un brâhmane descendant de <a href="#entry-Umadhu" class="skt-link">Madhu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. relatif à l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Umadhu_1" class="skt-link">Madhu_1</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Mādhavācārya, ministre du roi <a href="#entry-Ubukka_1" class="skt-link">Bukka_1</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvijayanagara" class="skt-link">Vijayanagara</a> et frère aîné de <a href="#entry-Usaaya_na" class="skt-link">Sāyaṇa</a> (14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il servit de mentor à trois générations de rois de <a href="#entry-Uvijayanagara" class="skt-link">Vijayanagara</a>; considéré comme un saint, un temple lui est consacré; d'obédience <a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>, il devint supérieur du monastère [<a href="#entry-ma_thaadhipati" class="skt-link">maṭhādhipati</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uz_rfgerii" class="skt-link">Śṛṅgerī</a> en 1331; il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Ukaalanir_naya" class="skt-link">Kālanirṇaya</a> et du <a href="#entry-Uparaazaramaadhaviiya" class="skt-link">Parāśaramādhavīya</a>; on lui attribue aussi le <a href="#entry-Usarvadarzanasafgraha" class="skt-link">Sarvadarśanasaṃgraha</a>, le <a href="#entry-Upa_ncadazii" class="skt-link">Pañcadaśī</a>, et le <a href="#entry-Ujiivanmuktiviveka" class="skt-link">Jīvanmuktiviveka</a>; on l'appelle aussi <a href="#entry-Uvidyaara_nya" class="skt-link">Vidyāraṇya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du mathématicien et astronome Mādhava_1, de famille brâhmane <i>nampūtiri</i> (1340–1425); il fonda une école de mathématiques au <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>; il calcula une approximation de π correcte jusqu'à la 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ décimale; on connaît son œuvre d'analyse par la <a href="#entry-Uga_nitayuktibhaa_saa" class="skt-link">Gaṇitayuktibhāṣā</a> de <a href="#entry-Ujye_s_thadeva" class="skt-link">Jyeṣṭhadeva</a>; ses œuvres préservées jusqu'à nous sont le Venvaroha et la Sphuṭacandrāpti, qui traitent du calcul de la position de la Lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. np. de Mādhava_2, auteur du <a href="#entry-Umaadhavanidaana" class="skt-link">Mādhavanidāna</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de Mādhava_3, héros de la pièce de théâtre <a href="#entry-Umaalatiimaadhava" class="skt-link">Mālatīmādhava</a> — <a href="#entry-maadhavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhavī</i> natu. bot. <i>Hiptage benghalensis</i>, arbuste grimpant aux fleurs blanches odoriférantes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. jasmin; cf. <a href="#entry-maadhavikaa" class="skt-link">mādhavikā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mādhavī «Jasmine», fille du roi <a href="#entry-Uyayaati" class="skt-link">Yayāti</a>; elle vivait recluse en ermite, vêtue d'une peau de daim; elle avait reçu le don d'être toujours vierge; son père la confia à <a href="#entry-Ugaalava" class="skt-link">Gālava</a> pour qu'elle se marie; afin que ce dernier puisse payer sa dette d'étudiant [<a href="#entry-gurudak_si_naa" class="skt-link">gurudakṣiṇā</a>], elle épousa le roi d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> <a href="#entry-Uharyazva" class="skt-link">Haryaśva</a>, dont elle eut pour fils <a href="#entry-Uvasumanaa" class="skt-link">Vasumanā</a>; puis le roi de <a href="#entry-Ukaazii" class="skt-link">Kāśī</a> <a href="#entry-Udivodaasa" class="skt-link">Divodāsa</a>, dont elle eut pour fils <a href="#entry-Upratardana" class="skt-link">Pratardana</a>; puis le roi de <a href="#entry-Ubhojapurii" class="skt-link">Bhojapurī</a> <a href="#entry-Uuziinara" class="skt-link">Uśīnara</a>, dont elle eut pour fils <a href="#entry-Uzibi" class="skt-link">Śibi</a>; elle épousa enfin <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a>, dont elle eut pour fils <a href="#entry-Ua_s_taka" class="skt-link">Aṣṭaka</a>; lorsque son père <a href="#entry-Uyayaati" class="skt-link">Yayāti</a> fut déchu du ciel, elle lui accorda la moitié de ses mérites.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhavacandrakara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhavacandrakara</i> [<a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">candra</a>-<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhavacandrakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. np. du médecin Mādavacandrakara; cf. <a href="#entry-Umaadhava_2" class="skt-link">Mādhava_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhavaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhavaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhavaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> hydromel — <a href="#entry-maadhavikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhavikā</i> natu. bot. <i>Gærtnera racemosa</i>, jasmin blanc, au parfum suave; syn. <a href="#entry-maadhavii" class="skt-link">mādhavī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhavakara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhavakara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhavakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. np. du médecin Mādavakara ou <a href="#entry-Umaadavacandrakara" class="skt-link">Mādavacandrakara</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Umaadhava_2" class="skt-link">Mādhava_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhavanidaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhavanidāna</i> [<a href="#entry-nidhaana" class="skt-link">nidhāna</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhavanidaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. np. du Mādhavanidāna, épith. du <a href="#entry-Urogavinizcaya" class="skt-link">Rogaviniścaya</a> de <a href="#entry-Umaadhava_2" class="skt-link">Mādhava_2</a>, synthèse des connaissances médicales de l'Inde sur 2000 ans, écrit entre le 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ et le 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècles; il comporte not. une compilation des traités de la <a href="#entry-Ub_rhattrayii" class="skt-link">Bṛhattrayī</a>, et un glossaire important, le <a href="#entry-Umadhukoza" class="skt-link">Madhukośa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhaviiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhavīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhaviiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhaviiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhaviiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhavīyā</i> lit. composé par Mādhava ou <a href="#entry-Umaadhavaacaarya" class="skt-link">Mādhavācārya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. np. du Mādhavīya <a href="#entry-Udhaatuv_rtti" class="skt-link">Dhātuvṛtti</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Umaadhaviiyadhaatuv_rtti" class="skt-link">Mādhavīyadhātuvṛtti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhaviiyadhaatuv_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhavīyadhātuvṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-dhaatuv_rtti" class="skt-link">dhātuvṛtti</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhaviiyadhaatuv_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. np. de la Mādhavīyadhātuvṛtti, traité sur les racines verbales de <a href="#entry-Usaaya_na" class="skt-link">Sāyaṇa</a> (dédié à son frère <a href="#entry-Umaadhavaacaarya" class="skt-link">Mādhavācārya</a>); il restitue le <a href="#entry-Udhaatuv_rtti" class="skt-link">Dhātuvṛtti</a> utilisé par <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maadhaviiya" class="skt-link">mādhavīya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-maadhava" class="skt-link">mādhava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माधव्य</span> <i>mādhavya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">madhu</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de Mādhavya «Printanier», bouffon [<a href="#entry-viduu_saka" class="skt-link">vidūṣaka</a>] du drame de <a href="#entry-Uzakuntalaa" class="skt-link">Śakuntalā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माधु</span> <i>mādhu</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">madhu</a>] iic. de l'abeille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhukara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhukara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhukara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhukara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhukarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhukarī</i> relatif à l'abeille ou au miel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> semblable à l'abeille — <a href="#entry-maadhukarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhukarī</i> soc. [«butinage»] don de nourriture à des renonçants ou des étudiants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhukoza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhukośa</i> [<a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhukoza" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. np. du Madhukośa, glossaire du <a href="#entry-Umaadhavanidaana" class="skt-link">Mādhavanidāna</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maadhu" class="skt-link">mādhu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhuparkika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माधुपर्किक</span> <i>mādhuparkika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhuparka" class="skt-link">madhuparka</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhuparkika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhuparkika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhuparkikii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhuparkikī</i> soc. présenté comme cadeau à un hôte avec le <a href="#entry-madhuparka" class="skt-link">madhuparka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhurya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माधुर्य</span> <i>mādhurya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhura" class="skt-link">madhura</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhurya" class="skt-link">n.</a> douceur; grâce, charme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माध्व</span> <i>mādhva</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhva" class="skt-link">madhva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhvā</i> phil. tenant de la doctrine de <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uvyaasatiirtha" class="skt-link">Vyāsatīrtha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माध्य</span> <i>mādhya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">madhya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhyā</i> central, médian.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhyama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माध्यम</span> <i>mādhyama</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">madhyama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhyama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhyama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhyamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhyamā</i> relatif au milieu; moyen; central.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhyamaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माध्यमक</span> <i>mādhyamaka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">madhyama</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhyamaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhyamaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhyamikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhyamikā</i> moyen; central <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. concernant l'atmosphère ou ciel intermédiaire — <a href="#entry-maadhyamikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhyamikā</i> lit. np. de la Mādyamikā, section centrale du <a href="#entry-Ukaa_thaka" class="skt-link">Kāṭhaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhyamika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माध्यमिक</span> <i>mādhyamika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">madhyama</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhyamika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhyamika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhyamikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhyamikā</i> phil. relatif à la doctrine bouddhiste de la Voie du Milieu développée par <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a> — <a href="#entry-maadhyamika" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>mādhyamikās</i> bd. adeptes de cette doctrine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhyamikav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhyamikavṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhyamikav_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> bd. np. de la Mādhyamikavṛtti, commentaire par <a href="#entry-Ucandrakiirti" class="skt-link">Candrakīrti</a> de la doctrine de <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uprasannapadaa" class="skt-link">Prasannapadā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maadhyamika" class="skt-link">mādhyamika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhyandina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
