    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माध्यंदिन</span> <i>mādhyaṃdina</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhyandina" class="skt-link">madhyaṃdina</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhya_mdina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhya_mdina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhya_mdinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhyaṃdinī</i> relatif à midi; méridional <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> concernant <a href="#entry-Umadhyandina" class="skt-link">Madhyaṃdina</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. qualifie le deuxième chant de louange [<a href="#entry-stotra" class="skt-link">stotra</a>] lors du sacrifice <a href="#entry-jyoti_s_toma" class="skt-link">jyotiṣṭoma</a> pour le pressurage [<a href="#entry-savana_1" class="skt-link">savana_1</a>] de midi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhyandinasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhyaṃdinasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhya_mdinasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-maadhyandiniiyazaakhaa" class="skt-link">mādhyaṃdinīyaśākhā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhyandiniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhyaṃdinīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhya_mdiniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhya_mdiniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhya_mdiniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhyaṃdinīyā</i> soc. relatif à l'oblation de midi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. relatif à l'école de <a href="#entry-Umadhyandina" class="skt-link">Madhyaṃdina</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhyandiniiyazaakhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mādhyaṃdinīyaśākhā</i> [<a href="#entry-zaakhaa" class="skt-link">śākhā</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhya_mdiniiyazaakhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. recension de la <a href="#entry-vaajasaneyisa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">vājasaneyisaṃhitā</a> (<a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> blanc) due à <a href="#entry-Umadhyandina" class="skt-link">Madhyaṃdina</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maadhyandiniiya" class="skt-link">mādhyaṃdinīya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-maadhyandina" class="skt-link">mādhyaṃdina</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhyastha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माध्यस्थ</span> <i>mādhyastha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhyastha" class="skt-link">madhyastha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadhyastha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhyastha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadhyasthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādhyasthā</i> [«qui adopte une position moyenne»] indifférent, neutre; impartial — <a href="#entry-maadhyastha" class="skt-link">n.</a> indifférence; neutralité; impartialité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadhyasthya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माध्यस्थ्य</span> <i>mādhyasthya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madhyastha" class="skt-link">madhyastha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maadhyasthya" class="skt-link">n.</a> indifférence; neutralité; impartialité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मादिन्</span> <i>mādin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mad_1" class="skt-link">mad_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maadin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maadin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maadinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādinī</i> intoxicant; stupéfiant — <a href="#entry-maadinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mādinī</i> chanvre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maadrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माद्री</span> <i>mādrī</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-madra" class="skt-link">madra</a>] <a href="#entry-maadrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Mādrī, princesse <a href="#entry-madra" class="skt-link">madra</a>, 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ épouse de <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_du" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍu</a>, mère des jumeaux <a href="#entry-Unakula" class="skt-link">Nakula</a> et <a href="#entry-Usahadeva" class="skt-link">Sahadeva</a> par les <a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a>, grâce au <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> que lui avait prêté <a href="#entry-Ukuntii" class="skt-link">Kuntī</a>; elle fit s'accomplir le destin de <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_du" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍu</a> en se donnant à lui, puis se jeta sur son bûcher funéraire comme <a href="#entry-satii" class="skt-link">satī</a>; on la considère incarnation de <a href="#entry-Udh_rti" class="skt-link">Dhṛti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maafgalika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माङ्गलिक</span> <i>māṅgalika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mafgala" class="skt-link">maṅgala</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maafgalika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maafgalika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maafgalikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māṅgalikā</i> qui désire le succès <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui porte chance — <a href="#entry-maafgalika" class="skt-link">n.</a> objet qui porte chance, amulette.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maafgalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माङ्गल्य</span> <i>māṅgalya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mafgala" class="skt-link">maṅgala</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maafgalya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maafgalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maafgalyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māṅgalyā</i> qui porte chance, qui donne la fortune ou le bonheur — <a href="#entry-maafgalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> objet ou cérémonie de bon augure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maagadha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मागध</span> <i>māgadha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-magadha" class="skt-link">magadha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maagadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maagadha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maagadhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māgadhī</i> relatif au pays <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a> — <a href="#entry-maagadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> prince du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. homme de caste mélangée, not. de mère <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a> et de père <a href="#entry-vaizya" class="skt-link">vaiśya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. barde; panégyriste princier; espion informateur du roi — <a href="#entry-maagadhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māgadhī</i> langue du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>; elle était la langue administrative de l'empire <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a>, et la langue de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>; elle est utilisée dans le théâtre sanskrit pour les personnages de rang inférieur, not. les serviteurs et les brigands <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> princesse du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cumin, fenouil, et autres épices anisées.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maagadhabhaa_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māgadhabhāṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_saa" class="skt-link">bhāṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-maagadhabhaa_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> langue du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>, parlée not. par <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-maagadhii" class="skt-link">māgadhī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maagadha" class="skt-link">māgadha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maagha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माघ</span> <i>māgha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-magha" class="skt-link">magha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maaghii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māghī</i> relatif aux <a href="#entry-Umaghaas" class="skt-link">Maghās</a> — <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. le mois des <a href="#entry-Umaghaas" class="skt-link">Maghās</a> (janvier-février) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de Māgha, poète du 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur du <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> <a href="#entry-Uzizupaalavadha" class="skt-link">Śiśupālavadha</a> — <a href="#entry-maaghii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māghī</i> astr. la pleine lune du mois des <a href="#entry-Umaghaas" class="skt-link">Maghās</a>.<br> <i>māghe</i> <i>santi</i> <i>trayo</i> <i>guṇāḥ</i> La poésie de <a href="#entry-Umaagha" class="skt-link">Māgha</a> possède toutes les qualités.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maaghamela">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māghamela</i> [<a href="#entry-mela" class="skt-link">mela</a>] <a href="#entry-maaghamela" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-makarasafkraanti" class="skt-link">makarasaṃkrānti</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">māgha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maaghya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माघ्य</span> <i>māghya</i> [<a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">māgha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maaghya" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. fleur du bot. <i>Jasminum multiflorum</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maahaaraa_s_tra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माहाराष्ट्र</span> <i>māhārāṣṭra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mahaaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">mahārāṣṭra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maahaaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maahaaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maahaaraa_s_trii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māhārāṣṭrī</i> géo. concernant le peuple et le pays des <a href="#entry-Umaraa_thaas" class="skt-link">Marāṭhās</a> — <a href="#entry-maahaaraa_s_trii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māhārāṣṭrī</i> leur langue, la Marāṭhī, «meilleur <a href="#entry-praak_rta" class="skt-link">prākṛta</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maahaatmya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माहात्म्य</span> <i>māhātmya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mahaatmya" class="skt-link">mahātmya</a>] <a href="#entry-maahaatmya" class="skt-link">n.</a> grandeur d'âme, magnanimité; majesté, dignité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> panégyrique — ifc. lit. glorification de (titre générique d'ouvrages glorifiant une divinité, une rivière ou un lieu saint); section d'éloges d'un ouvrage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maahezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माहेश्वर</span> <i>māheśvara</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mahezvara" class="skt-link">maheśvara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maahezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maahezvara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maahezvarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māheśvarī</i> myth. qui concerne <a href="#entry-Umahezvara" class="skt-link">Maheśvara</a>; qui honore <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> — <a href="#entry-maahezvarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māheśvarī</i> myth. np. de Māheśvarī, <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> de <a href="#entry-Umahezvara" class="skt-link">Maheśvara</a>, une <a href="#entry-saptamaat_rkaa" class="skt-link">saptamātṛkā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maahi_smatii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माहिष्मती</span> <i>māhiṣmatī</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mahi_smat" class="skt-link">mahiṣmat</a>] <a href="#entry-maahi_smatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Māhiṣmatī; c'était la capitale des rois <a href="#entry-Ukalacuri" class="skt-link">Kalacuri</a>, not. <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_naraaja" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇarāja</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Umahi_smatii" class="skt-link">Mahiṣmatī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maahi_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माहिष्य</span> <i>māhiṣya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mahi_sa" class="skt-link">mahiṣa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maahi_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. membre d'une caste mélangée (fils d'un <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a> et d'une <a href="#entry-vaizyaa" class="skt-link">vaiśyā</a> affectée aux soins du bétail).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माल</span> <i>māla</i> <a href="#entry-maala" class="skt-link">m.</a> territoire — <a href="#entry-maala" class="skt-link">n.</a> champ.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माला</span> <i>mālā</i> <a href="#entry-maalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> guirlande, couronne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> collier, chapelet — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(mālayati)</i> couronner; orner d'une guirlande.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalaakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālākāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-maalaakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> fleuriste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalaaphala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālāphala</i> [<a href="#entry-phala" class="skt-link">phala</a>] <a href="#entry-maalaaphala" class="skt-link">n.</a> graine de l'<i>Elæocarpus ganitrus</i> (dont on fait des chapelets); syn. <a href="#entry-rudraak_sa" class="skt-link">rudrākṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalatii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मालती</span> <i>mālatī</i> <a href="#entry-maalatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Jasminum grandiflorum</i>; ses fleurs blanches à l'odeur suave s'ouvrent le soir; syn. <a href="#entry-cetakii" class="skt-link">cetakī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jeune fille vierge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> clair de lune; nuit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de Mālātī, héroïne du <a href="#entry-Umaalatiimaadhava" class="skt-link">Mālatīmādhava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalatiimaadhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālatīmādhava</i> [<a href="#entry-maadhava" class="skt-link">mādhava</a>] <a href="#entry-maalatiimaadhava" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mālatīmādhava, pièce de théâtre de style comédie [<a href="#entry-prakara_na" class="skt-link">prakaraṇa</a>] en 10 actes de <a href="#entry-Ubhavabhuuti" class="skt-link">Bhavabhūti</a>; il conte les amours de l'étudiant <a href="#entry-Umaadhava_3" class="skt-link">Mādhava_3</a> avec <a href="#entry-Umaalaatii" class="skt-link">Mālātī</a>, fille de ministre.<br> <a href="#entry-maalatii" class="skt-link">mālatī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मालव</span> <i>mālava</i> <a href="#entry-maalava" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du Mālava, pays d'Inde centrale, à l'ouest du Deccan, au nord de la <a href="#entry-Unarmadaa" class="skt-link">Narmadā</a> (mod. Mālva); sa capitale était <a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a>-<a href="#entry-Uavanti" class="skt-link">Avanti</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uyazodharmaa" class="skt-link">Yaśodharmā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>mālavās</i> ses habitants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalavaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālavaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-maalavaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> pays <a href="#entry-maalava" class="skt-link">mālava</a> — f. cf. <a href="#entry-maalavikaa" class="skt-link">mālavikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalaviiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālavīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maalaviiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maalaviiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maalaviiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mālavīyā</i> provenant du Mālava, natif du Mālava.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalavikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मालविका</span> <i>mālavikā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-maalavaka" class="skt-link">mālavaka</a>] <a href="#entry-maalavikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de Mālavikā, héroïne du <a href="#entry-Umaalavikaagnimitra" class="skt-link">Mālavikāgnimitra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalavikaagnimitra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālavikāgnimitra</i> [<a href="#entry-agnimitra" class="skt-link">agnimitra</a>] <a href="#entry-maalavikaagnimitra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mālavikāgnimitra «<a href="#entry-Uagnimitra" class="skt-link">Agnimitra</a> et <a href="#entry-Umaalavikaa" class="skt-link">Mālavikā</a>», drame de <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a> (le roi <a href="#entry-Uagnimitra" class="skt-link">Agnimitra</a>, amoureux de la servante <a href="#entry-Umaalavikaa" class="skt-link">Mālavikā</a>, provoque la colère de ses épouses <a href="#entry-Uiraavatii_1" class="skt-link">Irāvatī_1</a> et <a href="#entry-Udhaari_nii" class="skt-link">Dhāriṇī</a>, mais <a href="#entry-Umaalavikaa" class="skt-link">Mālavikā</a> se révèle être une princesse en exil, et est acceptée comme co-épouse-tout est bien qui finit bien).<br> <a href="#entry-maalavikaa" class="skt-link">mālavikā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālavya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maalavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maalavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maalavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mālavyā</i> relatif au <a href="#entry-maalava" class="skt-link">mālava</a> — <a href="#entry-maalavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> type d'homme; cf. <a href="#entry-pa_ncamanu_syavibhaaga" class="skt-link">pañcamanuṣyavibhāga</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maalava" class="skt-link">mālava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मालय</span> <i>mālaya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-malaya" class="skt-link">malaya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maalaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maalaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maalayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mālayā</i> relatif au Malabar; issu du Malabar — <a href="#entry-maalaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> santal — <a href="#entry-maalaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> onguent de santal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मालिन्</span> <i>mālin</i> [<a href="#entry-maalaa" class="skt-link">mālā</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maalin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mālinī</i> qui porte une guirlande, couronné; ceint de ⟨i. iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-maalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mālinī</i> myth. np. de la rivière Mālinī, sur les berges de laquelle vivait le sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Mālinī_1 «Enguirlandée», fausse identité de <a href="#entry-Udraupadii" class="skt-link">Draupadī</a> dissimulée comme <a href="#entry-sairandhrii" class="skt-link">sairandhrī</a> à la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Uviraa_ta" class="skt-link">Virāṭa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. mètre poétique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [tantr.] np. de la déesse Mālinī_2, personnifiant la guirlande des lettres de l'alphabet dans l'ordre <a href="#entry-naadiphaantakrama" class="skt-link">nādiphāntakrama</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मालिनी</span> <i>mālinī</i> iic. f. de <a href="#entry-maalin" class="skt-link">mālin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maaliniivijayottara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālinīvijayottara</i> [<a href="#entry-vijaya" class="skt-link">vijaya</a>-<a href="#entry-uttara_1" class="skt-link">uttara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-maaliniivijayottara" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>mālinīvijayottaratantra</i>] lit. np. du Mālinīvijayottara <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>, ouvrage de la doctrine <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maalinii" class="skt-link">mālinī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalopamaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālopamā</i> [<a href="#entry-upamaa_2" class="skt-link">upamā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-maalopamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. litanie de comparaisons.<br> <a href="#entry-maalaa" class="skt-link">mālā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माल्य</span> <i>mālya</i> [<a href="#entry-maalaa" class="skt-link">mālā</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> guirlande, couronne; collier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalyagrathana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālyagrathana</i> [<a href="#entry-grathana" class="skt-link">grathana</a>] <a href="#entry-maalyagrathana" class="skt-link">n.</a> liage d'une guirlande.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalyagrathanavikalpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālyagrathanavikalpa</i> [<a href="#entry-vikalpa" class="skt-link">vikalpa</a>] <a href="#entry-maalyagrathanavikalpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> l'art de confection des guirlandes, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-maalyagrathana" class="skt-link">mālyagrathana</a> <br> <a href="#entry-maalya" class="skt-link">mālya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maalyagu_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mālyaguṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-maalyagu_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> fil d'un collier, d'une guirlande ou d'une couronne — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(mālyaguṇāyate)</i> être semblable au lien d'une guirlande <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> devenir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> apparaître comme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माम्</span> <i>mām</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-aham" class="skt-link">aham</a>] ind. me.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maamaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मामक</span> <i>māmaka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mamaka" class="skt-link">mamaka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maamaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maamaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maamakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māmakā</i> véd. mon, mien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maamakiina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māmakīna</i> [<a href="#entry--iina" class="skt-link">-īna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maamakiina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maamakiina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maamakiinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māmakīnā</i> mon, mien.<br> <a href="#entry-maamaka" class="skt-link">māmaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मान्</span> <i>mān</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(mānayati)</i> honorer, respecter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maana_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मान</span> <i>māna_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maana_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maana_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mānā</i> estimé, honoré — <a href="#entry-maana_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> opinion, conception <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> égard, respect, estime, honneur rendu; l'honneur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fierté, haute opinion de soi, orgueil, arrogance; caprice, bouderie, jalousie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maana_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मान</span> <i>māna_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-maana_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> mesure; dimension, taille, hauteur; extension, longueur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> proportion; ressemblance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. démonstration, preuve — ifc. fois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanaadhyak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānādhyakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-adhyak_sa" class="skt-link">adhyakṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-maanaadhyak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. superintendant des mesures.<br> <a href="#entry-maana_2" class="skt-link">māna_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मानस</span> <i>mānasa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maanasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maanasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maanasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mānasī</i> mental, spirituel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en pensées (not. récitation silencieuse) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> présent à l'esprit; imaginable, concevable — <a href="#entry-maanasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> esprit, compréhension; âme, sentiments <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>mānasasarovara</i>] géo. np. du Mānasasarovara «Lac de l'Esprit», à 4600 m d'altitude près du Mont <a href="#entry-Ukailaasa" class="skt-link">Kailāsa</a>, où prend naissance la Śutudrī (mod. Sutlej); c'est là que les oies sauvages [<a href="#entry-ha_msa" class="skt-link">haṃsa</a>] migrent pour s'accoupler au début de la saison des pluies — ifc. lit. [«lac spirituel de»] nom de recueils dévotionnels.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanasaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānasāra</i> [<a href="#entry-saara" class="skt-link">sāra</a>] <a href="#entry-maanasaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maanasaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> suffisance, morgue — <a href="#entry-maanasaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [DKC.] np. du roi du <a href="#entry-Umaalava" class="skt-link">Mālava</a> Mānasāra «Orgueilleux»; battu par son rival <a href="#entry-Uraajaha_msa" class="skt-link">Rājahaṃsa</a>, il implora <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> qui lui donna une masse d'armes magique lui permettant de regagner la victoire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanasaarazilpazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānasāraśilpaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zilpazaastra" class="skt-link">śilpaśāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-maanasaarazilpazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mānasāraśilpaśāstra, traité d'architecture, de sculpture et d'urbanisme.<br> <a href="#entry-maanasaara" class="skt-link">mānasāra</a> <br> <a href="#entry-maana_1" class="skt-link">māna_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanasapratyak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānasapratyakṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-pratyak_sa" class="skt-link">pratyakṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-maanasapratyak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] perception mentale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanasaputra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānasaputra</i> [<a href="#entry-putra" class="skt-link">putra</a>] <a href="#entry-maanasaputra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. un des 6 «fils de l'esprit» (de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>), créés pour peupler le Monde: <a href="#entry-Umariici" class="skt-link">Marīci</a>, <a href="#entry-Uatri" class="skt-link">Atri</a>, <a href="#entry-Uafgiraa" class="skt-link">Aṅgirā</a>, <a href="#entry-Upulastya" class="skt-link">Pulastya</a>, <a href="#entry-Upulaha" class="skt-link">Pulaha</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukratu" class="skt-link">Kratu</a>; parfois on inclut les dix géniteurs [<a href="#entry-prajaapati" class="skt-link">prajāpati</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanasavega">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānasavega</i> [<a href="#entry-vega" class="skt-link">vega</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maanasavega" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maanasavega" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maanasavegaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mānasavegā</i> rapide comme la pensée — <a href="#entry-maanasavega" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukathaasaritsaagara" class="skt-link">Kathāsaritsāgara</a>] np. du prince <a href="#entry-vidyaadhara" class="skt-link">vidyādhara</a> Mānasavega; il enleva la belle <a href="#entry-Umadanama_ncukaa" class="skt-link">Madanamañcukā</a>, dont <a href="#entry-Unaravaahanadatta" class="skt-link">Naravāhanadatta</a> était épris; sa sœur <a href="#entry-Uvegavatii" class="skt-link">Vegavatī</a> aidera ce dernier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanasollaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānasollāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-ullaasa" class="skt-link">ullāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-maanasollaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Mānasollāsa «Divertissement de l'esprit», ouvrage encyclopédique attribué au roi <a href="#entry-Ucaalukya" class="skt-link">Cālukya</a> <a href="#entry-Usomezvara_3" class="skt-link">Someśvara_3</a> (1129); il traite de l'acquisition et de l'administration d'un royaume, et des divertissements d'un roi, not. musicaux et culinaires; il comporte 5 sections [<a href="#entry-vi_mzati" class="skt-link">viṃśati</a>] de 20 chapitres [<a href="#entry-adhyaaya" class="skt-link">adhyāya</a>] chacune; on l'appelle aussi <a href="#entry-Uabhila_sitaarthacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">Abhilaṣitārthacintāmaṇi</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uupabhoga" class="skt-link">Upabhoga</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du Mānasollāsa_1, ouvrage <a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> de <a href="#entry-Usurezvara" class="skt-link">Sureśvara</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maanasa" class="skt-link">mānasa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मानव</span> <i>mānava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maanava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maanava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maanavii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mānavī</i> humain, propre à l'homme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. relatif à <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a> — <a href="#entry-maanava" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme, être humain <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. l'humanité; l'ensemble des races humaines.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanavadharmazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānavadharmaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-dharmazaastra" class="skt-link">dharmaśāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-maanavadharmazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> «Lois de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a>», code de lois traditionnelles brahmaniques, attribué à <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a>; il fut rédigé entre le 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle avant et le 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; syn. <a href="#entry-manusm_rti" class="skt-link">manusmṛti</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maanava" class="skt-link">mānava</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanavag_rhasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānavagṛhasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-g_rhasuutra" class="skt-link">gṛhasūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-maanavag_rhasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mānavagṛhasūtra, manuel véd. de rites domestiques; cf. <a href="#entry-Umaitraaya_niiyag_rhasuutra" class="skt-link">Maitrāyaṇīyagṛhasūtra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanavaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānavaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-maanavaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> garçon, jeune homme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maandhaat_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मान्धातृ</span> <i>māndhātṛ</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mandhaat_r" class="skt-link">mandhātṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-maandhaat_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Māndhātā «fils du pieux (roi)», né du ventre de son père <a href="#entry-Uyuvanaazva" class="skt-link">Yuvanāśva</a> à la suite d'une erreur rituelle; sur les conseils de ses ministres le roi l'abandonna dans les bois, mais les dieux le secoururent; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> le nomma <a href="#entry-Umaandhaataa" class="skt-link">Māṃdhātā</a> (<i>māṃ</i> <i>dhātṛ</i>), signifiant qu'il serait sa propre nourrice, et lui fit téter son gros orteil; il devint grand et fort, et monta sur le trône à la mort de son père; il conquit des royaumes et terrorisa les barbares [<a href="#entry-dasyu" class="skt-link">dasyu</a>]; il épousa la chaste <a href="#entry-Ubindumatii" class="skt-link">Bindumatī</a>, dont il eut pour fils <a href="#entry-Upurukutsa" class="skt-link">Purukutsa</a> et <a href="#entry-Umucukunda" class="skt-link">Mucukunda</a>; il régnait avec sagesse et le pays était prospère; il advint qu'il ne plut pas pendant trois années consécutives, et le roi consulta les ermites; ceux-ci expliquèrent qu'un <a href="#entry-zuudra" class="skt-link">śūdra</a> faisait pénitence, prérogative des brâhmanes en ce <a href="#entry-k_rtayuga" class="skt-link">kṛtayuga</a>, et que la pluie reviendrait s'il était mis à mort; le roi ne voulut pas tuer un ascète innocent, et demanda un palliatif; on lui indiqua de faire jeûne les 11⟨sup⟩es⟨/sup⟩ jours de chaque quinzaine [<a href="#entry-ekaadazii" class="skt-link">ekādaśī</a>]; le roi et ses sujets s'y conformèrent, et la pluie et la prospérité revinrent; <a href="#entry-Umaandhaataa" class="skt-link">Māndhātā</a>, qui avait appris le <a href="#entry-raajadharma" class="skt-link">rājadharma</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uutathya" class="skt-link">Utathya</a>, est le modèle du monarque pieux et juste; [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] il est dit aussi père de <a href="#entry-Ususandhi" class="skt-link">Susandhi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. du village éponyme au Madhya Pradesh, au sud de la <a href="#entry-Unarmadaa" class="skt-link">Narmadā</a>, début du pélerinage [<a href="#entry-parikrama" class="skt-link">parikrama</a>] d'<a href="#entry-Uofkaarezvara" class="skt-link">Oṃkāreśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maandya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मान्द्य</span> <i>māndya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-manda" class="skt-link">manda</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maandya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lenteur; indolence; paresse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faiblesse; lenteur d'esprit; indisposition; maladie.<br> <i>māndyaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> se rendre malade.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maandyavyaaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māndyavyāja</i> [<a href="#entry-vyaaja" class="skt-link">vyāja</a>] <a href="#entry-maandyavyaaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> simulation de maladie.<br> <a href="#entry-maandya" class="skt-link">māndya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanii_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मानीषा</span> <i>mānīṣā</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-manii_saa" class="skt-link">manīṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-maanii_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> pensée; conception, idée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> réflexion, considération; sagesse, intelligence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> prière, requête; désir, souhait.<br> <i>paro</i> <i>mānīṣayā</i> au delà de toute conception.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanii_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānīṣin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maanii_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maanii_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maanii_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mānīṣiṇī</i> réfléchi, sage.<br> <a href="#entry-maanii_saa" class="skt-link">mānīṣā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मानिन्</span> <i>mānin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maanin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maanin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maaninii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māninī</i> fier, orgueilleux — ifc. qui se croit, qui se prend pour, qui passe pour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui se croit le ⟨iic.⟩ de ⟨g.⟩ — <a href="#entry-maaninii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māninī</i> femme dédaigneuse ou boudeuse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. femme blessée dans son orgueil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Māninī «l'Orgueilleuse», fille de <a href="#entry-Ut_r_nabindu" class="skt-link">Tṛṇabindu</a>; elle est mère de <a href="#entry-Uvizravaa" class="skt-link">Viśravā</a> par <a href="#entry-Upulastya" class="skt-link">Pulastya</a>.<br> <i>paṇḍitamānī</i> qui se prend pour une sommité.<br> <i>śūramānī</i> qui se croit un héros.<br> <i>rājamānī</i> qui se prend pour le roi.<br> <i>darśanīyamānī</i> qui se croit beau; vaniteux.<br> <i>ripumānī</i> <i>bhrātuḥ</i> (usage exocentrique) qui se croit l'ennemi de son frère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānitā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-maanitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> fait de se croire; fait d'honorer — ifc. imagination de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanitva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mānitva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-maanitva" class="skt-link">n.</a> fierté; orgueil; fait d'être honoré — ifc. fait qu'on s'imagine d'être.<br> <a href="#entry-maanin" class="skt-link">mānin</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanu_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मानुष</span> <i>mānuṣa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-manus" class="skt-link">manus</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maanu_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maanu_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maanu_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mānuṣī</i> humain, propre à l'homme — <a href="#entry-maanu_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme — <a href="#entry-maanu_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> nature humaine; humanité — <a href="#entry-maanu_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mānuṣī</i> femme.<br> <i>ātmānam</i> <i>mānuṣam</i> <i>manye</i> [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] [<a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>] Je me considère comme un humain.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maanya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मान्य</span> <i>mānya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maanya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maanya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maanyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mānyā</i> respectable, honorable, vénérable.<br> <i>mānye</i> adr. Madame.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माप्</span> <i>māp</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(māpayati)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>māpya</i><i>)</i> mesurer; ériger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maapaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मापक</span> <i>māpaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-maap" class="skt-link">māp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maapaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maapaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maapikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māpikā</i> qui sert à mesurer ⟨g.⟩ — <a href="#entry-maapaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> instrument de mesure — <a href="#entry-maapikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māpikā</i> balance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maapana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मापन</span> <i>māpana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-maap" class="skt-link">māp</a>] <a href="#entry-maapana" class="skt-link">m.</a> balance — <a href="#entry-maapana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de mesurer ou de mettre en forme — <a href="#entry-maapanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māpanā</i> mesure, placement (not. de l'aire sacrificielle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maapya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माप्य</span> <i>māpya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-maap" class="skt-link">māp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maapya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maapya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maapyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māpyā</i> mesurable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्</span> <i>mār</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-m_r" class="skt-link">mṛ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(mārayati)</i> faire mourir, tuer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार</span> <i>māra</i> [act. <a href="#entry-m_r" class="skt-link">mṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-maara" class="skt-link">m.</a> mort, destruction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> obstacle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> passion amoureuse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> (obstacle à la libération) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. np. de Māra, la Mort; il est l'esprit du mal, et le tentateur du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. mort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-maara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> meurtre, tuerie, mise à mort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maaraari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mārāri</i> [<a href="#entry-ari" class="skt-link">ari</a>] <a href="#entry-maaraari" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «ennemi de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>».<br> <a href="#entry-maara" class="skt-link">māra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarajit">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mārajit</i> [<a href="#entry-jit" class="skt-link">jit</a>] <a href="#entry-maarajit" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> «qui conquiert <a href="#entry-Umaara" class="skt-link">Māra</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maararipu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māraripu</i> [<a href="#entry-ripu" class="skt-link">ripu</a>] <a href="#entry-maararipu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «ennemi de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मारव</span> <i>mārava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-maru" class="skt-link">maru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maarava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maarava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maaravii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māravī</i> désertique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>Mārvār</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maaravijaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māravijaya</i> [<a href="#entry-vijaya" class="skt-link">vijaya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maaravijaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maaravijaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maaravijayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māravijayā</i> bd. se dit de la posture de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> méditant, surmontant la tentation de <a href="#entry-Umaara" class="skt-link">Māra</a>, et prenant la Terre à témoin de ses mérites; syn. <a href="#entry-bhuumisparzamudraa" class="skt-link">bhūmisparśamudrā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maardava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्दव</span> <i>mārdava</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-m_rdu" class="skt-link">mṛdu</a>] <a href="#entry-maardava" class="skt-link">n.</a> douceur, gentillesse, tendresse; bonté, amabilité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarg">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्ग्</span> <i>mārg</i> [vn. <i>mārga</i>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(mārgayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mārgita</i><i>)</i> chercher, aller à la recherche de; poursuivre, chasser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'efforcer de, tâcher d'atteindre; demander, solliciter qqc. ⟨acc.⟩ de qqn. ⟨abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्ग</span> <i>mārga</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-m_rga" class="skt-link">mṛga</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maarga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maargaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārgā</i> relatif à l'antilope <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. conforme à la norme du sanskrit; opp. <a href="#entry-dezii" class="skt-link">deśī</a> — <a href="#entry-maarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«piste d'antilope»] voie, chemin, rue, route <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>magga</i>.<br> <i>mārgāya</i> en vue de ⟨iic.⟩.<br> <i>mārgeṇa</i> par, au moyen de, par voie de ⟨iic.⟩.<br> <i>mārgais</i> à travers ⟨iic.⟩.<br> <i>mārgaṃ</i> <i>dā</i> donner libre cours à.<br> <i>mārgaṃ</i> <i>yam</i> id.<br> <i>mārgeṇa</i> <i>yā</i> subir le même sort que.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarga_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्गण</span> <i>mārgaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-maarg" class="skt-link">mārg</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maarga_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maarga_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maarga_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārgaṇā</i> ifc. qui désire, qui s'enquiert de, qui recherche — <a href="#entry-maarga_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> mendiant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> flèche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 5 (pour les 5 flèches de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>) — <a href="#entry-maarga_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> investigation, recherche, enquête <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sollicitation, requête.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maargaziir_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mārgaśīrṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ziir_sa" class="skt-link">śīrṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maargaziir_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maargaziir_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maargaziir_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārgaśīrṣī</i> né sous la constellation <a href="#entry-Um_rgaziras" class="skt-link">Mṛgaśiras</a> (Orion) — <a href="#entry-maargaziir_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. mois de l'Antilope (novembre-décembre), où la Lune est pleine dans Orion; il est sacré à <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-agrahaaya_na" class="skt-link">agrahāyaṇa</a> — <a href="#entry-maargaziir_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārgaśīrṣī</i> jour de sa pleine lune.<br> <i>māsānāṃ</i> <i>mārgaśīrṣo'ham</i> [BhG.] Parmi les mois je suis Mārgaśīrṣa.<br> <a href="#entry-maarga" class="skt-link">mārga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maargin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्गिन्</span> <i>mārgin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-maarg" class="skt-link">mārg</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maargin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maargin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maargi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārgiṇī</i> qui ouvre la voie; guide — ifc. qui suit la voie de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maargita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्गित</span> <i>mārgita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-maarg" class="skt-link">mārg</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maargita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maargita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maargitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārgitā</i> recherché; sollicité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maari_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मारिष</span> <i>māriṣa</i> <a href="#entry-maari_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme de bien; patron <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rôle de directeur de troupe au théâtre — <a href="#entry-maari_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māriṣā</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de la reine Māriṣā, épouse de <a href="#entry-Uzuura" class="skt-link">Śūra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maariica">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मारीच</span> <i>mārīca</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mariici" class="skt-link">marīci</a>] <a href="#entry-maariica" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. patr. de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Umariici" class="skt-link">Marīci</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Mārīca «Maître du mirage», magicien fils de <a href="#entry-Usunda_1" class="skt-link">Sunda_1</a> et <a href="#entry-Utaa_takaa" class="skt-link">Tāṭakā</a>, devenu <a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a> par la malédiction d'<a href="#entry-Uagastya" class="skt-link">Agastya</a>; il était conseiller de la cour de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>; il se métamorphosa en daim d'or [<a href="#entry-hemam_rga" class="skt-link">hemamṛga</a>] pour éloigner <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> de <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a> avec laquelle il se trouvait en forêt afin que <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> puisse l'approcher et l'enlever — <a href="#entry-maariicii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārīcī</i> bd. np. de la déesse Mārīcī.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्ज्</span> <i>mārj</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-m_rj" class="skt-link">mṛj</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(mārjayati)</i> frotter, polir; orner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्ज</span> <i>mārja</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-m_rj" class="skt-link">mṛj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maarja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maarja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maarjaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārjā</i> qui nettoie — <a href="#entry-maarja" class="skt-link">m.</a> nettoyeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> nettoyage; purification.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarjaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्जार</span> <i>mārjāra</i> [<a href="#entry-maarja" class="skt-link">mārja</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-maarjaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> chat [«qui se nettoie»]; chat sauvage — <a href="#entry-maarjaarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārjārī</i> chatte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarjaaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mārjāraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-maarjaaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> chat; paon — <a href="#entry-maarjaarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārjārikā</i> civette.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarjaarakizora">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mārjārakiśora</i> [<a href="#entry-kizora" class="skt-link">kiśora</a>] <a href="#entry-maarjaarakizora" class="skt-link">m.</a> chaton <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>mārjārakiśoranyāya</i>] phil. doctrine de <a href="#entry-bhakti" class="skt-link">bhakti</a> du Sud, où le croyant s'abandonne à Dieu comme le chaton transporté dans la gueule de sa mère; opp. <a href="#entry-marka_takizora" class="skt-link">markaṭakiśora</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarjaaramuu_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mārjāramūṣaka</i> [<a href="#entry-muu_saka" class="skt-link">mūṣaka</a>] <a href="#entry-maarjaaramuu_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> sg. le chat et la souris.<br> <a href="#entry-maarjaara" class="skt-link">mārjāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarjaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्जक</span> <i>mārjaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-m_rj" class="skt-link">mṛj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maarjaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maarjaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maarjikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārjikā</i> qui nettoie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarjana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्जन</span> <i>mārjana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-m_rj" class="skt-link">mṛj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maarjana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maarjana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maarjanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārjanā</i> ifc. qui efface, qui nettoie, qui purifie — <a href="#entry-maarjana" class="skt-link">m.</a> balai; brosse — <a href="#entry-maarjanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārjanī</i> lavandière — <a href="#entry-maarjana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maarjanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārjanā</i> essuyage, nettoyage; purification.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarka_n_deya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्कण्डेय</span> <i>mārkaṇḍeya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-m_rka_n_da" class="skt-link">mṛkaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-maarka_n_deya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. patr. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Mārkaṇḍeya; son père <a href="#entry-Um_rka_n_du" class="skt-link">Mṛkaṇḍu</a> ayant longtemps prié pour avoir un fils, il lui fut accordé le choix entre de nombreux fils stupides ou un seul très intelligent, mais de vie limitée à 16 ans; ayant fait ce dernier choix, son épouse <a href="#entry-Umanasvinii" class="skt-link">Manasvinī</a> donna le jour à <a href="#entry-Umaarka_n_deya" class="skt-link">Mārkaṇḍeya</a>, qui grandit en sagesse jusqu'à sa 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ année; à son 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ anniversaire, il adorait le <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> dans un sanctuaire de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> quand <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a> vint le chercher; <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> en colère jaillit du <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>, frappa <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a> dans la poitrine, et promit à <a href="#entry-Umaarka_n_deya" class="skt-link">Mārkaṇḍeya</a> de rester éternellement jeune; immortel [<a href="#entry-cira_njiivin" class="skt-link">ciraṃjīvin</a>], il voyage dans le corps de <a href="#entry-Unaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">Nārāyaṇa</a> endormi sur les eaux primordiales, et <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> lui révèle les secrets de sa <a href="#entry-maayaa" class="skt-link">māyā</a>; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] lorsque <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a> en exil se plaint de son sort, <a href="#entry-Umaarka_n_deya" class="skt-link">Mārkaṇḍeya</a> lui conte le <a href="#entry-Uraamopaakhyaana" class="skt-link">Rāmopākhyāna</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Udiirghaayu" class="skt-link">Dīrghāyu</a>, <a href="#entry-Usuratama_njarii" class="skt-link">Suratamañjarī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarka_n_deyapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-maarka_n_deyapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇa, qui décrit les actes glorieux de la Déesse; <a href="#entry-Ujaimini" class="skt-link">Jaimini</a> en aurait reçu l'enseignement de <a href="#entry-Umaarka_n_deya" class="skt-link">Mārkaṇḍeya</a> transmis par 4 oiseaux; sa composition est antérieure au 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; la partie appelée <a href="#entry-Uca_n_dii_1" class="skt-link">Caṇḍī_1</a>, très révérée, est récitée dans la <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> <a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maarka_n_deya" class="skt-link">mārkaṇḍeya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarmika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्मिक</span> <i>mārmika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-marman" class="skt-link">marman</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maarmika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maarmika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maarmikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārmikā</i> spécialiste de, versé dans ⟨loc. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarta_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्तण्ड</span> <i>mārtaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-maartaa_n_da" class="skt-link">mārtāṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-maarta_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> Soleil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mārtaṇḍa <a href="#entry-Ubhairava" class="skt-link">Bhairava</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukha_n_dobaa" class="skt-link">Khaṇḍobā</a> — ifc. lit. lumière sur (titre d'ouvrages).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maartaa_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्ताण्ड</span> <i>mārtāṇḍa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-m_rtaa_n_da" class="skt-link">mṛtāṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-maartaa_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«né d'un œuf (semblant) mort»] oiseau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> Soleil (plus tard écrit <a href="#entry-maarta_n_da" class="skt-link">mārtaṇḍa</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. du temple au Soleil Mārtāṇḍa, construit par l'empereur <a href="#entry-Ulalitaaditya" class="skt-link">Lalitāditya</a> <a href="#entry-Umuktaapii_da" class="skt-link">Muktāpīḍa</a> dans un site splendide au <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> au 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il fut détruit par le sultan Sikander au 15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; ses ruines se trouvent près du village de Mattan.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maarttikaavata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्त्तिकावत</span> <i>mārttikāvata</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-m_rttikaavatii" class="skt-link">mṛttikāvatī</a>] <a href="#entry-maarttikaavata" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du royaume <a href="#entry-yadu" class="skt-link">yadu</a> de Mārttikāvata; cf. <a href="#entry-Ucitraratha_1" class="skt-link">Citraratha_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maaruta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मारुत</span> <i>māruta</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maaruta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maaruta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maarutaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mārutā</i> relatif aux <a href="#entry-marutas" class="skt-link">marutas</a>, relatif au vent; orageux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aérien, céleste; divin — <a href="#entry-maaruta" class="skt-link">m.</a> souffle; respiration <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Māruta, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvaayu" class="skt-link">Vāyu</a> «Maître des Vents».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maaruti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मारुति</span> <i>māruti</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>] <a href="#entry-maaruti" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Māruti, patr. de <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a> «Fils du Vent»; cf. <a href="#entry-Umarutputra" class="skt-link">Marutputra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मास्</span> <i>mās</i> [<a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-maas" class="skt-link">m.</a> lune — nom. <i>mās</i> lune; mois <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>mensis</i>; ang. month; fr. mois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मास</span> <i>māsa</i> [<a href="#entry-maas" class="skt-link">mās</a>] <a href="#entry-maasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> mois; on distingue les mois solaires [<a href="#entry-saura" class="skt-link">saura</a>], naturels [<a href="#entry-saavana" class="skt-link">sāvana</a>], stellaires [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>] et lunaires [<a href="#entry-caandra" class="skt-link">cāndra</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. mois lunaire, divisé en 30 jours lunaires [<a href="#entry-tithi" class="skt-link">tithi</a>]; il y en a douze, chacun associé à la constellation [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>] dans laquelle a lieu la pleine lune: <a href="#entry-caitra" class="skt-link">caitra</a> (mars-avril), mois de <a href="#entry-Ucitraa" class="skt-link">Citrā</a>; <a href="#entry-vaizaakha" class="skt-link">vaiśākha</a> (avril-mai), mois de <a href="#entry-Uvizaakhe" class="skt-link">Viśākhe</a>; <a href="#entry-jyai_s_tha" class="skt-link">jyaiṣṭha</a> (mai-juin), mois de <a href="#entry-Ujye_s_thaa" class="skt-link">Jyeṣṭhā</a>; <a href="#entry-aa_saa_dha" class="skt-link">āṣāḍha</a> (juin-juillet), mois des <a href="#entry-Ua_saa_dhaas" class="skt-link">Aṣāḍhās</a>; <a href="#entry-zraava_na_2" class="skt-link">śrāvaṇa_2</a> (juillet-août), mois de <a href="#entry-Uzrava_naa" class="skt-link">Śravaṇā</a>; <a href="#entry-bhaadrapada" class="skt-link">bhādrapada</a> (août-septembre), mois des <a href="#entry-Ubhaadrapadaas" class="skt-link">Bhādrapadās</a>; <a href="#entry-aazvina" class="skt-link">āśvina</a> (septembre-octobre), mois d'<a href="#entry-Uazvinii" class="skt-link">Aśvinī</a>; <a href="#entry-kaarttika" class="skt-link">kārttika</a> (octobre-novembre), mois des <a href="#entry-Uk_rttikaas" class="skt-link">Kṛttikās</a>; <a href="#entry-maargaziir_sa" class="skt-link">mārgaśīrṣa</a> (novembre-décembre), mois de <a href="#entry-Um_rgaziras" class="skt-link">Mṛgaśiras</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-agrahaaya_na" class="skt-link">agrahāyaṇa</a>; <a href="#entry-pau_sa" class="skt-link">pauṣa</a> (décembre-janvier), mois de <a href="#entry-Upu_sya" class="skt-link">Puṣya</a>; <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">māgha</a> (janvier-février), mois des <a href="#entry-Umaghaas" class="skt-link">Maghās</a>; <a href="#entry-phaalguna" class="skt-link">phālguna</a> (février-mars), mois de <a href="#entry-Uphalgunii" class="skt-link">Phalgunī</a>; suivant la région de l'Inde, les mois commencent à la pleine ou à la nouvelle lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. les douze mois védiques: <a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">madhu</a>, <a href="#entry-maadhava" class="skt-link">mādhava</a>, <a href="#entry-zukra" class="skt-link">śukra</a>, <a href="#entry-zuci" class="skt-link">śuci</a>, <a href="#entry-nabhas" class="skt-link">nabhas</a>, <a href="#entry-nabhasya" class="skt-link">nabhasya</a>, <a href="#entry-i_sa" class="skt-link">iṣa</a>, <a href="#entry-uurja" class="skt-link">ūrja</a>, <a href="#entry-sahas" class="skt-link">sahas</a>, <a href="#entry-sahasya" class="skt-link">sahasya</a>, <a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a> et <a href="#entry-tapasya" class="skt-link">tapasya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 12.<br> <i>māsena</i> durant 1 mois.<br> <i>māse</i> au bout d'un mois.<br> <i>māsam</i> <i>ekam</i> pendant 1 mois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maasamaatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māsamātra</i> [<a href="#entry-maatra" class="skt-link">mātra</a>] <a href="#entry-maasamaatra" class="skt-link">m.</a> durée d'un mois.<br> <i>māsamātre</i> adv. en l'espace d'un mois.<br> <i>māsamātram</i> adv. pendant un mois entier; à longueur de mois.<br> <a href="#entry-maasa" class="skt-link">māsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maat_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मातृ</span> <i>mātṛ</i> <a href="#entry-maat_r" class="skt-link">f.</a> mère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> formule d'adresse respectueuse pour une femme d'un certain âge ou rang <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. np. de Mātā la Mère divine, la Déesse en Mère universelle; cf. <a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>, <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. une des six mères qui élevèrent <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uk_rttikaas" class="skt-link">Kṛttikās</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] une «mère à l'enfant», être femelle démoniaque à pacifier pour éviter les maladies infantiles; cf. <a href="#entry-maat_rga_na" class="skt-link">mātṛgaṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. une énergie divine, associée à un dieu principal; d'abord au nombre de 7 [<a href="#entry-saptamaat_rkaas" class="skt-link">saptamātṛkās</a>], puis de 8,9 ou 16 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> base du bâton à feu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>mātrī</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μητηρ; lat. <i>mater</i>; ang. mother; all. Mutter; fr. mère.<br> <i>mātṛdevo</i> <i>bhava</i> Traite ta mère comme Dieu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maat_rbhaa_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātṛbhāṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_saa" class="skt-link">bhāṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-maat_rbhaa_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> langue maternelle.<br> <i>mātṛbhāṣā</i> <i>mātṛdugdha</i> La langue maternelle est pareille au lait maternel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maat_rdatta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātṛdatta</i> [<a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">datta</a>] <a href="#entry-maat_rdatta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du <a href="#entry-trivedin" class="skt-link">trivedin</a> Mātṛdatta, contemporain et ami de <a href="#entry-Uda_n_dii_1" class="skt-link">Daṇḍī_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maat_rga_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātṛgaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_na" class="skt-link">gaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-maat_rga_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] le groupe des 7 «mères (dangereuses) aux enfants» [<a href="#entry-zizumaataras" class="skt-link">śiśumātaras</a>] associées à <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>: <a href="#entry-Ukaakii" class="skt-link">Kākī</a>, <a href="#entry-Uhalimaa" class="skt-link">Halimā</a>, <a href="#entry-Urudraa" class="skt-link">Rudrā</a>, <a href="#entry-Ub_rhalii" class="skt-link">Bṛhalī</a>, <a href="#entry-Uaaryaa" class="skt-link">Āryā</a>, <a href="#entry-Upalaalaa" class="skt-link">Palālā</a> et <a href="#entry-Umitraa" class="skt-link">Mitrā</a>; plus tard elles furent identifiées aux énergies divines [<a href="#entry-saptamaat_rkaas" class="skt-link">saptamātṛkās</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maat_rhan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātṛhan</i> var. <i>mātṛghāta</i> [<a href="#entry-han_2" class="skt-link">han_2</a>] <a href="#entry-maat_rhan" class="skt-link">m.</a> matricide.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maat_rka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मातृक</span> <i>mātṛka</i> [<a href="#entry-maat_r" class="skt-link">mātṛ</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maat_rka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maat_rka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maat_rkaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mātṛkā</i> maternel — <a href="#entry-maat_rka" class="skt-link">m.</a> oncle maternel — <a href="#entry-maat_rka" class="skt-link">n.</a> nature de mère — <a href="#entry-maat_rkaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mātṛkā</i> mère <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> déesse, mère divine; <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> d'un dieu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> alphabet; not. alphabet mystique associé à un diagramme [<a href="#entry-yantra" class="skt-link">yantra</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>mātṛkās</i> cf. <a href="#entry-saptamaat_rkaas" class="skt-link">saptamātṛkās</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. les 16 énergies sonores, comprenant les voyelles, l'<a href="#entry-anusvaara" class="skt-link">anusvāra</a>, et le <a href="#entry-visarga" class="skt-link">visarga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maat_rsvas_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātṛsvasṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-svas_r" class="skt-link">svasṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-maat_rsvas_r" class="skt-link">f.</a> tante maternelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maat_rsvasriiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātṛsvasrīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-maat_rsvasriiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> cousin (fils de tante maternelle) — <a href="#entry-maat_rsvasriiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mātṛsvasrīyā</i> cousine.<br> <a href="#entry-maat_rsvas_r" class="skt-link">mātṛsvasṛ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माता</span> <i>mātā</i> [nom. <a href="#entry-maat_r" class="skt-link">mātṛ</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maataamaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātāmaha</i> [<a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">mahat</a>] <a href="#entry-maataamaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> grand-père maternel — <a href="#entry-mataamahii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>matāmahī</i> grand-mère maternelle.<br> <a href="#entry-maataa" class="skt-link">mātā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maataapit_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātāpitṛ</i> [<a href="#entry-pit_r" class="skt-link">pitṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-maataapit_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. <i>mātāpitarau</i> les parents (par ordre de respect).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मातंग</span> <i>mātaṃga</i> var. <i>mātaṅga</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-matafga" class="skt-link">mataṃga</a>] <a href="#entry-maata_mga" class="skt-link">m.</a> éléphant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. homme de caste impure; syn. <a href="#entry-ca_n_daala" class="skt-link">caṇḍāla</a>, <a href="#entry-kiraata" class="skt-link">kirāta</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. np. du <a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a> Mātaṅga, associé à <a href="#entry-Umahaaviira_1" class="skt-link">Mahāvīra_1</a>; c'est un dieu de la richesse, analogue à <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> et <a href="#entry-Ujambhala" class="skt-link">Jambhala</a> — ifc. le meilleur des — <a href="#entry-maata_mgii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mātaṃgī</i> éléphante <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [tantr.] np. de Mātaṅgī ou Mātaṅginī «Éléphante», un aspect terrible [<a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">mahāvidyā</a>] de la Déesse [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mātaṅgī_1, fille de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukrodhavazaa" class="skt-link">Krodhavaśā</a>; elle engendra la lignée des éléphants <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. femme <a href="#entry-ca_n_daalaa" class="skt-link">caṇḍālā</a> ou <a href="#entry-kiraatii" class="skt-link">kirātī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatafgaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मातंगक</span> <i>mātaṃgaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-maata_mgaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. chef tribal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatafgaliilaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātaṃgalīlā</i> [<a href="#entry-liilaa" class="skt-link">līlā</a>] <a href="#entry-maata_mgaliilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Mātaṃgalīlā «Divertissement de l'éléphant», œuvre de <a href="#entry-Uniilaka_n_tha_5" class="skt-link">Nīlakaṇṭha_5</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maatafga" class="skt-link">mātaṃga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मातलि</span> <i>mātali</i> <a href="#entry-maatali" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mātali, cocher d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, né d'un prodige; le sage <a href="#entry-Uzamika" class="skt-link">Śamika</a> et son épouse <a href="#entry-Utapasvinii" class="skt-link">Tapasvinī</a> ayant un fils, ils en désirèrent un second; un astrologue leur prédit qu'en plaçant leur fils dans un trou à même le sol, un frère jumeau leur viendrait, ce qui arriva; à peine né, celui-ci offrit à <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> de conduire son char pour faire la guerre aux <a href="#entry-asuraas" class="skt-link">asurās</a>; ce dernier accepta et le baptisa <a href="#entry-Umaatali" class="skt-link">Mātali</a>; il enseigna la sagesse à <a href="#entry-Uyayaati" class="skt-link">Yayāti</a>; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] il voulut marier sa fille, la très belle <a href="#entry-Ugu_nakezii" class="skt-link">Guṇakeśī</a>, à un dragon [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>]; <a href="#entry-Unaarada" class="skt-link">Nārada</a> lui fit visiter <a href="#entry-Ubhogavatii" class="skt-link">Bhogavatī</a>, où il rencontra <a href="#entry-Usumukha" class="skt-link">Sumukha</a>, prince <a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a> de haute lignée; mais son père <a href="#entry-Ucikura" class="skt-link">Cikura</a> venait d'être mangé par <a href="#entry-Ugaru_da" class="skt-link">Garuḍa</a>, qui avait menacé de revenir pour tuer <a href="#entry-Usumukha" class="skt-link">Sumukha</a>; <a href="#entry-Umaatali" class="skt-link">Mātali</a> emmena alors <a href="#entry-Usumukha" class="skt-link">Sumukha</a> chez <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, où <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> lui donna l'ambroisie [<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>]; plein de colère, <a href="#entry-Ugaru_da" class="skt-link">Garuḍa</a> se plaignit à <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> qui l'humilia.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मातरि</span> <i>mātari</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-maat_r" class="skt-link">mātṛ</a>] ind. dans la mère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatarizvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātariśvan</i> [<a href="#entry-zvan_2" class="skt-link">śvan_2</a>] <a href="#entry-maatarizvan" class="skt-link">m.</a> véd. np. de Mātariśvā «Qui enfle dans la mère», chef des souffles [<a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>], forme d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> personnifiant le pouvoir de procréation, et naissant dans la matrice du bâton à feu; Prométhée indien, il apporta le Feu [<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>] aux hommes, et le confia au clan des <a href="#entry-Ubhaargavaas" class="skt-link">Bhārgavās</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maatari" class="skt-link">mātari</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माथ</span> <i>mātha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-manth" class="skt-link">manth</a>] <a href="#entry-maatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de baratter, d'agiter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> destruction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maathura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माथुर</span> <i>māthura</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mathuraa" class="skt-link">mathurā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maathura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maathura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maathurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māthurī</i> originaire de <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a> — <a href="#entry-maathurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māthurī</i> lit. np. de la Māthurī, commentaire par <a href="#entry-Umathuraanaatha_1" class="skt-link">Mathurānātha_1</a> de la <a href="#entry-Utattvacintaama_ni" class="skt-link">Tattvacintāmaṇi</a> [<a href="#entry-Utattvacintaama_nirahasya" class="skt-link">Tattvacintāmaṇirahasya</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मात्र</span> <i>mātra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-maatra" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. brâhmane seulement par la naissance — <a href="#entry-maatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> petite quantité, élément, atome; unité de mesure — f. cf. <a href="#entry-maatraa" class="skt-link">mātrā</a>.<br> <i>mātrayā</i> adv. dans une faible mesure.<br> <i>kroṣāmātre</i> adv. à portée de voix.<br> <i>māsamātre</i> adv. en l'espace d'un mois.<br> <i>aṅgulamātram</i> d'une largeur d'un doigt.<br> <i>arthamātram</i> une certaine somme d'argent.<br> <i>bhayamātram</i> un danger quelconque.<br> <i>ratimātram</i> rien que de la sensualité.<br> <i>rājāmātram</i> seulement des rois.<br> <i>śatamātram</i> au nombre d'une centaine.<br> <i>śabdamātreṇa</i> seulement par un son.<br> <i>jātamātraṃ</i> <i>na</i> <i>yaḥ</i> <i>śatrūṃ</i> <i>vyādhiṃ</i> <i>ca</i> <i>praśamaṃ</i> <i>nayet</i> Qui ne jugule pas l'ennemi ou la maladie dès son apparition.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
