    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मात्रा</span> <i>mātrā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-maatra" class="skt-link">mātra</a>] <a href="#entry-maatraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> mesure (taille, durée, profondeur, etc.) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> unité de mesure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. more, temps pour prononcer une syllabe brève, valant 2 temps [<a href="#entry-druta" class="skt-link">druta</a>] — ifc. qui a pour mesure (taille, durée, profondeur, etc.) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> de la taille de, pas plus grand que; juste, seulement, rien que, pur et simple; dès que, à peine que ⟨pp.⟩.<br> <i>rājeti</i> <i>kā</i> <i>mātrā</i> que m'importe qu'il soit roi?
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatraachandas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātrāchandas</i> [<a href="#entry-chandas" class="skt-link">chandas</a>] <a href="#entry-maatraachandas" class="skt-link">n.</a> phon. mètre basé sur la longueur des syllabes, plutôt que sur leur nombre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatraalaaghava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātrālāghava</i> [<a href="#entry-laaghava" class="skt-link">lāghava</a>] <a href="#entry-maatraalaaghava" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. concision d'expression par minimisation de syllabes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatraameru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātrāmeru</i> [<a href="#entry-meru" class="skt-link">meru</a>] <a href="#entry-maatraameru" class="skt-link">m.</a> math. suite des nombres de Fibonacci, étudiée par <a href="#entry-Upifgala_2" class="skt-link">Piṅgala_2</a> pour la prosodie, puis par <a href="#entry-Ugopaala_2" class="skt-link">Gopāla_2</a> et <a href="#entry-Uhemacandra" class="skt-link">Hemacandra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatraasamaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātrāsamaka</i> [<a href="#entry-samaka" class="skt-link">samaka</a>] <a href="#entry-maatraasamaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. mètre poétique de 16 temps syllabiques divisés en groupes de 4.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatraasparza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātrāsparśa</i> [<a href="#entry-sparza" class="skt-link">sparśa</a>] <a href="#entry-maatraasparza" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. perception physique.<br> <a href="#entry-maatraa" class="skt-link">mātrā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatrarcanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātrarcanā</i> [<a href="#entry-arcanaa" class="skt-link">arcanā</a>] <a href="#entry-maatrarcanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> vénération de la mère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatricchaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātricchā</i> [<a href="#entry-icchaa" class="skt-link">icchā</a>] <a href="#entry-maatricchaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> désir de la mère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मात्री</span> <i>mātrī</i> cf. <a href="#entry-maat_r" class="skt-link">mātṛ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatrupaasanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātrupāsanā</i> [<a href="#entry-upaasanaa" class="skt-link">upāsanā</a>] <a href="#entry-maatrupaasanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soumission à la mère.<br> <a href="#entry-maat_r" class="skt-link">mātṛ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatsarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मात्सर्य</span> <i>mātsarya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-matsara" class="skt-link">matsara</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maatsarya" class="skt-link">n.</a> égoïsme, envie, jalousie — <a href="#entry-maatsarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mātsarya, l'Envie personnifiée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatsya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मात्स्य</span> <i>mātsya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-matsya" class="skt-link">matsya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maatsya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maatsya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maatsyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mātsyā</i> relatif au poisson ou à l'<a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a> <a href="#entry-Umatsya" class="skt-link">Matsya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatsyapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātsyapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-maatsyapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-matsyapuraa_na" class="skt-link">matsyapurāṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maatsya" class="skt-link">mātsya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मातुल</span> <i>mātula</i> [<a href="#entry-maat_r" class="skt-link">mātṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-maatula" class="skt-link">m.</a> oncle maternel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. année solaire (opp. <a href="#entry-vimala" class="skt-link">vimala</a>, année lunaire) — <a href="#entry-maatulaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mātulā</i> épouse d'oncle maternel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>mātulī</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>mātulānī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatuleya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātuleya</i> [<a href="#entry--iya" class="skt-link">-iya</a>] <a href="#entry-maatuleya" class="skt-link">m.</a> cousin (fils d'un oncle maternel) — <a href="#entry-maatuleyii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mātuleyī</i> cousine.<br> <a href="#entry-maatula" class="skt-link">mātula</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatulufga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मातुलुङ्ग</span> <i>mātuluṅga</i> var. <i>luṅga</i> <a href="#entry-maatulufga" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Citrus medica</i>, citronnier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maatulufgaphala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mātuluṅgaphala</i> [<a href="#entry-phala" class="skt-link">phala</a>] <a href="#entry-maatulufgaphala" class="skt-link">n.</a> citron.<br> <a href="#entry-maatulufga" class="skt-link">mātuluṅga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माय</span> <i>māya</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māyī</i> ifc. qui mesure ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui crée l'illusion, trompeur; se dit de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> — <a href="#entry-maaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> mime, jongleur, magicien — f. cf. <a href="#entry-maayaa" class="skt-link">māyā</a>.<br> <i>dhānyamāya</i> m. <a href="#entry-dhaanyamaayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>dhānyamāyī</i> qui pèse le grain.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">माया</span> <i>māyā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-maaya" class="skt-link">māya</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> illusion, apparition; magie, sorcellerie; tromperie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] le monde des apparences; l'Illusion Divine, créée par <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Māyā, l'Illusion Divine personnifiée; elle émane de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Umahaamaayaa" class="skt-link">Mahāmāyā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uyogamaayaa" class="skt-link">Yogamāyā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] épith. de la Déesse sous son pouvoir d'illusion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-siddhaanta" class="skt-link">siddhānta</a>] matière première des mondes à laquelle la conscience de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> donne forme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de la reine Māyā_1, mère du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>.<br> <i>māyā</i> <i>idaṃ</i> <i>sarvam</i> [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] Tout ici-bas n'est qu'illusion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaabiija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyābīja</i> [<a href="#entry-biija" class="skt-link">bīja</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaabiija" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-biijamantra" class="skt-link">bījamantra</a> ‘<a href="#entry-hrii_m" class="skt-link">hrīṃ</a>’, d'évocation de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaadevii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyādevī</i> [<a href="#entry-devii" class="skt-link">devī</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaadevii" class="skt-link">f.</a> hist. np. de la reine <a href="#entry-Umaayaa_1" class="skt-link">Māyā_1</a>, mère du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaak_rt">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyākṛt</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rt_2" class="skt-link">kṛt_2</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaak_rt" class="skt-link">m.</a> magicien, illusioniste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyākāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> magicien, illusioniste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaam_rga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyāmṛga</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rga" class="skt-link">mṛga</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaam_rga" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] «daim d'illusion», daim d'or en lequel <a href="#entry-Umaariica" class="skt-link">Mārīca</a> se déguisa pour tromper <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-hemam_rga" class="skt-link">hemamṛga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyāpura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du village de Māyāpura près de <a href="#entry-Unavadviipa" class="skt-link">Navadvīpa</a> sur le Gange au Nord du Bengale; c'est le lieu de naissance de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a> (1486).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyāvāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. doctrine affirmant que le monde est illusion; s'applique au <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a> et au bouddhisme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaavaadin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyāvādin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaavaadin" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. adepte du <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a> ou du bouddhisme; il se rase le crâne complètement.<br> <a href="#entry-maayaavaada" class="skt-link">māyāvāda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyāvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maayaavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maayaavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maayaavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māyāvatī</i> qui provoque l'illusion, qui a des pouvoirs magiques; trompeur — <a href="#entry-maayaavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māyāvatī</i> myth. np. de Māyāvatī «Magicienne», épouse de <a href="#entry-Uzambara_1" class="skt-link">Śambara_1</a>; elle recueillit <a href="#entry-Upradyumna" class="skt-link">Pradyumna</a> enfant, s'en éprit et plus tard l'épousa; leur fils est <a href="#entry-Uaniruddha" class="skt-link">Aniruddha</a>; elle est une incarnation de <a href="#entry-Urati" class="skt-link">Rati</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaavin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyāvin</i> [<a href="#entry--vin" class="skt-link">-vin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maayaavin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maayaavin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maayaavinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māyāvinī</i> qui possède des pouvoirs magiques; qui crée l'illusion; qui trompe — <a href="#entry-maayaavin" class="skt-link">m.</a> magicien <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Māyāvin «Magicien», fils aîné de <a href="#entry-Umaya" class="skt-link">Maya</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uhemaa" class="skt-link">Hemā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyāyoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> pratique de la magie ou de l'illusion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaayogin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyāyogin</i> [<a href="#entry-yogin" class="skt-link">yogin</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaayogin" class="skt-link">m.</a> magicien — <a href="#entry-maayaayoginii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māyāyoginī</i> myth. np. de Māyāyoginī, personnifiant <a href="#entry-Umaayaa" class="skt-link">Māyā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayaayoginiigranthi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyāyoginīgranthi</i> [<a href="#entry-granthi" class="skt-link">granthi</a>] <a href="#entry-maayaayoginiigranthi" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. hypophyse.<br> <a href="#entry-maayaayogin" class="skt-link">māyāyogin</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>māyīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maayiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maayiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maayiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māyīyā</i> phil. provenant de l'illusion divine <a href="#entry-Umaayaa" class="skt-link">Māyā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maayaa" class="skt-link">māyā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मायिक</span> <i>māyika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maayika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maayika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maayikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māyikā</i> illusoire, trompeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मायिन्</span> <i>māyin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maayin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maayinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>māyinī</i> qui crée l'illusion, qui trompe — <a href="#entry-maayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> magicien, illusioniste; tricheur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> — <a href="#entry-maayin" class="skt-link">n.</a> magie, illusion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maayu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मायु</span> <i>māyu</i> [<a href="#entry-maa_4" class="skt-link">mā_4</a>] <a href="#entry-maayu" class="skt-link">m.</a> mugissement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मच्</span> <i>mac</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-mat" class="skt-link">mat</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-macchariira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maccharīra</i> [<a href="#entry-zariira" class="skt-link">śarīra</a>] <a href="#entry-macchariira" class="skt-link">n.</a> mon corps.<br> <a href="#entry-mac" class="skt-link">mac</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maccitta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maccitta</i> [<a href="#entry-citta" class="skt-link">citta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maccitta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maccitta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maccittaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maccittā</i> qui a l'esprit fixé sur son moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mad_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मद्</span> <i>mad_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(mādyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>matta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i>, <i>pra</i><i>)</i> s'enivrer; être ivre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se réjouir; s'exciter — ca. <i>(madayati)</i> ca. <i>(mādayati)</i> enivrer; réjouir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μαδαω; lat. <i>madeo</i>; ang. mad.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mad_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मद्</span> <i>mad_2</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-mat" class="skt-link">mat</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मद</span> <i>mada</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mad_1" class="skt-link">mad_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mada" class="skt-link">m.</a> ivresse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> folie, fureur; passion pour ⟨iic.⟩; excitation sexuelle, rut <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. temporine, liqueur qui suinte des tempes de l'éléphant en rut <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> liqueur enivrante, alcool <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> orgueil, présomption <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mada, l'Orgueil personnifié (ou l'Ivresse).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madaamada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madāmada</i> [<a href="#entry-amada" class="skt-link">amada</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madaamada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madaamada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madaamadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madāmadā</i> phil. passionné et impassible à la fois (qualifie la nature duale du divin <a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-mada" class="skt-link">mada</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madaandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madāndha</i> [<a href="#entry-andha" class="skt-link">andha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madaandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madaandha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madaandhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madāndhā</i> aveuglé par l'orgueil, par le désir ou par l'ivresse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madahetu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madahetu</i> [<a href="#entry-hetu" class="skt-link">hetu</a>] <a href="#entry-madahetu" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Woodfordia fruticosa</i>, arbuste flamboyant; syn. <a href="#entry-dhaatakii" class="skt-link">dhātakī</a>, <a href="#entry-madaniiyahetu" class="skt-link">madanīyahetu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madajala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madajala</i> [<a href="#entry-jala" class="skt-link">jala</a>] <a href="#entry-madajala" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. temporine, sérosité qui suinte des tempes de l'éléphant en rut; sa forte odeur est comparée à celle de l'arbre quinquina [<a href="#entry-saptacchada" class="skt-link">saptacchada</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madalekhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madalekhā</i> [<a href="#entry-lekhaa" class="skt-link">lekhā</a>] <a href="#entry-madalekhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> ligne sur les tempes formée par la temporine suintant de l'éléphant en rut <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. de la nymphe Madalekhā, confidente de <a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मदन</span> <i>madana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mad_1" class="skt-link">mad_1</a>] <a href="#entry-madana" class="skt-link">m.</a> amour charnel, passion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Madana l'Amour, un nom du dieu <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> — <a href="#entry-madana" class="skt-link">n.</a> ébriété, excitation, intoxication — <a href="#entry-madanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madanā</i> var. <i>madanī</i> boisson enivrante — <a href="#entry-madanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madanā</i> ifc. passion pour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanaafkuza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanāṅkuśa</i> [<a href="#entry-afkuza" class="skt-link">aṅkuśa</a>] <a href="#entry-madanaafkuza" class="skt-link">m.</a> pénis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanaantaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanāntaka</i> [<a href="#entry-antaka" class="skt-link">antaka</a>] <a href="#entry-madanaantaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Madanāntaka, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «qui a détruit <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanaazaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanāśaya</i> [<a href="#entry-aazaya" class="skt-link">āśaya</a>] <a href="#entry-madanaazaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> désir sexuel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanacaturdazii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanacaturdaśī</i> [<a href="#entry-caturdazii" class="skt-link">caturdaśī</a>] <a href="#entry-madanacaturdazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. festival en l'honneur de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>, le 14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-caitra" class="skt-link">caitra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanadamana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanadamana</i> [<a href="#entry-damana" class="skt-link">damana</a>] <a href="#entry-madanadamana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Madanadamana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «qui dompte ses passions» en annihilant le désir <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanadhvajaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanadhvajā</i> [<a href="#entry-dhvajaa" class="skt-link">dhvajā</a>] <a href="#entry-madanadhvajaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. festival en l'honneur de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>, le 15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-caitra" class="skt-link">caitra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanadvaadazii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanadvādaśī</i> [<a href="#entry-dvaadazii" class="skt-link">dvādaśī</a>] <a href="#entry-madanadvaadazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. festival en l'honneur de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>, le 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-caitra" class="skt-link">caitra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanalekha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanalekha</i> [<a href="#entry-lekha" class="skt-link">lekha</a>] <a href="#entry-madanalekha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madanalekhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madanalekhā</i> lettre d'amour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanamaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanamaha</i> [<a href="#entry-maha" class="skt-link">maha</a>] <a href="#entry-madanamaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. festival du printemps, en l'honneur de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-madanotsava" class="skt-link">madanotsava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanama_ncukaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanamañcukā</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_ncukaa" class="skt-link">mañcukā</a>] <a href="#entry-madanama_ncukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukathaasaritsaagara" class="skt-link">Kathāsaritsāgara</a>] np. de la belle Madanamañcukā, amante de <a href="#entry-Unaravaahanadatta" class="skt-link">Naravāhanadatta</a>; elle fut enlevée par <a href="#entry-Umaanasavega" class="skt-link">Mānasavega</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanamodaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanamodaka</i> [<a href="#entry-modaka" class="skt-link">modaka</a>] <a href="#entry-madanamodaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> drogue stimulante à base de cannabis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanamohana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanamohana</i> [<a href="#entry-mohana" class="skt-link">mohana</a>] <a href="#entry-madanamohana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Madanamohana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «qui égare le dieu <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanatrayodazii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanatrayodaśī</i> [<a href="#entry-trayodazii" class="skt-link">trayodaśī</a>] <a href="#entry-madanatrayodazii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. festival en l'honneur de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>, le 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-caitra" class="skt-link">caitra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madaniiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madaniiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madaniiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madaniiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madanīyā</i> intoxicant; qui excite le désir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madaniiyahetu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanīyahetu</i> [<a href="#entry-hetu" class="skt-link">hetu</a>] <a href="#entry-madaniiyahetu" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Woodfordia fruticosa</i>, arbuste flamboyant; syn. <a href="#entry-dhaatakii" class="skt-link">dhātakī</a>, <a href="#entry-madahetu" class="skt-link">madahetu</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-madaniiya" class="skt-link">madanīya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madanotsava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madanotsava</i> [<a href="#entry-utsava" class="skt-link">utsava</a>] <a href="#entry-madanotsava" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. festival du printemps, en l'honneur de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-madanamaha" class="skt-link">madanamaha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-madana" class="skt-link">madana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madartha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-madartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> mon propre usage.<br> <i>madartham</i> var. <i>madarthe</i> adv. pour mon propre usage, pour moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madasthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madasthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-sthaana" class="skt-link">sthāna</a>] <a href="#entry-madasthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> mod. bar.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madayitnu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madayitnu</i> [<a href="#entry--yitnu" class="skt-link">-yitnu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madayitnu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madayitnu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madayitnu" class="skt-link">f.</a> intoxicant — <a href="#entry-madayitnu" class="skt-link">m.</a> liqueur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> homme ivre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>, l'Amour qui enivre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>madayiṣṇu</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madbhakta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madbhakta</i> [<a href="#entry-bhakta" class="skt-link">bhakta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madbhakta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madbhakta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madbhaktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madbhaktā</i> qui est mon adepte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madg_rha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madgṛha</i> [<a href="#entry-g_rha" class="skt-link">gṛha</a>] <a href="#entry-madg_rha" class="skt-link">n.</a> ma maison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madgu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मद्गु</span> <i>madgu</i> <a href="#entry-madgu" class="skt-link">m.</a> oiseau plongeur; zoo. <i>Mergus</i>, canard plongeur, harle; cormoran.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मधु</span> <i>madhu</i> a. <a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhuu" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhū</i> <a href="#entry-madhvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhvī</i> doux, sucré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> plaisant, charmant; délicieux — <a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">n.</a> douceur, miel, sucre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> breuvage enivrant (hydromel?) — <a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">m.</a> premier mois véd. du printemps; syn. <a href="#entry-caitra" class="skt-link">caitra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> printemps <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du roi Madhu, fils de <a href="#entry-Ukaartaviirya" class="skt-link">Kārtavīrya</a>; <a href="#entry-Uv_r_s_ni" class="skt-link">Vṛṣṇi</a> est dit l'aîné de ses cent fils <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Madhu_1 «Douceur»; avec son frère <a href="#entry-Ukai_tabha" class="skt-link">Kaiṭabha</a> ils étaient nés comme gouttes de rosée dans le lotus primordial issu du nombril de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> (ou étaient issus du cérumen de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> endormi); ils avaient obtenu de <a href="#entry-Udevii" class="skt-link">Devī</a> par de grandes pénitences de ne mourir que s'ils le souhaitaient; ils personnifient l'orgueil; <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> les combattit en vain, puis devant leur arrogance leur demanda un vœu qui lui permît de les tuer; la Terre se forma de leur graisse; ils se réincarnèrent comme resp. <a href="#entry-Ukhara" class="skt-link">Khara</a> et <a href="#entry-Uatikaaya" class="skt-link">Atikāya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Madhu_2, qui fut tué par <a href="#entry-Uzatrughna" class="skt-link">Śatrughna</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>madhavas</i> soc. le peuple de <a href="#entry-Umadhu" class="skt-link">Madhu</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-yaadavaas" class="skt-link">yādavās</a> — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(madhusyati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(madhvasyati)</i> vouloir du miel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μεθυ; lat. <i>mel</i>; ang. mead; all. Meth; fr. miel.<br> <i>madhuprasaṅgamadhu</i> miel de printemps.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhudvi_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhudviṣ</i> [<a href="#entry-dvi_s_2" class="skt-link">dviṣ_2</a>] <a href="#entry-madhudvi_s" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Madhudviṭ, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «Ennemi de <a href="#entry-Umadhu_1" class="skt-link">Madhu_1</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मधुक</span> <i>madhuka</i> [<a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">madhu</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madhuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhukā</i> sucré; mélodieux; couleur de miel — <a href="#entry-madhuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-madhuuka" class="skt-link">madhūka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhukara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhukara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-madhukara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhukarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhukarī</i> abeille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhukarka_tii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhukarkaṭī</i> [<a href="#entry-karka_tii" class="skt-link">karkaṭī</a>] <a href="#entry-madhukarka_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Carica papaya</i>, papaye.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhulih">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhulih</i> [<a href="#entry-lih_2" class="skt-link">lih_2</a>] <a href="#entry-madhulih" class="skt-link">m.</a> abeille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhumat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhumat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madhumat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhumat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhumatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhumatī</i> doux; sucré — <a href="#entry-madhumat" class="skt-link">m.</a> (pays) où se trouve du miel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhumattama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhumattama</i> [<a href="#entry--tama" class="skt-link">-tama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madhumattama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhumattama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhumattamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhumattamā</i> super. très sucré.<br> <i>jihvā</i> <i>me</i> <i>madhumattamā</i> Que ma langue soit suavissime.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhupa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_1" class="skt-link">pa_1</a>] <a href="#entry-madhupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> abeille [«qui boit du suc»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhuparka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhuparka</i> [<a href="#entry-parka" class="skt-link">parka</a>] <a href="#entry-madhuparka" class="skt-link">m.</a> boisson sucrée à base de lait fermenté et de miel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. son offrande en bienvenue à un hôte.<br> <i>samāṃsa</i> <i>madhuparka</i> offrande à l'hôte de passage de lait caillé au miel accompagné de viande (prescription [<a href="#entry-aamnaaya" class="skt-link">āmnāya</a>] védique).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मधुर</span> <i>madhura</i> [<a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">madhu</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madhura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhuraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhurā</i> doux, suave, charmant, harmonieux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (mets) savoureux; sucré — <a href="#entry-madhura" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] le sucré, un type de goût [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] — <a href="#entry-madhuraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhurā</i> géo. np. de Madhurā «la Douce ville», épith. de <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de <a href="#entry-Umadhuraa" class="skt-link">Madhurā</a>, nymphe céleste qui visita le <a href="#entry-Ukailaasa" class="skt-link">Kailāsa</a> pour honorer <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> et s'unit à lui; <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> la maudit de devenir grenouille pendant 12 ans; <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> lui promit qu'à l'issue des 12 ans elle deviendrait une belle jeune fille et qu'elle épouserait un prince; cf. <a href="#entry-Umandodarii" class="skt-link">Mandodarī</a>.<br> <i>madhuram</i> adv. agréablement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhuraa_s_taka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhurāṣṭaka</i> [<a href="#entry-a_s_taka" class="skt-link">aṣṭaka</a>] <a href="#entry-madhuraa_s_taka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Madhurāṣṭaka, recueil de 8 vers de dévotion dédiés à la beauté de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uvallabhaacaarya" class="skt-link">Vallabhācārya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-madhura" class="skt-link">madhura</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhuraalaapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhurālāpa</i> [<a href="#entry-aalaapa" class="skt-link">ālāpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madhuraalaapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhuraalaapa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhuraalaapaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhurālāpā</i> d'adresse agréable; qui dit des mots doux — <a href="#entry-madhuraalaapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> chant mélodieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhuraamla">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhurāmla</i> [<a href="#entry-amla" class="skt-link">amla</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madhuraamla" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhuraamla" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhuraamlaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhurāmlā</i> aigre-doux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhurataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhuratā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-madhurataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> douceur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhuratva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhuratva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-madhuratva" class="skt-link">n.</a> douceur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhusuudana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhusūdana</i> [<a href="#entry-suudana" class="skt-link">sūdana</a>] <a href="#entry-madhusuudana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Madhusūdana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «Destructeur de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Umadhu_1" class="skt-link">Madhu_1</a>» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du philosophe Madhusūdana_1 <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_2</a>, né Kamalajanayana au Bengale au 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il devint ascète jeune, son maître était Viśveśvara <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_2</a>; il étudia à <a href="#entry-Unavadviipa" class="skt-link">Navadvīpa</a>, et s'établit à <a href="#entry-Uvaaraa_nasii" class="skt-link">Vārāṇasī</a>; on dit qu'il fut honoré à la cour de l'empereur moghol Akbar; il est l'auteur d'œuvres d'<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>, et not. de l'<a href="#entry-Uadvaitasiddhi" class="skt-link">Advaitasiddhi</a>, du <a href="#entry-Uprasthaanabheda" class="skt-link">Prasthānabheda</a> et du <a href="#entry-Usiddhaantabindu" class="skt-link">Siddhāntabindu</a>; sa <a href="#entry-Uguu_dhaarthadiipikaa" class="skt-link">Gūḍhārthadīpikā</a> est un commentaire de la <a href="#entry-Ugiitaa" class="skt-link">Gītā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhuuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मधूक</span> <i>madhūka</i> [<a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">madhu</a><a href="#entry--uka" class="skt-link">-uka</a>] <a href="#entry-madhuuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> abeille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. bot. <i>Madhuca Latifolia</i>, grand arbre Mahua du Sud de l'Inde, aux fleurs jaune pâle groupées à l'extrémité des branches, à l'écorce brune; utilisé en médecine, not. l'huile de Mahua extraite de ses graines — <a href="#entry-madhuuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> sa graine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cire d'abeille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhuvrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhuvrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] <a href="#entry-madhuvrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> abeille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मध्व</span> <i>madhva</i> [contr. <i>madhvācārya</i>] <a href="#entry-madhva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Madhva ou <a href="#entry-Umadhvaacaarya" class="skt-link">Madhvācārya</a> (1208–1279), philosophe opposé au monisme de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uraamaanuja" class="skt-link">Rāmānuja</a>; il fut l'élève d'<a href="#entry-Uacyutaprek_sa" class="skt-link">Acyutaprekṣa</a>; il enseignait au <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_nama_tha" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇamaṭha</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uu_dupii" class="skt-link">Uḍupī</a>, au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a>; il fonda la doctrine <a href="#entry-dvaita" class="skt-link">dvaita</a>, basée sur la <a href="#entry-bhakti" class="skt-link">bhakti</a> à <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; il est l'auteur de l'<a href="#entry-Uanuvyaakhyaana" class="skt-link">Anuvyākhyāna</a> et du <a href="#entry-Utattvasafkhyaana" class="skt-link">Tattvasaṅkhyāna</a>; aussi du commentaire du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a> <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaaratataatparyanir_naya" class="skt-link">Mahābhāratatātparyanirṇaya</a>; son œuvre est collectivement appelée <a href="#entry-sarvamuula" class="skt-link">sarvamūla</a> <a href="#entry-grantha" class="skt-link">grantha</a>; on le dit incarnation de <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Upuur_napraj_na" class="skt-link">Pūrṇaprajña</a>, <a href="#entry-Uaanandatiirtha" class="skt-link">Ānandatīrtha</a>, <a href="#entry-Udvaadazastotra" class="skt-link">Dvādaśastotra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhvaacaarya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhvācārya</i> [<a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a>] <a href="#entry-madhvaacaarya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. et titre du maître Madhvācārya; cf. <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-madhu" class="skt-link">madhu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhvavijaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhvavijaya</i> [<a href="#entry-vijaya" class="skt-link">vijaya</a>] <a href="#entry-madhvavijaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Madhvavijaya, qui conte la vie de <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-madhva" class="skt-link">madhva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मध्य</span> <i>madhya</i> a. <a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhyā</i> au centre, au milieu; moyen; ordinaire — <a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> le milieu, le centre — <a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> math. mille billions (un million de milliards) — <a href="#entry-madhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhyā</i> le majeur — ind. au milieu; parmi, entre ⟨g.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>medius</i>; ang. middle; all. Mitte; fr. médian, milieu.<br> <i>madhye</i> prép. cl. parmi, au milieu de; en présence de ⟨g.⟩.<br> <i>madhyekṛtya</i> adv. eu égard à.<br> <i>madhyena</i> adv. au milieu de, entre ⟨g. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyadeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhyadeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-deza" class="skt-link">deśa</a>] <a href="#entry-madhyadeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> région centrale; milieu du corps, tronc, ventre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. du Madhyadeśa «Région centrale», corr. à la plaine du Gange.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मध्यम्</span> <i>madhyam</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">madhya</a>] ind. au milieu de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मध्यम</span> <i>madhyama</i> [super. <a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">madhya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhyamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhyamā</i> au milieu, central, intermédiaire; médian; d'âge moyen <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> modéré, impartial, neutre; ordinaire — <a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">m.</a> [<i>madhyamapuruṣa</i>] gram. 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ pers. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (mus.) 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ note de l'octave indienne — <a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">n.</a> milieu du corps, taille, ceinture — <a href="#entry-madhyamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhyamā</i> le majeur; la matrice <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-Ubhart_rhari" class="skt-link">Bhartṛhari</a>] son au 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ stade de la manifestation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyama_tiikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhyamaṭīkā</i> [<a href="#entry-_tiikaa" class="skt-link">ṭīkā</a>] <a href="#entry-madhyama_tiikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Madhyamaṭīkā, œuvre de <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukumaarila" class="skt-link">Kumārila</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyamaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मध्यमक</span> <i>madhyamaka</i> [<a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">madhyama</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madhyamaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhyamaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhyamikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhyamikā</i> moyen <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> commun — <a href="#entry-madhyamaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. doctrine de la voie moyenne de <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a>, école du bd. <a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a> — <a href="#entry-madhyamaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> l'intérieur de qqc. — <a href="#entry-madhyamikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhyamikā</i> femme à marier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyamakav_rtti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhyamakavṛtti</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>] <a href="#entry-madhyamakav_rtti" class="skt-link">f.</a> bd. np. de la Madhyamakavṛtti, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Umuulamadhyamaka" class="skt-link">Mūlamadhyamaka</a> par <a href="#entry-Udharmakiirti" class="skt-link">Dharmakīrti</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uprasannapadaa" class="skt-link">Prasannapadā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyamakazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhyamakaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-madhyamakazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Madhyamakaśāstra «Enseignement (de la Voie) du Milieu», ouvrage de <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a>, œuvre majeure du bd. <a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-maadhyamika" class="skt-link">mādhyamika</a>, <a href="#entry-Umuulamadhyamaka" class="skt-link">Mūlamadhyamaka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-madhyamaka" class="skt-link">madhyamaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyamapadalopin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhyamapadalopin</i> [<a href="#entry-pada" class="skt-link">pada</a>-<a href="#entry-lopin" class="skt-link">lopin</a>] <a href="#entry-madhyamapadalopin" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. élision du terme médian dans un mot composé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyamaprahara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhyamaprahara</i> [<a href="#entry-prahara" class="skt-link">prahara</a>] <a href="#entry-madhyamaprahara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. garde de midi (de 11 h à 14 h).<br> <a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">madhyama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyamiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मध्यमीय</span> <i>madhyamīya</i> [<a href="#entry-madhyama" class="skt-link">madhyama</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madhyamiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhyamiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhyamiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhyamīyā</i> central.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyandina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhyaṃdina</i> [<a href="#entry-dina" class="skt-link">dina</a>] <a href="#entry-madhya_mdina" class="skt-link">m.</a> midi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. offrande de midi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. np. de Madhyaṃdina, disciple de <a href="#entry-Uyaaj_navalkya" class="skt-link">Yājñavalkya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-madhyam" class="skt-link">madhyam</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyastha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhyastha</i> [<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madhyastha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madhyastha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madhyasthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madhyasthā</i> [«qui se tient au milieu»] indifférent, neutre, impartial; intermédiaire, moyen.<br> <a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">madhya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhyatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-madhyataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> médiocrité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhye">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मध्ये</span> <i>madhye</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-madhya" class="skt-link">madhya</a>] ind. parmi, au milieu de; en présence de ⟨g.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyegafgam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhyegaṅgam</i> [<a href="#entry-gafgaa" class="skt-link">gaṅgā</a>] adv. dans le Gange.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madhyejalaat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madhyejalāt</i> [<a href="#entry-jala" class="skt-link">jala</a>] adv. hors de l'eau.<br> <a href="#entry-madhye" class="skt-link">madhye</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madiiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madiiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madiiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madiiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madīyā</i> poss. mon, mien, à moi, de moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मदिर</span> <i>madira</i> [<a href="#entry-mada" class="skt-link">mada</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madirā</i> qui enivre, excitant — <a href="#entry-madiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madirā</i> vin, liqueur, alcool, breuvage enivrant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. de la nymphe Madirā «Enivrante», première reine du roi <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> <a href="#entry-Ucitraratha" class="skt-link">Citraratha</a>, dont elle enfanta <a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मद्र</span> <i>madra</i> <a href="#entry-madra" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du Madra, contrée et peuple du Pañjāb <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> son roi; cf. <a href="#entry-Uzalya" class="skt-link">Śalya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>madrās</i> les habitants du <a href="#entry-Umadra" class="skt-link">Madra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madvacana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>madvacana</i> [<a href="#entry-vacana" class="skt-link">vacana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madvacana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madvacana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madvacanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madvacanā</i> qui parle de moi, qui parle pour moi — abl. <i>madvacanāt</i> adv. en mon nom.<br> <a href="#entry-mad_2" class="skt-link">mad_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-madya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मद्य</span> <i>madya</i> [ca. pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-mad_1" class="skt-link">mad_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-madya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-madya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-madyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>madyā</i> intoxicant, euphorisant — <a href="#entry-madya" class="skt-link">n.</a> vin, liqueur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafgala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मङ्गल</span> <i>maṅgala</i> <a href="#entry-mafgala" class="skt-link">n.</a> bénédiction, bonheur, félicité; signe propice; objet de bon augure, porte-bonheur, amulette; cf. <a href="#entry-a_s_tamafgala" class="skt-link">aṣṭamaṅgala</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cérémonie, coutume, fête — <a href="#entry-mafgala" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. du <a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a> Maṅgala «Bénéfique», personnifiant la planète Mars, et de couleur rouge [<a href="#entry-Urudhira" class="skt-link">Rudhira</a>]; [SP.] on dit que <a href="#entry-Umafgala" class="skt-link">Maṅgala</a> est né d'une goutte de sueur de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> faisant pénitence après la mort de <a href="#entry-Usatii" class="skt-link">Satī</a>, et recueillie par la Terre <a href="#entry-Ubhuumi" class="skt-link">Bhūmi</a>; ou bien qu'il est fils de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Uvaraaha" class="skt-link">Varāha</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ubhuumi" class="skt-link">Bhūmi</a>, et ainsi appelé <a href="#entry-Ubhuumisuta" class="skt-link">Bhūmisuta</a>; on dit donc qu'il est <a href="#entry-Ukuja" class="skt-link">Kuja</a>/<a href="#entry-Ubhuumija" class="skt-link">Bhūmija</a> «Né de la Terre» ou <a href="#entry-Ubhauma" class="skt-link">Bhauma</a>; il est célibataire, on le dit impulsif et aventureux; il protège la propriété et l'épouse; astr. sous son aspect sombre, il personnifie la guerre, et est expert en sciences occultes; on l'associe au cuivre, au feu, au corail, à l'été, au Sud; on l'honore le mardi [<a href="#entry-mafgalavaara" class="skt-link">maṅgalavāra</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Uafgaaraka" class="skt-link">Aṅgāraka</a> — <a href="#entry-mafgalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṅgalā</i> myth. np. de la <a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a> Maṅgalā «Favorable», aspect de la Déesse, vénérée not. en Orissa comme <a href="#entry-Uaadizakti" class="skt-link">Ādiśakti</a> sous forme aniconique (pierres); à Kakatpur elle est adorée sous la forme d'une ancienne statue bd. de <a href="#entry-Utaaraa" class="skt-link">Tārā</a>, et invoquée par des rites de divination pour la construction de l'idole de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a> [<a href="#entry-navakalevara" class="skt-link">navakalevara</a>]; on célèbre sa <a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a> tous les mardis pendant un mois, à partir de <a href="#entry-caitrii" class="skt-link">caitrī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> thai mongkhon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafgalaacara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṅgalācaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-aacara_na" class="skt-link">ācaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-mafgalaacara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. bénédiction, prière de succès d'une entreprise; invocation liminaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. lit. litanies d'invocation des dieux servant d'introduction à un ouvrage.<br> <a href="#entry-mafgala" class="skt-link">maṅgala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafgalapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṅgalapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-mafgalapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Maṅgalapura sur la côte Ouest de l'Inde du Sud (mod. Mangalore); ce fut un centre bd. important jusqu'au 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, site du temple de Maṅgaladevī-<a href="#entry-Utaaraa" class="skt-link">Tārā</a> et à la colline Kadri du temple d'<a href="#entry-Uavalokitezvara" class="skt-link">Avalokiteśvara</a>, plus tard reconverti en <a href="#entry-Uma_njunaatha" class="skt-link">Mañjunātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafgalasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṅgalasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-mafgalasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. [«fil bénéfique»] cordon que le marié noue autour du cou de son épouse lors de la cérémonie de mariage, et qu'elle doit garder tant qu'il est en vie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafgalavaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṅgalavāra</i> [<a href="#entry-vaara" class="skt-link">vāra</a>] <a href="#entry-mafgalavaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> mardi, le jour de Mars.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafgalazloka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṅgalaśloka</i> [<a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">śloka</a>] <a href="#entry-mafgalazloka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. stance de bon augure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafgalika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मङ्गलिक</span> <i>maṅgalika</i> [<a href="#entry-mafgala" class="skt-link">maṅgala</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-mafgalika" class="skt-link">n.</a> amulette.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafgalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मङ्गल्य</span> <i>maṅgalya</i> [<a href="#entry-mafgala" class="skt-link">maṅgala</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mafgalya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mafgalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mafgalyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maṅgalyā</i> de bon augure — <a href="#entry-mafgalya" class="skt-link">n.</a> porte-bonheur; prière favorable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafgalyabhik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṅgalyabhikṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhik_saa" class="skt-link">bhikṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-mafgalyabhik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. don d'un gage de bonheur; not. de vivre longtemps avec son époux.<br> <a href="#entry-mafgalya" class="skt-link">maṅgalya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafk_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मङ्क्षु</span> <i>maṅkṣu</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-mak_su" class="skt-link">makṣu</a>] adv. immédiatement, directement, instantanément <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> très, à l'excès <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vraiment.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafka_naka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मङ्कणक</span> <i>maṅkaṇaka</i> <a href="#entry-mafka_naka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [VaP.] np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Maṅkaṇaka, issu de l'esprit de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a>; prenant un jour son bain à la rivière, il y fut rejoint par la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] <a href="#entry-Urambhaa" class="skt-link">Rambhā</a> et ses suivantes; il perdit sa semence, qu'il divisa en 7 portions, donnant naissance à 7 souffles divins [<a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>]; s'étant coupé à la main avec de l'herbe <a href="#entry-kuza" class="skt-link">kuśa</a>, il en coula de la sève, ce qui le mit en joie, et sa danse extatique fit vibrer tout l'univers, provoquant l'inquiétude des dieux; <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> le rabaissa, en lui montrant que s'il se blessait lui-même c'est de la cendre qui venait; <a href="#entry-Umafka_naka" class="skt-link">Maṅkaṇaka</a> se prosterna à ses pieds, et fut pardonné; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukadaliigarbhaa" class="skt-link">Kadalīgarbhā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafkha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मङ्ख</span> <i>maṅkha</i> <a href="#entry-mafkha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. membre d'un ordre mendiant du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mafkhali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maṅkhali</i> <a href="#entry-mafkhali" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe Maṅkhali ou Makkhali; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugozaala" class="skt-link">Gośāla</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mafkha" class="skt-link">maṅkha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-magadha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मगध</span> <i>magadha</i> <a href="#entry-magadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du pays Magadha (mod. au sud du <a href="#entry-Ubihaara" class="skt-link">Bihāra</a>); sa capitale fut <a href="#entry-Uraajag_rha" class="skt-link">Rājagṛha</a>, puis <a href="#entry-Upaa_taliputra" class="skt-link">Pāṭaliputra</a> à partir du règne d'<a href="#entry-Uajaatazatru_1" class="skt-link">Ajātaśatru_1</a> (5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant.); il est le creuset de l'empire <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-magha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मघ</span> <i>magha</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-ma_mh" class="skt-link">maṃh</a>] <a href="#entry-magha" class="skt-link">m.</a> cadeau, récompense; butin — <a href="#entry-maghaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maghā</i> pl. astr. np. des Maghās «les Récompenses», formant le 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>] corr. à l'étoile Régulus (constellation du Lion); les mânes [<a href="#entry-pitaras" class="skt-link">pitaras</a>] y président; son symbole est une maison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maghavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मघवत्</span> <i>maghavat</i> [<a href="#entry-magha" class="skt-link">magha</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maghavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maghavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maghonii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maghonī</i> libéral, généreux — <a href="#entry-maghavan" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>maghavan</i> myth. np. de Maghavā, épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> «le Généreux».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-magna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मग्न</span> <i>magna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-majj" class="skt-link">majj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-magna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-magna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-magnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>magnā</i> plongé, immergé (dans ⟨loc. iic.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> disparu (not. dit de la lune après son coucher); tombé dans le malheur; qui retombe (not. seins) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a pénétré ou disparu dans ⟨loc. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मह्</span> <i>mah</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(mahati)</i> ppr. <i>(mahat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mahita</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>mahitvā</i><i>)</i> réjouir; exalter, exciter; accroître, magnifier; honorer, fêter — pr. md. <i>(mahate)</i> se réjouir — ca. <i>(mahayati)</i> faire grandir, amplifier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> réjouir; exalter; honorer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μεγας; lat. <i>magnus</i>; ang. much; fr. magnifier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मह</span> <i>maha</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">mahat</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महा</span> <i>mahā</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">mahat</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabaahu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābāhu</i> [<a href="#entry-baahu" class="skt-link">bāhu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaabaahu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaabaahu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaabaahu" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«qui a de grands bras»] puissant (se dit d'un guerrier).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābali</i> [<a href="#entry-bali" class="skt-link">bali</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaabali" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mahābali ou grand roi <a href="#entry-Ubali" class="skt-link">Bali</a>, considéré comme roi idéal de l'âge d'or, condamné à régner en enfer par la jalousie des dieux; on le célèbre au <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a> sous le nom du roi Maveli, et la fête d'Onam au nouvel an <i>malayālam</i> (au début du mois de <i>cinga</i>, corr. à <a href="#entry-bhaadrapada" class="skt-link">bhādrapada</a>) célèbre son retour sur Terre pour un jour par la faveur de <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>; dans l'Inde du Nord on le fête à <a href="#entry-diipaavali" class="skt-link">dīpāvali</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabalipura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābalipura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaabalipura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Mahābalipuram, dans le Tamil Nadu; ses temples sculptés dans le roc remontent au roi <a href="#entry-Upallava" class="skt-link">Pallava</a> <a href="#entry-Umahendravarmaa_1" class="skt-link">Mahendravarmā_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mahaabali" class="skt-link">mahābali</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabhaa_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābhāṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mahābhāṣya «Grand commentaire (sur la grammaire de <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a>)», œuvre de <a href="#entry-Upata_njali_1" class="skt-link">Patañjali_1</a> (vers-200); il donne le commentaire de 1228 règles de <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a>, et discute des règles annexes [<a href="#entry-vaarttika" class="skt-link">vārttika</a>] de <a href="#entry-Ukaatyaayana" class="skt-link">Kātyāyana</a>; il est divisé en 85 sections [<a href="#entry-aahnika" class="skt-link">āhnika</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabhaa_syadiipikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābhāṣyadīpikā</i> [<a href="#entry-diipikaa" class="skt-link">dīpikā</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaabhaa_syadiipikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. du Mahābhāṣyadīpikā «Éclaicissement du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Mahābhāṣya</a>», nom d'un traité de grammaire de <a href="#entry-Ubhart_rhari" class="skt-link">Bhartṛhari</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mahaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">mahābhāṣya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabhaaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābhāga</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaaga" class="skt-link">bhāga</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaabhaaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaabhaaga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaabhaagaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahābhāgā</i> bienheureux, fortuné; éminent — voc. adr. Votre Éminence, Votre Excellence, Noble Sire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabhaarata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābhārata</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaarata" class="skt-link">bhārata</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. récit d'épopée [<a href="#entry-itihaasa" class="skt-link">itihāsa</a>] du Mahābhārata «La Grande Inde»; il comporte 90000 strophes [<a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">śloka</a>] en 18 livres [<a href="#entry-parvan" class="skt-link">parvan</a>], plus un supplément, le <a href="#entry-Uhariva_mza" class="skt-link">Harivaṃśa</a>; il décrit la lutte fratricide entre les <a href="#entry-paa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">pāṇḍavās</a> et leurs cousins les <a href="#entry-kauravaas" class="skt-link">kauravās</a>; sa compilation prit place entre le 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant. et le 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; myth. on dit que son compositeur <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a> le dicta à <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a>; son disciple <a href="#entry-Uvaizampaayana" class="skt-link">Vaiśampāyana</a> le récita à <a href="#entry-Ujanamejaya" class="skt-link">Janamejaya</a> lors de son <a href="#entry-sarpasattra" class="skt-link">sarpasattra</a>, où <a href="#entry-Uugrazravaa" class="skt-link">Ugraśravā</a> <a href="#entry-Usauti_2" class="skt-link">Sauti_2</a> l'entendit pour le transmettre à son tour; cf. <a href="#entry-Ubhaarata_1" class="skt-link">Bhārata_1</a> — <a href="#entry-mahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>mahābhāratākhyāna</i>] id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabhaaratataatparyanir_naya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābhāratatātparyanirṇaya</i> [<a href="#entry-taatparya" class="skt-link">tātparya</a>-<a href="#entry-nir_naya" class="skt-link">nirṇaya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaabhaaratataatparyanir_naya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Mahābhāratatātparyanirṇaya, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a> par <a href="#entry-Umadhva" class="skt-link">Madhva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabhii_sma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābhīṣma</i> [<a href="#entry-bhii_sma" class="skt-link">bhīṣma</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaabhii_sma" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mahābhīṣma «Père de <a href="#entry-Ubhii_sma" class="skt-link">Bhīṣma</a>», épith. du roi <a href="#entry-Uzaantanu" class="skt-link">Śāṃtanu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabhuuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābhūta</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuuta" class="skt-link">bhūta</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaabhuuta" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] un élément grossier; syn. <a href="#entry-sthuulabhuuta" class="skt-link">sthūlabhūta</a>; opp. <a href="#entry-suuk_smabhuuta" class="skt-link">sūkṣmabhūta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabodhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābodhi</i> [<a href="#entry-bodhi" class="skt-link">bodhi</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaabodhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> bd. grande sagesse du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> — <a href="#entry-mahaabodhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. épith. du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> «Grand sage» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. du temple de Mahābodhi à <a href="#entry-Ugayaa" class="skt-link">Gayā</a>; il est le premier lieu de pélerinage bd. au Monde; il date du 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaabraahma_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahābrāhmaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaabraahma_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> (ironique) grand brâhmane <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. qualifie le <a href="#entry-viduu_saka" class="skt-link">vidūṣaka</a> not. dans son rôle de bouc émissaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. prêtre chargé des rites funèbres [<a href="#entry-zraaddha" class="skt-link">śrāddha</a>] à <a href="#entry-Uvaaraa_nasii" class="skt-link">Vārāṇasī</a> — <a href="#entry-mahaabraahma_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mahābrāhmaṇa, épith. du <a href="#entry-Utaa_n_dyabraahma_na" class="skt-link">Tāṇḍyabrāhmaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaciina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahācīna</i> [<a href="#entry-ciina" class="skt-link">cīna</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaciina" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. la Grande Chine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. les chinois.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaadaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahādāna</i> [<a href="#entry-daana" class="skt-link">dāna</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaadaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. don considérable; on en énumère 16 variétés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaadaanin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahādānin</i> [<a href="#entry-daanin" class="skt-link">dānin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaadaanin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaadaanin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaadaaninii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahādāninī</i> qui donne beaucoup — <a href="#entry-mahaadaanin" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mahādānī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukar_na" class="skt-link">Karṇa</a> qui donna tous ses ornements d'or à <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, bien qu'il dût en mourir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaadeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahādeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaadeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mahādeva, «Dieu suprême», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; il est le patron de la grammaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du médecin Māhadeva_1 <a href="#entry-Upa_n_dita" class="skt-link">Paṇḍita</a>, auteur du <a href="#entry-Uhikmatprakaaza" class="skt-link">Hikmatprakāśa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaadevii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahādevī</i> [<a href="#entry-devii" class="skt-link">devī</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaadevii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. première reine; elle est mère du prince héritier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaadhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahādhana</i> [<a href="#entry-dhana" class="skt-link">dhana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaadhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaadhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaadhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahādhanā</i> [«qui coûte cher»] précieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaahanu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāhanu</i> [<a href="#entry-hanu" class="skt-link">hanu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaahanu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaahanu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaahanu" class="skt-link">f.</a> à la mâchoire puissante.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaajana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahājana</i> [<a href="#entry-jana" class="skt-link">jana</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaajana" class="skt-link">m.</a> populace; foule <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> homme éminent, grand homme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. usurier.<br> <i>mahājane</i> adv. devant une foule; en public.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaajanapada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahājanapada</i> [<a href="#entry-janapada" class="skt-link">janapada</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaajanapada" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. l'un des 16 grands royaumes antiques de l'Inde: <a href="#entry-Ukaazi" class="skt-link">Kāśi</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukosala" class="skt-link">Kosala</a>, <a href="#entry-Uafga" class="skt-link">Aṅga</a>, <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uv_rji" class="skt-link">Vṛji</a>, <a href="#entry-Umalla" class="skt-link">Malla</a>, <a href="#entry-Ucedi" class="skt-link">Cedi</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvatsa" class="skt-link">Vatsa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukuru" class="skt-link">Kuru</a>, <a href="#entry-Upa_ncaala" class="skt-link">Pañcāla</a>, <a href="#entry-Umatsya_2" class="skt-link">Matsya_2</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzuurasena" class="skt-link">Śūrasena</a>, <a href="#entry-Uazmaka" class="skt-link">Aśmaka</a>, <a href="#entry-Uavanti" class="skt-link">Avanti</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugandhaara" class="skt-link">Gandhāra</a> et <a href="#entry-Ukamboja" class="skt-link">Kamboja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaakaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahākāla</i> [<a href="#entry-kaala_2" class="skt-link">kāla_2</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaakaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mahākāla «Grand destructeur» (ou «Eternité»), épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> sous sa forme terrible de <a href="#entry-Ubhairava" class="skt-link">Bhairava</a>; on le représente noir et effrayant, accompagné d'un chien noir; il est not. adoré comme le <a href="#entry-jyotirlifga" class="skt-link">jyotirliṅga</a> <a href="#entry-Umahaakaalezvara" class="skt-link">Mahākāleśvara</a> à <a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a>, et aussi au temple <a href="#entry-Ukaalabhairava" class="skt-link">Kālabhairava</a> au nord de la ville.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaakaalezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahākāleśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaakaalezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Mahākāleśvara, l'un des 12 signes radieux de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> [<a href="#entry-jyotirlifga" class="skt-link">jyotirliṅga</a>]; on dit qu'il est le nombril [<a href="#entry-naabhi" class="skt-link">nābhi</a>] au centre du Monde; il fut établi par <a href="#entry-Uvedapriya" class="skt-link">Vedapriya</a> et ses fils pour se protéger du démon <a href="#entry-Uduu_sa_na_1" class="skt-link">Dūṣaṇa_1</a>; établi dans le plus grand temple d'<a href="#entry-Uujjayinii" class="skt-link">Ujjayinī</a>, il est constamment ondoyé de lait, et est décoré diversement suivant les festivals; le pays [<a href="#entry-k_setra" class="skt-link">kṣetra</a>] alentour contient 84 <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>-sanctuaires, décrits dans le <a href="#entry-Uskandapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Skandapurāṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mahaakaala" class="skt-link">mahākāla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaakaavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
