    <i>mahākāvya</i> [<a href="#entry-kaavya" class="skt-link">kāvya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. [«grand poème»] poème épique de cour; les 5 les plus célèbres [<i>pañcamahākāvya</i>] sont le <a href="#entry-Uraghuva_mza" class="skt-link">Raghuvaṃśa</a> et le <a href="#entry-Ukumaarasambhava" class="skt-link">Kumārasambhava</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a>, le <a href="#entry-Uzizupaalavadha" class="skt-link">Śiśupālavadha</a> de <a href="#entry-Umaagha" class="skt-link">Māgha</a>, le <a href="#entry-Ukiraataarjuniiya" class="skt-link">Kirātārjunīya</a> de <a href="#entry-Ubhaaravi" class="skt-link">Bhāravi</a> et le <a href="#entry-Unai_sadhacarita" class="skt-link">Naiṣadhacarita</a> de <a href="#entry-Uzriihar_sa" class="skt-link">Śrīharṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaakalpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahākalpa</i> [<a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaakalpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. grand cycle de temps de l'existence de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>, pendant 100 ans brahmiques, c.-à-d. 311000 milliards d'années; il se termine par une grande extinction [<a href="#entry-mahaapralaya" class="skt-link">mahāpralaya</a>]; puis <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Unaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">Nārāyaṇa</a> dort sur le dragon Vestige, [<a href="#entry-Uze_sa" class="skt-link">Śeṣa</a>], symbolisant le résidu de l'Univers brûlé par le feu cosmique, jusqu'au cycle suivant de naissance de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaakavi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahākavi</i> [<a href="#entry-kavi" class="skt-link">kavi</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaakavi" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. titre Mahākavi de «Grand poète».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaakriyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahākriyā</i> [<a href="#entry-kriyaa" class="skt-link">kriyā</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaakriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-kriyaayoga" class="skt-link">kriyāyoga</a>] action/posture principale; on en dénombre 18.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaalak_smii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahālakṣmī</i> [<a href="#entry-lak_smii" class="skt-link">lakṣmī</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaalak_smii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Mahālakṣmī, <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a> en gloire, <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. jeune fille impubère de 13 ans représentant <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> au festival de <a href="#entry-durgaapuujaa" class="skt-link">durgāpūjā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaalifga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāliṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaalifga" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. signe de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> associé à l'un des 5 éléments; cf. <a href="#entry-pa_ncabhuutamahaalifga" class="skt-link">pañcabhūtamahāliṅga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaam_rtyu_njaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāmṛtyuñjaya</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rtyu_njaya" class="skt-link">mṛtyuñjaya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaam_rtyu_njaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>mahāmṛtyuñjayamantra</i>] phil. [<a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a>] np. du Mahāmṛtyuñjaya, grand <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> «qui vainc la mort».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaamaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāmāna</i> [<a href="#entry-maana_1" class="skt-link">māna_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaamaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> orgueil, fierté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaamaayaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāmāyā</i> [<a href="#entry-maayaa" class="skt-link">māyā</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaamaayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Mahāmāyā «l'Illusion Suprême», déesse tantr., première <a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a> parmi les 64 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-siddhaanta" class="skt-link">siddhānta</a>] la matière pure de la conscience de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.<br> <i>namaste'stu</i> <i>mahāmāye</i> <i>śrīpīṭhe</i> <i>surapūjite</i> Honneur à toi Ô <a href="#entry-Umahaamaayaa" class="skt-link">Mahāmāyā</a>, toi le socle-<a href="#entry-yoni" class="skt-link">yoni</a>, toi adorée par les dieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaamahopaadhyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāmahopādhyāya</i> [<a href="#entry-mahopaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">mahopādhyāya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaamahopaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. «Très grand maître», titre d'érudition exceptionnelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaamalla">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāmalla</i> [<a href="#entry-malla" class="skt-link">malla</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaamalla" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. np. de Mahāmalla, titre de «Grand lutteur»; utilisé not. comme titre de gloire [<a href="#entry-viruda" class="skt-link">viruda</a>] par la dynastie <a href="#entry-Upallava" class="skt-link">Pallava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaamanas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāmanas</i> [<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaamanas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaamanas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaamanas" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«qui a un grand cœur»] magnanime <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [«qui pense hautement (de soi)»] fier, hautain; arrogant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaamantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāmantra</i> [<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaamantra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaamantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. [«<i>hare</i> <i>kṛṣṇa</i> <i>hare</i> <i>kṛṣṇa</i> <i>kṛṣṇa</i> <i>kṛṣṇa</i> <i>hare</i> <i>hare</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>hare</i> <i>rāma</i> <i>hare</i> <i>rāma</i> <i>rāma</i> <i>rāma</i> <i>hare</i> <i>hare</i> ||»] Ô Dieu <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, Ô Dieu <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, Ô Dieu Ô Dieu, Ô Dieu <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>, Ô Dieu <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>, <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>, <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>, Ô Dieu Ô Dieu; <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> de <a href="#entry-bhakti" class="skt-link">bhakti</a>, à réciter en <a href="#entry-japa" class="skt-link">japa</a> ou à chanter en procession; on dit que sa récitation continuelle apporte la libération.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaamantrin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāmantrin</i> [<a href="#entry-mantrin" class="skt-link">mantrin</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaamantrin" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. titre de ministre principal d'un roi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaamoha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāmoha</i> [<a href="#entry-moha" class="skt-link">moha</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaamoha" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. illusion divine suprême <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mahāmoha «le Malin», symbolisant le pouvoir divin d'illusion, fils de <a href="#entry-Umanas" class="skt-link">Manas</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uprav_rtti" class="skt-link">Pravṛtti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaamudraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāmudrā</i> [<a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaamudraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> bd. doctrine ésotérique de la nature ultime de l'esprit; <a href="#entry-Utilopa" class="skt-link">Tilopa</a> la reçut de <a href="#entry-Uvajradhara" class="skt-link">Vajradhara</a> et l'enseigna à <a href="#entry-Unaaropa" class="skt-link">Nāropa</a>; elle fut transmise au Tibet dans la tradition Kagyūpa par <a href="#entry-Umarpa" class="skt-link">Marpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaamukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāmukha</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaamukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«Grandgousier»] np. d'un roi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaanaaraaya_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānārāyaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-naaraaya_na" class="skt-link">nārāyaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaanaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mahānārāyaṇa, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaanaaraaya_nopani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānārāyaṇopaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaanaaraaya_nopani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Mahānārāyaṇopaniṣad; cf. <a href="#entry-Utaittiriiyaara_nyaka" class="skt-link">Taittirīyāraṇyaka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mahaanaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">mahānārāyaṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaanadii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānadī</i> [<a href="#entry-nadii" class="skt-link">nadī</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaanadii" class="skt-link">f.</a> grande rivière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] épith. du Gange <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de la «Grande rivière» Mahānadī, issue du Bengale, qui se divise à <a href="#entry-Uka_taka" class="skt-link">Kaṭaka</a> en Orissa, formant un grand delta débouchant dans la baie du Bengale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaanasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānasa</i> [<a href="#entry-nasa" class="skt-link">nasa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaanasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> cuisine [«aux bonnes odeurs»] — <a href="#entry-mahaanasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahānasī</i> cuisinière.<br> <i>mahānasādau</i> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] dans la cuisine, etc. (exemple à l'appui [<a href="#entry-udaahara_na" class="skt-link">udāharaṇa</a>] dans le syllogisme utilisant la concomitance inévitable [<a href="#entry-vyaapti" class="skt-link">vyāpti</a>] entre la fumée et le feu).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaanavamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānavamī</i> [<a href="#entry-navamii" class="skt-link">navamī</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaanavamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour du festival de <a href="#entry-durgaapuujaa" class="skt-link">durgāpūjā</a>, où l'on célèbre <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>-<a href="#entry-Uaparaajitaa" class="skt-link">Aparājitā</a> en lui offrant de la canne à sucre; cf. <a href="#entry-navaraatra" class="skt-link">navarātra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaniila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānīla</i> [<a href="#entry-niila" class="skt-link">nīla</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaniila" class="skt-link">m.</a> variété de saphir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaanirvaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānirvāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-nirvaa_na" class="skt-link">nirvāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaanirvaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>mahānirvāṇatantra</i>] lit. np. du Mahānirvāṇatantra, texte ésotérique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaantaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāntaka</i> [<a href="#entry-antaka" class="skt-link">antaka</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaantaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mahāntaka, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «le grand destructeur».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaanubhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānubhāva</i> [<a href="#entry-anubhaava" class="skt-link">anubhāva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaanubhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaanubhaava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaanubhaavaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahānubhāvā</i> très puissant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> magnanime, généreux, aux idées élevées.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaanubhaavataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānubhāvatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaanubhaavataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> magnanimité, générosité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaanubhaavatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānubhāvatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaanubhaavatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> magnanimité, générosité.<br> <a href="#entry-mahaanubhaava" class="skt-link">mahānubhāva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaanuzaasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānuśāsana</i> [<a href="#entry-anuzaasana" class="skt-link">anuśāsana</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaanuzaasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mahānuśāsana, texte spécifiant les qualifications d'un <a href="#entry-ma_thaadhipati" class="skt-link">maṭhādhipati</a>, attribué à <a href="#entry-Uzafkaraacaarya" class="skt-link">Śaṅkarācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaanyaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahānyāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-nyaasa" class="skt-link">nyāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaanyaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Mahānyāsa, compilation de formules [<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] d'éloges de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaapaataka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāpātaka</i> [<a href="#entry-paataka" class="skt-link">pātaka</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaapaataka" class="skt-link">n.</a> péché mortel, sacrilège; opp. <a href="#entry-upapaataka" class="skt-link">upapātaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaapaatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāpātra</i> [<a href="#entry-paatra" class="skt-link">pātra</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaapaatra" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. premier ministre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaapadma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāpadma</i> [<a href="#entry-padma" class="skt-link">padma</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaapadma" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Mahāpadma «Grand lotus», <a href="#entry-dizaagaja" class="skt-link">diśāgaja</a> du Sud <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. l'un des 9 trésors [<a href="#entry-navanidhi" class="skt-link">navanidhi</a>] de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>; il est gardé par un dragon [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>] éponyme — <a href="#entry-mahaapadma" class="skt-link">n.</a> math. un billion (mille milliards).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaparinirvaa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāparinirvāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-parinirvaa_na" class="skt-link">parinirvāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaparinirvaa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mahāparinirvāṇa <a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>, texte bd. qui a pour objet la crémation du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> et le partage de ses cendres <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>mahaparinibbanasutta</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaapraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāprāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-praa_na" class="skt-link">prāṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaapraa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaapraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaapraa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahāprāṇā</i> qui a une grande endurance physique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. (phonème) aspiré; opp. <a href="#entry-alpapraa_na" class="skt-link">alpaprāṇa</a> — <a href="#entry-mahaapraa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. aspiration.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaprabandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāprabandha</i> [<a href="#entry-prabandha" class="skt-link">prabandha</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaprabandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. grande œuvre littéraire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaprabhu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāprabhu</i> [<a href="#entry-prabhu" class="skt-link">prabhu</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaprabhu" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. (titre) grand seigneur, prince <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mahāprabhu «Grand Seigneur», épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> ou de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; un nom de Dieu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. titre de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a> divinisé par la secte <a href="#entry-gau_diiya" class="skt-link">gauḍīya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaapraj_naapaaramitaazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāprajñāpāramitāśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-praj_naapaaramitaazaastra" class="skt-link">prajñāpāramitāśāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaapraj_naapaaramitaazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mahāprajñāpāramitāśāstra; cf. <a href="#entry-Upraj_naapaaramitaazaastra" class="skt-link">Prajñāpāramitāśāstra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaapralaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāpralaya</i> [<a href="#entry-pralaya" class="skt-link">pralaya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaapralaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. dissolution de l'univers dans la <a href="#entry-prak_rti" class="skt-link">prakṛti</a> originelle à l'extinction de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> [<a href="#entry-brahmapralaya" class="skt-link">brahmapralaya</a>] (à la fin d'un <a href="#entry-mahaakalpa" class="skt-link">mahākalpa</a>, tous les 100 ans brahmiques, c.-à-d. 311000 milliards d'années).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaprasthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāprasthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-prasthaana" class="skt-link">prasthāna</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaprasthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> grand départ, décès.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaprasthaanika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāprasthānika</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaaprasthaanika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaaprasthaanika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaaprasthaanikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahāprasthānikā</i> relatif au grand départ, qui concerne la mort — <a href="#entry-mahaaprasthaanika" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>mahāprasthānikaparvan</i>] lit. 17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>, «du grand voyage».<br> <a href="#entry-mahaaprasthaana" class="skt-link">mahāprasthāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaapratisaraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāpratisarā</i> [<a href="#entry-pratisara" class="skt-link">pratisara</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaapratisaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Mahāpratisarā «Grande protection», texte de la tradition Vajrayāna donnant des formules magiques protectrices, datant du 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle environ; aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Umahaavidyaaraaj_nii" class="skt-link">Mahāvidyārājñī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāpurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. l'un des 18 principaux livres mythologiques [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>], attribués à <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a>: <a href="#entry-Ubrahmapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Brahmapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Upadmapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Padmapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nupuraa_na" class="skt-link">Viṣṇupurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaayupuraa_na" class="skt-link">Vāyupurāṇa</a> (<a href="#entry-Uzivapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Śivapurāṇa</a>), <a href="#entry-Ubhaagavatapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Bhāgavatapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Unaaradapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Nāradapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Umaarka_n_deyapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uagnipuraa_na" class="skt-link">Agnipurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubhavi_syapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Bhaviṣyapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubrahmavaivartapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Brahmavaivartapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ulifgapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Liṅgapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaaraahapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Vārāhapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uskaandapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Skāndapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaamanapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Vāmanapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukuurmapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Kūrmapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Umatsyapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Matsyapurāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugaru_dapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Garuḍapurāṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa_n_dapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Brahmāṇḍapurāṇa</a>; opp. <a href="#entry-upapuraa_na" class="skt-link">upapurāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaapuru_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāpuruṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-puru_sa" class="skt-link">puruṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaapuru_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> grand homme; personne éminente; héros; sage, saint <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. Être suprême; épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaapuru_sanir_naya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāpuruṣanirṇaya</i> [<a href="#entry-nir_naya" class="skt-link">nirṇaya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaapuru_sanir_naya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Mahāpuruṣanirṇaya «Démonstration de l'existence de Dieu», œuvre phil. due à <a href="#entry-Uyamunaacaarya" class="skt-link">Yamunācārya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaar_nava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahārṇava</i> [<a href="#entry-ar_nava" class="skt-link">arṇava</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaar_nava" class="skt-link">m.</a> océan.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaraa_naa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahārāṇā</i> [<a href="#entry-raa_naa" class="skt-link">rāṇā</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaraa_naa" class="skt-link">m.</a> Mahārāṇā, titre de grand-roi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaraa_s_tra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahārāṣṭra</i> [<a href="#entry-raa_s_tra" class="skt-link">rāṣṭra</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. géo. le peuple des Marāṭhās — <a href="#entry-mahaaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du Mahārāṣṭra «Grand Royaume»; not. le pays marathe — <a href="#entry-mahaaraa_s_trii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahārāṣṭrī</i> leur langue, la Marāṭhī, «meilleur <a href="#entry-praak_rta" class="skt-link">prākṛta</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahārāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. np. de Mahārāja, titre de grand-roi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. titre de renonçant; adr. à un brâhmane <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. chef de congrégation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> titre de cuisinier — <a href="#entry-mahaaraaj_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahārājñī</i> reine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaraajaadhiraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahārājādhirāja</i> [<a href="#entry-adhiraaja" class="skt-link">adhirāja</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaraajaadhiraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> Mahārājādhirāja, titre de roi suprême des grand-rois; cf. <a href="#entry-Ucandragupta" class="skt-link">Candragupta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaratha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāratha</i> [<a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaratha" class="skt-link">m.</a> grand guerrier [«qui a un grand char»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaratna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāratna</i> [<a href="#entry-ratna" class="skt-link">ratna</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaratna" class="skt-link">n.</a> pierre précieuse: <a href="#entry-vajra" class="skt-link">vajra</a> le diamant, <a href="#entry-muktaa" class="skt-link">muktā</a> la perle, <a href="#entry-maa_nikya" class="skt-link">māṇikya</a> le rubis, <a href="#entry-niila" class="skt-link">nīla</a> le saphir et <a href="#entry-marakata" class="skt-link">marakata</a> l'émeraude.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaarbuda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahārbuda</i> [<a href="#entry-arbuda" class="skt-link">arbuda</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaarbuda" class="skt-link">n.</a> math. un milliard; syn. <a href="#entry-padma" class="skt-link">padma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaarha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahārha</i> var. <i>mahārgha</i> [<a href="#entry-arha" class="skt-link">arha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaarha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaarha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaarhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahārhā</i> très digne, très précieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaroman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāroman</i> [<a href="#entry-roman" class="skt-link">roman</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaroman" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [BhP.] np. du roi Mahāromā «aux cheveux longs», fils de <a href="#entry-Ukiirtiraata" class="skt-link">Kīrtirāta</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Usvar_naromaa" class="skt-link">Svarṇaromā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaasaafghika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāsāṃghika</i> [<a href="#entry-saafghika" class="skt-link">sāṃghika</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaasaa_mghika" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. bd. np. de l'école bd. des Mahāsāṃghikās; elle prospéra not. autour de Termeś (Bactriane) de 50 à 650.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaasaamanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāsāmanta</i> [<a href="#entry-saamanta" class="skt-link">sāmanta</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaasaamanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. grand vassal, titre honorifique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaasamaadhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāsamādhi</i> [<a href="#entry-samaadhi" class="skt-link">samādhi</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaasamaadhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> décès.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaasattaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāsattā</i> [<a href="#entry-sattaa" class="skt-link">sattā</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaasattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. existence absolue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaasattra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāsattra</i> [<a href="#entry-sattra" class="skt-link">sattra</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaasattra" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. grand sacrifice de <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaasattva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāsattva</i> [<a href="#entry-sattva" class="skt-link">sattva</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaasattva" class="skt-link">m.</a> grande créature — a. <a href="#entry-mahaasattva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaasattva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaasattvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahāsattvā</i> de noble essence: courageux; princier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaasena">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāsena</i> [<a href="#entry-senaa" class="skt-link">senā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaasena" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaasena" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaasenaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahāsenā</i> qui a une grande armée — <a href="#entry-mahaasena" class="skt-link">m.</a> général <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>-<a href="#entry-Ukaarttikeya" class="skt-link">Kārttikeya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du roi de <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a> Mahāsena «à la Grande armée» (fin du 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il tenta d'imposer le bd. <a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a> au détriment de la doctrine <a href="#entry-Utheravaada" class="skt-link">Theravāda</a> d'origine; il fit de grands travaux d'irrigation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. cf. <a href="#entry-Uca_n_damahaasena" class="skt-link">Caṇḍamahāsena</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaasiddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāsiddha</i> [<a href="#entry-siddha_1" class="skt-link">siddha_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaasiddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-yogin" class="skt-link">yogin</a> accompli, possédant de grands pouvoirs <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. accompli parfait; la tradition en compte 64; cf. <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a>, <a href="#entry-Utilopa" class="skt-link">Tilopa</a>, <a href="#entry-Usamantabhadra" class="skt-link">Samantabhadra</a>-<a href="#entry-Unaaropa" class="skt-link">Nāropa</a>, <a href="#entry-Umarpa" class="skt-link">Marpa</a>, <a href="#entry-Umatsyendranaatha" class="skt-link">Matsyendranātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Umilarepa" class="skt-link">Milarepa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugorak_sanaatha" class="skt-link">Gorakṣanātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaasiddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāsiddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-siddhi" class="skt-link">siddhi</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaasiddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> grand pouvoir d'un <a href="#entry-yogin" class="skt-link">yogin</a> accompli <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. pouvoir d'un accompli parfait.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaasthaamapraapta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāsthāmaprāpta</i> [<a href="#entry-sthaaman" class="skt-link">sthāman</a>-<a href="#entry-praapta" class="skt-link">prāpta</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaasthaamapraapta" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. [<a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a>] np. du <a href="#entry-bodhisattva" class="skt-link">bodhisattva</a> Mahāsthāmaprāpta.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaasvaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāsvāda</i> [<a href="#entry-svaada" class="skt-link">svāda</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaasvaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaasvaada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaasvaadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahāsvādā</i> savoureux, délicieux — <a href="#entry-mahaasvaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. brâmane cuisinier de temple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaatala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahātala</i> [<a href="#entry-tala" class="skt-link">tala</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaatala" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. du monde [<a href="#entry-loka" class="skt-link">loka</a>] Mahātala, sixième enfer souterrain.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaatapa_hsaptamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahātapaḥsaptamī</i> [<a href="#entry-saptamii" class="skt-link">saptamī</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaatapa_hsaptamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour d'une quinzaine (<a href="#entry-pak_sa" class="skt-link">pakṣa</a>), consacré au jeûne.<br> <a href="#entry-mahaatapas" class="skt-link">mahātapas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaatapas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahātapas</i> [<a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaatapas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaatapas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaatapas" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui pratique de sévères austérités — <a href="#entry-mahaatapas" class="skt-link">m.</a> grand ascète; qualifie not. <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> et <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaatattva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahātattva</i> [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaatattva" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">mahat</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaatejas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahātejas</i> [<a href="#entry-tejas" class="skt-link">tejas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaatejas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaatejas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaatejas" class="skt-link">f.</a> très glorieux; qui a une grande force spirituelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaatiirtha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahātīrtha</i> [<a href="#entry-tiirtha" class="skt-link">tīrtha</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaatiirtha" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. grand lieu de pélerinage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaatman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahātman</i> [<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaatman" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaatman" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaatmanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahātmanī</i> magnanime, noble, sage — <a href="#entry-mahaatman" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. l'Esprit suprême; être ayant atteint la réalisation divine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. np. de Mahātmā «Grande Âme», épith. de Gandhi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaatmya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahātmya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaatmya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaatmya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaatmyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahātmyā</i> magnanime — <a href="#entry-mahaatmya" class="skt-link">n.</a> magnanimité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> grandeur (d'un endroit ou d'un personnage) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. glorification (not. de lieux de pélerinage).<br> <a href="#entry-mahaatman" class="skt-link">mahātman</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaava_mza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvaṃśa</i> [<a href="#entry-va_mza" class="skt-link">vaṃśa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaava_mza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Mahāvaṃśa «Grande chronique» des rois de <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>, plus long poème épique en langue <a href="#entry-paali" class="skt-link">pāli</a>, dû à Mahānāma (5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il commence avec le règne du roi mythique <a href="#entry-Uvijaya_2" class="skt-link">Vijaya_2</a>-<a href="#entry-Usi_mhala" class="skt-link">Siṃhala</a> et finit avec celui du roi <a href="#entry-Umahaasena" class="skt-link">Mahāsena</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaavaakya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvākya</i> [<a href="#entry-vaakya" class="skt-link">vākya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaavaakya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. grande œuvre littéraire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. discours, par opposition à énonciation d'une phrase sans contexte (<a href="#entry-vaakya" class="skt-link">vākya</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] formule importante de l'enseignement moniste, telle que <i>tat</i> <i>tvam</i> <i>asi</i> ou <i>so'ham</i> ou <i>brahmāsmi</i> ou <i>neti</i> <i>neti</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaavak_sas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvakṣas</i> [<a href="#entry-vak_sas" class="skt-link">vakṣas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaavak_sas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaavak_sas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaavak_sas" class="skt-link">f.</a> à la large poitrine (se dit des héros).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaavana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvana</i> [<a href="#entry-vana" class="skt-link">vana</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaavana" class="skt-link">n.</a> grande forêt, jungle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaavedi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvedi</i> [<a href="#entry-vedi" class="skt-link">vedi</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaavedi" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. [«grand autel»] aire englobante du rituel véd. solennel, à l'est de la hutte contenant le petit autel [<a href="#entry-praaciinava_mza" class="skt-link">prācīnavaṃśa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaavibhaa_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvibhāṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-vibhaa_saa" class="skt-link">vibhāṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaavibhaa_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> alternative, option <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. problème général de l'optionalité des règles de <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaavidyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvidyā</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> grande science; connaissance suprême <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [tantr.] aspect de la puissance [<a href="#entry-Uzakti" class="skt-link">Śakti</a>] de la Déesse [<a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>]; on en liste traditionnellement 10: cf. <a href="#entry-dazamahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">daśamahāvidyā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaavidyaalaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvidyālaya</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaalaya" class="skt-link">vidyālaya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaavidyaalaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> collège.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaavidyaaraaj_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvidyārājñī</i> [<a href="#entry-raaj_nii" class="skt-link">rājñī</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaavidyaaraaj_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Mahāvidyārājñī «Reine des incantations protectrices»; cf. <a href="#entry-Umahaapratisaraa" class="skt-link">Mahāpratisarā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mahaavidyaa" class="skt-link">mahāvidyā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaavihaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvihāra</i> [<a href="#entry-vihaara" class="skt-link">vihāra</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaavihaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. grand monastère; cf. <a href="#entry-Uvikramaziila" class="skt-link">Vikramaśīla</a>, <a href="#entry-Unaalandaa" class="skt-link">Nālandā</a>, <a href="#entry-Usomapura" class="skt-link">Somapura</a>, <a href="#entry-Uuddaantapurii" class="skt-link">Uddāntapurī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ujagaddala" class="skt-link">Jagaddala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaviira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvīra</i> [<a href="#entry-viira" class="skt-link">vīra</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaviira" class="skt-link">m.</a> héros <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mahāvīra, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a> le «Grand Héros» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. épith. de l'ascète <a href="#entry-Uvardhamaana" class="skt-link">Vardhamāna</a> Mahāvīra_1, fondateur du Jaïnisme (~599–527 ant.); né prince à <a href="#entry-Uvaizaalii_1" class="skt-link">Vaiśālī_1</a> au <a href="#entry-Ubihaara" class="skt-link">Bihāra</a>, il quitta sa famille à 28 ans et passa 12 ans en austérités avant d'atteindre la libération [<a href="#entry-kaivalya" class="skt-link">kaivalya</a>]; il parcourut alors le <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a> pour prêcher la doctrine victorieuse [<a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>]; selon la tradition <a href="#entry-zvetaambara" class="skt-link">śvetāmbara</a>, après sa conception par la brâhmane Devānandā, son embryon aurait été transféré dans la matrice de la princesse Triśalā <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. pot servant au sacrifice de <a href="#entry-pravargya" class="skt-link">pravargya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaviiracarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvīracarita</i> [<a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">carita</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaaviiracarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mahāvīracarita «Exploits du Grand Héros», célèbre drame de <a href="#entry-Ubhavabhuuti" class="skt-link">Bhavabhūti</a>; il conte l'histoire de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> jusqu'à son départ dans la forêt; Vīrarāghava en fit un commentaire.<br> <a href="#entry-mahaaviira" class="skt-link">mahāvīra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaaviirya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvīrya</i> [<a href="#entry-viirya" class="skt-link">vīrya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaaviirya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaaviirya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaaviiryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahāvīryā</i> très puissant, d'une grande force; très efficace — <a href="#entry-mahaaviirya" class="skt-link">m.</a> patate douce <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de nombreux dieux ou héros <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [BhP.] np. du «très énergique» roi Mahāvīrya <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Ub_rhadratha_2" class="skt-link">Bṛhadratha_2</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Usudh_rti" class="skt-link">Sudhṛti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaavrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaavrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] grand vœu (de non-violence, de véracité, de probité, de chasteté et de désintéressement) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. «grande observance» religieuse, cérémonie véd. du solstice d'hiver, variante du rituel de l'<a href="#entry-Uagni_s_toma" class="skt-link">Agniṣṭoma</a> célébrée l'avant-dernier jour de la période du <a href="#entry-gavaamayana" class="skt-link">gavāmayana</a>; il comporte le balancement d'un prêtre sur une escarpolette, un dialogue obscène entre un étudiant [<a href="#entry-brahmacarya" class="skt-link">brahmacarya</a>] et une prostituée, une copulation rituelle entre la prostituée et un homme du <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>, le combat d'un <a href="#entry-aarya" class="skt-link">ārya</a> et d'un <a href="#entry-zuudra" class="skt-link">śūdra</a> pour un disque de peau blanche représentant le soleil, et une danse aux amphores pratiquée par les jeunes filles autour du feu; finalement le roi sur son char, après la circumambulation de l'espace sacrificiel [<a href="#entry-vedi" class="skt-link">vedi</a>], lance trois flèches sur une peau circulaire tendue entre deux poteaux pour «gagner le soleil»; on y consomme le <a href="#entry-sthaaliipaaka" class="skt-link">sthālīpāka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. observance rituelle <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>; la liste traditionnelle en comporte douze: les 5 vœux mineurs [<a href="#entry-a_nuvrata" class="skt-link">aṇuvrata</a>] de tout disciple, les 3 vœux méritoires [<a href="#entry-gu_navrata" class="skt-link">guṇavrata</a>] et les 4 vœux disciplinaires [<a href="#entry-zik_saavrata" class="skt-link">śikṣāvrata</a>] du renonçant.<br> <i>jāti</i> <i>deśa</i> <i>kāla</i> <i>samayān</i> <i>avicchinnaḥ</i> <i>sārvabhaumaḥ</i> <i>tad</i> <i>mahāvratam</i> [YS.] Indépendant de la naissance, du lieu, du temps et des circonstances, ceci est le grand vœu universel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaavyutpatti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāvyutpatti</i> [<a href="#entry-vyutpatti" class="skt-link">vyutpatti</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaavyutpatti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Mahāvyutpatti, dictionnaire bilingue sanskrit-tibétain de notions bd. compilé au Tibet au 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle avec le patronage du roi
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaayaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāyāna</i> [<a href="#entry-yaana" class="skt-link">yāna</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaayaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> bd. np. du Mahāyāna «grande voie (de libération)» ou «grand véhicule», doctrine bouddhiste réformée, opposée au «petit véhicule» [<a href="#entry-Uhiinayaana" class="skt-link">Hīnayāna</a>]; sa source philosophique remonte à <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a> et <a href="#entry-Uaaryadeva" class="skt-link">Āryadeva</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-madhyamaka" class="skt-link">madhyamaka</a>, <a href="#entry-cittamaatra" class="skt-link">cittamātra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaayaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāyajña</i> [<a href="#entry-yaj_na" class="skt-link">yajña</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaayaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [SB.] véd. l'un des cinq rites majeurs du rituel <a href="#entry-zrauta" class="skt-link">śrauta</a> prescrits d'un maître de maison accompli [<a href="#entry-aahitaagni" class="skt-link">āhitāgni</a>]: <a href="#entry-brahmayaj_na" class="skt-link">brahmayajña</a> le sacrifice au <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a>, <a href="#entry-devayaj_na" class="skt-link">devayajña</a> le sacrifice aux dieux, <a href="#entry-manu_syayaj_na" class="skt-link">manuṣyayajña</a> le sacrifice aux hommes, <a href="#entry-pit_ryaj_na" class="skt-link">pitṛyajña</a> le sacrifice aux ancêtres et <a href="#entry-bhuutayaj_na" class="skt-link">bhūtayajña</a> le sacrifice aux esprits.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaayamaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāyamaka</i> [<a href="#entry-yamaka" class="skt-link">yamaka</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaayamaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. rhét. figure de style [<a href="#entry-alafkaara" class="skt-link">alaṃkāra</a>] où les quatre quarts d'une stance sont identiques, mais de sens différents.<br> <i>vikāśamīyurjagatīśamārgaṇāḥ</i> <i>vikāśamīyurjagatīśamārgaṇāḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>vikāśamīyurjagatīśamārgaṇāḥ</i> <i>vikāśamīyurjagatīśamārgaṇāḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<a href="#entry-Ukiraataarjuniiya" class="skt-link">Kirātārjunīya</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaayuga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāyuga</i> [<a href="#entry-yuga" class="skt-link">yuga</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaayuga" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. ère, période cumulée des 4 âges du monde [<a href="#entry-yuga" class="skt-link">yuga</a>], valant environ 4,32 millions d'années; il y en a 1000 dans un <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>.<br> <i>sahasra</i> <i>yuga</i> <i>paryantam</i> <i>ahar</i> <i>yad</i> <i>brahmaṇo</i> <i>viduḥ</i> [BhG.] On sait qu'un jour (1/2 journée) de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> vaut 1 OOO ères.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaazaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāśāla</i> [<a href="#entry-zaala" class="skt-link">śāla</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaazaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaazaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaazaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahāśālā</i> soc. riche maître de maison.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaazaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāśaya</i> [<a href="#entry-aazaya" class="skt-link">āśaya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahaazaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«grand réceptacle»] océan — a. <a href="#entry-mahaazaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaazaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaazayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahāśayā</i> qui a de nobles sentiments, respectable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaazveta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahāśveta</i> [<a href="#entry-zveta" class="skt-link">śveta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaazveta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaazveta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaazvetaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahāśvetā</i> d'un blanc éclatant — <a href="#entry-mahaazvetaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahāśvetā</i> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. de la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] Mahāśvetā «Blanchissime», fille du <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> <a href="#entry-Uha_msa_1" class="skt-link">Haṃsa_1</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ugaurii_1" class="skt-link">Gaurī_1</a>; elle pleure la perte du bel ascète <a href="#entry-Upu_n_dariika_2" class="skt-link">Puṇḍarīka_2</a> qui a préféré la mort à rompre ses vœux en cédant à l'amour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महन्त</span> <i>mahanta</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">mahat</a>] <a href="#entry-mahanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. supérieur d'un monastère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahar_si">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maharṣi</i> [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-mahar_si" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. grand sage; cf. <a href="#entry-saptar_si" class="skt-link">saptarṣi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. np. du Maharṣi <a href="#entry-Umaheza" class="skt-link">Maheśa</a> Yogī, titre du maître spirituel Maheśa Prasāda Varma (1917–2008); après des études de physique, il devint disciple du <a href="#entry-zafkaraacaarya" class="skt-link">śaṃkarācārya</a> <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaananda" class="skt-link">Brahmānanda</a> <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_2</a> à <a href="#entry-Ubadariinaatha" class="skt-link">Badarīnātha</a>; il créa le mouvement international de Méditation Transcendantale en 1955.<br> <a href="#entry-maha" class="skt-link">maha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maharloka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maharloka</i> [<a href="#entry-loka" class="skt-link">loka</a>] <a href="#entry-maharloka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mahar le «Ciel de gloire», 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ monde céleste [<a href="#entry-loka" class="skt-link">loka</a>], au-dessus de l'étoile polaire; <a href="#entry-Ubh_rgu" class="skt-link">Bhṛgu</a> y demeure, avec les sages [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] qui survivent à la dissolution des mondes.<br> <a href="#entry-mahas" class="skt-link">mahas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महस्</span> <i>mahas</i> [<a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">mahat</a>] <a href="#entry-mahas" class="skt-link">n.</a> puissance, gloire, grandeur — ind. <i>mahar</i> [<a href="#entry-maharloka" class="skt-link">maharloka</a>] myth. cf. <a href="#entry-vyaah_rti" class="skt-link">vyāhṛti</a>.<br> <i>mahobhis</i> adv. puissamment, avec grandeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महत्</span> <i>mahat</i> [<a href="#entry-mah" class="skt-link">mah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahatī</i> grand (en espace, en temps, en quantité); haut, vaste, éminent, important <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> riche en ⟨i.⟩ — <a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">n.</a> chose importante; connaissance sacrée — <a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">m.</a> [<i>mahātattva</i>] phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] l'essence [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>] de l'Intellect [<a href="#entry-buddhi" class="skt-link">buddhi</a>], ou pouvoir de discrimination; c'est le premier stade d'évolution de <a href="#entry-prak_rti" class="skt-link">prakṛti</a>, où apparaît la distinction de la qualité [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. supérieur d'un temple ou d'un monastère.<br> <i>mahāntaṃ</i> <i>kālam</i> pendant longtemps.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महता</span> <i>mahatā</i> [<a href="#entry-maha" class="skt-link">maha</a><a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-mahataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> grandeur; importance; puissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahattama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महत्तम</span> <i>mahattama</i> [super. <a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">mahat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahattama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahattama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahattamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahattamā</i> le plus grand, le plus important.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahaujas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahaujas</i> [<a href="#entry-ojas" class="skt-link">ojas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahaujas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaujas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahaujas" class="skt-link">f.</a> très fort.<br> <a href="#entry-mahaa" class="skt-link">mahā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahe">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महे</span> <i>mahe</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-mahaa" class="skt-link">mahā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahe_svaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maheṣvāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-i_svaasa" class="skt-link">iṣvāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahe_svaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> maître archer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahendra</i> [<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-mahendra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mahendra, épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> «Majestueux» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>mahendragiri</i>] géo. np. des monts Mahendra_1 (1500 m) au sud de l'Orissa (mod. district de Gajapati); myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] <a href="#entry-Uparazuraama" class="skt-link">Paraśurāma</a>-<a href="#entry-Ujaamadagnya" class="skt-link">Jāmadagnya</a> y établit son ermitage et y fit pénitence; <a href="#entry-Ukar_na" class="skt-link">Karṇa</a> lui rendit visite pour apprendre de lui la science des armes; il y tua par accident le veau de la vache d'un brâhmane, qui le maudit en prophétisant sa mort au combat, la roue de son chariot enlisée dans la boue; <a href="#entry-Uparazuraama" class="skt-link">Paraśurāma</a> lui révéla le secret du <a href="#entry-brahmaastra" class="skt-link">brahmāstra</a>, mais comprenant qu'il lui avait menti en se faisant passer pour un brâhmane, il le chassa en prophétisant qu'il oublierait le <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> de l'arme lors de son combat avec son principal ennemi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahendravarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahendravarman</i> [<a href="#entry-varman" class="skt-link">varman</a>] <a href="#entry-mahendravarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du prince <a href="#entry-Upallava" class="skt-link">Pallava</a> Mahendravarmā_1, fils de Siṃhaviṣṇu (571-630); il régnait à <a href="#entry-Ukaa_ncii" class="skt-link">Kāñcī</a>, son titre de gloire [<a href="#entry-viruda" class="skt-link">viruda</a>] était Śatrumalla; il protégea les arts, et fit exécuter les premiers temples creusés dans le roc à Mahābalipuram; il est l'auteur de la pièce satirique <a href="#entry-Umattavilaasa" class="skt-link">Mattavilāsa</a> <a href="#entry-prahasana" class="skt-link">prahasana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maheza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maheśa</i> [<a href="#entry-iiza" class="skt-link">īśa</a>] <a href="#entry-maheza" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Maheśa «Grand Seigneur», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maheśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-mahezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> souverain, dieu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Maheśvara «le Seigneur Suprême», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> (ou plus rarement de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>) — <a href="#entry-mahezvarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maheśvarī</i> cf. <a href="#entry-maahezvarii" class="skt-link">māheśvarī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahezvarakha_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maheśvarakhaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-kha_n_da" class="skt-link">khaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahezvarakha_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahezvarakha_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Maheśvarakhaṇḍa, section du <a href="#entry-Uskandapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Skandapurāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahezvarasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maheśvarasūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-mahezvarasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-pratyaahaarasuutra" class="skt-link">pratyāhārasūtra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mahezvara" class="skt-link">maheśvara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महि</span> <i>mahi</i> [<a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">mahat</a>] ind. véd. grand <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> grandeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>mahī</i>] la Terre — adv. très; trop.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahi_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महिष्ठ</span> <i>mahiṣṭha</i> [super. <a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">mahat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahi_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahi_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahi_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahiṣṭhā</i> le plus grand.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahi_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महिष</span> <i>mahiṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-mahi" class="skt-link">mahi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahi_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahi_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahi_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahiṣī</i> important, puissant — <a href="#entry-mahi_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> buffle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cf. <a href="#entry-mahi_saasura" class="skt-link">mahiṣāsura</a> — <a href="#entry-mahi_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahiṣī</i> buffle femelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. première reine d'un roi, femme de haut rang; opp. <a href="#entry-vaavaataa" class="skt-link">vāvātā</a>, <a href="#entry-pariv_rktaa" class="skt-link">parivṛktā</a>, <a href="#entry-paalaagalii" class="skt-link">pālāgalī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mahiṣī, sœur de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Umahi_sa" class="skt-link">Mahiṣa</a>, qui pour le venger fit de telles pénitences que <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> lui accorda la royauté sur les trois mondes, et d'être invulnérable à toute créature n'ayant pas la force combinée de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; elle fut vaincue par leur synthèse <a href="#entry-Uhariharaputra" class="skt-link">Hariharaputra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahi_saasura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahiṣāsura</i> [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] <a href="#entry-mahi_saasura" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Mahiṣa, démon-buffle invulnérable à tout mâle; il fut vaincu par la Déesse <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>, utilisant les armes de tous les dieux réunis; les dieux lui avaient accordé de ne mourir qu'après avoir contemplé le sexe de la Déesse; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahi_saasuramardinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahiṣāsuramardinī</i> [<a href="#entry-mardinii" class="skt-link">mardinī</a>] <a href="#entry-mahi_saasuramardinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Mahiṣāsuramardinī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a> «Terrassant le démon-buffle» grâce aux armes de tous les dieux réunis.<br> <a href="#entry-mahi_saasura" class="skt-link">mahiṣāsura</a> <br> <a href="#entry-mahi_sa" class="skt-link">mahiṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahi_smat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महिष्मत्</span> <i>mahiṣmat</i> [<a href="#entry-mahi_sa" class="skt-link">mahiṣa</a><a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahi_smat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahi_smat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahi_smatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahiṣmatī</i> (pays) riche en buffles — <a href="#entry-mahi_smatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahiṣmatī</i> géo. np. de la ville sacrée de Mahiṣmatī «Riche en buffles», sur les berges de la <a href="#entry-Unarmadaa" class="skt-link">Narmadā</a>; c'était la capitale des <a href="#entry-Uhaihayaas" class="skt-link">Haihayās</a>, not. de <a href="#entry-Ukaartaviirya" class="skt-link">Kārtavīrya</a> <a href="#entry-Uarjuna_1" class="skt-link">Arjuna_1</a>; mod. Maheswar au Madhya Pradesh, à 70 km au sud-ouest d'Indore.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahidaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahidāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-daasa" class="skt-link">dāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahidaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de Mahidāsa «Esclave de la Terre», auteur de l'<a href="#entry-Uaitareya" class="skt-link">Aitareya</a> <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahii_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मही</span> <i>mahī_1</i> [<a href="#entry-mahatii" class="skt-link">mahatī</a>] <a href="#entry-mahii_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> la Terre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> territoire, sol.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahii_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मही</span> <i>mahī_2</i> [vn. <i>mahā</i>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(mahīyate)</i> être honoré, être adoré; être heureux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahiibhuj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahībhuj</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuj_3" class="skt-link">bhuj_3</a>] <a href="#entry-mahiibhuj" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«qui jouit de la terre»].<br> <a href="#entry-mahii_1" class="skt-link">mahī_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahiidhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahīdhara</i> [<a href="#entry-dhara" class="skt-link">dhara</a>] <a href="#entry-mahiidhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui supporte la Terre»] montagne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Mahīdhara, commentateur du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> du 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on lui doit le <a href="#entry-Umantramahodadhi" class="skt-link">Mantramahodadhi</a> et le <a href="#entry-Uvedadiipa" class="skt-link">Vedadīpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahiidhra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahīdhra</i> [<a href="#entry-mahiidhara" class="skt-link">mahīdhara</a>] <a href="#entry-mahiidhra" class="skt-link">m.</a> montagne <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 7.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahiidhraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahīdhraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-mahiidhraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi Mahīdhraka <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Uvibudha" class="skt-link">Vibudha</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Ukiirtiraata" class="skt-link">Kīrtirāta</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mahiidhra" class="skt-link">mahīdhra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahiipaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahīpāla</i> [<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-mahiipaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi [«protecteur de la terre»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahiipati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahīpati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-mahiipati" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi, souverain [«maître de la terre»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahiitala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahītala</i> [<a href="#entry-tala" class="skt-link">tala</a>] <a href="#entry-mahiitala" class="skt-link">n.</a> surface de la terre; sol.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahiiyas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महीयस्</span> <i>mahīyas</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-mahat" class="skt-link">mahat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahiiyas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahiiyas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahiiyasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahīyasī</i> plus grand.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahilaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महिला</span> <i>mahilā</i> [<a href="#entry-mah" class="skt-link">mah</a>] <a href="#entry-mahilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> femme; femme exhaltée ou ivre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sorte de drogue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [PT.] np. de la rivière fictive Mahilā «Impétueuse».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahilaaropya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahilāropya</i> [<a href="#entry-aaropya" class="skt-link">āropya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahilaaropya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. de Mahilāropya «Perchée sur la Mahilā», ville d'Inde du Sud fictive évoquée dans les recensions nordiques du <a href="#entry-Upa_ncatantra" class="skt-link">Pañcatantra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mahilaa" class="skt-link">mahilā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahima">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महिम</span> <i>mahima</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-mahiman" class="skt-link">mahiman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahimabha_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahimabhaṭṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-bha_t_ta" class="skt-link">bhaṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-mahimabha_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Mahimabhaṭṭa, théoricien de l'esthétique littéraire, auteur du <a href="#entry-Uvyaktiviveka" class="skt-link">Vyaktiviveka</a> (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il nie l'autonomie d'une fonction sémantique de sens suggéré [<a href="#entry-vya_njanaa" class="skt-link">vyañjanā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-mahima" class="skt-link">mahima</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahiman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महिमन्</span> <i>mahiman</i> [<a href="#entry-mahi" class="skt-link">mahi</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-mahiman" class="skt-link">m.</a> grandeur, majesté; gloire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. la toute-grandeur, un attribut de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] le pouvoir de grandir à volonté, un <a href="#entry-a_s_tasiddhi" class="skt-link">aṣṭasiddhi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> np. d'homme Mahimā «le Grand» — <a href="#entry-mahimaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahimā</i> grandeur, majesté.<br> <i>mahimādharma</i> mod. mouvement de dissidents hindous né en Orissa au 19⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, opposé au culte des idoles et not. de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahimatī</i> abondant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महित</span> <i>mahita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mah" class="skt-link">mah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahitā</i> honoré, célébré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahivrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahivrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahivrata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahivrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahivrataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahivratā</i> qui a de grand pouvoirs, puissant.<br> <a href="#entry-mahi" class="skt-link">mahi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maho">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">महो</span> <i>maho</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-mahaa" class="skt-link">mahā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahodadhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahodadhi</i> [<a href="#entry-udadhi" class="skt-link">udadhi</a>] <a href="#entry-mahodadhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> océan.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahodara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahodara</i> [<a href="#entry-udara" class="skt-link">udara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahodara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahodara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahodaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahodarā</i> qui a une grosse panse — <a href="#entry-mahodara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mahodara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> «au gros ventre».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahodaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahodaya</i> [<a href="#entry-udaya" class="skt-link">udaya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahodaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahodaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahodayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahodayā</i> qui donne beaucoup (de bonheur); très prospère — cl. adr. bienheureux; très cher; Monsieur — <a href="#entry-mahodaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> grande prospérité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du Mont Mahodaya qu'<a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a> ramena avec ses simples pour guérir <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a> de ses blessures; cf. <a href="#entry-Uv_r_sa" class="skt-link">Vṛṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de l'ascète Mahodaya_1, qui refusa d'assister au sacrifice de <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a> pour <a href="#entry-Utrizafku" class="skt-link">Triśaṅku</a> et qui en fut maudit de devenir hors-caste — <a href="#entry-mahodayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahodayā</i> myth. np. de Mahodayā «Prospère», épith. de la ville de <a href="#entry-Ukanyaakubjaa" class="skt-link">Kanyākubjā</a>, capitale de <a href="#entry-Ukuzanaabha" class="skt-link">Kuśanābha</a>.<br> <i>devadatta</i> <i>mahodayasya</i> l'estimé Devadatta.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahodayapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahodayapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-mahodayapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Mahodayapura; cf. <a href="#entry-Umahodayaa" class="skt-link">Mahodayā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahok_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahokṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-uk_san" class="skt-link">ukṣan</a>] <a href="#entry-mahok_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> gros taureau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahopaadhyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahopādhyāya</i> [<a href="#entry-upaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">upādhyāya</a>] <a href="#entry-mahopaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. «Grand maître», titre d'érudition et d'accomplissement spirituel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahopani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahopaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-mahopani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. leçon ésotérique [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] majeure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahoraga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahoraga</i> [<a href="#entry-uraga" class="skt-link">uraga</a>] <a href="#entry-mahoraga" class="skt-link">m.</a> grand serpent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. une classe de démons, les <i>mahoragās</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahoraska">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahoraska</i> [<a href="#entry-uraska" class="skt-link">uraska</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mahoraska" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mahoraska" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mahoraskaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahoraskā</i> à la large poitrine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahotsava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mahotsava</i> [<a href="#entry-utsava" class="skt-link">utsava</a>] <a href="#entry-mahotsava" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. grand festival <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. ville de Mahotsava, fondée par Candravarman vers 800; elle succéda à <a href="#entry-Ukhajuraaho" class="skt-link">Khajurāho</a> comme capitale du royaume <a href="#entry-Ucandela" class="skt-link">Candela</a>; mod. Mahoba — <a href="#entry-mahotsavaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mahotsavā</i> ifc. soc. grand festival de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mahyam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मह्यम्</span> <i>mahyam</i> [dat. <a href="#entry-aham" class="skt-link">aham</a>] ind. pour moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mainaaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मैनाक</span> <i>maināka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-menaa" class="skt-link">menā</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-mainaaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Maināka, fils de <a href="#entry-Uhimavaan" class="skt-link">Himavān</a> et de <a href="#entry-Umenaa" class="skt-link">Menā</a>; il personnifie une montagne au nord du <a href="#entry-Ukailaasa" class="skt-link">Kailāsa</a>; lorsqu'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> coupa les ailes des montagnes, il fut le seul à garder les siennes; on dit que l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Ukrau_nca" class="skt-link">Krauñca</a> est son fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maireya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मैरेय</span> <i>maireya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-miira" class="skt-link">mīra</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maireya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maireya" class="skt-link">n.</a> boisson alcoolisée, not. à base de rhum [<a href="#entry-aasava" class="skt-link">āsava</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maireyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maireyaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-maireyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maireyaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-maireya" class="skt-link">maireya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maithila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मैथिल</span> <i>maithila</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mithila" class="skt-link">mithila</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maithila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maithila" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maithilii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maithilī</i> relatif à <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a> — <a href="#entry-maithila" class="skt-link">m.</a> habitant, roi de <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. le Maithila, l'un des cinq pays de l'Inde du Nord [<a href="#entry-pa_ncagau_da" class="skt-link">pañcagauḍa</a>]; mod. Bihar — <a href="#entry-maithilii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maithilī</i> langue parlée à <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Maithilī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a>, fille de <a href="#entry-Ujanakaraaja" class="skt-link">Janakarāja</a>, «princesse de <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maithuna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मैथुन</span> <i>maithuna</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mithuna" class="skt-link">mithuna</a>] <a href="#entry-maithuna" class="skt-link">n.</a> union, association; mariage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accouplement, copulation, coït.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मैत्र</span> <i>maitra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mitra" class="skt-link">mitra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maitra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maitra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maitrī</i> amical — <a href="#entry-maitra" class="skt-link">n.</a> amitié <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>mettā</i>.<br> <i>sarvabhūtānāṃ</i> <i>maitra</i> ami de toutes les créatures.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitraavaru_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
