    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मैत्रावरुण</span> <i>maitrāvaruṇa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mitraavaru_na" class="skt-link">mitrāvaruṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-maitraavaru_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. nom du prêtre premier assistant du <a href="#entry-hot_r" class="skt-link">hotṛ</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-prazaast_r" class="skt-link">praśāstṛ</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Maitrāvaruṇa «issu de <a href="#entry-Umitra" class="skt-link">Mitra</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>», épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uagasti" class="skt-link">Agasti</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> — <a href="#entry-maitraavaru_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maitrāvaruṇī</i> myth. np. de Maitrāvaruṇī, un nom d'<a href="#entry-Uarundhatii" class="skt-link">Arundhatī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitraaya_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maitrāyaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ayana" class="skt-link">ayana</a>] <a href="#entry-maitraaya_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> bon traitement, bienveillance — <a href="#entry-maitraaya_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. nom d'une école du <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> — f. cf. <a href="#entry-maitraaya_nii" class="skt-link">maitrāyaṇī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitraaya_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मैत्रायणी</span> <i>maitrāyaṇī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-maitraaya_na" class="skt-link">maitrāyaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-maitraaya_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Maitrāyaṇī <a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>, leçon [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] tardive de l'école <a href="#entry-maitraaya_na" class="skt-link">maitrāyaṇa</a>; elle fait partie du <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a> noir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitraaya_niisa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maitrāyaṇīsaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-maitraaya_niisa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Maitrāyaṇīsaṃhitā, texte du <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_nayajurveda" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇayajurveda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maitraaya_nii" class="skt-link">maitrāyaṇī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitraaya_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maitrāyaṇīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] <a href="#entry-maitraaya_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. nom d'une école du <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a>, nommée après <a href="#entry-Umaitri" class="skt-link">Maitri</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-maitraaya_na" class="skt-link">maitrāyaṇa</a> — <a href="#entry-maitraaya_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>maitrāyaṇīyagṛhasūtra</i>] lit. np. du Maitrāyaṇīyagṛhasūtra, manuel véd. de rites domestiques; aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Umaanavag_rhasuutra" class="skt-link">Mānavagṛhasūtra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maitraaya_na" class="skt-link">maitrāyaṇa</a> <br> <a href="#entry-maitra" class="skt-link">maitra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maitraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-maitraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> amitié.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitreya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मैत्रेय</span> <i>maitreya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mitra" class="skt-link">mitra</a><a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maitreya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maitreya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maitreyii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maitreyī</i> amical — <a href="#entry-maitreya" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. du <a href="#entry-bodhisattva" class="skt-link">bodhisattva</a> Maitreya «l'Amical», <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> du futur; il règne sur le ciel <a href="#entry-tu_sita" class="skt-link">tuṣita</a>; il est représenté avec un <a href="#entry-stuupa" class="skt-link">stūpa</a> à l'avant de sa coiffure <a href="#entry-ja_taamuku_ta" class="skt-link">jaṭāmukuṭa</a>, et tenant à la main un pot à eau sacrée [<a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du bouffon [<a href="#entry-viduu_saka" class="skt-link">vidūṣaka</a>] Maitreya_1 dans la <a href="#entry-Um_rcchaka_tikaa" class="skt-link">Mṛcchakaṭikā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. caste déclassée; cf. <a href="#entry-maitreyaka" class="skt-link">maitreyaka</a> — <a href="#entry-maitreyii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maitreyī</i> phil. np. de Maitreyī «Amicale», épouse de <a href="#entry-Uyaaj_navalkya" class="skt-link">Yājñavalkya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitreyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maitreyaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-maitreyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. caste mêlée; se dit d'un descendant d'un homme <a href="#entry-vaideha" class="skt-link">vaideha</a> et d'une femme <a href="#entry-ayogavii" class="skt-link">ayogavī</a>; leur fonction est de chanter les louanges des puissants et de sonner la cloche à l'aube.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitreyanaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maitreyanātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-maitreyanaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du maître bd. Maitreyanātha, auteur de l'<a href="#entry-Uabhisamayaalafkaaranaamapraj_naapaaramitopadezazaastra" class="skt-link">Abhisamayālaṅkāranāmaprajñāpāramitopadeśaśāstra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maitreya" class="skt-link">maitreya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मैत्रि</span> <i>maitri</i> <a href="#entry-maitri" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de Maitri, auteur présumé de la <a href="#entry-Umaitraaya_nii" class="skt-link">Maitrāyaṇī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मैत्री</span> <i>maitrī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-maitra" class="skt-link">maitra</a>] <a href="#entry-maitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> amitié; bienveillance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. la fraternité, une des quatre qualités morales [<a href="#entry-brahmavihaara" class="skt-link">brahmavihāra</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Maitrī, l'Amitié personnifiée, fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitriipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maitrīpa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_2" class="skt-link">pa_2</a>] <a href="#entry-maitriipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du phil. bd. Maitrīpa (~1007–~1085), auteur de la <a href="#entry-Utattvaratnaavalii" class="skt-link">Tattvaratnāvalī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maitrii" class="skt-link">maitrī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maitryupani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maitryupaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-maitryupani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Maitryupaniṣad; cf. <a href="#entry-Umaitraaya_nii" class="skt-link">Maitrāyaṇī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maitri" class="skt-link">maitri</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-majj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मज्ज्</span> <i>majj</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(majjati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>magna</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>-majjya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ni</i><i>)</i> plonger dans ⟨acc. loc.⟩, pénétrer; être immergé, couler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se noyer, périr, tomber (en enfer) — ca. <i>(majjayati)</i> immerger, noyer; inonder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faire pénétrer, enfoncer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>mergo</i>; ang. to merge; fr. immerger.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-majjan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मज्जन्</span> <i>majjan</i> [<a href="#entry-majj" class="skt-link">majj</a>] <a href="#entry-majjan" class="skt-link">m.</a> mœlle; sève, jus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-majjana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मज्जन</span> <i>majjana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-majj" class="skt-link">majj</a>] <a href="#entry-majjana" class="skt-link">n.</a> immersion, ablution, bain; perdition, chute (en enfer).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mak_sikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मक्षिका</span> <i>makṣikā</i> <a href="#entry-mak_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> mouche, moucheron <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>musca</i>; fr. mouche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mak_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मक्षु</span> <i>makṣu</i> adv. immédiatement, directement, instantanément <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> très, à l'excès <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vraiment.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-makaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मकार</span> <i>makāra</i> [<i>(ma)</i>-<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-makaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> le son ou la lettre ‘<i>ma</i>’.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-makaarapa_ncaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>makārapañcaka</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_ncaka" class="skt-link">pañcaka</a>] <a href="#entry-makaarapa_ncaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [tantr.] le groupe des «cinq M», ingrédients de transgression du <a href="#entry-kulaacaara" class="skt-link">kulācāra</a> comprenant l'alcool [<a href="#entry-madya" class="skt-link">madya</a>], la viande [<a href="#entry-maa_msa" class="skt-link">māṃsa</a>], le poisson [<a href="#entry-matsya" class="skt-link">matsya</a>], l'union sexuelle [<a href="#entry-maithuna" class="skt-link">maithuna</a>] et l'imposition des mains [<a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-makaara" class="skt-link">makāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-makara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मकर</span> <i>makara</i> <a href="#entry-makara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-makarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>makarī</i> myth. Makara ou monstre marin, chimère de dauphin et de crocodile, monture de <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ugafgaa" class="skt-link">Gaṅgā</a>, emblème de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. signe zodiacal [<a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">rāśi</a>] et constellation du Capricorne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-makaradhvaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>makaradhvaja</i> [<a href="#entry-dhvaja" class="skt-link">dhvaja</a>] <a href="#entry-makaradhvaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Makaradhvaja «Qui a le <a href="#entry-makara" class="skt-link">makara</a> pour emblème», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. [<a href="#entry-Uaayurveda" class="skt-link">Āyurveda</a>] mélange de sulphide de mercure et d'or, produit par sublimation d'un amalgame de mercure, de soufre et d'or, utilisé not. comme tonique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-makaraketana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>makaraketana</i> [<a href="#entry-ketana" class="skt-link">ketana</a>] <a href="#entry-makaraketana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Makaraketana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> «à l'étendard orné d'un <a href="#entry-makara" class="skt-link">makara</a>».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-makaraku_n_dala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>makarakuṇḍala</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_n_dala" class="skt-link">kuṇḍala</a>] <a href="#entry-makaraku_n_dala" class="skt-link">n.</a> boucle d'oreille en forme de <a href="#entry-makara" class="skt-link">makara</a>, symbole mâle; <a href="#entry-Una_taraaja" class="skt-link">Naṭarāja</a> en porte une à l'oreille droite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-makaranda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मकरन्द</span> <i>makaranda</i> <a href="#entry-makaranda" class="skt-link">m.</a> nectar, pollen, miel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> variété de jasmin ou de mangue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Uvaasavadattaa" class="skt-link">Vāsavadattā</a>] np. de Makaranda «Suave», ami de <a href="#entry-Ukandarpaketu_1" class="skt-link">Kandarpaketu_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-makarasafkraanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>makarasaṃkrānti</i> [<a href="#entry-safkraanti" class="skt-link">saṃkrānti</a>] <a href="#entry-makarasa_mkraanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. fête du nouvel an, au début du mois de <a href="#entry-maagha" class="skt-link">māgha</a>; elle correspondait originellement au solstice d'hiver [<a href="#entry-dak_si_naayana" class="skt-link">dakṣiṇāyana</a>]; on la célèbre maintenant vers le 14 janvier, à cause de la précession des équinoxes [<a href="#entry-ayanaa_mza" class="skt-link">ayanāṃśa</a>]; c'est un jour propice pour le bain, not. à <a href="#entry-Uprayaaga" class="skt-link">Prayāga</a>; on l'appelle aussi <a href="#entry-maaghamela" class="skt-link">māghamela</a>; ce festival s'appelle Pongal <a href="#entry-safkraanti" class="skt-link">saṃkrānti</a> en Inde du Sud, on y cuit du riz dans du lait et on consacre une cérémonie [<a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a>] au Soleil.<br> <a href="#entry-makara" class="skt-link">makara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-makha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मख</span> <i>makha</i> a. <a href="#entry-makha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-makha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-makhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>makhā</i> joyeux; vigoureux, actif; turbulent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. toujours en mouvement; se dit des souffles [<a href="#entry-marutas" class="skt-link">marutas</a>] ou aspects de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> — <a href="#entry-makha" class="skt-link">m.</a> festival <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. sacrifice, oblation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. np. de Makha, dieu personnifiant le Sacrifice; refusant de partager avec les autres dieux son bénéfice, il en sera puni en étant décapité par son arc, dont les fourmis rongèrent la corde; sa tête devint le Soleil; pour restaurer la complétude du sacrifice, <a href="#entry-Udadhyaf" class="skt-link">Dadhyaṅ</a> enseigna aux <a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a> comment la recoller; pour ne pas être décapité lui-même par <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, il donna le secret avec une tête de cheval comme substitut.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मल</span> <i>mala</i> <a href="#entry-mala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mala" class="skt-link">n.</a> saleté, ordure; tache, souillure, impureté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a>] substance primordiale du péché; on en distingue trois [<a href="#entry-trimala" class="skt-link">trimala</a>].<br> <i>malam</i> <i>ajñānam</i> <i>icchanti</i> <i>saṃsārāṅkurakāraṇam</i> phil. L'impureté est l'ignorance, elle est la cause du germe du flux des devenirs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-malamaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>malamāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-maasa" class="skt-link">māsa</a>] <a href="#entry-malamaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. mois lunaire intercalaire, rajouté périodiquement pour que le cycle lunaire rattrape le cycle solaire; il est impur, et on ne doit pas y pratiquer de cérémonie religieuse.<br> <a href="#entry-mala" class="skt-link">mala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-malamallaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>malamallaka</i> [<a href="#entry-mallaka" class="skt-link">mallaka</a>] <a href="#entry-malamallaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> pièce d'étoffe couvrant les parties intimes; syn. <a href="#entry-kaupiina" class="skt-link">kaupīna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-malaprabhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>malaprabhā</i> [<a href="#entry-prabhaa_2" class="skt-link">prabhā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-malaprabhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de la rivière Malaprabhā au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a>, qui descend des Monts <a href="#entry-Usahyaadri" class="skt-link">Sahyādri</a>, coule près de <a href="#entry-Uvaataapi" class="skt-link">Vātāpi</a> (Badāmi) et d'<a href="#entry-Uaaryapura" class="skt-link">Āryapura</a> (Aihole), et se jette dans la <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_naa_1" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇā_1</a> à <a href="#entry-Ukudaalasafgama" class="skt-link">Kudālasaṃgama</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-malaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मलय</span> <i>malaya</i> <a href="#entry-malaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de la chaîne du Malabar, dans les Ghāts occidentaux; elle est riche en santal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. ses habitants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-malayadeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>malayadeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-deza" class="skt-link">deśa</a>] <a href="#entry-malayadeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. le pays Malaya (mod. Kerala).<br> <a href="#entry-malaya" class="skt-link">malaya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-malayaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>malayaja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-malayaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-malayaja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-malayajaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>malayajā</i> qui pousse au Malabar — <a href="#entry-malayaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Sirium myrtifolium</i>, arbre de santal [<a href="#entry-candana" class="skt-link">candana</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mali_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मलिष्ठ</span> <i>maliṣṭha</i> [super. <a href="#entry-malina" class="skt-link">malina</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mali_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mali_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mali_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maliṣṭhā</i> très sale; excessivement impur — <a href="#entry-mali_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maliṣṭhā</i> femme menstruant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maliimasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मलीमस</span> <i>malīmasa</i> [<a href="#entry-mala" class="skt-link">mala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maliimasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maliimasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maliimasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>malīmasā</i> sale, souillé, impur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> de couleur gris sale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maliiyas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मलीयस्</span> <i>malīyas</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-mala" class="skt-link">mala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maliiyas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maliiyas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maliiyasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>malīyasī</i> très sale, infect <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> très impur; très mal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-malina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मलिन</span> <i>malina</i> [<a href="#entry-mala" class="skt-link">mala</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] a. <a href="#entry-malina" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-malina" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-malinaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>malinā</i> sale, souillé, tarni; impur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gris — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(malinayati)</i> salir, souiller.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-malla">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मल्ल</span> <i>malla</i> <a href="#entry-malla" class="skt-link">m.</a> lutteur; athlète, homme d'une grande force physique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a> hors caste <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de la dynastie Malla du Népal (1483–1768) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>mallāsura</i>] myth. np. du démon [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] Malla_1 «Lutteur» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>mallās</i> géo. pays et peuple <a href="#entry-Umalla" class="skt-link">Malla</a>; leur capitale était <a href="#entry-Ukuzinagara" class="skt-link">Kuśinagara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mallaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मल्लार</span> <i>mallāra</i> <a href="#entry-mallaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> mus. mode [<a href="#entry-raaga" class="skt-link">rāga</a>] destiné à la saison des pluies; cf. <a href="#entry-am_rtavar_sin" class="skt-link">amṛtavarṣin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mallaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मल्लक</span> <i>mallaka</i> <a href="#entry-mallaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> récipient; emballage — f. cf. <a href="#entry-mallikaa" class="skt-link">mallikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-malli">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मल्लि</span> <i>malli</i> <a href="#entry-malli" class="skt-link">m.</a> possession, appartenance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-malli_se_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>malliṣeṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-senaa" class="skt-link">senā</a>] <a href="#entry-malli_se_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe jn. Malliṣeṇa (18⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), auteur de la <a href="#entry-Usyaadvaadama_njarii" class="skt-link">Syādvādamañjarī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-malli" class="skt-link">malli</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mallika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मल्लिक</span> <i>mallika</i> <a href="#entry-mallika" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. sorte d'oie — <a href="#entry-mallikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mallikā</i> natu. bot. <i>Jasminum sambac</i>, jasmin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mallikaarjuna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mallikārjuna</i> [<a href="#entry-arjuna" class="skt-link">arjuna</a>] <a href="#entry-mallikaarjuna" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Mallikārjuna, l'un des 12 signes radieux de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> [<a href="#entry-jyotirlifga" class="skt-link">jyotirliṅga</a>], établi au temple de <a href="#entry-Uzriizaila" class="skt-link">Śrīśaila</a>; c'est un <a href="#entry-trilifga" class="skt-link">triliṅga</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mallika" class="skt-link">mallika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mallinaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mallinātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-mallinaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. np. de Mallinātha «Maître de la Possession», 19⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a> mythique; son corps est bleu et or, son symbole est une cruche d'eau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Mallinātha_1, lettré jaïne du 14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ ou 15⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il écrivit not. des commentaires du <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मम</span> <i>mama</i> var. <i>me</i> [g. <a href="#entry-aham" class="skt-link">aham</a>] ind. à moi, de moi; mien, mienne, miens.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mamaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mamaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mamaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mamaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mamakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mamakā</i> véd. mien.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mamataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mamatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-mamataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> intérêt (en ⟨loc.⟩); sentiment de possession; égoïsme; fierté, arrogance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mamatā «la Fière», épouse d'<a href="#entry-Uutathya" class="skt-link">Utathya</a>; enceinte de son mari, elle fut violée par son beau-frère <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a>; l'embryon protesta, et repoussa son sperme qui tomba à terre; <a href="#entry-Ub_rhaspati" class="skt-link">Bṛhaspati</a> maudit l'enfant, qui naquit aveugle, d'où son nom de <a href="#entry-Udiirghatamaa" class="skt-link">Dīrghatamā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mamatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mamatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-mamatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> intérêt, attachement; sentiment de possession, fait d'accaparer.<br> <i>mamatvaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> être attaché à ⟨loc.⟩.<br> <a href="#entry-mama" class="skt-link">mama</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mamma_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मम्मट</span> <i>mammaṭa</i> <a href="#entry-mamma_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de Mammaṭa <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a> ou Mammaṭācarya, auteur du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> de la fin du 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on lui attribue l'essentiel du <a href="#entry-Ukaavyaprakaaza" class="skt-link">Kāvyaprakāśa</a>; et le <a href="#entry-Uzabdavyaapaaravicaara" class="skt-link">Śabdavyāpāravicāra</a>; il établit la doctrine de l'école de poésie évocatrice [<a href="#entry-dhvani" class="skt-link">dhvani</a>], à la suite d'<a href="#entry-Uaanandavardhana" class="skt-link">Ānandavardhana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-man">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्</span> <i>man</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(manyati)</i> pr. md. <i>(manyate)</i> impft. md. <i>(amanyata)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">8</span>] pr. md. <i>(manute)</i> pft. md. <i>(mene)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mata</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>mantavya</i>, <i>manya</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>matvā</i>, <i>-matya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>apa</i>, <i>abhi</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> penser, réfléchir, juger <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> savoir; croire, s'imaginer, supposer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> considérer comme, tenir pour ⟨2 acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> considérer, estimer, faire cas de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> penser peu de ⟨dat.⟩ — ps. <i>(manyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>māna</i><i>)</i> estimer hautement, apprécier, honorer — ca. <i>(mānayati)</i> honorer, respecter — dés. md. <i>(mīmāṃsate)</i> réfléchir, vouloir comprendre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μεμονα; lat. <i>moneo</i>; all. meinen; ang. to mean.<br> <i>manye</i> je pense; me semble-t-il; on dirait.<br> <i>laghu</i> <i>man</i> faire peu de cas de.<br> <i>sādhu</i> <i>man</i> approuver.<br> <i>rājyam</i> <i>triṇāya</i> <i>manye</i> Je me soucie du royaume comme d'une paille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mana_h">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनः</span> <i>manaḥ</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mana_hsa_mhara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manaḥsaṃharaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhara_na" class="skt-link">saṃharaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-mana_hsa_mhara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. détachement de l'esprit des objets extérieurs.<br> <a href="#entry-mana_h" class="skt-link">manaḥ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mana_hzilaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manaḥśilā</i> [<a href="#entry-zilaa" class="skt-link">śilā</a>] <a href="#entry-mana_hzilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> jaune du réalgar — <a href="#entry-mana_hzila" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>manaḥśila</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mana_aapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mana_āpa</i> [<a href="#entry-aapa" class="skt-link">āpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mana_aapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mana_aapa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mana_aapaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mana_āpā</i> [«qui atteint le cœur»] attirant, émouvant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mana_icchaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mana_icchā</i> [<a href="#entry-icchaa" class="skt-link">icchā</a>] <a href="#entry-mana_icchaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> désir mental.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनन</span> <i>manana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] a. <a href="#entry-manana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mananaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mananā</i> réfléchi, prudent — <a href="#entry-manana" class="skt-link">n.</a> réflexion, méditation; pensée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> compréhension.<br> <i>mananāt</i> adv. après réflexion; délibérément.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manara_njana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनरञ्जन</span> <i>manarañjana</i> [<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>-<a href="#entry-ra_njana" class="skt-link">rañjana</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-manara_njana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manara_njana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-manara_njanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manarañjanī</i> qui enchante l'esprit de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनस्</span> <i>manas</i> [<a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a><a href="#entry--as" class="skt-link">-as</a>] <a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">n.</a> pensée, esprit; faculté d'attention; intellect, mental <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> instinct; avis, opinion; intention, goût, penchant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. le sujet pensant et désirant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>] la substance [<a href="#entry-dravya_1" class="skt-link">dravya_1</a>] de l'Esprit; [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] l'essence [<a href="#entry-tattva" class="skt-link">tattva</a>] du Mental, un des constituants de l'instrument interne [<a href="#entry-anta_hkara_na" class="skt-link">antaḥkaraṇa</a>]; [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] son siège est le <a href="#entry-brahmarandhra" class="skt-link">brahmarandhra</a>, dans le cerveau; cf. <a href="#entry-manomayakoza" class="skt-link">manomayakośa</a> — <a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Manas le Mental personnifié, fils de <a href="#entry-Umaayaa" class="skt-link">Māyā</a> et d'<a href="#entry-Uiizvara" class="skt-link">Īśvara</a>; il a deux épouses: <a href="#entry-Uprav_rtti" class="skt-link">Pravṛtti</a> et <a href="#entry-Univ_rtti" class="skt-link">Nivṛtti</a>, personnifiant resp. l'activité et l'inactivité de l'intellect <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μνημη; lat. <i>mens</i>; ang. mind; fr. mental, dément.<br> <i>manaḥ</i> <i>kṛ</i> diriger sa pensée sur, penser à ⟨loc.⟩; avoir du goût pour ⟨g.⟩.<br> <i>manīkṛ</i> prendre à cœur.<br> <i>mano</i> <i>dā</i> fixer l'esprit sur ⟨loc.⟩.<br> <i>mano</i> <i>dhā</i> penser à, méditer sur ⟨loc. dat. <a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>-inf.⟩.<br> <i>mano</i> <i>vidhā</i> penser à, méditer sur ⟨loc. dat. <a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>-inf.⟩.<br> <i>manaḥ</i> <i>samādhā</i> se ressaisir.<br> <i>manovākkāyakarmabhiḥ</i> en pensées, en paroles et en actes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manasaa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनसा</span> <i>manasā_1</i> [i. <a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>] adv. par l'esprit, en imagination; volontiers, de bon cœur; avec la permission de ⟨g.⟩.<br> <i>manasā</i> <i>kṛ</i> songer, méditer.<br> <i>manasā</i> <i>man</i> être enclin à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manasaa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनसा</span> <i>manasā_2</i> [<a href="#entry-manasaa_1" class="skt-link">manasā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-manasaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Manasā «Née de l'esprit», déesse-serpent [<a href="#entry-naagamaat_r" class="skt-link">nāgamātṛ</a>], sœur des dragons [<a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a>] <a href="#entry-Uananta" class="skt-link">Ananta</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvaasuki" class="skt-link">Vāsuki</a>; elle protège des morsures de cobras [<a href="#entry-vi_saapahaarii" class="skt-link">viṣāpahārī</a>]; elle est adorée not. au Bengale et en Orissa; elle est l'épouse de <a href="#entry-Ujaratkaaru" class="skt-link">Jaratkāru</a>, et <a href="#entry-Uaastika" class="skt-link">Āstika</a> est leur fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manasaapa_ncamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manasāpañcamī</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_ncamii" class="skt-link">pañcamī</a>] <a href="#entry-manasaapa_ncamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. fête en l'honneur de <a href="#entry-Umanasaa" class="skt-link">Manasā</a>, où l'on honore les cobras; elle se tient le 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine sombre du mois <a href="#entry-aa_saa_dha" class="skt-link">āṣāḍha</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-naagapa_ncamii" class="skt-link">nāgapañcamī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manasaavijaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manasāvijaya</i> [<a href="#entry-vijaya" class="skt-link">vijaya</a>] <a href="#entry-manasaavijaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Manasāvijaya «Victoire de <a href="#entry-Umanasaa" class="skt-link">Manasā</a>», poème bengali de <a href="#entry-Uvipradaasa" class="skt-link">Vipradāsa</a> (1495); il décrit la manière cruelle dont la déesse des serpents établit son culte.<br> <a href="#entry-manasaa_2" class="skt-link">manasā_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manasaayaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manasāyajña</i> [<a href="#entry-yaj_na" class="skt-link">yajña</a>] <a href="#entry-manasaayaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. sacrifice intériorisé.<br> <a href="#entry-manasaa_1" class="skt-link">manasā_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manasi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनसि</span> <i>manasi</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>] adv. en esprit.<br> <i>manasi</i> <i>nidhā</i> se représenter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manasigafgaaku_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manasigaṅgākuṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-gafgaa" class="skt-link">gaṅgā</a>-<a href="#entry-ku_n_da" class="skt-link">kuṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-manasigafgaaku_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du lac Manasigaṅgākuṇḍa près de <a href="#entry-Ugovardhana" class="skt-link">Govardhana</a>; après avoir tué <a href="#entry-Uvatsaasura" class="skt-link">Vatsāsura</a>, <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> vint s'y purifier, y ayant convoqué le Gange par la pensée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manasija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manasija</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] <a href="#entry-manasija" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«né dans le cœur»] amour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-manasi" class="skt-link">manasi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manastaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manastāla</i> [<a href="#entry-taala" class="skt-link">tāla</a>] <a href="#entry-manastaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du lion Manastāla «Rapide comme l'esprit», monture [<a href="#entry-vaahana" class="skt-link">vāhana</a>] de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manasvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनस्वत्</span> <i>manasvat</i> [<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-manasvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manasvat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-manasvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manasvatī</i> intelligent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manasvin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनस्विन्</span> <i>manasvin</i> [<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a><a href="#entry--vin" class="skt-link">-vin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-manasvin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manasvin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-manasvinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manasvinī</i> intelligent, sage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> de bonne humeur — <a href="#entry-manasvinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manasvinī</i> myth. np. de Manasvinī «Sereine», épouse de <a href="#entry-Um_rka_n_du" class="skt-link">Mṛkaṇḍu</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Umaarka_n_deya" class="skt-link">Mārkaṇḍeya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mand_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्द्</span> <i>mand_1</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-mad_1" class="skt-link">mad_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(mandate)</i> se réjouir, être gai; être ivre ou intoxiqué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être splendide — ca. <i>(mandayati)</i> intoxiquer; réjouir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mand_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्द्</span> <i>mand_2</i> v. rester, demeurer, marquer la pause.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्द</span> <i>manda</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mand_2" class="skt-link">mand_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-manda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mandaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandā</i> lent, tardif; opp. <a href="#entry-ziighra" class="skt-link">śīghra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faible, mou; endormi, paresseux, indolent; apathique, indifférent à ⟨dat.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> doux; balourd; sot, stupide <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Manda, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a> «Lent».<br> <i>mandam</i> adv. lentement, faiblement; pudiquement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandaadara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mandādara</i> [<a href="#entry-aadara" class="skt-link">ādara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mandaadara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mandaadara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mandaadaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandādarā</i> indifférent (à ⟨loc.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandaakinii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्दाकिनी</span> <i>mandākinī</i> [<a href="#entry-manda" class="skt-link">manda</a>-<a href="#entry-ac" class="skt-link">ac</a>] <a href="#entry-mandaakinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> astr. np. de la Mandākinī ou Gange Céleste (Voie lactée); myth. elle est issue de l'orteil de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, et coule dans les cheveux de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-chaayaapatha" class="skt-link">chāyāpatha</a>, <a href="#entry-rasaa" class="skt-link">rasā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. nom d'un bras du Gange dans la vallée de <a href="#entry-Ukedaaranaatha" class="skt-link">Kedāranātha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [BhP.] nom d'un jardin de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> dans l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>; ses fils <a href="#entry-Unalakuuvara" class="skt-link">Nalakūvara</a> et <a href="#entry-Uma_nigriiva" class="skt-link">Maṇigrīva</a> s'y enivrèrent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandaakraanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mandākrānta</i> [<a href="#entry-aakraanta" class="skt-link">ākrānta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mandaakraanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mandaakraanta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mandaakraantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandākrāntā</i> qui progresse lentement — <a href="#entry-mandaakraantaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandākrāntā</i> phon. nom d'un mètre homosyllabique [<a href="#entry-samav_rtta" class="skt-link">samavṛtta</a>] de 17 syllabes avec 2 césures; cf. <a href="#entry-Umeghaduuta" class="skt-link">Meghadūta</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandaanila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mandānila</i> [<a href="#entry-anila" class="skt-link">anila</a>] <a href="#entry-mandaanila" class="skt-link">m.</a> brise agréable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्दार</span> <i>mandāra</i> <a href="#entry-mandaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Erythrina indica</i>, arbre corail; il perd ses feuilles en juin et se couvre de grandes fleurs au long pistil; avec ses feuilles on fait un shampooing; on distingue 3 variétés suivant la couleur des fleurs: jaune, utilisé pour la <a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a> tantrique, noir, pour la <a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a> à <a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a>, et rouge corail («de la couleur du sang de <a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a>») pour la <a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a> à <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-paaribhadra" class="skt-link">pāribhadra</a>, <a href="#entry-bahupu_spa" class="skt-link">bahupuṣpa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. un des 5 arbres du paradis <a href="#entry-Usvarga" class="skt-link">Svarga</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-pa_ncav_rk_sa" class="skt-link">pañcavṛkṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandabhaagya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mandabhāgya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaagya_1" class="skt-link">bhāgya_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mandabhaagya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mandabhaagya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mandabhaagyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandabhāgyā</i> [«qui a peu de chance»] infortuné, malchanceux — <a href="#entry-mandabhaagya" class="skt-link">n.</a> infortune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mandaga</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mandaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mandaga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mandagaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandagā</i> qui se meut lentement — <a href="#entry-mandaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. épith. de la planète Saturne [<a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandamati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mandamati</i> [<a href="#entry-mati" class="skt-link">mati</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mandamati" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mandamati" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mandamati" class="skt-link">f.</a> à l'esprit lent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्दन</span> <i>mandana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mand_1" class="skt-link">mand_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mandana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mandana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mandanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandanā</i> gai, joyeux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्दर</span> <i>mandara</i> [<a href="#entry-manda" class="skt-link">manda</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mandara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mandara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mandaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandarā</i> gros, massif; lent, paresseux — <a href="#entry-mandara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du Mont Mandara «l'Énorme», qui servit à baratter la mer de lait primordiale [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukuurma" class="skt-link">Kūrma</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaasuki" class="skt-link">Vāsuki</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandautsukya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mandautsukya</i> [<a href="#entry-autsukya" class="skt-link">autsukya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mandautsukya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mandautsukya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mandautsukyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandautsukyā</i> peu enclin à ⟨acc. <a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>⟩.<br> <i>mandautsukyaḥ</i> <i>asmi</i> <i>nagaragamanam</i> <i>prati</i> Je n'ai pas envie d'aller à la ville.<br> <a href="#entry-manda" class="skt-link">manda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandavisarpin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mandavisarpin</i> [<a href="#entry-visarpin" class="skt-link">visarpin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mandavisarpin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mandavisarpin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mandavisarpi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandavisarpiṇī</i> qui rampe lentement, qui s'insinue — <a href="#entry-mandavisarpi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandavisarpiṇī</i> lit. [<a href="#entry-Upa_ncatantra" class="skt-link">Pañcatantra</a>] np. de Mandavisarpiṇī, punaise de lit royal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandhaat_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्धातृ</span> <i>mandhātṛ</i> [contr. <i>manodhātṛ</i>] <a href="#entry-mandhaat_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme avisé ou pieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्दिर</span> <i>mandira</i> [<a href="#entry-mand_2" class="skt-link">mand_2</a>] <a href="#entry-mandira" class="skt-link">n.</a> habitation, demeure, maison; palais <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>devamandira</i>] temple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandiraabhyantara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mandirābhyantara</i> [<a href="#entry-abhyantara" class="skt-link">abhyantara</a>] <a href="#entry-mandiraabhyantara" class="skt-link">n.</a> intérieur du temple, du palais.<br> <a href="#entry-mandira" class="skt-link">mandira</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandodarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mandodarī</i> [<a href="#entry-udara" class="skt-link">udara</a>] <a href="#entry-mandodarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Mandodarī «au ventre douloureux»; dans une première vie elle fut <a href="#entry-Umadhuraa" class="skt-link">Madhurā</a> transformée en grenouille par le sort de <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>; elle vécut douze ans dans le puits près duquel l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> <a href="#entry-Umaya" class="skt-link">Maya</a> et l'<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a> <a href="#entry-Uhemaa" class="skt-link">Hemā</a> firent des pénitences pour obtenir une fille; elle fut recueillie par eux lorsque son sort prit fin; d'une très grande beauté, elle fut remarquée par <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> qui en fit sa reine principale; elle est mère d'<a href="#entry-Uindrajit" class="skt-link">Indrajit</a>; c'est une épouse parfaite [<a href="#entry-pativrataa" class="skt-link">pativratā</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mandra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्द्र</span> <i>mandra</i> [<a href="#entry-mand_1" class="skt-link">mand_1</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mandra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mandra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mandraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mandrā</i> plaisant, agréable, charmant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bas, sourd, grave (se dit de la voix).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manduraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्दुरा</span> <i>mandurā</i> [<a href="#entry-mand_2" class="skt-link">mand_2</a>] <a href="#entry-manduraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> écurie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> paillasse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manii_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनीषा</span> <i>manīṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>] <a href="#entry-manii_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> réflexion, considération; intelligence, sagesse; conception, idée, pensée.<br> <i>paro</i> <i>manīṣayā</i> au delà de toute imagination.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manii_saapa_ncaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manīṣāpañcaka</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_ncaka" class="skt-link">pañcaka</a>] <a href="#entry-manii_saapa_ncaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Manīṣāpañcaka «5 réflexions» de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>, inspirées par sa rencontre avec un hors-caste [<a href="#entry-caa_n_daala" class="skt-link">cāṇḍāla</a>] lui reprochant de ne pas mettre en pratique sa philosophie [<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-manii_saa" class="skt-link">manīṣā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manii_sikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनीषिका</span> <i>manīṣikā</i> [<a href="#entry-manii_saa" class="skt-link">manīṣā</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-manii_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> sagesse, intelligence.<br> <i>svamanīṣikayā</i> selon son propre jugement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manii_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनीषिन्</span> <i>manīṣin</i> [<a href="#entry-manii_saa" class="skt-link">manīṣā</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-manii_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manii_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-manii_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manīṣiṇī</i> réfléchi, sage, prudent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lettré, érudit; syn. <a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a>.<br> <i>pravadanti</i> <i>manīṣinaḥ</i> ainsi parlent les sages.<br> <i>kavitvaṃ</i> <i>kiyadaunnatyaṃ</i> <i>cintāmaṇimanīṣiṇaḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>nipītakālakūṭasyaharasyevāhikhelanam</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> Celui [<a href="#entry-Uraghunaatha" class="skt-link">Raghunātha</a>] qui est expert du Cintāmaṇi atteint aux sommets de la poésie, comme Ziva ayant absorbé le noir poison se joue des serpents.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manmatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्मथ</span> <i>manmatha</i> [int. <a href="#entry-manth" class="skt-link">manth</a>] <a href="#entry-manmatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> amour charnel, passion [«qui bouleverse»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Manmatha «Passion», un nom du dieu <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>; il personnifie l'an 29 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mano">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनो</span> <i>mano</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manobhuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manobhū</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuu_2" class="skt-link">bhū_2</a>] <a href="#entry-manobhuu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Manobhū, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> «né par la pensée».<br> <i>nāstyasādhyaṃ</i> <i>nāma</i> <i>manobhuvaḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] En vérité, rien n'est impossible à l'Amour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manohara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manohara</i> [<a href="#entry-hara" class="skt-link">hara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-manohara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manohara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-manoharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manoharā</i> ravissant, charmant, agréable, beau, captivant [«qui ravit l'esprit»].<br> <a href="#entry-mano" class="skt-link">mano</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manojava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ma'nojava</i> [<a href="#entry-java" class="skt-link">java</a>] a. <a href="#entry-manojava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manojava" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-manojavaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manojavā</i> rapide comme la pensée — <a href="#entry-manojava" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>manojava</i> vitesse de la pensée — <a href="#entry-manojavaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manojavā</i> myth. np. de l'une des 7 langues d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> [<a href="#entry-saptajihva" class="skt-link">saptajihva</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manomathana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manomathana</i> [<a href="#entry-mathana" class="skt-link">mathana</a>] <a href="#entry-manomathana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Manomathana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> «qui bouleverse l'esprit».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manomaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manomaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-manomaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manomaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-manomayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manomayī</i> spirituel, mental, intellectuel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manomayakoza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manomayakośa</i> [<a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] <a href="#entry-manomayakoza" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] l'enveloppe mentale, formant avec <a href="#entry-vij_naanamayakoza" class="skt-link">vijñānamayakośa</a> le corps subtil ou intellectuel [<a href="#entry-suuk_smazariira" class="skt-link">sūkṣmaśarīra</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-manomaya" class="skt-link">manomaya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manoraajya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manorājya</i> [<a href="#entry-raajya" class="skt-link">rājya</a>] <a href="#entry-manoraajya" class="skt-link">n.</a> règne de l'imaginaire; pays des rêves.<br> <i>manorājyāni</i> <i>kṛ</i> bâtir des châteaux en Espagne.<br> <i>manorājyavijṛṃbhaṇaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> bâtir des châteaux en Espagne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manorama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manorama</i> [<a href="#entry-rama" class="skt-link">rama</a>] a. <a href="#entry-manorama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manorama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-manoramaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manoramā</i> plaisant, charmant, attirant; joli — <a href="#entry-manoramaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manoramā</i> jolie femme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de la belle nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] Manoramā <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. de Manoramā_1, épouse de <a href="#entry-Uzukanaasa" class="skt-link">Śukanāsa</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Uvaizampaayana_1" class="skt-link">Vaiśampāyana_1</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. d'œuvres.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manoratha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manoratha</i> [<a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>] <a href="#entry-manoratha" class="skt-link">m.</a> vœu, désir [«qui transporte l'esprit»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> illusion, imagination, rêve <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du philosophe bd. Manoratha (4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).<br> <i>manorathānāmagatirna</i> <i>vidyate</i> Rien n'est inaccessible au désir.<br> <i>udyamena</i> <i>hi</i> <i>siddhayanti</i> <i>kāryāṇi</i> <i>na</i> <i>manorathaiḥ</i> C'est en se mettant au travail que les tâches sont accomplies, et non en y rêvant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manovega">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manovega</i> [<a href="#entry-vega" class="skt-link">vega</a>] <a href="#entry-manovega" class="skt-link">m.</a> vitese de la pensée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्तव्य</span> <i>mantavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mantavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mantavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mantavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mantavyā</i> à quoi l'on doit penser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qu'il faut admettre, croire ou approuver.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manth">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्थ्</span> <i>manth</i> var. <i>math</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(manthati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(mathati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mathita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pra</i><i>)</i> agiter avec force; frotter du bois (pour faire du feu); baratter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bouleverser, endommager, détruire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μοθος; all. Mangel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्थ</span> <i>mantha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-manth" class="skt-link">manth</a>] <a href="#entry-mantha" class="skt-link">m.</a> agitation, barattage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gruau d'orge mêlé de lait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cuiller ou bâton pour remuer, bâton de baratte — <a href="#entry-manthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manthā</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्थान</span> <i>manthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-mathana" class="skt-link">mathana</a>] <a href="#entry-manthaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> bâton à feu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Manthāna, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> «qui baratte l'Univers».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manthaanabhairava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manthānabhairava</i> [<a href="#entry-bhairava" class="skt-link">bhairava</a>] <a href="#entry-manthaanabhairava" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Manthānabhairava <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>, ouvrage ésotérique de la tradition <a href="#entry-pazcimaamnaaya" class="skt-link">paścimāmnāya</a> du culte de <a href="#entry-Ukubjikaa" class="skt-link">Kubjikā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-manthaana" class="skt-link">manthāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manthan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्थन्</span> <i>manthan</i> cf. <a href="#entry-mathin" class="skt-link">mathin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manthana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्थन</span> <i>manthana</i> cf. <a href="#entry-mathana" class="skt-link">mathana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manthara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्थर</span> <i>manthara</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-manda" class="skt-link">manda</a>] a. <a href="#entry-manthara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manthara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mantharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mantharā</i> lent, stupide; lourdeau; encombrant — <a href="#entry-mantharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mantharā</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de la servante bossue Mantharā, incarnation de la <a href="#entry-gandharvii" class="skt-link">gandharvī</a> <a href="#entry-Udundubhii" class="skt-link">Dundubhī</a>; alors que le roi <a href="#entry-Udazaratha" class="skt-link">Daśaratha</a> allait introniser son aîné <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> comme son successeur, elle suscita la jalousie de <a href="#entry-Ukaikeyii" class="skt-link">Kaikeyī</a>, qui rappela au roi sa promesse de lui accorder un vœu, pour exiger l'exil de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> et le couronnement de son propre fils <a href="#entry-Ubharata_1" class="skt-link">Bharata_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्त्र</span> <i>mantra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> délibération, avis; résolution, conseil, plan; maxime <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hymne sacré; formule mystique; incantation magique, charme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [«instrument de pensée»] <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> ou formule sacrée ésotérique; instrument de connaissance et de pouvoir, sa récitation précise est prescrite par le maître [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>] — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(mantrayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mantrita</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>mantrayitvā</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>ni</i><i>)</i> prendre conseil (auprès de ⟨i.-<a href="#entry-saha" class="skt-link">saha</a>⟩, au sujet de ⟨dat.⟩); délibérer, discuter — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(mantrayati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> parler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantraalaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantrālaya</i> [<a href="#entry-aalaya" class="skt-link">ālaya</a>] <a href="#entry-mantraalaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> mod. siège du gouvernement (not. du <a href="#entry-Umahaaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Mahārāṣṭra</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrad_rz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantradṛś</i> [<a href="#entry-d_rz_2" class="skt-link">dṛś_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mantrad_rz" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mantrad_rz" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mantrad_rz" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui voit (le sens) d'un <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantramaarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantramārga</i> [<a href="#entry-maarga" class="skt-link">mārga</a>] <a href="#entry-mantramaarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. école tantrique du Mantramārga «Voie de la formule»; cf. <a href="#entry-Usadyojyoti" class="skt-link">Sadyojyoti</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantramahodadhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantramahodadhi</i> [<a href="#entry-mahodadhi" class="skt-link">mahodadhi</a>] <a href="#entry-mantramahodadhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Mantramahodadhi de <a href="#entry-Umahiidhara" class="skt-link">Mahīdhara</a> (1588).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantramuktaavalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantramuktāvalī</i> [<a href="#entry-muktaavalii" class="skt-link">muktāvalī</a>] <a href="#entry-mantramuktaavalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] np. de la Mantramuktāvalī, manuel de rituel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrapaa_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantrapāṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-paa_tha" class="skt-link">pāṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-mantrapaa_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> récitation d'un texte sacré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrapu_spa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantrapuṣpa</i> [<a href="#entry-pu_spa" class="skt-link">puṣpa</a>] <a href="#entry-mantrapu_spa" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. offrande de fleur en récitant un <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrasa_mskaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantrasaṃskāra</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mskaara" class="skt-link">saṃskāra</a>] <a href="#entry-mantrasa_mskaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. rite de perfectionnement d'un mantra.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrasaadhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantrasādhana</i> [<a href="#entry-saadhana" class="skt-link">sādhana</a>] <a href="#entry-mantrasaadhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] procédure rituelle d'initiation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrasiddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantrasiddha</i> [<a href="#entry-siddha_1" class="skt-link">siddha_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mantrasiddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mantrasiddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mantrasiddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mantrasiddhā</i> accompli par un charme [<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> expert en proférations.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrasiddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantrasiddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-siddhi" class="skt-link">siddhi</a>] <a href="#entry-mantrasiddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> pouvoir magique d'un <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrasiddhyupaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantrasiddhyupāya</i> [<a href="#entry-upaaya" class="skt-link">upāya</a>] <a href="#entry-mantrasiddhyupaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. moyen pour rendre un mantra efficace.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrau_sadhivaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantrauṣadhivaśa</i> [<a href="#entry-o_sadhi" class="skt-link">oṣadhi</a>-<a href="#entry-vaza" class="skt-link">vaśa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mantrau_sadhivaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mantrau_sadhivaza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mantrau_sadhivazaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mantrauṣadhivaśā</i> dont on a raison par les formules et les herbes.<br> <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantraviirya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantravīrya</i> [<a href="#entry-viirya" class="skt-link">vīrya</a>] <a href="#entry-mantraviirya" class="skt-link">m.</a> efficacité d'un <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrayitvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्त्रयित्वा</span> <i>mantrayitvā</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] ind. ayant pris conseil — <a href="#entry-mantrya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>mantrya</i> ifc. ayant pris conseil de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantrayoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] <a href="#entry-mantrayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] magie incantatoire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantraśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-mantrazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> science des mantras.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrazikhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantraśikhā</i> [<a href="#entry-zikhaa" class="skt-link">śikhā</a>] <a href="#entry-mantrazikhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. «flamme» (pouvoir) d'un mantra.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्त्रि</span> <i>mantri</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-mantrin" class="skt-link">mantrin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्त्रिन्</span> <i>mantrin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mantrin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mantrin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mantri_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mantriṇī</i> conseiller du roi, ministre; np. Mantrī de premier ministre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> magicien <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (échecs [<a href="#entry-caturafgaa" class="skt-link">caturaṅgā</a>]) dame <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. mandarin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantripari_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantripariṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-pari_sad" class="skt-link">pariṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-mantripari_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> conseil des ministres.<br> <a href="#entry-mantri" class="skt-link">mantri</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्त्रित</span> <i>mantrita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mantrita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mantrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mantritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mantritā</i> délibéré; conseillé — <a href="#entry-mantrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> délibération.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantroccaara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mantroccāraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-uccaara_na" class="skt-link">uccāraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-mantroccaara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> récitation d'un <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>, forme de culte [<a href="#entry-vandana" class="skt-link">vandana</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantrya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्त्र्य</span> <i>mantrya</i> cf. <a href="#entry-mantrayitvaa" class="skt-link">mantrayitvā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mantu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्तु</span> <i>mantu</i> [<a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a><a href="#entry--tu" class="skt-link">-tu</a>] <a href="#entry-mantu" class="skt-link">m.</a> conseiller; décideur; arbitre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conseil, délibération <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (résultat d'une délibération) motion, recommendation; intention, plan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> offense; transgression — <a href="#entry-mantu" class="skt-link">f.</a> réflexion; compréhension; intellect — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(mantūyati)</i> offenser; se mettre en colère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनु</span> <i>manu</i> [<a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a><a href="#entry--u" class="skt-link">-u</a>] <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme [«créature pensante»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Manu-<a href="#entry-Usvaayambhuva" class="skt-link">Svāyaṃbhuva</a> «l'Homme Primordial», premier homme de l'ère, issu des deux moitiés <a href="#entry-Uviraa_t" class="skt-link">Virāṭ</a> et <a href="#entry-Uzataruupaa" class="skt-link">Śatarūpā</a> de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>; par <a href="#entry-Uzataruupaa" class="skt-link">Śatarūpā</a> il eut pour enfants <a href="#entry-Ui_daa" class="skt-link">Iḍā</a>, <a href="#entry-Upriyavrata" class="skt-link">Priyavrata</a> et les 10 géniteurs [<a href="#entry-prajaapati" class="skt-link">prajāpati</a>]; on lui attribue les Lois de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a> [<a href="#entry-maanavadharmazaastra" class="skt-link">mānavadharmaśāstra</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a> d'une ère cosmologique [<a href="#entry-manvantara" class="skt-link">manvantara</a>]; on en dénombre 14 dans un <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>; la tradition les nomme <a href="#entry-Usvaayambhuva" class="skt-link">Svāyaṃbhuva</a>, <a href="#entry-Usvaaroci_sa" class="skt-link">Svārociṣa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uauttami" class="skt-link">Auttami</a>, <a href="#entry-Utaamasa" class="skt-link">Tāmasa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uraivata" class="skt-link">Raivata</a>, <a href="#entry-Ucaak_su_sa" class="skt-link">Cākṣuṣa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaivasvata" class="skt-link">Vaivasvata</a> ou <a href="#entry-Usatyavrata" class="skt-link">Satyavrata</a> (le <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a>-<a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a> des temps actuels, fils de <a href="#entry-Uvivasvaan" class="skt-link">Vivasvān</a>), <a href="#entry-Usaavar_na" class="skt-link">Sāvarṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Udak_sasaavar_na" class="skt-link">Dakṣasāvarṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubrahmasaavar_na" class="skt-link">Brahmasāvarṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Udharmasaavar_na" class="skt-link">Dharmasāvarṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Urudrasaavar_na" class="skt-link">Rudrasāvarṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uraucya" class="skt-link">Raucya</a> et <a href="#entry-Ubhautya" class="skt-link">Bhautya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>manus</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ang. man; all. Mann.<br> <i>manur</i> <i>bhava</i> <i>janayā</i> <i>daivyaṃ</i> <i>janam</i> [RV.] Deviens Homme, pour créer la race divine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manu_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनुष</span> <i>manuṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-manus" class="skt-link">manus</a>] <a href="#entry-manu_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme — <a href="#entry-manu_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manuṣī</i> femme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. manouche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manu_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनुष्य</span> <i>manuṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-manus" class="skt-link">manus</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-manu_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manu_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-manu_syaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manuṣyā</i> humain; relatif à l'homme; masculin — <a href="#entry-manu_sya" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme (humain) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> all. Mensch; gitan manouche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manu_syataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manuṣyatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-manu_syataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> humanité; condition humaine; fait d'être un homme.<br> <i>manuṣyatām</i> <i>i</i> devenir un homme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manu_syatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manuṣyatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-manu_syatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> humanité; condition humaine; fait d'être un homme.<br> <i>manuṣyatvaṃ</i> <i>yā</i> devenir un homme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manu_syayaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manuṣyayajña</i> [<a href="#entry-yaj_na" class="skt-link">yajña</a>] <a href="#entry-manu_syayaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [SB.] véd. le sacrifice aux hommes, ou accueil des hôtes, un des cinq rites majeurs [<a href="#entry-mahaayaj_na" class="skt-link">mahāyajña</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-manu_sya" class="skt-link">manuṣya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manuja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनुज</span> <i>manuja</i> [<a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a>-<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] <a href="#entry-manuja" class="skt-link">m.</a> homme, humain — <a href="#entry-manujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manujā</i> var. <i>manujī</i> femme.<br> <i>manujīkṛ</i> changer en homme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manujanaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manujanātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-manujanaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi, prince.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manujapati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manujapati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-manujapati" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi, prince.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manujendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manujendra</i> [<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-manujendra" class="skt-link">m.</a> roi.<br> <a href="#entry-manuja" class="skt-link">manuja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manukula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manukula</i> [<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a>] <a href="#entry-manukula" class="skt-link">n.</a> humanité.<br> <i>jayatu</i> <i>saṃskṛtaṃ</i> <i>jayatu</i> <i>manukulam</i> La victoire du sanskrit est la victoire de l'humanité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manus">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मनुस्</span> <i>manus</i> cf. <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manusm_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manusmṛti</i> [<a href="#entry-sm_rti" class="skt-link">smṛti</a>] <a href="#entry-manusm_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. Lois de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a>, texte de lois traditionnelles, un <a href="#entry-dharmazaastra" class="skt-link">dharmaśāstra</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-maanavadharmazaastra" class="skt-link">mānavadharmaśāstra</a>.
  </div>
</div>
