<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manvantara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्वन्तर</span> <i>manvantara</i> [<a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a>-<a href="#entry-antara" class="skt-link">antara</a>] <a href="#entry-manvantara" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. période de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a> ou ère cosmologique entre deux déluges, comprenant 12000 années divines, ou 71 grandes ères [<a href="#entry-mahaayuga" class="skt-link">mahāyuga</a>] (307 millions d'années); le 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ du <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a> courant est en cours, il y en a 14 dans un <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. la création de la race humaine et des premiers hommes, un <a href="#entry-pa_ncalak_sa_na" class="skt-link">pañcalakṣaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manvartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manvartha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-manvartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. interprétation de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manvarthamuktaavalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manvarthamuktāvalī</i> [<a href="#entry-muktaavalii" class="skt-link">muktāvalī</a>] <a href="#entry-manvarthamuktaavalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Manvarthamuktāvalī, commentaire de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukulluuka" class="skt-link">Kullūka</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, suivant de près <a href="#entry-Umedhaatithi" class="skt-link">Medhātithi</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-manvartha" class="skt-link">manvartha</a> <br> <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्य</span> <i>manya</i> [<a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-manya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-manya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-manyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>manyā</i> qui se prend pour; qui se fait passer pour; qui ressemble à (⟨iic.⟩ acc.).<br> <i>paṇḍitammanyaḥ</i> qui se prend pour une sommité.<br> <i>paṇḍitammanyā'sau</i> il se pique d'étudier.<br> <i>śūrammanyaḥ</i> qui se croit un héros.<br> <i>darśanīyammanyaḥ</i> qui se croit beau; vaniteux.<br> <i>naraṃmanyaḥ</i> qui se prend pour un homme.<br> <i>rājaṃmanyaḥ</i> qui se prend pour le roi.<br> <i>śriyaṃmanyaḥ</i> qui se croit <a href="#entry-Uzrii" class="skt-link">Śrī</a>.<br> <i>gāṃmanyaḥ</i> qui se prend pour une vache.<br> <i>divāmanyaḥ</i> qui passe pour être diurne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्या</span> <i>manyā</i> <a href="#entry-manyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> du. pl. muscles de la nuque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manyu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्यु</span> <i>manyu</i> [<a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a><a href="#entry--yu" class="skt-link">-yu</a>] <a href="#entry-manyu" class="skt-link">m.</a> humeur, tempérament; ardeur, zèle, passion <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> indignation; colère, fureur, rage; chagrin, douleur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Manyu, la Fureur personnifiée.<br> <i>manyuṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> passer sa colère sur ⟨loc. <a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>-acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-manyutas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>manyutas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. avec rage, de colère.<br> <a href="#entry-manyu" class="skt-link">manyu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mar_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्ष्</span> <i>marṣ</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-m_r_s" class="skt-link">mṛṣ</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(marṣayati)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>marṣaṇīya</i><i>)</i> supporter; pardonner.<br> <i>na</i> <i>marṣ</i> molester.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्ष</span> <i>marṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-m_r_s" class="skt-link">mṛṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-mar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> patience; endurance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mar_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्षण</span> <i>marṣaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-m_r_s" class="skt-link">mṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mar_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mar_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mar_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>marṣaṇā</i> qui pardonne, indulgent — <a href="#entry-mar_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> patience, endurance; indulgence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mar_sa_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्षणीय</span> <i>marṣaṇīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-m_r_s" class="skt-link">mṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mar_sa_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mar_sa_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mar_sa_niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>marṣaṇīyā</i> qui mérite l'indulgence; qui doit être pardonné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mar_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्षिन्</span> <i>marṣin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-m_r_s" class="skt-link">mṛṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mar_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mar_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mar_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>marṣiṇī</i> patient, indulgent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर</span> <i>mara</i> [act. <a href="#entry-m_r" class="skt-link">mṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-mara" class="skt-link">m.</a> mort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> la Terre [«monde des mortels»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. les habitants des enfers.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मरण</span> <i>maraṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-m_r" class="skt-link">mṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-mara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> extinction, agonie, mort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mara_natva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maraṇatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-mara_natva" class="skt-link">n.</a> état de mort; mortalité.<br> <a href="#entry-mara_na" class="skt-link">maraṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maraa_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मराठ</span> <i>marāṭha</i> cf. <a href="#entry-mahaaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">mahārāṣṭra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marakata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मरकत</span> <i>marakata</i> <a href="#entry-marakata" class="skt-link">n.</a> émeraude <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>smaragdus</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maranda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मरन्द</span> <i>maranda</i> <a href="#entry-maranda" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. pollen ou nectar des fleurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maravijaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maravijaya</i> [<a href="#entry-vijaya" class="skt-link">vijaya</a>] <a href="#entry-maravijaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. [«victoire sur la mort»] épisode de l'Éveil de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> à <a href="#entry-Ugayaa" class="skt-link">Gayā</a>, où il surmonta les tentations démoniaques et «prit la Terre à témoin» de ses mérites.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maravijayamudraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maravijayamudrā</i> [<a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a>] <a href="#entry-maravijayamudraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> bd. geste du «toucher de la terre» dans les représentations du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> not. en Thaïlande; syn. <a href="#entry-bhuumisparzamudraa" class="skt-link">bhūmisparśamudrā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-maravijaya" class="skt-link">maravijaya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-mara" class="skt-link">mara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mard">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्द्</span> <i>mard</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-m_rd_1" class="skt-link">mṛd_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(mardayati)</i> presser, broyer; détruire, tuer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्द</span> <i>marda</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-m_rd_1" class="skt-link">mṛd_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-marda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-marda" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mardaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mardā</i> ifc. qui frotte; qui broye, qui détruit — <a href="#entry-marda" class="skt-link">n.</a> friction, choc; destruction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mardaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्दक</span> <i>mardaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-m_rd_1" class="skt-link">mṛd_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-mardaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mardaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mardikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mardikā</i> qui écrase, qui broie; qui fait souffrir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mardala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्दल</span> <i>mardala</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rd_1" class="skt-link">mṛd_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mardala" class="skt-link">m.</a> sorte de tambour; syn. <a href="#entry-m_rdafga" class="skt-link">mṛdaṅga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mardana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्दन</span> <i>mardana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-m_rd_1" class="skt-link">mṛd_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mardana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mardana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mardanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mardanī</i> qui frotte; qui broye, qui détruit — <a href="#entry-mardana" class="skt-link">n.</a> friction, massage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> destruction, ruine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mardin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्दिन्</span> <i>mardin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-m_rd_1" class="skt-link">mṛd_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mardin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mardin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mardinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mardinī</i> qui écrase, qui broye; destructeur, anéantisseur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marica">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मरिच</span> <i>marica</i> var. <i>marīca</i> <a href="#entry-marica" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Piper nigrum</i>, poivrier — <a href="#entry-marica" class="skt-link">n.</a> poivre noir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>mirc</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mariici">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मरीचि</span> <i>marīci</i> <a href="#entry-mariici" class="skt-link">f.</a> véd. rai de lumière (solaire ou lunaire) — <a href="#entry-mariici" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Marīci, 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ géniteur [<a href="#entry-prajaapati" class="skt-link">prajāpati</a>], issu de l'esprit de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> [<a href="#entry-vaidhaatra" class="skt-link">vaidhātra</a>]; <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> est [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] son fils ou [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] son jeune frère; c'est un parangon de vertu, et le <a href="#entry-Udazakumaaracarita" class="skt-link">Daśakumāracarita</a> raconte sa séduction par la courtisane <a href="#entry-Ukaamama_njarii" class="skt-link">Kāmamañjarī</a> aux fins de gagner un pari — <a href="#entry-mariici" class="skt-link">f.</a> éclat de lumière, rayon de soleil; aube <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mirage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. section d'une anthologie de poésie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Marīci_1 «Éclat de lumière», un nom d'<a href="#entry-Uu_saa" class="skt-link">Uṣā</a>, l'Aurore; la création naquit de ses amours incestueuses avec son père <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>-<a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a>; on la représente parfois comme une truie avec 7 porcelets représentant les 7 jours de la semaine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. divinité du panthéon lamaïque.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mariicikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>marīcikā</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-mariicikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> mirage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mariiciku_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>marīcikuṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_n_da" class="skt-link">kuṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-mariiciku_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du Marīcikuṇḍa, puits d'eau sacrée du temple <a href="#entry-Umuktezvara" class="skt-link">Mukteśvara</a> à <a href="#entry-Ubhubanezvar" class="skt-link">Bhubaneśvar</a>; son eau, qui accorde la fertilité, est tirée une fois par an et mise aux enchères pour le festival de chariot du <a href="#entry-Ulifgaraaja" class="skt-link">Liṅgarāja</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uazokaa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">Aśokāṣṭamī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mariici" class="skt-link">marīci</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marka_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्कट</span> <i>markaṭa</i> var. <i>marka</i> <a href="#entry-marka_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. singe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marka_takizora">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>markaṭakiśora</i> [<a href="#entry-kizora" class="skt-link">kiśora</a>] <a href="#entry-marka_takizora" class="skt-link">m.</a> petit singe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>markaṭakiśoranyāya</i>] phil. doctrine de <a href="#entry-bhakti" class="skt-link">bhakti</a> du Nord, où le croyant demande secours à Dieu comme le petit singe s'agrippe au ventre de sa mère; opp. <a href="#entry-maarjaarakizora" class="skt-link">mārjārakiśora</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-marka_ta" class="skt-link">markaṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्म</span> <i>marma</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-marman" class="skt-link">marman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marmaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>marmajña</i> [<a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">jña</a>] a. <a href="#entry-marmaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-marmaj_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-marmaj_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>marmajñā</i> qui connaît les points faibles; expert — ifc. expert en; exceptionnellement doué en.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marmaj_naana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>marmajñāna</i> [<a href="#entry-j_naana" class="skt-link">jñāna</a>] <a href="#entry-marmaj_naana" class="skt-link">n.</a> connaissance d'un secret.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्मन्</span> <i>marman</i> <a href="#entry-marman" class="skt-link">n.</a> point vulnérable (du corps), point faible; articulation, organe vital; chair à vif <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> secret, mystère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marmara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्मर</span> <i>marmara</i> [onomatopée] a. <a href="#entry-marmara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-marmara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-marmaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>marmarā</i> qui fait un bruit de froissement; qui murmure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. murmure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marmasthaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>marmasthāna</i> [<a href="#entry-sthaana" class="skt-link">sthāna</a>] <a href="#entry-marmasthaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> point vital, endroit vulnérable.<br> <a href="#entry-marma" class="skt-link">marma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marmasthala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>marmasthala</i> [<a href="#entry-sthala" class="skt-link">sthala</a>] <a href="#entry-marmasthala" class="skt-link">n.</a> point vital, endroit vulnérable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्प</span> <i>marpa</i> <a href="#entry-marpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du mystique tibétain bd. Marpa Lotsava (1012–1097), élève de <a href="#entry-Unaaropa" class="skt-link">Nāropa</a>, un grand accompli [<a href="#entry-mahaasiddha" class="skt-link">mahāsiddha</a>]; il traduisit en tibétain les doctrines tantriques, et fonda la lignée Kagyūpa; <a href="#entry-Umilarepa" class="skt-link">Milarepa</a> fut son élève.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-martya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्त्य</span> <i>martya</i> [<a href="#entry-mard" class="skt-link">mard</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-martya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-martya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-martyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>martyā</i> mortel; humain — <a href="#entry-martya" class="skt-link">m.</a> (un) mortel, homme — <a href="#entry-martya" class="skt-link">n.</a> corps.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मरु</span> <i>maru</i> [<a href="#entry-m_r" class="skt-link">mṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-maru" class="skt-link">m.</a> désert, espace désertique; rocher <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. privation de boisson (mode d'austérité) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>maravas</i> géo. [«hommes du désert»] nom du peuple Mārvār, et de son pays; mod. Rajasthan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [BhP.] np. du roi Maru <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a> «l'Hostile», fils de <a href="#entry-Uharyazva_1" class="skt-link">Haryaśva_1</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Upratiipaka" class="skt-link">Pratīpaka</a>; ou [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] père de <a href="#entry-Upratiindhaka" class="skt-link">Pratīndhaka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> Maru_1, fils de <a href="#entry-Uziighraga" class="skt-link">Śīghraga</a>, père de <a href="#entry-Uprazuzruka" class="skt-link">Praśuśruka</a>.<br> <i>marū</i> <i>bhū</i> se désertifier.<br> <i>maruṃ</i> <i>sādh</i> s'abstenir de boisson.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marudha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मरुध</span> <i>marudha</i> <a href="#entry-marudha" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Terminalia arjuna</i>, arbre arjun, grande combretacée, aux feuilles ovales opposées; il est utilisé en médecine; syn. <a href="#entry-arjuna" class="skt-link">arjuna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marudhajalapati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>marudhajalapati</i> [<a href="#entry-jala" class="skt-link">jala</a>-<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-marudhajalapati" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Marudhajalapati ou Marudhacalapati, «Maître de l'eau du <a href="#entry-marudha" class="skt-link">marudha</a>», épith. de Murugan-<a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> qui aurait fait jaillir une source au pied d'un arbre arjun pour répondre aux prières d'un <a href="#entry-siddha_1" class="skt-link">siddha_1</a> assoiffé.<br> <a href="#entry-marudha" class="skt-link">marudha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marudv_rdhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मरुद्वृधा</span> <i>marudvṛdhā</i> <a href="#entry-marudv_rdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de la rivière Marudvṛdhā, rivière de l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a> descendant du massif d'<a href="#entry-Uamaranaatha" class="skt-link">Amaranātha</a> pour se jeter dans la Chenab près de Kishtvār; mod. Maru Wardwan.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marukaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>marukānta</i> [<a href="#entry-kaanta" class="skt-link">kānta</a>] <a href="#entry-marukaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du «Désert splendide» Marukānta, asséché de l'océan par <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> armé du <a href="#entry-Ubrahmada_n_da" class="skt-link">Brahmadaṇḍa</a> (mod. désert de Bikaner et Mārvār).<br> <a href="#entry-maru" class="skt-link">maru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marut">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मरुत्</span> <i>marut</i> <a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">m.</a> vent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. souffle divin, principe d'immortalité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>marutas</i> myth. véd. np. des souffles <i>marutas</i>, génies des vents et orages, formant la troupe des jeunes guerriers tapageurs d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; le <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mahābhārata</a> en recense 11: <a href="#entry-Um_rgavyaadha" class="skt-link">Mṛgavyādha</a> «Chasseur», <a href="#entry-Usarpa" class="skt-link">Sarpa</a> «Serpent», <a href="#entry-Unir_rti_1" class="skt-link">Nirṛti_1</a> «Infortune», <a href="#entry-Uajaikapaad" class="skt-link">Ajaikapād</a> «Éternel Unijambe», <a href="#entry-Uahirbudhnya" class="skt-link">Ahirbudhnya</a> «Serpent de mer», <a href="#entry-Upinaakin" class="skt-link">Pinākin</a> «Archer», <a href="#entry-Udahana" class="skt-link">Dahana</a> «Brûlant», <a href="#entry-Uiizvara" class="skt-link">Īśvara</a> «Seigneur», <a href="#entry-Ukapaalii" class="skt-link">Kapālī</a> «Porteur de crâne», <a href="#entry-Usthaa_nu" class="skt-link">Sthāṇu</a> «Pilier», et <a href="#entry-Ubhaga_1" class="skt-link">Bhaga_1</a> «Chance»; ce sont les 11 aspects de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> [<a href="#entry-ekaadazarudraas" class="skt-link">ekādaśarudrās</a>]; on en recense parfois 48; leur chef est <a href="#entry-Umaatarizvaa" class="skt-link">Mātariśvā</a>; ils conduisent des chariots attelés à des antilopes [<a href="#entry-zarvaraas" class="skt-link">śarvarās</a>]; on les symbolise par 11 poteaux sacrificiels, avec au centre le poteau principal <a href="#entry-Uiizvara" class="skt-link">Īśvara</a>-<a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, et 5 poteaux de tailles décroissantes de chaque côté; on les représente aussi en <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> à 11 têtes; cf. <a href="#entry-rudra" class="skt-link">rudra</a>, <a href="#entry-praa_na" class="skt-link">prāṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-vaaha" class="skt-link">vāha</a>, <a href="#entry-gomaat_r" class="skt-link">gomātṛ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marutputra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>marutputra</i> [<a href="#entry-putra" class="skt-link">putra</a>] <a href="#entry-marutputra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Marutputra ou Marutiputra ou <a href="#entry-Umaaruti" class="skt-link">Māruti</a>, patr. de <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a> «Fils du Vent».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marutsakhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>marutsakhi</i> [<a href="#entry-sakhi" class="skt-link">sakhi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-marutsakhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-marutsakhi" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-marutsakhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. ami des <a href="#entry-marutas" class="skt-link">marutas</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marutta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>marutta</i> [<a href="#entry-datta" class="skt-link">datta</a>] <a href="#entry-marutta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du roi Marutta, qui demanda à <a href="#entry-Usa_mvarta" class="skt-link">Saṃvarta</a> de faire un sacrifice où il invita les dieux, et qui fut profané par <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-marya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्य</span> <i>marya</i> [<a href="#entry-m_r" class="skt-link">mṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-marya" class="skt-link">m.</a> mortel; gars; jeune homme, jeune guerrier; courtisan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. (voc.) gens.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maryaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मर्या</span> <i>maryā</i> <a href="#entry-maryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> limite; frontière.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maryaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maryāda</i> [<a href="#entry-da" class="skt-link">da</a>] <a href="#entry-maryaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui marque la frontière»] arbitre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maryaadaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maryādā</i> [<a href="#entry-aadaa_1" class="skt-link">ādā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-maryaadaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> frontière, limite; but <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> limites morales, règles coutumières; cadre légal; bonne conduite <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> convention, contrat.<br> <i>gṛhyatāṃ</i> <i>pāṇinā</i> <i>pāṇirmaryādā</i> <i>badhyatāṃ</i> <i>tvayā</i> [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] Main serrée dans la main, le contrat est conclu par toi.<br> <a href="#entry-maryaa" class="skt-link">maryā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mas_r_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मसृण</span> <i>masṛṇa</i> a. <a href="#entry-mas_r_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mas_r_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mas_r_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>masṛṇā</i> tendre, moelleux; doux; onctueux; syn. <a href="#entry-snigdha" class="skt-link">snigdha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mas_r_natva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>masṛṇatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-mas_r_natva" class="skt-link">n.</a> tendresse, douceur.<br> <a href="#entry-mas_r_na" class="skt-link">masṛṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-masta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मस्त</span> <i>masta</i> <a href="#entry-masta" class="skt-link">n.</a> tête, crâne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mastaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मस्तक</span> <i>mastaka</i> [<a href="#entry-masta" class="skt-link">masta</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-mastaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mastaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> tête, crâne; tempe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sommet, partie supérieure, cime.<br> <i>mastakam</i> prép. cl. au-dessus de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mastakaabhi_seka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mastakābhiṣeka</i> [<a href="#entry-abhi_seka" class="skt-link">abhiṣeka</a>] <a href="#entry-mastakaabhi_seka" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. pélerina­ge-festival [<a href="#entry-mela" class="skt-link">mela</a>] de Śravaṇabeḷagoḷā (<a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a>), où sont déversées des offrandes sur la tête d'une statue géante de <a href="#entry-Ubaahubalii" class="skt-link">Bāhubalī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugomma_ta" class="skt-link">Gommaṭa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mastaka" class="skt-link">mastaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mastakaa_njali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mastakāñjali</i> [<a href="#entry-a_njali" class="skt-link">añjali</a>] <a href="#entry-mastakaa_njali" class="skt-link">m.</a> posture de dévotion, mains jointes devant la poitrine, tête courbée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mastakazuula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mastakaśūla</i> [<a href="#entry-zuula" class="skt-link">śūla</a>] <a href="#entry-mastakazuula" class="skt-link">n.</a> mal de crâne, céphalée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-masti_ska">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मस्तिष्क</span> <i>mastiṣka</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-mastaka" class="skt-link">mastaka</a>] <a href="#entry-masti_ska" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-masti_ska" class="skt-link">n.</a> cerveau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> médecine affectant le cerveau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mastu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मस्तु</span> <i>mastu</i> crème aigre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mastulufga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mastuluṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-lufga" class="skt-link">luṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-mastulufga" class="skt-link">m.</a> cerveau.<br> <a href="#entry-mastu" class="skt-link">mastu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-masura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मसुर</span> <i>masura</i> <a href="#entry-masura" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>lens esculenta</i>, lentille — <a href="#entry-masuraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>masurā</i> prostituée, courtisane.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-masuura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मसूर</span> <i>masūra</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-masura" class="skt-link">masura</a>] <a href="#entry-masuura" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>lens esculenta</i>, lentille — <a href="#entry-masuuraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>masūrā</i> prostituée, courtisane — <a href="#entry-masuurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>masūrī</i> variole.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-masuuraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>masūraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-masuuraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> id. — <a href="#entry-masuurikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>masūrikā</i> méd. (éruption de) variole.<br> <a href="#entry-masuura" class="skt-link">masūra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मत्</span> <i>mat</i> var. <i>mac</i>, <i>mad_2</i> [abl. <a href="#entry-aham" class="skt-link">aham</a>] ind. de moi — iic. moi, de moi, à moi, par moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मत</span> <i>mata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>matā</i> pensé, cru, imaginé, compris <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> estimé; désiré — <a href="#entry-mata" class="skt-link">n.</a> doctrine, croyance, opinion; résolution <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> intention; désir, souhait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. vote.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matabheda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matabheda</i> [<a href="#entry-bheda" class="skt-link">bheda</a>] <a href="#entry-matabheda" class="skt-link">m.</a> différence d'opinion (avec ⟨g. i.-<a href="#entry-saha" class="skt-link">saha</a>⟩).<br> <a href="#entry-mata" class="skt-link">mata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मतंग</span> <i>mataṃga</i> var. <i>mataṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-mata" class="skt-link">mata</a>-<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mata_mga" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«qui va où il veut»] éléphant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Mataṅga; son ermitage était à <a href="#entry-U_r_syamuuka" class="skt-link">Ṛṣyamūka</a>; lorsque <a href="#entry-Uvaalii" class="skt-link">Vālī</a> tua <a href="#entry-Udundubhi" class="skt-link">Dundubhi</a>, un peu de son sang rejaillit sur l'ermitage; <a href="#entry-Umatafga" class="skt-link">Mataṅga</a> maudit son responsable de mourir s'il venait à <a href="#entry-U_r_syamuuka" class="skt-link">Ṛṣyamūka</a>; <a href="#entry-Usugriiva" class="skt-link">Sugrīva</a> s'y réfugia; [<a href="#entry-Uraghuva_mza" class="skt-link">Raghuvaṃśa</a>] il maudit le <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> <a href="#entry-Upriya_mvada" class="skt-link">Priyaṃvada</a> qui s'était baigné en amont de la rivière de lui; il devrait rôder comme éléphant sauvage jusqu'à être touché par une flèche d'<a href="#entry-Uaja" class="skt-link">Aja</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matafgaparamezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mataṅgaparameśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-paramezvara" class="skt-link">parameśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-matafgaparamezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Mataṅgaparameśvarāgama, ouvrage du shivaïsme du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>, du 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il fut commenté par <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a> <a href="#entry-Uraamaka_n_tha" class="skt-link">Rāmakaṇṭha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-matafga" class="skt-link">mataṃga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-math">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मथ्</span> <i>math</i> cf. <a href="#entry-manth" class="skt-link">manth</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मथ</span> <i>matha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-manth" class="skt-link">manth</a>] <a href="#entry-matha" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-maatha" class="skt-link">mātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mathana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मथन</span> <i>mathana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-manth" class="skt-link">manth</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mathana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mathana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mathanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mathanī</i> qui bouleverse, qui détruit — <a href="#entry-mathana" class="skt-link">n.</a> barattage; dommage, destruction; cf. <a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>manthana</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> production du feu par friction; bâton à feu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mathin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मथिन्</span> <i>mathin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-manth" class="skt-link">manth</a>] <a href="#entry-mathin" class="skt-link">m.</a> bâton (à baratter ou à faire du feu).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mathuraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मथुरा</span> <i>mathurā</i> <a href="#entry-mathuraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de Mathurā, ville du pays <a href="#entry-Uv_rji" class="skt-link">Vṛji</a> dans la province d'Agrā sur la rive droite de la <a href="#entry-Uyamunaa" class="skt-link">Yamunā</a>; myth. elle fut conquise par <a href="#entry-Uzatrughna" class="skt-link">Śatrughna</a>; <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> y naquit; on l'appelle aussi <a href="#entry-Umadhuraa" class="skt-link">Madhurā</a> «la douce ville»; c'est l'une des 7 villes sacrées [<a href="#entry-saptapurii" class="skt-link">saptapurī</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mathuraanaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mathurānātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-mathuraanaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mathurānātha, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «Seigneur de <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a>» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Mathurānātha_1 <a href="#entry-Utarkavaagiiza" class="skt-link">Tarkavāgīśa</a>, logicien du <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a> (~1600–~1675), fils de Rāma <a href="#entry-Utarkaalafkaara" class="skt-link">Tarkālaṅkāra</a>; élève de <a href="#entry-Uraamabhadra_1" class="skt-link">Rāmabhadra_1</a> <a href="#entry-Usaarvabhauma" class="skt-link">Sārvabhauma</a>; il est l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Utattvacintaama_nirahasya" class="skt-link">Tattvacintāmaṇirahasya</a> ou <a href="#entry-Umaathurii" class="skt-link">Māthurī</a>, du <a href="#entry-Upa_ncalak_sa_niisarvasva" class="skt-link">Pañcalakṣaṇīsarvasva</a> et du <a href="#entry-Uvyaaptipa_ncakarahasya" class="skt-link">Vyāptipañcakarahasya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mathuraavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mathurāvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mathuraavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mathuraavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mathuraavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mathurāvatī</i> comme à <a href="#entry-Umathuraa" class="skt-link">Mathurā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mathuraa" class="skt-link">mathurā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मति</span> <i>mati</i> [act. <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] <a href="#entry-mati" class="skt-link">f.</a> pensée, intention, résolution <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> opinion, croyance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> esprit, intelligence; mémoire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> estime, respect; sagesse.<br> <i>mama</i> <i>matyā</i> à mon avis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-matimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-matimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-matimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>matimatī</i> intelligent, malin, rusé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> avisé, sage, prudent.<br> <a href="#entry-mati" class="skt-link">mati</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matk_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matkṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">kṛta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-matk_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-matk_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-matk_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>matkṛtā</i> fait par moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matku_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मत्कुण</span> <i>matkuṇa</i> <a href="#entry-matku_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> insecte; not. punaise (de lit).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matpara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matpara</i> [<a href="#entry-para" class="skt-link">para</a>] a. <a href="#entry-matpara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-matpara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-matparaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>matparā</i> dévoué à moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matputra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matputra</i> [<a href="#entry-putra" class="skt-link">putra</a>] <a href="#entry-matputra" class="skt-link">m.</a> mon fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matsa_msthaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matsaṃsthā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_msthaa_2" class="skt-link">saṃsthā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-matsa_msthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> union avec moi; refuge en moi.<br> <a href="#entry-mat" class="skt-link">mat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matsara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मत्सर</span> <i>matsara</i> [<a href="#entry-mad_1" class="skt-link">mad_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-matsara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-matsara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-matsaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>matsarā</i> véd. qui exalte, qui intoxique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> joyeux, gai <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> égoïste, envieux; jaloux, hostile — <a href="#entry-matsara" class="skt-link">m.</a> égoïsme; envie, jalousie; passion pour ⟨loc. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. [«l'enivrant»] qualifie le <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matsaramanas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matsaramanas</i> [<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-matsaramanas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-matsaramanas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-matsaramanas" class="skt-link">f.</a> de disposition égoïste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matsarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matsarin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-matsarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-matsarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-matsari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>matsariṇī</i> envieux; envieux de ⟨loc.⟩.<br> <a href="#entry-matsara" class="skt-link">matsara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matsya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मत्स्य</span> <i>matsya</i> <a href="#entry-matsya" class="skt-link">m.</a> poisson <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Matsya le Poisson, 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, qui retrouva dans les eaux le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> qui y avait été caché par le démon <a href="#entry-Uhayagriiva" class="skt-link">Hayagrīva</a>; il sauva des eaux les sept sages [<a href="#entry-saptar_si" class="skt-link">saptarṣi</a>], qui s'accrochèrent à sa corne pendant le déluge primordial; on le célèbre à la pleine lune [<a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a>] du mois <a href="#entry-kaarttika" class="skt-link">kārttika</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Matsya_1, fils d'<a href="#entry-Uuparicara" class="skt-link">Uparicara</a> dont le sperme avait été avalé par la nymphe-poisson <a href="#entry-Uadrikaa" class="skt-link">Adrikā</a>; il fut trouvé dans son ventre avec sa sœur <a href="#entry-Umatsyagandhii" class="skt-link">Matsyagandhī</a> par le pêcheur <a href="#entry-Udaasaraaja" class="skt-link">Dāsarāja</a> — <a href="#entry-matsya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. cf. <a href="#entry-Umatsyapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Matsyapurāṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>matsyās</i> géo. nom du pays Matsya_2 (entre mod. Delhi et Jaïpur) et de son peuple les Matsyās; cf. <a href="#entry-Uviraa_ta" class="skt-link">Virāṭa</a>.<br> <i>matsyā</i> <i>iva</i> <i>janā</i> <i>nityaṃ</i> <i>bhakṣayanti</i> <i>parasparam</i> Tels des poissons, les hommes s'entre-dévorent sans cesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matsyaad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matsyād</i> [<a href="#entry-ad_2" class="skt-link">ad_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-matsyaad" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-matsyaad" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-matsyaad" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui mange du poisson.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matsyagandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matsyagandha</i> [<a href="#entry-gandha" class="skt-link">gandha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-matsyagandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-matsyagandha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-matsyagandhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>matsyagandhī</i> qui sent le poisson — <a href="#entry-matsyagandhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>matsyagandhī</i> myth. np. de Matsyagandhī, épith. de <a href="#entry-Usatyavatii" class="skt-link">Satyavatī</a> trouvée dans le ventre du poisson <a href="#entry-Uadrikaa" class="skt-link">Adrikā</a> par le pêcheur <a href="#entry-Udaasaraaja" class="skt-link">Dāsarāja</a> qui la recueillit; faisant traverser la <a href="#entry-Uyamunaa" class="skt-link">Yamunā</a> au sage <a href="#entry-Uparaazara" class="skt-link">Parāśara</a>, celui-ci la délivra de son odeur en échange de ses faveurs, qui donnèrent naissance à <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a>; elle épousa ensuite le roi <a href="#entry-Uzaantanu" class="skt-link">Śāṃtanu</a> fou de sa beauté pour engendrer les princes <a href="#entry-Ucitraafgada" class="skt-link">Citrāṅgada</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvicitraviirya" class="skt-link">Vicitravīrya</a>; après leur mort sans héritier, elle fit venir <a href="#entry-Uvyaasa" class="skt-link">Vyāsa</a> pour qu'il accomplisse son devoir de <a href="#entry-niyoga" class="skt-link">niyoga</a> et continue la lignée en engendrant <a href="#entry-Udh_rtaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Dhṛtarāṣṭra</a> et <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_du" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍu</a>, dont les descendants se battront dans la grande bataille fratricide du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matsyamudraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matsyamudrā</i> [<a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a>] <a href="#entry-matsyamudraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. geste du poisson, effectué par le prêtre sur un pot à eau avant de l'offrir à la divinité; on superpose les deux mains à plat horizontalement, main droite sur la gauche, paumes vers le sol, pouces écartés, autres doigts joints tendus; on tourne les pouces pour nager.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matsyanyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matsyanyāya</i> [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] <a href="#entry-matsyanyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> [«loi des poissons»] la loi du plus fort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matsyapuraa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matsyapurāṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-matsyapuraa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Matsyapurāṇa ou Mātsyapurāṇa «Récit du temps du Poisson», texte mythologique mixte, en 291 chapitres [<a href="#entry-adhyaaya" class="skt-link">adhyāya</a>]; il relate le déluge où <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> incarné comme <a href="#entry-Umatsya" class="skt-link">Matsya</a> sauva <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a> accroché à sa corne.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matsyendranaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>matsyendranātha</i> [<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>-<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-matsyendranaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Matsyendranātha «Né d'un poisson», accompli [<a href="#entry-mahaasiddha" class="skt-link">mahāsiddha</a>] qui fonda l'école de <a href="#entry-ha_thayoga" class="skt-link">haṭhayoga</a> au Népal au 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il était le maître [<a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a>] de Gorakṣanātha; il est révéré par les bouddhistes comme par les hindous comme saint patron du Népal; il réforma la doctrine <a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a> et instaura le <a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a> <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>; on lui doit le <a href="#entry-Ukaulaj_naananir_naya" class="skt-link">Kaulajñānanirṇaya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-matsya" class="skt-link">matsya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मत्त</span> <i>matta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mad_1" class="skt-link">mad_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-matta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-matta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mattaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mattā</i> ivre, enivré de; furieux; réjoui, joyeux, passionné; excité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en rut (not. éléphant).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mattamatafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mattamataṃga</i> [<a href="#entry-matafga" class="skt-link">mataṃga</a>] <a href="#entry-mattamata_mga" class="skt-link">m.</a> éléphant en rut.<br> <i>mattamataṃgagāmin</i> [«qui a la démarche d'un éléphant en rut»] majestueux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mattas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मत्तस्</span> <i>mattas</i> cl. pour <i>mat</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mattavilaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mattavilāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-vilaasa" class="skt-link">vilāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-mattavilaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. de la pièce satirique [<a href="#entry-prahasana" class="skt-link">prahasana</a>] Mattavilāsa «Jeux d'ivrognes», composée par le roi <a href="#entry-Upallava" class="skt-link">Pallava</a> <a href="#entry-Umahendravarmaa_1" class="skt-link">Mahendravarmā_1</a> au 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; elle se moque des excès des sectes <a href="#entry-paazupata" class="skt-link">pāśupata</a>, <a href="#entry-kaapaalika" class="skt-link">kāpālika</a>, et <a href="#entry-bauddha" class="skt-link">bauddha</a> en mettant en scène des ascètes ivres.<br> <a href="#entry-matta" class="skt-link">matta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मत्वा</span> <i>matvā</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] ind. ayant pensé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-matya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मत्य</span> <i>matya</i> <a href="#entry-matya" class="skt-link">n.</a> herse; rouleau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mus. cycle rythmique [<a href="#entry-taala" class="skt-link">tāla</a>] comprenant 1 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a>, 1 <a href="#entry-druta" class="skt-link">druta</a> et 1 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maudgalya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौद्गल्य</span> <i>maudgalya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mudgala" class="skt-link">mudgala</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maudgalya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de l'ascète Maudgalya «du pays <a href="#entry-Umudgala" class="skt-link">Mudgala</a>»; son épouse est <a href="#entry-Uindrasenaa_1" class="skt-link">Indrasenā_1</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Umudgala_1" class="skt-link">Mudgala_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maudgalyaayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maudgalyāyana</i> [<a href="#entry-aayana" class="skt-link">āyana</a>] <a href="#entry-maudgalyaayana" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Maudgalyāyana ou Mahāmaudgalyāyana, disciple du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> réputé accompli dans les pouvoirs magiques; on dit qu'il lisait les pensées, parlait avec les esprits, traversait les murs, etc.; il mourut assassiné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>Mahāmoggallāna</i>.<br> <a href="#entry-maudgalya" class="skt-link">maudgalya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maugdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौग्ध</span> <i>maugdha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mugdha" class="skt-link">mugdha</a>] <a href="#entry-maugdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> simplicité, innocence; inexpérience, naïveté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> charme, grâce féminine; rouerie (not. demander à un amant de dire ce qui est déjà connu).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maujavata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौजवत</span> <i>maujavata</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-muujavat" class="skt-link">mūjavat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maujavata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maujavata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maujavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maujavatī</i> originaire de la montagne <a href="#entry-Umuujavat" class="skt-link">Mūjavat</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. qualifie not. un <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> réputé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौख</span> <i>maukha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maukhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maukhā</i> relatif à la bouche; oral <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> basé sur l'instruction orale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maukhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौखर</span> <i>maukhara</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mukhara" class="skt-link">mukhara</a>] <a href="#entry-maukhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. np. de la race Maukhara.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maukhari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौखरि</span> <i>maukhari</i> [<a href="#entry-maukhara" class="skt-link">maukhara</a>] <a href="#entry-maukhari" class="skt-link">m.</a> patr. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de la dynastie Maukhari de rois de <a href="#entry-Ukanyaakubjaa" class="skt-link">Kanyākubjā</a> (6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); cf. <a href="#entry-Ugrahavarmaa" class="skt-link">Grahavarmā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maukharya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौखर्य</span> <i>maukharya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mukhara" class="skt-link">mukhara</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maukharya" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait d'être bavard ou bruyant; malséance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> injure, diffamation, calomnie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mauktika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौक्तिक</span> <i>mauktika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-muktaa" class="skt-link">muktā</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-mauktika" class="skt-link">n.</a> perle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौल</span> <i>maula</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maula" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maulii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maulī</i> issu de racines (not. poison) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> antique, immémorial, ancient (not. coutume) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> héréditaire (not. charge) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aborigène, indigène, autochtone — <a href="#entry-maula" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. ministre ayant hérité sa charge de famille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mauli">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौलि</span> <i>mauli</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a>] <a href="#entry-mauli" class="skt-link">m.</a> tête, sommet; principal, chef <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> diadème, couronne; crête; chignon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mèche de cheveux réservée après tonsure; syn. <a href="#entry-cuu_daa" class="skt-link">cūḍā</a>.<br> <i>maulau</i> <i>nidhā</i> placer sur la tête; recevoir avec respect.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maulika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौलिक</span> <i>maulika</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maulika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maulika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maulikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maulikā</i> qui produit des racines <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dérivé d'une racine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> inférieur; opp. <a href="#entry-kuliina" class="skt-link">kulīna</a> — <a href="#entry-maulika" class="skt-link">m.</a> vendeur de racines.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maulima_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maulimaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">maṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-maulima_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. turban ornemental de la noblesse <a href="#entry-ku_saa_na" class="skt-link">kuṣāṇa</a>, comportant une large cocarde circulaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> broche de coiffure orné d'une gemme.<br> <a href="#entry-mauli" class="skt-link">mauli</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mauna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौन</span> <i>mauna</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a>] <a href="#entry-mauna" class="skt-link">n.</a> ascétisme, état de <a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a>; silence, vœu de silence.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maunavrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maunavrata</i> [<a href="#entry-vrata" class="skt-link">vrata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maunavrata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maunavrata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maunavrataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maunavratā</i> qui a fait vœu de silence.<br> <a href="#entry-mauna" class="skt-link">mauna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maunin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौनिन्</span> <i>maunin</i> [<a href="#entry-mauna" class="skt-link">mauna</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maunin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maunin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mauninii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mauninī</i> silencieux; muet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maurva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौर्व</span> <i>maurva</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-muurvaa" class="skt-link">mūrvā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-maurva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maurva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-maurvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maurvī</i> fait de chanvre — <a href="#entry-maurvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>maurvī</i> corde d'arc (en chanvre) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. ceinture d'ascète; syn. <a href="#entry-mekhalaa" class="skt-link">mekhalā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Maurvī, fille du roi <a href="#entry-Umuru" class="skt-link">Muru</a>; elle épousa <a href="#entry-Ugha_totkaca" class="skt-link">Ghaṭotkaca</a> dont elle enfanta de <a href="#entry-Ubarbariika" class="skt-link">Barbarīka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maurya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौर्य</span> <i>maurya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mura" class="skt-link">mura</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-maurya" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de la dynastie Maurya, fondée par le roi <a href="#entry-Ucandragupta" class="skt-link">Candragupta</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>mauryās</i> les rois de la dynastie <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mausala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौसल</span> <i>mausala</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-musala" class="skt-link">musala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mausala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mausala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mausalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mausalā</i> relatif à la massue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mausalaparvan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mausalaparvan</i> [<a href="#entry-parvan" class="skt-link">parvan</a>] <a href="#entry-mausalaparvan" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>, «(du combat) à la massue».<br> <a href="#entry-mausala" class="skt-link">mausala</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mau_nja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौञ्ज</span> <i>mauñja</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-mu_nja" class="skt-link">muñja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mau_nja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mau_nja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mau_njii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mauñjī</i> en osier — <a href="#entry-mau_nja" class="skt-link">m.</a> feuille de roseau <a href="#entry-mu_nja" class="skt-link">muñja</a> — f. cf. <a href="#entry-mau_njii" class="skt-link">mauñjī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mau_njii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मौञ्जी</span> <i>mauñjī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-mau_nja" class="skt-link">mauñja</a>] <a href="#entry-mau_njii" class="skt-link">f.</a> tresse de trois feuilles de roseau; not. ceinture d'ascète, cordon sacré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mau_njiibandhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mauñjībandhana</i> [<a href="#entry-bandhana" class="skt-link">bandhana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mau_njiibandhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mau_njiibandhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mau_njiibandhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mauñjībandhanā</i> qui porte le cordon d'herbe <a href="#entry-mu_nja" class="skt-link">muñja</a> — <a href="#entry-mau_njiibandhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. port du cordon d'herbe <a href="#entry-mu_nja" class="skt-link">muñja</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mau_njii" class="skt-link">mauñjī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मय</span> <i>maya</i> ifc. a. <a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mayī</i> forme des a. de matière: consistant en, fait de, formé de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à propos de, relatif à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maya_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मय</span> <i>maya_1</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-maaya" class="skt-link">māya</a>] <a href="#entry-maya_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de l'<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a> Maya «Magicien», premier parmi les ondins [<a href="#entry-daanava" class="skt-link">dānava</a>]; architecte des <a href="#entry-devaas" class="skt-link">devās</a> et des <a href="#entry-daityaas" class="skt-link">daityās</a>, il construisit la triple forteresse <a href="#entry-Utripura" class="skt-link">Tripura</a>; son épouse est la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] <a href="#entry-Uhemaa" class="skt-link">Hemā</a>; leurs fils sont <a href="#entry-Umaayaavin" class="skt-link">Māyāvin</a> et <a href="#entry-Udundubhi" class="skt-link">Dundubhi</a>; ils adoptèrent <a href="#entry-Umandodarii" class="skt-link">Mandodarī</a> comme leur fille; [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] lors de l'incendie de la forêt <a href="#entry-Ukhaa_n_dava" class="skt-link">Khāṇḍava</a>, <a href="#entry-Umaya" class="skt-link">Maya</a> y était caché, déguisé en <a href="#entry-Utak_saka" class="skt-link">Takṣaka</a>; il s'en échappa, et par sa prière à <a href="#entry-Uarjuna" class="skt-link">Arjuna</a> fut sauvé des flammes d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> et du <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a> de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>; en signe de reconnaissance, il construisit pour les <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_davaas" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍavās</a> la ville de <a href="#entry-Ukhaa_n_davaprastha" class="skt-link">Khāṇḍavaprastha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मया</span> <i>mayā</i> [i. <a href="#entry-aham" class="skt-link">aham</a>] ind. par moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मयि</span> <i>mayi</i> [loc. <a href="#entry-aham" class="skt-link">aham</a>] ind. en moi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मयु</span> <i>mayu</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] <a href="#entry-mayu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. chimère, être céleste mi-homme mi-bête; cf. <a href="#entry-kinnara" class="skt-link">kinnara</a>, <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayuraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mayurāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-mayuraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mayurāja, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> «Roi des chimères».<br> <a href="#entry-mayu" class="skt-link">mayu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayuukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मयूख</span> <i>mayūkha</i> <a href="#entry-mayuukha" class="skt-link">m.</a> cheville; patère; piquet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. véd. piquet pour attacher l'animal du sacrifice <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> axe d'un cadran solaire; syn. <a href="#entry-kiila" class="skt-link">kīla</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayuura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मयूर</span> <i>mayūra</i> <a href="#entry-mayuura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mayuurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mayūrī</i> natu. zoo. paon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Mayūra, poète maniéré de la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Uhar_sa" class="skt-link">Harṣa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayuuraasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mayūrāsana</i> [<a href="#entry-aasana" class="skt-link">āsana</a>] <a href="#entry-mayuuraasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] posture du paon: allongé à plat ventre, les coudes de chaque côté du nombril, en prenant appui avec les deux mains tournées vers l'arrière, on soulève le corps en le laissant bien droit [<a href="#entry-da_n_davat" class="skt-link">daṇḍavat</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-mayuura" class="skt-link">mayūra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayuurapattra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mayūrapattra</i> [<a href="#entry-pattra" class="skt-link">pattra</a>] <a href="#entry-mayuurapattra" class="skt-link">n.</a> plume de paon, symbole iconographique d'immortalité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayuurapo_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mayūrapoṣaka</i> [<a href="#entry-po_saka" class="skt-link">poṣaka</a>] <a href="#entry-mayuurapo_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> éleveur de paon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Mayūrapoṣaka, père de <a href="#entry-Ucandragupta" class="skt-link">Candragupta</a> <a href="#entry-Umaurya" class="skt-link">Maurya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayuuravarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mayūravarman</i> [<a href="#entry-varman" class="skt-link">varman</a>] <a href="#entry-mayuuravarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Mayūravarmā; cf. <a href="#entry-Umayuurazarmaa" class="skt-link">Mayūraśarmā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayuuravya_msaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mayūravyaṃsaka</i> [<a href="#entry-vya_msaka" class="skt-link">vyaṃsaka</a>] <a href="#entry-mayuuravya_msaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> paon voleur; syn. <a href="#entry-dhuurtamayuura" class="skt-link">dhūrtamayūra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. exemple paradigmatique d'un composé déterminatif irrégulier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mayuurazarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mayūraśarman</i> [<a href="#entry-zarman" class="skt-link">śarman</a>] <a href="#entry-mayuurazarman" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Mayūraśarmā qui fonda la dynastie <a href="#entry-Ukadamba" class="skt-link">Kadamba</a> au <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_ta" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭa</a> en 345; sa capitale était <a href="#entry-Uvanavaasii" class="skt-link">Vanavāsī</a>; il prit le nom de <a href="#entry-Umayuuravarmaa" class="skt-link">Mayūravarmā</a> pour indiquer son passage de la classe <a href="#entry-braahma_na" class="skt-link">brāhmaṇa</a> à la classe <a href="#entry-k_satriya" class="skt-link">kṣatriya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-maza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मश</span> <i>maśa</i> <a href="#entry-maza" class="skt-link">m.</a> bourdonnement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> moustique; moucheron.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mazaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>maśaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-mazaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> moustique; en gén. insecte qui pique ou mord.<br> <a href="#entry-maza" class="skt-link">maśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मञ्च</span> <i>mañca</i> <a href="#entry-ma_nca" class="skt-link">m.</a> plate-forme, estrade; dais; piédestal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit, couche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_ncaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mañcaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_ncaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_ncaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> plate-forme, estrade; piédestal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit, couche.<br> <a href="#entry-ma_nca" class="skt-link">mañca</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_ncukaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मञ्चुका</span> <i>mañcukā</i> <a href="#entry-ma_ncukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-madanama_ncukaa" class="skt-link">madanamañcukā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_njara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मञ्जर</span> <i>mañjara</i> <a href="#entry-ma_njara" class="skt-link">n.</a> grappe de fleurs <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perle — f. cf. <a href="#entry-ma_njarii" class="skt-link">mañjarī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_njarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मञ्जरी</span> <i>mañjarī</i> [f. <a href="#entry-ma_njara" class="skt-link">mañjara</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_njarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> bouquet, grappe de fleurs <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fleur, bouton, bourgeon — ifc. [«bouquet de»] nomme des œuvres littéraires.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nji_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मञ्जिष्ठ</span> <i>mañjiṣṭha</i> [super. <a href="#entry-ma_nju" class="skt-link">mañju</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ma_nji_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nji_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nji_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mañjiṣṭhā</i> rouge foncé, rouge garance — <a href="#entry-ma_nji_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mañjiṣṭhā</i> natu. bot. <i>Rubia munjista</i>, garance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nji_s_thakula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mañjiṣṭhakula</i> [<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_nji_s_thakula" class="skt-link">n.</a> natu. famille des rubiacées.<br> <a href="#entry-ma_nji_s_tha" class="skt-link">mañjiṣṭha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_njiira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मञ्जीर</span> <i>mañjīra</i> <a href="#entry-ma_njiira" class="skt-link">n.</a> anneau de chevilles — <a href="#entry-ma_njiiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mañjīrā</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_nju">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मञ्जु</span> <i>mañju</i> a. <a href="#entry-ma_nju" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nju" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ma_nju" class="skt-link">f.</a> joli, plaisant, gracieux, charmant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_njugho_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mañjughoṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-gho_sa" class="skt-link">ghoṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_njugho_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. de Mañjughoṣa «à la voix mélodieuse», saint bouddhiste civilisateur du Népal, identifié comme l'accompli [<a href="#entry-bodhisattva" class="skt-link">bodhisattva</a>] <a href="#entry-Uma_njuzrii" class="skt-link">Mañjuśrī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-vaagiizvara" class="skt-link">vāgīśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_njunaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mañjunātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_njunaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mañjunātha, identification à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> de <a href="#entry-Uma_njuzrii" class="skt-link">Mañjuśrī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Umafgalapura" class="skt-link">Maṅgalapura</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_njuu_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मञ्जूषा</span> <i>mañjūṣā</i> <a href="#entry-ma_njuu_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> boîte, coffret, écrin; coffre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. recueil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. cf. <a href="#entry-Ulaghuma_njuu_saa" class="skt-link">Laghumañjūṣā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>mañjuṣā</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_njuzrii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mañjuśrī</i> [<a href="#entry-zrii" class="skt-link">śrī</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_njuzrii" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. de l'accompli [<a href="#entry-bodhisattva" class="skt-link">bodhisattva</a>] Mañjuśrī «Saint Gracieux», déification du moine <a href="#entry-Uma_njugho_sa" class="skt-link">Mañjughoṣa</a>; éternellement jeune, symbolisant le Savoir [<a href="#entry-praj_naa" class="skt-link">prajñā</a>], gardien du paradis de l'Est, il est souvent représenté avec un glaive dressé [<a href="#entry-j_naanakha_dga" class="skt-link">jñānakhaḍga</a>] enflammé (le savoir qui tranche l'ignorance) et une couronne à 5 pointes; sa monture est un lion; son culte se répandit en Chine au 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ma_njuzriinaamasafgiiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mañjuśrīnāmasaṃgīti</i> [<a href="#entry-naama_2" class="skt-link">nāma_2</a>-<a href="#entry-safgiiti" class="skt-link">saṃgīti</a>] <a href="#entry-ma_njuzriinaamasa_mgiiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. bd. np. de la Mañjuśrīnāmasaṃgīti «Chant des noms de <a href="#entry-Uma_njuzrii" class="skt-link">Mañjuśrī</a>», texte d'enseignement de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> à son disciple divin <a href="#entry-Uvajrapaa_ni" class="skt-link">Vajrapāṇi</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ma_njuzrii" class="skt-link">mañjuśrī</a> <br> <a href="#entry-ma_nju" class="skt-link">mañju</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-me">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मे</span> <i>me</i> cl. pour <i>mama</i>, <i>mahyam</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-me_dhii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेढी</span> <i>meḍhī</i> cf. <a href="#entry-methi" class="skt-link">methi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-me_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेष</span> <i>meṣa</i> <a href="#entry-me_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> bélier; mouton <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. signe zodiacal [<a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">rāśi</a>] du Bélier — <a href="#entry-me_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>meṣī</i> brebis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-me_sakukku_talaavakayuddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>meṣakukkuṭalāvakayuddha</i> [<a href="#entry-kukku_ta" class="skt-link">kukkuṭa</a>-<a href="#entry-laavaka" class="skt-link">lāvaka</a>-<a href="#entry-yuddha" class="skt-link">yuddha</a>] <a href="#entry-me_sakukku_talaavakayuddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. le combat de béliers, de coqs ou de perdrix.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-me_sakukku_talaavakayuddhavidhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>meṣakukkuṭalāvakayuddhavidhi</i> [<a href="#entry-vidhi" class="skt-link">vidhi</a>] <a href="#entry-me_sakukku_talaavakayuddhavidhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. l'organisation de combat d'animaux, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-me_sakukku_talaavakayuddha" class="skt-link">meṣakukkuṭalāvakayuddha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-me_sapaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>meṣapāla</i> [<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-me_sapaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> berger.<br> <a href="#entry-me_sa" class="skt-link">meṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेदस्</span> <i>medas</i> [<a href="#entry-mid" class="skt-link">mid</a>] <a href="#entry-medas" class="skt-link">n.</a> graisse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medasvin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>medasvin</i> [<a href="#entry--vin" class="skt-link">-vin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-medasvin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-medasvin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-medasvinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>medasvinī</i> gras, corpulent.<br> <a href="#entry-medas" class="skt-link">medas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
