    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेध</span> <i>medha</i> <a href="#entry-medha" class="skt-link">m.</a> jus de viande, sauce, bouillon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> moelle (not. de la victime du scarifice); sève; essence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. victime sacrificielle; sacrifice animal; not. cf. <a href="#entry-azvamedha" class="skt-link">aśvamedha</a> — <a href="#entry-medhas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <i>medhas</i> sacrifice — f. cf. <a href="#entry-medhaa" class="skt-link">medhā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> avestique mazda.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेधा</span> <i>medhā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-medha" class="skt-link">medha</a>] <a href="#entry-medhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> vigueur intellectuelle, intelligence; prudence, sagesse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>medhās</i> opinions <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Medhā «Sagesse», fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, une des épouses de <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medhaatithi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>medhātithi</i> [<a href="#entry-atithi" class="skt-link">atithi</a>] <a href="#entry-medhaatithi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Medhātithi «l'Hôte du sacrifice», père de <a href="#entry-Uka_nva" class="skt-link">Kaṇva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de Medhātithi_1, commentateur bd. de <a href="#entry-Umanu" class="skt-link">Manu</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-medha" class="skt-link">medha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medhaavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>medhāvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-medhaavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-medhaavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-medhaavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>medhāvatī</i> intelligent; avisé, sage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medhaavin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>medhāvin</i> [<a href="#entry--vin" class="skt-link">-vin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-medhaavin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-medhaavin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-medhaavinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>medhāvinī</i> intelligent, malin; avisé, sage — <a href="#entry-medhaavin" class="skt-link">m.</a> érudit, enseignant; syn. <a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a> — <a href="#entry-medhaavinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>medhāvinī</i> myth. np. de Medhāvinī «Savante», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubraahmii" class="skt-link">Brāhmī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-medhaa" class="skt-link">medhā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medhapati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>medhapati</i> [<a href="#entry-pati" class="skt-link">pati</a>] <a href="#entry-medhapati" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [«maître du sacrifice»] divinité honorée par le sacrifice.<br> <i>medhapataye</i> soc. [AB.] (<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> rituel) pour honorer le maître du sacrifice (<i>medhapatibhyām</i> pour 2 divinités, <i>medhapatibhyaḥ</i> pour plusieurs).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medhira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेधिर</span> <i>medhira</i> [<a href="#entry-medha" class="skt-link">medha</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-medhira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-medhira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-medhiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>medhirā</i> véd. sage, intelligent; se dit de <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, <a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medhra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेध्र</span> <i>medhra</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-mih" class="skt-link">mih</a>] <a href="#entry-medhra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-medhra" class="skt-link">n.</a> pénis — <a href="#entry-medhra" class="skt-link">m.</a> bélier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medhracarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>medhracarman</i> [<a href="#entry-carman" class="skt-link">carman</a>] <a href="#entry-medhracarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> prépuce.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medhraroga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>medhraroga</i> [<a href="#entry-roga" class="skt-link">roga</a>] <a href="#entry-medhraroga" class="skt-link">n.</a> maladie vénérienne.<br> <a href="#entry-medhra" class="skt-link">medhra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेध्य</span> <i>medhya</i> [<a href="#entry-medha" class="skt-link">medha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-medhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-medhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-medhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>medhyā</i> plein de sève, vigoureux; propre au sacrifice; pur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> intelligent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेदिन्</span> <i>medin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mid" class="skt-link">mid</a>] a. <a href="#entry-medin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-medin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-medinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>medinī</i> qui possède la vigueur — <a href="#entry-medinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>medinī</i> terre, sol.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medo">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेदो</span> <i>medo</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-medas" class="skt-link">medas</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medodo_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>medodoṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-do_sa" class="skt-link">doṣa</a>] corpulence, obésité.<br> <a href="#entry-medo" class="skt-link">medo</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-medura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेदुर</span> <i>medura</i> [<a href="#entry-mid" class="skt-link">mid</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-medura" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-medura" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-meduraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>medurā</i> gras; onctueux; lisse, doux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> épais; dense; plein de ⟨i. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-megha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेघ</span> <i>megha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mih" class="skt-link">mih</a>] <a href="#entry-megha" class="skt-link">m.</a> nuage — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(meghāyate)</i> se couvrir de nuages.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-meghaduuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>meghadūta</i> [<a href="#entry-duuta" class="skt-link">dūta</a>] <a href="#entry-meghaduuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Meghadūta «Le nuage messager», célèbre poème de <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a> en mètre <a href="#entry-mandaakraantaa" class="skt-link">mandākrāntā</a>; c'est une élégie évoquant les peines de cœur d'un <a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a> séparé de sa bien-aimée, car banni pour un an par <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> pour abandon de poste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-meghanaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>meghanāda</i> [<a href="#entry-naada" class="skt-link">nāda</a>] <a href="#entry-meghanaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Meghanāda «Tonitruant», fils de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> et <a href="#entry-Umandodarii" class="skt-link">Mandodarī</a>; il vainquit <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> [<a href="#entry-Uindrajit" class="skt-link">Indrajit</a>] et ne le libéra que contre le don d'immortalité; il fut néanmoins tué par <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Ukaadambarii" class="skt-link">Kādambarī</a>] np. du chef d'armée Meghanāda_1, fils de <a href="#entry-Ubalaahaka_1" class="skt-link">Balāhaka_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-meghapu_spa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>meghapuṣpa</i> [<a href="#entry-pu_spa" class="skt-link">puṣpa</a>] <a href="#entry-meghapu_spa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [PP.] np. de Meghapuṣpa «Fleur de nuage», l'un des 4 chevaux du char de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, de couleur de nuage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-meghavahana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>meghavahana</i> [<a href="#entry-vahana" class="skt-link">vahana</a>] <a href="#entry-meghavahana" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de la lignée de princes Meghavahana; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukhaaravela" class="skt-link">Khāravela</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-meghazyaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>meghaśyāma</i> [<a href="#entry-zyaama" class="skt-link">śyāma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-meghazyaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-meghazyaama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-meghazyaamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>meghaśyāmā</i> noir comme un nuage.<br> <a href="#entry-megha" class="skt-link">megha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-meha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेह</span> <i>meha</i> [obj. <a href="#entry-mih" class="skt-link">mih</a>] <a href="#entry-meha" class="skt-link">m.</a> urine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. maladie urinaire; diabète.<br> <i>mehaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> uriner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mehala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mehala</i> [<a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mehala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mehala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mehalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mehalā</i> qui a l'odeur d'urine — <a href="#entry-mehala" class="skt-link">m.</a> odeur d'urine.<br> <a href="#entry-meha" class="skt-link">meha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mehana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेहन</span> <i>mehana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mih" class="skt-link">mih</a>] <a href="#entry-mehana" class="skt-link">n.</a> pénis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conduit urinaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> urine; miction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mekhalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेखला</span> <i>mekhalā</i> <a href="#entry-mekhalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> ceinture; not. ceinture d'un étudiant brahmanique; sangle, baudrier; ventrière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] cercle entourant un <a href="#entry-yantra" class="skt-link">yantra</a> géométrique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mekhalin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mekhalin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mekhalin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mekhalin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mekhalinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mekhalinī</i> qui porte une ceinture; not. étudiant brahmanique.<br> <a href="#entry-mekhalaa" class="skt-link">mekhalā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mela">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेल</span> <i>mela</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mil" class="skt-link">mil</a>] <a href="#entry-mela" class="skt-link">m.</a> réunion, contact <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pélerinage; cf. <a href="#entry-kumbhamela" class="skt-link">kumbhamela</a> — <a href="#entry-melaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>melā</i> société, compagnie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-melaap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेलाप्</span> <i>melāp</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-mil" class="skt-link">mil</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(melāpayati)</i> faire rencontrer qqn. avec qqn. d'autre ⟨g.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-melaapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेलाप</span> <i>melāpa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-melaap" class="skt-link">melāp</a>] <a href="#entry-melaapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> réunion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-melaapaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>melāpaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-melaapaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> réunion, rassemblement; conjonction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. conjonction de planètes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. [tantr.] union avec une <a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-melaapa" class="skt-link">melāpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-melaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>melaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-melaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> réunion, rencontre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. [tantr.] union avec une <a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a>.<br> <i>melakaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> assembler.<br> <a href="#entry-mela" class="skt-link">mela</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-melana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेलन</span> <i>melana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mil" class="skt-link">mil</a>] <a href="#entry-melana" class="skt-link">n.</a> réunion, rencontre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-menaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेना</span> <i>menā</i> <a href="#entry-menaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-menakaa" class="skt-link">menakā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-menakaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेनका</span> <i>menakā</i> var. <i>menā</i> <a href="#entry-menakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. véd. np. de la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] Menakā ou Menā; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> lui demanda de séduire le sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a>, pour le distraire de ses austérités qui menaçaient les dieux; elle en enfanta de <a href="#entry-Uzakuntalaa" class="skt-link">Śakuntalā</a>; par son époux <a href="#entry-Uhimavaan" class="skt-link">Himavān</a> elle est mère de <a href="#entry-Uraagi_nii" class="skt-link">Rāgiṇī</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugafgaa" class="skt-link">Gaṅgā</a>, d'<a href="#entry-Uumaa" class="skt-link">Umā</a>-<a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>, et de <a href="#entry-Umainaaka" class="skt-link">Maināka</a>.<br> <i>niyamavighnakāriṇī</i> <i>menakā</i> <i>nāma</i> <i>apsarāḥ</i> <i>preṣitā</i> La nymphe <a href="#entry-Umenakaa" class="skt-link">Menakā</a> a été envoyée pour faire obstacle à l'ascèse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-meru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेरु</span> <i>meru</i> <a href="#entry-meru" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. le mont Meru, montagne fabuleuse, au centre du <a href="#entry-Ujambudviipa" class="skt-link">Jambudvīpa</a>, et donc au centre du monde; un arbre <a href="#entry-Ujambu" class="skt-link">Jambu</a> immense le couronne; le Gange y tombe des cieux pour s'y subdiviser en 4 fleuves dans les 4 directions; les gardiens [<a href="#entry-dikpaala" class="skt-link">dikpāla</a>] des directions y résident; ses pentes sont d'or à l'Est, d'argent à l'Ouest, de cristal au Sud-Est et d'agathe au Nord-Ouest; il possède 4 sommets, le 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ ayant été précipité dans la mer par <a href="#entry-Uvaayu" class="skt-link">Vāyu</a> pour former l'ile de <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>; sur ses pentes se trouvent <a href="#entry-Usvarga" class="skt-link">Svarga</a>, le paradis d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, et les demeures des 33 dieux (cf. <a href="#entry-trayastri_mza" class="skt-link">trayastriṃśa</a>); <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> y règne au sommet; cf. <a href="#entry-Usumeru" class="skt-link">Sumeru</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Meru_1, un <a href="#entry-cakravartin" class="skt-link">cakravartin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-meruprastaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>meruprastāra</i> [<a href="#entry-prastaara" class="skt-link">prastāra</a>] <a href="#entry-meruprastaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> math. triangle de Pascal (inventé par <a href="#entry-Upifgala_2" class="skt-link">Piṅgala_2</a>).<br> <a href="#entry-meru" class="skt-link">meru</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-methi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेथि</span> <i>methi</i> <a href="#entry-methi" class="skt-link">m.</a> poteau; not. véd. poteau pour attacher l'animal du sacrifice — <a href="#entry-methii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>methī</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>methikā</i> natu. bot. <i>Trigonella foenum-graecum</i> fénugrec, luzerne aromatique; ses graines riches en mucilage sont utilisées comme condiment et en médecine <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>meḍhī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-methikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>methikā</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-methikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-methii" class="skt-link">methī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-methi" class="skt-link">methi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-meya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मेय</span> <i>meya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-meya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-meya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-meyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>meyā</i> mesurable.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मि</span> <i>mi</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(minoti)</i> pr. md. <i>(minute)</i> pft. <i>(mimāya)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mita_2</i><i>)</i> attacher; établir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mesurer, délimiter <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> juger, percevoir, connaître — ps. <i>(mīyate)</i> être attaché; être mesuré; être connu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mi_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिष्</span> <i>miṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(miṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>miṣita</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(miṣat)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i>, <i>ni</i><i>)</i> ouvrir les yeux (utilisé au simple seulement au ppr. g. absolu).<br> <i>miṣatas</i> adv. sous les yeux de, en présence de; en dépit (du regard) de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mi_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिष्ट</span> <i>miṣṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-m_r_s_ta_1" class="skt-link">mṛṣṭa_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mi_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mi_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mi_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>miṣṭā</i> doux, délicat, agréable; sucré — <a href="#entry-mi_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> friandise.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mi_s_taanna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>miṣṭānna</i> [<a href="#entry-anna" class="skt-link">anna</a>] <a href="#entry-mi_s_taanna" class="skt-link">n.</a> nourriture savoureuse; mets sucré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mi_s_taannapaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>miṣṭānnapāna</i> [<a href="#entry-paana" class="skt-link">pāna</a>] <a href="#entry-mi_s_taannapaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> du. mets et boissons sucrés.<br> <a href="#entry-mi_s_taanna" class="skt-link">miṣṭānna</a> <br> <a href="#entry-mi_s_ta" class="skt-link">miṣṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिद्</span> <i>mid</i> var. <i>mind</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(medate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(mindayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>minna</i><i>)</i> être gros — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(medyati)</i> grossir, engraisser — ca. <i>(medayati)</i> faire grossir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mih">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिह्</span> <i>mih</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(mehati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mīḍha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ava</i><i>)</i> uriner, arroser; pleuvoir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>mingere</i>; fr. miction.<br> <i>mehati</i> il pleut.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mihira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिहिर</span> <i>mihira</i> <a href="#entry-mihira" class="skt-link">m.</a> le Soleil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mihirakula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mihirakula</i> [<a href="#entry-kula" class="skt-link">kula</a>] <a href="#entry-mihirakula" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du tyran <a href="#entry-huu_na" class="skt-link">hūṇa</a> Mihirakula «de la maison Solaire», fils du roi <a href="#entry-Utoramaa_na" class="skt-link">Toramāṇa</a>; il conquit un empire en Inde du Nord au début du 6⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il en fut chassé par le roi <a href="#entry-Uyazodharmaa" class="skt-link">Yaśodharmā</a> du <a href="#entry-Umaalava" class="skt-link">Mālava</a> en 528, et se réfugia à <a href="#entry-Uzaakala" class="skt-link">Śākala</a> au <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a>, où il mourut en 533; il était <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a>, et persécuta les bouddhistes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mihirapura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mihirapura</i> [<a href="#entry-pura" class="skt-link">pura</a>] <a href="#entry-mihirapura" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Mihirapura, fondée par <a href="#entry-Umihirakula" class="skt-link">Mihirakula</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mihirezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mihireśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-mihirezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du temple shivaïte Mihireśvara, fondé par le roi <a href="#entry-Umihirakula" class="skt-link">Mihirakula</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mihira" class="skt-link">mihira</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मी</span> <i>mī</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(mīnāti)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] pr. <i>(minoti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mīta</i><i>)</i> renverser, détruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mii_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मीढ</span> <i>mīḍha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mih" class="skt-link">mih</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mii_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mii_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mii_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mīḍhā</i> uriné — <a href="#entry-mii_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> bélier — <a href="#entry-mii_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> compétition; prix, récompense.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miil">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मील्</span> <i>mīl</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(mīlati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mīlita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i>, <i>ni</i><i>)</i> fermer les yeux, cligner; se fermer (yeux, fleurs) — ca. <i>(mīlayati)</i> faire se fermer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miimaa_ms">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मीमांस्</span> <i>mīmāṃs</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] v. pr. md. <i>(mīmāṃsate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mīmāṃsita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>mīmāṃsya</i><i>)</i> réfléchir; vouloir comprendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miimaa_msaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मीमांसा</span> <i>mīmāṃsā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-miimaa_ms" class="skt-link">mīmāṃs</a>] <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> désir de connaissance; investigation, examen; exégèse; casuistique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. doctrine philosophique; cf. <a href="#entry-puurvamiimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">pūrvamīmāṃsā</a>, <a href="#entry-uttaramiimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">uttaramīmāṃsā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miimaa_msaanyaayaprakaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mīmāṃsānyāyaprakāśa</i> [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>-<a href="#entry-prakaaza" class="skt-link">prakāśa</a>] <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaanyaayaprakaaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Mīmāṃsānyāyaprakāśa d'<a href="#entry-Uaapadeva" class="skt-link">Āpadeva</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uaapadevii" class="skt-link">Āpadevī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miimaa_msaasuutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mīmāṃsāsūtra</i> [<a href="#entry-suutra" class="skt-link">sūtra</a>] <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaasuutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> pl. lit. np. du Mīmāṃsāsūtra, exposant la doctrine <a href="#entry-puurvamiimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">pūrvamīmāṃsā</a>; il est postérieur au 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant., mais attribué à <a href="#entry-Ujaimini" class="skt-link">Jaimini</a>; le <a href="#entry-Usafkar_sa_nakaa_n_da" class="skt-link">Saṃkarṣaṇakāṇḍa</a> en est une annexe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miimaa_msaasuutrabhaa_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mīmāṃsāsūtrabhāṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_sya" class="skt-link">bhāṣya</a>] <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaasuutrabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mīmāṃsāsūtrabhāṣya ou Mīmāṃsābhāṣya, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Umiimaa_msaasuutra" class="skt-link">Mīmāṃsāsūtra</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uzabara_1" class="skt-link">Śabara_1</a> <a href="#entry-Usvaamii" class="skt-link">Svāmī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaasuutra" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsāsūtra</a> <br> <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miimaa_msaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मीमांसक</span> <i>mīmāṃsaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-miimaa_ms" class="skt-link">mīmāṃs</a>] <a href="#entry-miimaa_msikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mīmāṃsikā</i> examinateur, investigateur; exégète <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. adepte ou doctrinaire de la doctrine <a href="#entry-puurvamiimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">pūrvamīmāṃsā</a> — <a href="#entry-miimaa_msikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mīmāṃsikā</i> phil. syn. <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miimaa_msin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मीमांसिन्</span> <i>mīmāṃsin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-miimaa_ms" class="skt-link">mīmāṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-miimaa_msin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-miimaa_msin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-miimaa_msinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mīmāṃsinī</i> phil. raisonneur casuiste.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miimaa_msita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मीमांसित</span> <i>mīmāṃsita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-miimaa_ms" class="skt-link">mīmāṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-miimaa_msita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-miimaa_msita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-miimaa_msitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mīmāṃsitā</i> réfléchi, pesé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miimaa_msya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मीमांस्य</span> <i>mīmāṃsya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-miimaa_ms" class="skt-link">mīmāṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-miimaa_msya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-miimaa_msya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-miimaa_msyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mīmāṃsyā</i> discutable, à mettre en question.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मीन</span> <i>mīna</i> <a href="#entry-miina" class="skt-link">m.</a> poisson <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. signe zodiacal [<a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">rāśi</a>] du Poisson.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miinaak_sii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mīnākṣī</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_si" class="skt-link">akṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-miinaak_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Mīnākṣī «(la Déesse) aux yeux en forme de poisson», fille de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>, une des formes de <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, déification d'une princesse <a href="#entry-Upaa_n_dya" class="skt-link">Pāṇḍya</a> vénérée dans le grand temple de <a href="#entry-Umiinaak_sii" class="skt-link">Mīnākṣī</a>-<a href="#entry-Usundarezvara" class="skt-link">Sundareśvara</a> à Madurai, au Tamil Nadu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miinakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mīnakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-miinakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> poissonnier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miinaraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mīnarāja</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>] <a href="#entry-miinaraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Mīnarāja, astronome de l'Inde de l'Ouest du 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on lui doit le <a href="#entry-Uv_rddhayavanajaataka" class="skt-link">Vṛddhayavanajātaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miinaraajajaataka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mīnarājajātaka</i> [<a href="#entry-jaataka" class="skt-link">jātaka</a>] <a href="#entry-miinaraajajaataka" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. np. du Mīnarājajātaka; cf. <a href="#entry-Uv_rddhayavanajaataka" class="skt-link">Vṛddhayavanajātaka</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-miinaraaja" class="skt-link">mīnarāja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miinayugala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mīnayugala</i> [<a href="#entry-yugala" class="skt-link">yugala</a>] <a href="#entry-miinayugala" class="skt-link">n.</a> jn. paire de poissons, symbole de bon augure; cf. <a href="#entry-a_s_tamafgala" class="skt-link">aṣṭamaṅgala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miinendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mīnendra</i> [<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-miinendra" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. de Mīnendra «Roi des poissons», épith. du <a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">rāśi</a> <a href="#entry-makara" class="skt-link">makara</a> (Capricorne).<br> <a href="#entry-miina" class="skt-link">mīna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मीर</span> <i>mīra</i> <a href="#entry-miira" class="skt-link">m.</a> mer, océan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> boisson — <a href="#entry-miiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mīrā</i> hist. np. de Mīra ou Mīrabai (1498–1547), princesse <i>rājput</i>, célèbre pour sa totale dévotion à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>; elle composa de nombreux poèmes dévotionnels; sa conduite extatique faisait scandale dans sa famille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mil">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिल्</span> <i>mil</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(milati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>milita</i><i>)</i> rencontrer qqn. ⟨i.-<a href="#entry-saha" class="skt-link">saha</a> g. loc.⟩ — ca. <i>(melāpayati)</i> faire rencontrer qqn. avec qqn. d'autre ⟨g.⟩.<br> <i>kutra</i> <i>milāmaḥ</i> où nous rencontrons-nous?<br> <i>punaḥ</i> <i>milāmaḥ</i> à bientôt, au revoir.<br> <i>sarve</i> <i>militvā</i> <i>sapta</i> <i>vayam</i> nous sommes 7 en tout.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-milana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिलन</span> <i>milana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mil" class="skt-link">mil</a>] <a href="#entry-milana" class="skt-link">n.</a> rencontre; contact.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-milarepa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिलरेप</span> <i>milarepa</i> <a href="#entry-milarepa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du mystique tibétain bd. Mila Raspa ou Milarepa (1040–1123), né dans la province de Goungthang d'un propriétaire terrien qui mourut lorsqu'il avait 7 ans; il vécut avec sa mère comme domestique de son oncle qui confisqua ses biens; pour se venger, il étudia la magie noire et devint un magicien renommé; il pouvait conjurer la grêle; il prit <a href="#entry-Umarpa" class="skt-link">Marpa</a> comme maître, qui lui fit faire d'innombrables épreuves; c'est un saint accompli [<a href="#entry-mahaasiddha" class="skt-link">mahāsiddha</a>] et un poète; son disciple Retchung écrivit sa biographie sur ses indications.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-milinda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिलिन्द</span> <i>milinda</i> <a href="#entry-milinda" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. abeille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du roi indo-grec Milinda (Ménandre), originaire de Bactriane; il régnait à <a href="#entry-Uzaakala" class="skt-link">Śākala</a> (Pañjāb) au 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle ant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-milindaprazna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>milindapraśna</i> [<a href="#entry-prazna" class="skt-link">praśna</a>] <a href="#entry-milindaprazna" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Milindapraśna «Questions de Ménandre», ouvrage bd. <a href="#entry-paali" class="skt-link">pāli</a> sur le dialogue entre le roi <a href="#entry-Umilinda" class="skt-link">Milinda</a> et le moine <a href="#entry-Unaagasena" class="skt-link">Nāgasena</a> à propos du <a href="#entry-nirvaa_na" class="skt-link">nirvāṇa</a>; ce bréviaire bd. de la culture du <a href="#entry-Ugandhaara" class="skt-link">Gandhāra</a> est probablement antérieur au 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>Milindapañha</i>.<br> <a href="#entry-milinda" class="skt-link">milinda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-milita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिलित</span> <i>milita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mil" class="skt-link">mil</a>] a. <a href="#entry-milita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-milita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-militaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>militā</i> rencontré; réuni; réalisé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mim">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिम्</span> <i>mim</i> cf. <a href="#entry-maa_3" class="skt-link">mā_3</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-minna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिन्न</span> <i>minna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mid" class="skt-link">mid</a>] a. <a href="#entry-minna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-minna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-minnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>minnā</i> devenu gros, obèse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mita_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मित</span> <i>mita_1</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mita_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mita_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mitaa_1" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mitā_1</i> mesuré, limité; modéré, modique; court, petit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mita_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मित</span> <i>mita_2</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mi" class="skt-link">mi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mita_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mita_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mitaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mitā_2</i> attaché, établi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitaak_saraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mitākṣarā</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sara" class="skt-link">akṣara</a>] <a href="#entry-mitaak_saraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Mitākṣarā, commentaire de l'<a href="#entry-Ua_s_taadhyaayii" class="skt-link">Aṣṭādhyāyī</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uannambha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Annambhaṭṭa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mita_1" class="skt-link">mita_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitampaca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mitaṃpaca</i> [<a href="#entry-paca" class="skt-link">paca</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mita_mpaca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mita_mpaca" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mita_mpacaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mitaṃpacā</i> [«qui cuit maigre»] avare.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mith">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिथ्</span> <i>mith</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(methati)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>mithitvā</i><i>)</i> rencontrer; s'apparier; s'accoupler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> confronter; affronter, s'engager dans une altercation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिथस्</span> <i>mithas</i> [<a href="#entry-mith" class="skt-link">mith</a>] adv. en compagnie de ⟨i.⟩; l'un l'autre, réciproquement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिथि</span> <i>mithi</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-mathana" class="skt-link">mathana</a>] <a href="#entry-mithi" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Mithi (ou <a href="#entry-Umithila" class="skt-link">Mithila</a>), «Baratté» de la cuisse de <a href="#entry-Unimi" class="skt-link">Nimi</a>, fondateur de <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a>, ancêtre de la lignée <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a>; <a href="#entry-Uudaavasu" class="skt-link">Udāvasu</a> est son fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithila">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिथिल</span> <i>mithila</i> [<a href="#entry-mithi" class="skt-link">mithi</a><a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] <a href="#entry-mithila" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Mithila; cf. <a href="#entry-Umithi" class="skt-link">Mithi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>mithilās</i> habitants de <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a> — <a href="#entry-mithilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mithilā</i> géo. np. de Mithilā, capitale du <a href="#entry-Uvideha" class="skt-link">Videha</a> (mod. Janakpur au sud du Népal); ses rois étaient dits descendre de <a href="#entry-Unimi" class="skt-link">Nimi</a> par <a href="#entry-Umithi" class="skt-link">Mithi</a>, ancêtre de la lignée <a href="#entry-Ujanaka" class="skt-link">Janaka</a>; [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] les jeunes <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> et <a href="#entry-Ulak_sma_na" class="skt-link">Lakṣmaṇa</a>, avec leur maître <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a>, y rendirent visite au roi <a href="#entry-Ujanakaraaja" class="skt-link">Janakarāja</a>.<br> <i>mithilāyām</i> <i>pradīptāyām</i> <i>na</i> <i>me</i> <i>dahyati</i> <i>kiṃcana</i> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] Même si <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a> était en flammes je ne serais pas tourmenté (<a href="#entry-Ujanakaraaja" class="skt-link">Janakarāja</a> montrant son détachement du monde).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिथु</span> <i>mithu</i> [<a href="#entry-mith" class="skt-link">mith</a>] adv. alternativement, contradictoirement; faussement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithuna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिथुन</span> <i>mithuna</i> [<a href="#entry-mith" class="skt-link">mith</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mithuna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mithuna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mithunaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mithunā</i> formant couple — <a href="#entry-mithuna" class="skt-link">n.</a> couple, paire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> union, accouplement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> arch. thème iconographique du couple amoureux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Mithuna, signe zodiacal [<a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">rāśi</a>] des Gémeaux.<br> <i>mithunyas</i> se mettre en couple; cohabiter sexuellement.<br> <i>mithunīkṛ</i> faire s'apparier; faire s'accoupler.<br> <i>mithunībhū</i> être apparié.<br> <i>mithunībhāva</i> m. copulation.<br> <i>mithunīcārin</i> a. m. n. qui s'accouple.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithuyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिथुया</span> <i>mithuyā</i> [<a href="#entry-mithu" class="skt-link">mithu</a>-<a href="#entry-yaa_3" class="skt-link">yā_3</a>] adv. inversement, contrairement; faussement, incorrectement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिथ्या</span> <i>mithyā</i> [contr. <i>mithuyā</i>] adv. à l'envers, de travers; à tort, faussement, mal <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en apparence, de manière illusoire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en vain, inutilement, stérilement — iic. faux; faussement — <a href="#entry-mithyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Mithyā «Fausseté», sa personnification, épouse d'<a href="#entry-Uadharma" class="skt-link">Adharma</a>.<br> <i>mithyā</i> <i>kṛ</i> nier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> manquer à sa parole.<br> <i>na</i> <i>mithyā</i> <i>kṛ</i> tenir sa parole.<br> <i>mithyā</i> <i>kār</i> mal prononcer.<br> <i>mithyā</i> <i>pracar</i> agir à tort.<br> <i>mithyā</i> <i>pravṛt</i> mal se comporter.<br> <i>mithyā</i> <i>bhū</i> s'avérer faux.<br> <i>mithyā</i> <i>brū</i> mentir.<br> <i>mithyā</i> <i>vac</i> mentir (opp. <i>satyaṃ</i> <i>vad</i>).<br> <i>brahma</i> <i>satyaṃ</i> <i>jaganmithyā</i> [<a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a>] Brahman est la vérité, le monde n'est qu'illusion.<br> <i>sarvam</i> <i>mithyā</i> <i>bravīmi</i> [<a href="#entry-Ubhart_rhari" class="skt-link">Bhartṛhari</a>] Tout ce que je dis est faux.<br> <i>sarvam</i> <i>jñānam</i> <i>mithyā</i> [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] Toute connaissance est fausse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithyaacaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mithyācāra</i> [<a href="#entry-caara" class="skt-link">cāra</a>] <a href="#entry-mithyaacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> mauvaise conduite; mauvais traitement (méd.) — a. <a href="#entry-mithyaacaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mithyaacaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mithyaacaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mithyācārā</i> qui se conduit mal; hypocrite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithyaajalpita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mithyājalpita</i> [<a href="#entry-jalpita" class="skt-link">jalpita</a>] <a href="#entry-mithyaajalpita" class="skt-link">n.</a> fausse nouvelle, rumeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithyaaj_naana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mithyājñāna</i> [<a href="#entry-j_naana" class="skt-link">jñāna</a>] <a href="#entry-mithyaaj_naana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fausse connaissance, misconception; erreur, faute.<br> <i>mithyājñānam</i> adv. par erreur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithyaak_rta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mithyākṛta</i> [<a href="#entry-k_rta" class="skt-link">kṛta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mithyaak_rta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mithyaak_rta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mithyaak_rtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mithyākṛtā</i> fait à tort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithyaakarman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mithyākarman</i> [<a href="#entry-karman" class="skt-link">karman</a>] <a href="#entry-mithyaakarman" class="skt-link">n.</a> erreur, faute; acte manqué, échec.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithyaakopa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mithyākopa</i> [<a href="#entry-kopa" class="skt-link">kopa</a>] <a href="#entry-mithyaakopa" class="skt-link">m.</a> colère feinte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mithyaapratij_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mithyāpratijña</i> [<a href="#entry-pratij_naa_2" class="skt-link">pratijñā_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mithyaapratij_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mithyaapratij_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mithyaapratij_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mithyāpratijñā</i> qui ne tient pas sa parole, parjure.<br> <a href="#entry-mithyaa" class="skt-link">mithyā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-miti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिति</span> <i>miti</i> [<a href="#entry-maa_1" class="skt-link">mā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-miti" class="skt-link">f.</a> mesure; poids <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. justification, preuve <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] phonème ‘m’, évoquant la dissolution dans le <a href="#entry-pra_nava" class="skt-link">praṇava</a> <a href="#entry-o_m" class="skt-link">oṃ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मित्र</span> <i>mitra</i> [<a href="#entry-mith" class="skt-link">mith</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-mitra" class="skt-link">n.</a> ami; allié — <a href="#entry-mitra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. véd. np. du radieux [<a href="#entry-aaditya" class="skt-link">āditya</a>] Mitra «l'Ami»; il représente le côté clair du divin associé au Soleil, opposé à son côté sombre, personnifié par <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> associé aux Eaux souterraines; il symbolise le contrat social; et est le dieu des Alliances; phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] il est régent [<a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">niyantṛ</a>] de la faculté d'évacuation [<a href="#entry-paayu" class="skt-link">pāyu</a>]; astr. il préside le <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a> <a href="#entry-Uanuraadhaas" class="skt-link">Anurādhās</a> (δ du Scorpion) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> du. <i>mitrā</i> myth. ellipse pour le couple <a href="#entry-Umitraavaru_nau" class="skt-link">Mitrāvaruṇau</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du <a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a> Mitra_1 <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a> (17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), auteur du <a href="#entry-Uviiramitrodaya" class="skt-link">Vīramitrodaya</a> — <a href="#entry-mitraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mitrā</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Mitrā, l'une des 7 «mères (dangereuses) aux enfants» [<a href="#entry-zizumaataras" class="skt-link">śiśumātaras</a>] associées à <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> avestique Mithra.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitraavaru_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mitrāvaruṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-varu_na" class="skt-link">varuṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-mitraavaru_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> du. <i>mitrāvaruṇau</i> myth. [RV.] np. de Mitrāvaruṇau «<a href="#entry-Umitra" class="skt-link">Mitra</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> ensemble»; premiers des radieux [<a href="#entry-aadityaas" class="skt-link">ādityās</a>], ils sont les titans devenus des dieux [<a href="#entry-devaavasurau" class="skt-link">devāvasurau</a>]; ils sont souverains [<a href="#entry-samraaj" class="skt-link">samrāj</a>] sur la Terre et le Ciel, et régissent la justice, la vérité et la lumière; [MP.] un jour où la nymphe <a href="#entry-Uurvazii" class="skt-link">Urvaśī</a> visita leur ermitage, ils perdirent leur semence devant sa beauté; elle la recueillit dans un pot, et engendra ainsi <a href="#entry-Uagasti" class="skt-link">Agasti</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a>; le premier partit répandre le brahmanisme au Sud de l'Inde pour en devenir le saint patron, le second restant au Nord comme chapelain [<a href="#entry-purohita" class="skt-link">purohita</a>] de la lignée du roi <a href="#entry-Uik_svaaku" class="skt-link">Ikṣvāku</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitrabheda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mitrabheda</i> [<a href="#entry-bheda" class="skt-link">bheda</a>] <a href="#entry-mitrabheda" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. «Désunion des amis», titre du 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Upa_ncatantra" class="skt-link">Pañcatantra</a>, qui montre le danger de prêter l'oreille à la médisance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitradroha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mitradroha</i> [<a href="#entry-droha" class="skt-link">droha</a>] <a href="#entry-mitradroha" class="skt-link">m.</a> traîtrise.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitradruh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mitradruh</i> [<a href="#entry-druh_2" class="skt-link">druh_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mitradruh" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mitradruh" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mitradruh" class="skt-link">f.</a> traître; faux ami.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitrapraapti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mitraprāpti</i> [<a href="#entry-praapti" class="skt-link">prāpti</a>] <a href="#entry-mitrapraapti" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. «Acquisition des amis», titre du 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ livre du <a href="#entry-Upa_ncatantra" class="skt-link">Pañcatantra</a>, qui montre l'intérêt de s'entraider.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitrasaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mitrasaha</i> [<a href="#entry-saha_1" class="skt-link">saha_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mitrasaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mitrasaha, épith. du roi <a href="#entry-Ukalmaa_sapaada" class="skt-link">Kalmāṣapāda</a> «indulgent envers ses amis».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitravat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mitravat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mitravat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mitravat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mitravatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mitravatī</i> qui a des amis — adv. comme un ami.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitravindaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mitravindā</i> [<a href="#entry-vindaa" class="skt-link">vindā</a>] <a href="#entry-mitravindaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Mitravindā «Qui possède un ami», une épouse de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Uvardhana" class="skt-link">Vardhana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mitrodaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mitrodaya</i> [<a href="#entry-udaya" class="skt-link">udaya</a>] <a href="#entry-mitrodaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lever de Soleil.<br> <a href="#entry-mitra" class="skt-link">mitra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mizra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिश्र</span> <i>miśra</i> a. <a href="#entry-mizra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mizra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mizraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>miśrā</i> mélangé, mêlé; varié, multiple <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> accompagné de, muni de ⟨i.-<a href="#entry-samam" class="skt-link">samam</a> g. iic.⟩ — iic. adr. (honorifique) Honorable ⟨np.⟩ — ifc. pl. id. — <a href="#entry-mizra" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. de Miśra, titre éminent de brâhmane not. en Orissa — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(miśrayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>miśrita</i><i>)</i> mélanger; combiner (avec ⟨i.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>miscere</i>; ang. mix; fr. miscible.<br> <i>miśrībhū</i> se mélanger (à ⟨i.⟩); s'accoupler.<br> <i>āryamiśrāḥ</i> honorables personnes; votre honneur.<br> <i>āryamiśrāḥ</i> <i>pramāṇam</i> Votre honneur fait autorité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mizra_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>miśraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-mizra_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> mélange.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mizraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>miśraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mizraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mizraka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mizrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>miśrikā</i> mélangé; impur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mizramata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>miśramata</i> [<a href="#entry-mata" class="skt-link">mata</a>] <a href="#entry-mizramata" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a>] école de <a href="#entry-Umuraari_1" class="skt-link">Murāri_1</a> <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mizra" class="skt-link">miśra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mizrita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मिश्रित</span> <i>miśrita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mizra" class="skt-link">miśra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mizrita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mizrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mizritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>miśritā</i> mélangé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mla">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्ल</span> <i>mla</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mlaa" class="skt-link">mlā</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-mla" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mla" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mlaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mlā</i> abattu, déprimé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mlaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्ला</span> <i>mlā</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(mlāyati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mlāna</i><i>)</i> se faner, se flétrir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être abattu, être mélancolique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μαλακος; fr. mélancolie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mlaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्लान</span> <i>mlāna</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mlaa" class="skt-link">mlā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mlaana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mlaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mlaanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mlānā</i> fané, flétri <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> alangui, abattu, mélancolique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mlecch">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्लेच्छ्</span> <i>mlecch</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(mlecchati)</i> pft. <i>(mimleccha)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(amlecchīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mlecchita</i>, <i>mliṣṭa</i><i>)</i> parler de manière incompréhensible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mleccha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्लेच्छ</span> <i>mleccha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mlecch" class="skt-link">mlecch</a>] <a href="#entry-mleccha" class="skt-link">m.</a> barbare non-Arya; étranger; hors-caste; infidèle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ignorance du sanskrit; faute, barbarisme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>mlecchās</i> les barbares, les étrangers.<br> <i>gomāṃsakhādako</i> <i>yastu</i> <i>viruddhaṃ</i> <i>bahubhāṣate</i> <i>sarvācāravihīnasya</i> <i>mleccha</i> <i>iti</i> <i>abhidhīyate</i> [<a href="#entry-Ubaudhaayana" class="skt-link">Baudhāyana</a>] Celui qui mange de la vache, qui parle avec hostilité et dont tout le comportement est vulgaire, on l'appelle un barbare.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mlecchasamparka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mlecchasaṃparka</i> [<a href="#entry-samparka" class="skt-link">saṃparka</a>] <a href="#entry-mlecchasa_mparka" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. contact avec un étranger (ou un hindou ayant traversé l'océan); cet interdit doit être expié.<br> <a href="#entry-mleccha" class="skt-link">mleccha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mlecchayaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mlecchayajña</i> [<a href="#entry-yaj_na" class="skt-link">yajña</a>] <a href="#entry-mlecchayaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [<a href="#entry-Ubhavi_syapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Bhaviṣyapurāṇa</a>] massacre d'étrangers offerts en sacrifice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mlecchita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्लेच्छित</span> <i>mlecchita</i> var. <i>mliṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mlecch" class="skt-link">mlecch</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mlecchita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mlecchita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mlecchitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mlecchitā</i> (langue) incompréhensible — <a href="#entry-mlecchita" class="skt-link">n.</a> langue étrangère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mlecchitavikalpa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mlecchitavikalpa</i> [<a href="#entry-vikalpa" class="skt-link">vikalpa</a>] <a href="#entry-mlecchitavikalpa" class="skt-link">m.</a> la connaissance des langues étrangères, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-mlecchita" class="skt-link">mlecchita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mli_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्लिष्ट</span> <i>mliṣṭa</i> var. <i>mlecchita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mlecch" class="skt-link">mlecch</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mli_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mli_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mli_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mliṣṭā</i> (langue) incompréhensible — <a href="#entry-mli_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> langue étrangère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mnaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्ना</span> <i>mnā</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-man" class="skt-link">man</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(manati)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] <i>(amnāsīt)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mnāta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-moc">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोच्</span> <i>moc</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(mocayati)</i> mettre en liberté, délier; libérer de ⟨abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mocaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोचक</span> <i>mocaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mocaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mocaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mocikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mocikā</i> qui libère, qui émancipe — <a href="#entry-mocaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> ascète, renonçant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mocana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोचन</span> <i>mocana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mocana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mocana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mocanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mocanī</i> ifc. qui libère de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui lance, qui jette — <a href="#entry-mocana" class="skt-link">n.</a> libération; fait de dételer (un chariot) — ifc. émission de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mod">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोद्</span> <i>mod</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-mud_1" class="skt-link">mud_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(modayati)</i> réjouir, amuser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-moda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोद</span> <i>moda</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mud_1" class="skt-link">mud_1</a>] <a href="#entry-moda" class="skt-link">m.</a> joie, plaisir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-modaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोदक</span> <i>modaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mud_1" class="skt-link">mud_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-modaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-modaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-modikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>modikā</i> ifc. qui réjouit — <a href="#entry-modaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> friandise, sucrerie; not. boulette de riz (ou de semoule) aromatisée à la noix de coco et cardamome et caramélisée; <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> en est friand, et on en prépare pour célébrer <a href="#entry-ga_nezacaturthii" class="skt-link">gaṇeśacaturthī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-modakapriya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>modakapriya</i> [<a href="#entry-priya" class="skt-link">priya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-modakapriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-modakapriya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-modakapriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>modakapriyā</i> qui aime les friandises <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> «Gourmand».<br> <a href="#entry-modaka" class="skt-link">modaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-modana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोदन</span> <i>modana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mud_1" class="skt-link">mud_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-modana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-modana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-modanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>modanā</i> ifc. qui réjouit — <a href="#entry-modana" class="skt-link">n.</a> réjouissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-modata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोदत</span> <i>modata</i> [imp. <a href="#entry-mud_1" class="skt-link">mud_1</a>] ind. réjouissez-vous!
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-modin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोदिन्</span> <i>modin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mud_1" class="skt-link">mud_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-modin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-modin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-modinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>modinī</i> qui se réjouit, heureux — ifc. qui réjouit.<br> <i>bālamodinī</i> f. histoire pour enfants.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mogha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोघ</span> <i>mogha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muh" class="skt-link">muh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mogha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mogha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-moghaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>moghā</i> vain, inutile, inefficace, décevant; erroné, absurde; délaissé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-moh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोह्</span> <i>moh</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-muh" class="skt-link">muh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(mohayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>vimohita</i><i>)</i> affoler, égarer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-moha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोह</span> <i>moha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-muh" class="skt-link">muh</a>] <a href="#entry-moha" class="skt-link">m.</a> défaillance, évanouissement, syncope <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> illusion, égarement, confusion, erreur; stupéfaction; hallucination, folie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. égarement dû à l'attachement au monde phénoménal; cf. <a href="#entry-maayaa" class="skt-link">māyā</a>.<br> <i>mohaṃ</i> <i>gam</i> s'évanouir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mohacuu_dottara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mohacūḍottara</i> [<a href="#entry-cuu_daa" class="skt-link">cūḍā</a>-<a href="#entry-uttara_1" class="skt-link">uttara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mohacuu_dottara" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mohacūḍottara, texte sur l'architecture de temples <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a> et l'installation du <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mohamudgara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mohamudgara</i> [<a href="#entry-mudgara" class="skt-link">mudgara</a>] <a href="#entry-mohamudgara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Mohamudgara «Marteau à frapper l'erreur», poème attribué à <a href="#entry-Uzafkaraacaarya" class="skt-link">Śaṅkarācārya</a>; on l'appelle aussi <a href="#entry-Ubhajagovindastotra" class="skt-link">Bhajagovindastotra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mohana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोहन</span> <i>mohana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muh" class="skt-link">muh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mohana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mohana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mohanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mohanī</i> qui égare, qui trouble l'esprit, qui rend fou — <a href="#entry-mohana" class="skt-link">n.</a> trouble d'esprit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> enchantement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> plaisir d'amour <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mohana «Enchantement», nom d'une des cinq flèches de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> [<a href="#entry-pu_spabaa_na" class="skt-link">puṣpabāṇa</a>] — <a href="#entry-mohana" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Datura inoxia</i>, plante solanacée dont la racine contient une drogue hallucinogène; cf. <a href="#entry-dhattuura" class="skt-link">dhattūra</a> — <a href="#entry-mohanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mohanī</i> myth. «Celle qui égare», l'une des 9 forces [<a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a>] de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a> — <a href="#entry-mohanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mohanā</i> géo. np. de la rivière Mohanā, qui rejoint la <a href="#entry-Uniilaa_njanaa" class="skt-link">Nīlāñjanā</a> pour former la <a href="#entry-Uphalgu" class="skt-link">Phalgu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-moharaajaparaajaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>moharājaparājaya</i> [<a href="#entry-raaja" class="skt-link">rāja</a>-<a href="#entry-paraajaya" class="skt-link">parājaya</a>] <a href="#entry-moharaajaparaajaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Moharājaparājaya «Défaite du roi Illusion», drame jaïne de Yaśaḥpāla (<a href="#entry-Ugurjara" class="skt-link">Gurjara</a>, 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); c'est une allégorie sur la conversion au jaïnisme du roi <a href="#entry-Ukumaarapaala" class="skt-link">Kumārapāla</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-moha" class="skt-link">moha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mohin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोहिन्</span> <i>mohin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muh" class="skt-link">muh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mohin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mohin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mohinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mohinī</i> qui confond, qui trompe, qui égare — <a href="#entry-mohinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mohinī</i> myth. np. de Mohinī «Celle qui trompe», forme féminine assumée par la magie de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> pour reprendre l'elixir de vie [<a href="#entry-am_rta" class="skt-link">amṛta</a>] aux démons [<a href="#entry-asura" class="skt-link">asura</a>] qui l'avaient dérobé, et le donner aux dieux [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] pour les immortaliser, lors du barattage de l'océan primordial [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>]; même <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> en tomba amoureux; cf. <a href="#entry-Uhariharaputra" class="skt-link">Hariharaputra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mohita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोहित</span> <i>mohita</i> [<a href="#entry-moha" class="skt-link">moha</a><a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mohita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mohita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mohitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mohitā</i> stupéfié, confondu, égaré, trompé.<br> <i>kāmam</i> <i>mohita</i> aveuglé par la passion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mok_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोक्ष्</span> <i>mokṣ</i> [vn. <i>mokṣa</i>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(mokṣayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mokṣita</i><i>)</i> libérer, délivrer de ⟨abl.⟩ — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(mokṣate)</i> se libérer, se délivrer de ⟨acc.⟩.<br> <i>ahaṃ</i> <i>tvā</i> <i>sarvapāpebhyo</i> <i>mokṣayiṣyāmi</i> <i>mā</i> <i>śucaḥ</i> [BhG.] Ne t'afflige pas, je te libérerai de tous les péchés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mok_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोक्ष</span> <i>mokṣa</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mok_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> libération, abandon <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. délivrance, salut; but ultime de la doctrine orthodoxe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mok_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोक्षण</span> <i>mokṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mok_s" class="skt-link">mokṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-mok_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> obtention de la libération; fait de libérer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mok_sada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mokṣada</i> [<a href="#entry-mok_sa" class="skt-link">mokṣa</a>-<a href="#entry-da" class="skt-link">da</a>] <a href="#entry-mok_sada" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mokṣada, aspect de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> «accordant la libération».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mok_samuulara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mokṣamūlara</i> [<a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-mok_samuulara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Mokṣamūlara «Source de Libération», sanskritisation du nom de Max Müller, linguiste, philologue et indianiste germanique établi en Angleterre (1823–1900), premier éditeur du <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mok_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोक्षित</span> <i>mokṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mok_s" class="skt-link">mokṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mok_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mok_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mok_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mokṣitā</i> libéré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mok_sopaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mokṣopāya</i> [<a href="#entry-upaaya" class="skt-link">upāya</a>] <a href="#entry-mok_sopaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. moyen de délivrance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. du Mokṣopāya ou Mokṣopāyaśāstra «Obtention de la libération», important ouvrage (30000 strophes [<a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">śloka</a>]) du shivaïsme du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> du 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, d'auteur inconnu; il préfigure le <a href="#entry-Uyogavaasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Yogavāsiṣṭha</a> qui en découle; ses sections se nomment <a href="#entry-Uutpattiprakara_na" class="skt-link">Utpattiprakaraṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvairaagyaprakara_na" class="skt-link">Vairāgyaprakaraṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Umumuk_suvyavahaaraprakara_na" class="skt-link">Mumukṣuvyavahāraprakaraṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uupazamaprakara_na" class="skt-link">Upaśamaprakaraṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Usthitiprakara_na" class="skt-link">Sthitiprakaraṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uupazaantiprakara_na" class="skt-link">Upaśāntiprakaraṇa</a>, et <a href="#entry-Unirvaa_naprakara_na" class="skt-link">Nirvāṇaprakaraṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mok_sopaaya_tiikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mokṣopāyaṭīkā</i> [<a href="#entry-_tiikaa" class="skt-link">ṭīkā</a>] <a href="#entry-mok_sopaaya_tiikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Mokṣopāyaṭīkā, commentaire du <a href="#entry-Umok_sopaaya" class="skt-link">Mokṣopāya</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Ubhaaskaraka_n_tha" class="skt-link">Bhāskarakaṇṭha</a> (fin du 17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).<br> <a href="#entry-mok_sopaaya" class="skt-link">mokṣopāya</a> <br> <a href="#entry-mok_sa" class="skt-link">mokṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-moka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोक</span> <i>moka</i> [<a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-moka" class="skt-link">n.</a> dépouille d'un animal.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mokt_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोक्तृ</span> <i>moktṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mokt_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mokt_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-moktrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>moktrī</i> qui libère, qui relâche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-moktavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मोक्तव्य</span> <i>moktavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-moktavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-moktavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-moktavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>moktavyā</i> qui doit être libéré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à quoi l'on doit renoncer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui doit être jeté (contre ⟨loc. acc.-<a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mradas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्रदस्</span> <i>mradas</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rd_1" class="skt-link">mṛd_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mradas" class="skt-link">n.</a> douceur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mradiman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्रदिमन्</span> <i>mradiman</i> [<a href="#entry-m_rdu" class="skt-link">mṛdu</a><a href="#entry--man" class="skt-link">-man</a>] <a href="#entry-mradiman" class="skt-link">m.</a> douceur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mre_d">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्रेड्</span> <i>mreḍ</i> ca. <i>(mreḍayati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i><i>)</i> [inusité sans pf.].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुण्ड</span> <i>muṇḍa</i> a. <a href="#entry-mu_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mu_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mu_n_daa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muṇḍā</i> chauve; qui a la tête rasée — <a href="#entry-mu_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Muṇḍa, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uketu" class="skt-link">Ketu</a> «le Chauve» — <a href="#entry-mu_n_dii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muṇḍī</i> natu. bot. <i>Sphæranthus indicus</i>, aster globuleux, plante médicinale — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(muṇḍayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>muṇḍita</i><i>)</i> raser la tête.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_n_daka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुण्डक</span> <i>muṇḍaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mu_n_da" class="skt-link">muṇḍa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mu_n_daka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mu_n_daka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mu_n_dikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muṇḍikā</i> rasé; chauve; élagué.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_n_dakopani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muṇḍakopaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-mu_n_dakopani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Muṇḍakopaniṣad (MO.) «Libératrice», leçon [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] de l'<a href="#entry-Uatharvaveda" class="skt-link">Atharvaveda</a>; <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṅkara_1</a> en fit un commentaire; elle enseigne que le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> n'apporte qu'une connaissance superficielle, et que la connaissance suprême [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] découle de l'expérience directe du <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a> Impérissable [<a href="#entry-ak_sara" class="skt-link">akṣara</a>] par le renoncement.<br> <a href="#entry-mu_n_daka" class="skt-link">muṇḍaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_n_damaalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muṇḍamālā</i> [<a href="#entry-maalaa" class="skt-link">mālā</a>] <a href="#entry-mu_n_damaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> guirlande de têtes coupées.<br> <a href="#entry-mu_n_da" class="skt-link">muṇḍa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_n_dana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुण्डन</span> <i>muṇḍana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mu_n_da" class="skt-link">muṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-mu_n_dana" class="skt-link">n.</a> tonsure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. rasage du crâne, not. en signe de deuil familial.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_n_dita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
