    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुण्डित</span> <i>muṇḍita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mu_n_da" class="skt-link">muṇḍa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mu_n_dita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mu_n_dita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mu_n_ditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muṇḍitā</i> qui a la tête rasée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_s_ti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुष्टि</span> <i>muṣṭi</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mu_s_1" class="skt-link">muṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mu_s_ti" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mu_s_ti" class="skt-link">f.</a> vol <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> main fermée, poing (dissimulant le vol) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> poignée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_s_tika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muṣṭika</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-mu_s_tika" class="skt-link">m.</a> poignée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> position des mains <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>muṣṭikās</i> soc. parias, hors-castes — f. cf. <a href="#entry-mu_s_tikaa" class="skt-link">muṣṭikā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mu_s_ti" class="skt-link">muṣṭi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_s_tikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुष्टिका</span> <i>muṣṭikā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-mu_s_tika" class="skt-link">muṣṭika</a>] <a href="#entry-mu_s_tikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> avec les mains.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_s_tikaakathana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muṣṭikākathana</i> [<a href="#entry-kathana" class="skt-link">kathana</a>] <a href="#entry-mu_s_tikaakathana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de raconter une histoire avec les mains.<br> <a href="#entry-mu_s_tikaa" class="skt-link">muṣṭikā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_s_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुष्</span> <i>muṣ_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(muṣṇāti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>muṣita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i><i>)</i> voler, dérober, piller; ravir, tromper, berner; égarer, aveugler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_s_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुष्</span> <i>muṣ_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mu_s_1" class="skt-link">muṣ_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-mu_s_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mu_s_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mu_s_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui vole, qui ravit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुषित</span> <i>muṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mu_s_1" class="skt-link">muṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mu_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mu_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mu_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muṣitā</i> volé, pillé; emporté, ravi, soustrait, enlevé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> privé de ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aveuglé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_ska">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुष्क</span> <i>muṣka</i> <a href="#entry-mu_ska" class="skt-link">m.</a> scrotum, testicules.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muc_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुच्</span> <i>muc_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(muñcati)</i> fut. md. <i>(mokṣyate)</i> pft. <i>(mumoca)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <i>(amucat)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(amūmucat)</i> aor. md. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] <i>(amukta)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mukta</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(moktum)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>muktvā</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>adhi</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>nis</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> libérer, délivrer; lâcher, délier, délivrer de ⟨abl.⟩ — ps. <i>(mucyate)</i> être libéré, être privé; se libérer, s'abstenir — ca. <i>(mocayati)</i> mettre en liberté, délier; libérer de ⟨abl.⟩ — dés. <i>(mumukṣati)</i> désirer libérer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>mūcus</i>.<br> <i>prāṇān</i> <i>muc</i> tuer.<br> <i>mukṣīya</i> (bénédictif) fasse que je sois libéré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muc_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुच्</span> <i>muc_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muc_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muc_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muc_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> ifc. qui délivre de, qui libère de — <a href="#entry-muc_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> libération
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुच</span> <i>muca</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-muca" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muca" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mucii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mucī</i> qui libère de ⟨iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mucilinda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुचिलिन्द</span> <i>mucilinda</i> <a href="#entry-mucilinda" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. de Mucilinda ou Mucalinda, cobra géant mythique, qui abrita de son capuchon le <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> pour le protéger de la tempête pendant 7 jours alors qu'il méditait sous l'arbre d'éveil à <a href="#entry-Ugayaa" class="skt-link">Gayā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>mucalinda</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mucu_tii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुचुटी</span> <i>mucuṭī</i> [<a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mucu_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> méd. paire de forceps.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mucukunda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुचुकुन्द</span> <i>mucukunda</i> <a href="#entry-mucukunda" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mucukunda, fils du roi <a href="#entry-Umaandhaataa" class="skt-link">Māndhātā</a>; il assista les dieux dans leur guerre contre les démons, et obtint comme récompense le sommeil éternel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mud_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुद्</span> <i>mud_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(modate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(modati)</i> pft. <i>(mumude)</i> aor. md. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(amodiṣṭa)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mudita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>pra</i><i>)</i> être joyeux, se réjouir de ⟨i. loc.⟩ — ca. <i>(modayati)</i> réjouir, amuser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mud_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुद्</span> <i>mud_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mud_1" class="skt-link">mud_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mud_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> joie, gaieté; bonheur (aussi pl.) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> délire, ivresse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>mudā</i> joie, gaieté; plaisir; bonheur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>mudī</i> clair de lune — <a href="#entry-muda" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>muda</i> la Joie personnifiée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mudga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुद्ग</span> <i>mudga</i> <a href="#entry-mudga" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Phaseolus mungo</i>, fève.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mudgala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुद्गल</span> <i>mudgala</i> <a href="#entry-mudgala" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du pays Mudgala; il fut conquis par <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] Mudgala_1 ou <a href="#entry-Umaudgalya" class="skt-link">Maudgalya</a> «du <a href="#entry-Umudgala" class="skt-link">Mudgala</a>»; jamais il ne se laissait aller à la colère; <a href="#entry-Udurvaasaa" class="skt-link">Durvāsā</a> visita son ermitage pour l'éprouver; chaque jour, il prenait toute sa nourriture, mangeait à sa faim, et gâchait le reste en l'écrasant sur son corps nu; satisfait qu'il contrôlât ainsi sa colère, <a href="#entry-Udurvaasaa" class="skt-link">Durvāsā</a> lui offrit d'accéder au ciel avec son corps mortel; mais le sage refusa une résidence aussi luxueuse; son épouse était <a href="#entry-Uindrasenaa_1" class="skt-link">Indrasenā_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mudgara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुद्गर</span> <i>mudgara</i> <a href="#entry-mudgara" class="skt-link">m.</a> marteau, maillet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mudita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुदित</span> <i>mudita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mud_1" class="skt-link">mud_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mudita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mudita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muditā</i> joyeux (de ⟨i. iic.⟩); heureux — <a href="#entry-muditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muditā</i> joie, allégresse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. la gaieté, une des quatre qualités morales [<a href="#entry-brahmavihaara" class="skt-link">brahmavihāra</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mudraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुद्रा</span> <i>mudrā</i> <a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> sceau, (bague portant un) cachet; tampon, forme à imprimer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pièce de monnaie, médaille; timbre; papier timbré, passeport <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> attitude corporelle, position symbolique des mains, geste iconographique canonique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. gestes commémoratifs d'événements de la vie du <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-abhaya" class="skt-link">abhaya</a>, <a href="#entry-a_njali" class="skt-link">añjali</a>, <a href="#entry-dharmacakra" class="skt-link">dharmacakra</a>, <a href="#entry-dhyaana" class="skt-link">dhyāna</a>, <a href="#entry-bhuumisparza" class="skt-link">bhūmisparśa</a>, <a href="#entry-varada" class="skt-link">varada</a>, <a href="#entry-vitarka" class="skt-link">vitarka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [tantr.] imposition rituelle des doigts à fins magiques — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(mudrayati)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>mudrayitvā</i><i>)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mudrita</i><i>)</i> mettre un sceau; imprimer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mudraaraak_sasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mudrārākṣasa</i> [<a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>] <a href="#entry-mudraaraak_sasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Mudrārākṣasa «le Sceau de <a href="#entry-Uraak_sasa_1" class="skt-link">Rākṣasa_1</a>», drame en 7 actes de <a href="#entry-Uvizaakhadatta" class="skt-link">Viśākhadatta</a>, qui met en scène la politique de <a href="#entry-Ucaa_nakya" class="skt-link">Cāṇakya</a> pour gagner le ministre <a href="#entry-Uraak_sasa_1" class="skt-link">Rākṣasa_1</a> à la cause de <a href="#entry-Ucandragupta" class="skt-link">Candragupta</a> afin de renverser la dynastie des <a href="#entry-Unanda" class="skt-link">Nanda</a> au <a href="#entry-Umagadha" class="skt-link">Magadha</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mudrita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुद्रित</span> <i>mudrita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-mudraa" class="skt-link">mudrā</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mudrita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mudrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mudritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mudritā</i> tamponné, cacheté, imprimé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> marqué au fer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> scellé; relié — <a href="#entry-mudrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> impression (sur ⟨loc.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mod. imprimerie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mugdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुग्ध</span> <i>mugdha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-muh" class="skt-link">muh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mugdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mugdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mugdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mugdhā</i> naïf, simpliste, sot, niais, bête; fou <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perplexe, égaré, stupéfait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> candide, inexpérimenté, ingénu; jeune, aimable, charmant — <a href="#entry-mudghaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mudghā</i> jeune sotte, oie blanche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mugdhaabhisaarikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mugdhābhisārikā</i> [<a href="#entry-abhisaarikaa" class="skt-link">abhisārikā</a>] <a href="#entry-mugdhaabhisaarikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. ingénue se rendant à son premier rendez-vous galant.<br> <a href="#entry-mugdha" class="skt-link">mugdha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mugdhaak_sii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mugdhākṣī</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sa" class="skt-link">akṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-mugdhaak_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> jolie femme [«qui a un regard ingénu»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mugdhabodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mugdhabodha</i> [<a href="#entry-bodha" class="skt-link">bodha</a>] <a href="#entry-mugdhabodha" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. np. du Mugdhabodha <a href="#entry-vyaakara_na" class="skt-link">vyākaraṇa</a> «Instruction des ignorants», grammaire de <a href="#entry-Uvopadeva" class="skt-link">Vopadeva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mugdhabuddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mugdhabuddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-buddhi" class="skt-link">buddhi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mugdhabuddhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mugdhabuddhi" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mugdhabuddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> id. [«qui a l'esprit égaré»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुह्</span> <i>muh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(muhyati)</i> pft. <i>(mumoha)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mugdha</i>, <i>mūḍha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> être confondu, être égaré; être inconscient; être stupide; être dans l'erreur, être induit en erreur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> s'égarer, se tromper; perdre la tête — ca. <i>(mohayati)</i> affoler, égarer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muhur">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुहुर्</span> <i>muhur</i> adv. soudainement, tout à coup <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pour un moment; à plusieurs reprises, répétitivement; constamment.<br> <i>muhur</i> <i>...</i> <i>muhur</i> tantôt ... tantôt; toujours et toujours.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muhuurta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुहूर्त</span> <i>muhūrta</i> [<a href="#entry-muhur" class="skt-link">muhur</a>] <a href="#entry-muhuurta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muhuurta" class="skt-link">n.</a> minute, moment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> laps de temps équivalent à 48 minutes; il y en a 3 dans une période [<a href="#entry-kaala_1" class="skt-link">kāla_1</a>], 30 dans une journée complète; il vaut deux demi-laps [<a href="#entry-naa_dikaa" class="skt-link">nāḍikā</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> l'un des 30 moments de la journée; cf. <a href="#entry-brahmamuhuurta" class="skt-link">brahmamuhūrta</a>, <a href="#entry-rudramuhuurta" class="skt-link">rudramuhūrta</a>, <a href="#entry-praatastana" class="skt-link">prātastana</a>, <a href="#entry-safgava" class="skt-link">saṃgava</a>, <a href="#entry-abhijit" class="skt-link">abhijit</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. moment approprié pour entreprendre un acte important.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muhuurtazastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muhūrtaśastra</i> [<a href="#entry-zastra" class="skt-link">śastra</a>] <a href="#entry-muhuurtazastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. détermi­na­tion astrologique du temps propice à commencer une entreprise.<br> <a href="#entry-muhuurta" class="skt-link">muhūrta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुख</span> <i>mukha</i> <a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">n.</a> bouche, gueule, ouverture; face, visage; front; front d'une armée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tête, sommet; partie essentielle, meilleure partie; commencement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>mukhasandhi</i>] lit. [théâtre] germe de l'intrigue; c'est l'une des 5 articulations de récits dramatiques [<a href="#entry-pa_ncasandhi" class="skt-link">pañcasandhi</a>] — ifc. qui a pour chef, qui a pour partie essentielle ou initiale — <a href="#entry-mukhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mukhī</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhaagni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukhāgni</i> [<a href="#entry-agni" class="skt-link">agni</a>] <a href="#entry-mukhaagni" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. rite consistant à mettre un brandon dans la bouche d'un mort au moment de la crémation, afin d'éviter l'explosion du crâne; le fils aîné l'effectue pour son père.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhadaghna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukhadaghna</i> [<a href="#entry-daghna" class="skt-link">daghna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mukhadaghna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mukhadaghna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mukhadaghnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mukhadaghnā</i> <a href="#entry-mukhadaghnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mukhadaghnī</i> qui a (de l'eau) jusqu'à la bouche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhagha_n_taa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukhaghaṇṭā</i> [<a href="#entry-gha_n_taa" class="skt-link">ghaṇṭā</a>] <a href="#entry-mukhagha_n_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. youyous de joie des femmes, not. lors d'une cérémonie de mariage; syn. <a href="#entry-hulahulii" class="skt-link">hulahulī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुखर</span> <i>mukhara</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mukhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mukhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mukharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mukharā</i> bavard, loquace; éloquent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui gazouille, qui chante (oiseau, abeilles); qui sonne (anneau de chevilles) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bruyant; grossier — <a href="#entry-mukhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> corbeau; (coquille de) conque <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> chef <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du <a href="#entry-naaga" class="skt-link">nāga</a> Mukhara — <a href="#entry-mukharii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mukharī</i> mors — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(mukharayati)</i> faire parler, rendre loquace <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> annoncer, notifier.<br> <i>mukharīkṛ</i> faire résonner.<br> <i>ata</i> <i>eva</i> <i>māṃ</i> <i>prayojanaśuśrūṣā</i> <i>mukharayati</i> C'est pourquoi le désir de connaître vos motifs me rend si bavard.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukharataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukharatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-mukharataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> bavardage.<br> <a href="#entry-mukhara" class="skt-link">mukhara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhastha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukhastha</i> [<a href="#entry-stha" class="skt-link">stha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mukhastha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mukhastha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mukhasthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mukhasthā</i> mémorisé.<br> <i>mukhasthā</i> <i>vidyā</i> connaissance mémorisée (par le <a href="#entry-pa_n_dita" class="skt-link">paṇḍita</a>).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhatas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukhatas</i> [<a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] adv. par la bouche <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> en tête.<br> <i>sarve</i> <i>vedā</i> <i>mukhato</i> <i>gṛhītāḥ</i> Tout le <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> est retenu par récitation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhebhaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukhebhaga</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaga" class="skt-link">bhaga</a>] <a href="#entry-mukhebhaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> homosexuel [«qui pratique la fellation»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhopaadhyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukhopādhyāya</i> [<a href="#entry-upaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">upādhyāya</a>] <a href="#entry-mukhopaadhyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> titre d'érudits; mod. Mukherji.<br> <a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुख्य</span> <i>mukhya</i> [<a href="#entry-mukha" class="skt-link">mukha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mukhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mukhya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mukhyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mukhyā</i> à la tête, principal, le premier; excellent, le plus beau; prééminent, chef <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. cf. <a href="#entry-dvikarmaka" class="skt-link">dvikarmaka</a> — ifc. le meilleur parmi; principal dans.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhyaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukhyārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-mukhyaartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. sens principal ou littéral d'un mot; syn. <a href="#entry-abhidhaartha" class="skt-link">abhidhārtha</a>; opp. <a href="#entry-gau_naartha" class="skt-link">gauṇārtha</a> — a. <a href="#entry-mukhyaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mukhyaartha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mukhyaarthaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mukhyārthā</i> gram. utilisé dans son sens littéral.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhyaarthabaadhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukhyārthabādhā</i> [<a href="#entry-baadhaa" class="skt-link">bādhā</a>] <a href="#entry-mukhyaarthabaadhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. perte du sens littéral; se dit de la métaphore [<a href="#entry-lak_sa_na" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇa</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-mukhyaartha" class="skt-link">mukhyārtha</a> <br> <a href="#entry-mukhya" class="skt-link">mukhya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhyavize_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukhyaviśeṣya</i> [<a href="#entry-vize_sya" class="skt-link">viśeṣya</a>] <a href="#entry-mukhyavize_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. théorie de la qualification principale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukhyavize_syaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukhyaviśeṣyaka</i> [<a href="#entry-vize_syaka" class="skt-link">viśeṣyaka</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-mukhyavize_syaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mukhyavize_syaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mukhyavize_syakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mukhyaviśeṣyakā</i> gram. qui admet comme qualification principale.<br> <i>dhātvarthamukhyaviśeṣyaka</i> gram. se dit de l'analyse sémantique [<a href="#entry-zabdabodha" class="skt-link">śabdabodha</a>] dirigée par le sens du verbe (école des grammairiens [<a href="#entry-vaiyaakara_na" class="skt-link">vaiyākaraṇa</a>]).<br> <i>prathamāntamukhyaviśeṣyaka</i> gram. se dit de l'analyse sémantique [<a href="#entry-zabdabodha" class="skt-link">śabdabodha</a>] dirigée par le sujet (école des logiciens [<a href="#entry-naiyaayika" class="skt-link">naiyāyika</a>]).<br> <i>bhāvanārthamukhyaviśeṣyaka</i> gram. se dit de l'analyse sémantique [<a href="#entry-zabdabodha" class="skt-link">śabdabodha</a>] dirigée par la conséquence de l'action (école des exégètes [<a href="#entry-miimaa_msaka" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsaka</a>]).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुक्त</span> <i>mukta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muktā</i> libéré, délivré; abandonné, disparu; lancé, lâché, versé, émis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. délivré (des liens de la transmigration) — loc. <i>mukte</i> prép. cl. excepté, sauf ⟨i.⟩ — f. cf. <a href="#entry-muktaa" class="skt-link">muktā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pali <i>mutta</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुक्ता</span> <i>muktā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-mukta" class="skt-link">mukta</a>] <a href="#entry-muktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> [secrétée] perle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktaacarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muktācarita</i> [<a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">carita</a>] <a href="#entry-muktaacarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Muktācarita «Histoire des perles», poème de <a href="#entry-Uraghunaathadaasa" class="skt-link">Raghunāthadāsa</a>; <a href="#entry-Usatyabhaamaa" class="skt-link">Satyabhāmā</a> y demande à <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> si les perles poussent sur les arbres, et il lui dit avoir fait pousser des perles sur un arbre à <a href="#entry-Uv_rndaavana" class="skt-link">Vṛndāvana</a>, du temps où il s'amusait avec <a href="#entry-Uraadhaa" class="skt-link">Rādhā</a> et autres bergères [<a href="#entry-gopii" class="skt-link">gopī</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktaakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muktākāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muktaakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muktaakaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muktaakaaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muktākārā</i> qui a la forme d'une perle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktaama_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muktāmaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_ni" class="skt-link">maṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-muktaama_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> perle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktaapii_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muktāpīḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-aapii_da" class="skt-link">āpīḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-muktaapii_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> Muktāpīḍa (733–769); il établit la dynastie <a href="#entry-Ukaarko_ta" class="skt-link">Kārkoṭa</a>-Nāga et fit du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> une grande puissance, alliée de la Chine; il renversa le roi <a href="#entry-Uyazovarmaa" class="skt-link">Yaśovarmā</a> et devint l'empereur [<a href="#entry-saarvabhauma" class="skt-link">sārvabhauma</a>] <a href="#entry-Ulalitaaditya" class="skt-link">Lalitāditya</a>; il construisit l'admirable temple au Soleil de <a href="#entry-Umaartaa_n_da" class="skt-link">Mārtāṇḍa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktaavali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muktāvali</i> [<a href="#entry-aavali" class="skt-link">āvali</a>] <a href="#entry-muktaavali" class="skt-link">f.</a> rangée de perles; collier de perles — ifc. lit. nom d'ouvrages <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>muktāvalī</i> id.; not. np. de la Muktāvalī, ou <a href="#entry-Unyaayasiddhaantamuktaavalii" class="skt-link">Nyāyasiddhāntamuktāvalī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-muktaa" class="skt-link">muktā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktabuddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muktabuddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-buddhi" class="skt-link">buddhi</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muktabuddhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muktabuddhi" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muktabuddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> dont l'esprit a été libéré; émancipé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुक्तक</span> <i>muktaka</i> [<a href="#entry-mukta" class="skt-link">mukta</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muktaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muktaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muktakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muktakā</i> détaché, séparé; indépendant — <a href="#entry-muktaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. vers [<a href="#entry-zloka" class="skt-link">śloka</a>] tiré de son contexte, citation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. simple prose (sans mots composés).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktaka_n_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muktakaṇṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_n_tha" class="skt-link">kaṇṭha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muktaka_n_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muktaka_n_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muktaka_n_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muktakaṇṭhā</i> avec la gorge libre.<br> <i>muktakaṇṭham</i> à voix forte, à tue-tête.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktakeza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muktakeśa</i> [<a href="#entry-keza" class="skt-link">keśa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muktakeza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muktakeza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muktakezii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>muktakeśī</i> qui a les cheveux défaits.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukteśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-muktezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du temple <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a> Mukteśvara à <a href="#entry-Ubhubanezvar" class="skt-link">Bhubaneśvar</a> (Orissa), construit vers 970; devant son entrée se dresse une arche [<a href="#entry-tora_na" class="skt-link">toraṇa</a>], et il possède un <a href="#entry-ma_n_dapa" class="skt-link">maṇḍapa</a> au plafond sculpté; dans le même complexe se trouvent le temple Siddheśvara et le puits <a href="#entry-Umariiciku_n_da" class="skt-link">Marīcikuṇḍa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mukta" class="skt-link">mukta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुक्ति</span> <i>mukti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-mukti" class="skt-link">f.</a> libération <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. délivrance finale, salut; cf. <a href="#entry-saalokyaadicatu_s_taya" class="skt-link">sālokyādicatuṣṭaya</a>.<br> <i>muktaye</i> <i>hariṃ</i> <i>bhajati</i> On obtient la libération en adorant <a href="#entry-Uhari" class="skt-link">Hari</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुक्तिका</span> <i>muktikā</i> [<a href="#entry-mukti" class="skt-link">mukti</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-muktikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> perle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktikopani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muktikopaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-muktikopani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Muktikopaniṣad «Perle des leçons».<br> <a href="#entry-muktikaa" class="skt-link">muktikā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktivaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muktivāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-muktivaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Muktivāda «Pouvoir de la libération», traité de sémantique dû à <a href="#entry-Ugadaadhara" class="skt-link">Gadādhara</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mukti" class="skt-link">mukti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muktvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुक्त्वा</span> <i>muktvā</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] ind. ayant libéré — prép. sans, sauf ⟨acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muku_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुकुट</span> <i>mukuṭa</i> <a href="#entry-muku_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muku_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> tiare, diadème.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muku_tin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukuṭin</i> [<a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muku_tin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muku_tin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muku_tinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mukuṭinī</i> couronné; qui porte un diadème.<br> <a href="#entry-muku_ta" class="skt-link">mukuṭa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुकुल</span> <i>mukula</i> <a href="#entry-mukula" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mukula" class="skt-link">n.</a> bourgeon, bouton <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> première dent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np de Mukula <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Ukalla_ta" class="skt-link">Kallaṭa</a> (9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); on lui doit l'<a href="#entry-Uabhidhaav_rttimaat_rkaa" class="skt-link">Abhidhāvṛttimātṛkā</a>, traité sur les fonctions dénotatives critiquant le concept <a href="#entry-dhvani" class="skt-link">dhvani</a> d'<a href="#entry-Uaanandavardhana" class="skt-link">Ānandavardhana</a>; on lui doit aussi un commentaire des <a href="#entry-Uspandakaarikaas" class="skt-link">Spandakārikās</a> (perdu) — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(mukulayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mukulita</i><i>)</i> fermer (les yeux); fermer (comme un bouton).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukunda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुकुन्द</span> <i>mukunda</i> <a href="#entry-mukunda" class="skt-link">m.</a> pierre précieuse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. l'un des 9 trésors cachés, gardés par <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-navanidhi" class="skt-link">navanidhi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukundadeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukundadeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-mukundadeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de princes de l'Orissa.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mukundamaalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mukundamālā</i> [<a href="#entry-maalaa" class="skt-link">mālā</a>] <a href="#entry-mukundamaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. du poème dévotionnel [<a href="#entry-stotra" class="skt-link">stotra</a>] Mukundamālā, du roi <a href="#entry-Ukulazekhara" class="skt-link">Kulaśekhara</a>; il glorifie <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mukunda" class="skt-link">mukunda</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mumuk_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुमुक्ष्</span> <i>mumukṣ</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-muc_1" class="skt-link">muc_1</a>] v. pr. <i>(mumukṣati)</i> désirer libérer — pr. md. <i>(mumukṣate)</i> désirer la délivrance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mumuk_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुमुक्षु</span> <i>mumukṣu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mumuk_s" class="skt-link">mumukṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mumuk_su" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mumuk_su" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mumuk_su" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. qui souhaite la délivrance; opp. <a href="#entry-bubhuk_su" class="skt-link">bubhukṣu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mumuk_sutva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mumukṣutva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-mumuk_sutva" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a>] élan vers la délivrance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mumuk_suvyavahaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mumukṣuvyavahāra</i> [<a href="#entry-vyavahaara" class="skt-link">vyavahāra</a>] <a href="#entry-mumuk_suvyavahaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. question de la délivrance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mumuk_suvyavahaaraprakara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mumukṣuvyavahāraprakaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakara_na" class="skt-link">prakaraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-mumuk_suvyavahaaraprakara_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Mumukṣuvyavahāraprakaraṇa, 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ section du <a href="#entry-Umok_sopaaya" class="skt-link">Mokṣopāya</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-mumuk_suvyavahaara" class="skt-link">mumukṣuvyavahāra</a> <br> <a href="#entry-mumuk_su" class="skt-link">mumukṣu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mumuur_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुमूर्ष्</span> <i>mumūrṣ</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-m_r" class="skt-link">mṛ</a>] v. pr. <i>(mumūrṣati)</i> désirer mourir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mumuur_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुमूर्षा</span> <i>mumūrṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-mumuur_s" class="skt-link">mumūrṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-mumuur_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> désir de mourir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mumuur_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुमूर्षु</span> <i>mumūrṣu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-mumuur_s" class="skt-link">mumūrṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-mumuur_su" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mumuur_su" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-mumuur_su" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui désire la mort.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुनि</span> <i>muni</i> <a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">m.</a> sage; ascète, anachorète; ermite ayant fait vœu de silence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. la conscience <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>munayas</i> myth. les sept sages célestes [syn. <a href="#entry-saptar_si" class="skt-link">saptarṣi</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. la Grande Ourse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 7 — <a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">f.</a> var. <i>munī</i> myth. np. de Munī, fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a>, mère des génies centaures [<a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a>] et trolls [<a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-munisuvrata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>munisuvrata</i> [<a href="#entry-suvrata" class="skt-link">suvrata</a>] <a href="#entry-munisuvrata" class="skt-link">m.</a> jn. np. de Munisuvrata «Vertueux sage», 20⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-tiirthafkara" class="skt-link">tīrthaṅkara</a> mythique; son corps est brun, son symbole est une tortue.<br> <a href="#entry-muni" class="skt-link">muni</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-munitraya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>munitraya</i> [<a href="#entry-traya" class="skt-link">traya</a>] <a href="#entry-munitraya" class="skt-link">n.</a> gram. la triade des maîtres de la tradition grammaticale sanskrite: <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukaatyaayana" class="skt-link">Kātyāyana</a> et <a href="#entry-Upata_njali_1" class="skt-link">Patañjali_1</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-trimuni" class="skt-link">trimuni</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-munivara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>munivara</i> [<a href="#entry-vara_2" class="skt-link">vara_2</a>] <a href="#entry-munivara" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. titre de «meilleur des sages».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mura">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुर</span> <i>mura</i> [<a href="#entry-m_r" class="skt-link">mṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-mura" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mura ou Murāsura «Tueur», démon [<a href="#entry-daanava" class="skt-link">dānava</a>] fils de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et <a href="#entry-Udanu" class="skt-link">Danu</a>; pour ses austérités, <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> lui avait accordé de faire mourir tout être qu'il toucherait, même immortel; il gardait <a href="#entry-Upraagjyoti_sa" class="skt-link">Prāgjyotiṣa</a>, capitale de <a href="#entry-Unaraka" class="skt-link">Naraka</a>, et avait 4000 fils; il mit au défi <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, qui préféra s'exiler sur Terre; il perturba un sacrifice de <a href="#entry-Uraghu" class="skt-link">Raghu</a>, et <a href="#entry-Uvasi_s_tha" class="skt-link">Vasiṣṭha</a> lui conseilla de s'attaquer plutôt à <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>; lorsqu'il vint ordonner à <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a> de ne plus faire mourir, celui-ci lui dit qu'il était aux ordres de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>; c'est alors que <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> attaqua <a href="#entry-Upraagjyoti_sa" class="skt-link">Prāgjyotiṣa</a>, où il tua <a href="#entry-Unaraka" class="skt-link">Naraka</a> et <a href="#entry-Umura" class="skt-link">Mura</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du roi <a href="#entry-yaadava" class="skt-link">yādava</a> Mura_1 ou Muru.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muraari">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>murāri</i> [<a href="#entry-ari" class="skt-link">ari</a>] <a href="#entry-muraari" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Murāri, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>-<a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «ennemi de <a href="#entry-Umura" class="skt-link">Mura</a>» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Murāri_1 <a href="#entry-Umizra" class="skt-link">Miśra</a>, qui créa l'école <a href="#entry-mizramata" class="skt-link">miśramata</a> de <a href="#entry-miimaa_msaa" class="skt-link">mīmāṃsā</a> au 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle.<br> <a href="#entry-mura" class="skt-link">mura</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muraja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muraja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] <a href="#entry-muraja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-murajaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>murajā</i> sorte de tambour; tambourin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-murajabandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>murajabandha</i> [<a href="#entry-bandha" class="skt-link">bandha</a>] <a href="#entry-murajabandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. poème géométrique [<a href="#entry-citrakaavya" class="skt-link">citrakāvya</a>] en forme de tambour.<br> <a href="#entry-muraja" class="skt-link">muraja</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muralii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुरली</span> <i>muralī</i> <a href="#entry-muralii" class="skt-link">f.</a> flûte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muraliidhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muralīdhara</i> [<a href="#entry-dhara" class="skt-link">dhara</a>] <a href="#entry-muraliidhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Muralīdhara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «tenant la flûte».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muraliimanohara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>muralīmanohara</i> [<a href="#entry-manohara" class="skt-link">manohara</a>] <a href="#entry-muraliimanohara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Muralīmanohara, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «qui enchante avec sa flûte».<br> <a href="#entry-muralii" class="skt-link">muralī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-musala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुसल</span> <i>musala</i> <a href="#entry-musala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-musala" class="skt-link">n.</a> massue; pilon (en bois); battant de cloche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-musta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुस्त</span> <i>musta</i> <a href="#entry-musta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-mustaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mustā</i> natu. bot. <i>Cyperus rotundus</i>, souchet; plante herbacée de marais à rhizomes et tubercules, tige triquètre, fleurs en épis bruns; utilisée en médecine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mustakaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mustakā</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-mustakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-musta" class="skt-link">musta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muu_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूढ</span> <i>mūḍha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-muh" class="skt-link">muh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muu_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muu_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muu_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūḍhā</i> confus, stupéfié, égaré; sot, niais; stupide, idiot, crétin; insensé, fou <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à la dérive; erroné, égaré, hors service — <a href="#entry-muu_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> idiot — <a href="#entry-muu_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> confusion d'esprit, égarement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muu_dhadhii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūḍhadhī</i> [<a href="#entry-dhii_2" class="skt-link">dhī_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muu_dhadhii" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muu_dhadhii" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muu_dhadhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> simplet, idiot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muu_dhamanas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūḍhamanas</i> [<a href="#entry-manas" class="skt-link">manas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muu_dhamanas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muu_dhamanas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muu_dhamanas" class="skt-link">f.</a> idiot, stupide.<br> <a href="#entry-muu_dha" class="skt-link">mūḍha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muu_dhamati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūḍhamati</i> [<a href="#entry-mati" class="skt-link">mati</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muu_dhamati" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muu_dhamati" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muu_dhamati" class="skt-link">f.</a> idiot, stupide.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muu_dhatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūḍhatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-muu_dhatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> confusion; stupidité, folie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muu_s_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूष्</span> <i>mūṣ_1</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-mu_s_1" class="skt-link">muṣ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(mūṣati)</i> voler, dérober, piller.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muu_s_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूष्</span> <i>mūṣ_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muu_s_1" class="skt-link">mūṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-muu_s_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muu_s_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«voleur»] souris <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μυς; lat. <i>mūs</i>; ang. mouse; all. Maus.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muu_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूष</span> <i>mūṣa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muu_s_1" class="skt-link">mūṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-muu_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muu_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūṣā</i> <a href="#entry-muu_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūṣī</i> souris; rat.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muu_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूषक</span> <i>mūṣaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muu_s_1" class="skt-link">mūṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-muu_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muu_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūṣikā</i> voleur, pilleur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rat, souris.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muu_sika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूषिक</span> <i>mūṣika</i> [<a href="#entry-muu_sa" class="skt-link">mūṣa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-muu_sika" class="skt-link">m.</a> souris; rat <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. le rat-monture [<a href="#entry-vaahana" class="skt-link">vāhana</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. géo. nom du pays et du peuple des Mūṣikās, établis sur la côte du Kerala au sud de Quilon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muu_sikavaahana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūṣikavāhana</i> [<a href="#entry-vaahana" class="skt-link">vāhana</a>] <a href="#entry-muu_sikavaahana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Mūṣikavāhana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Uga_neza" class="skt-link">Gaṇeśa</a> «qui a une souris comme monture».<br> <a href="#entry-muu_sika" class="skt-link">mūṣika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muujavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूजवत्</span> <i>mūjavat</i> <a href="#entry-muujavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de la montagne Mūjavat, dans la région du Pamir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> avestique Muza.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूक</span> <i>mūka</i> a. <a href="#entry-muuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muuka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūkā</i> muet; silencieux — <a href="#entry-muuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> poisson <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. μυω; lat. <i>mūtus</i>; ang. mute; fr. muet.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muukabhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūkabhāva</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaava" class="skt-link">bhāva</a>] <a href="#entry-muukabhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait d'être muet, mutisme.<br> <a href="#entry-muuka" class="skt-link">mūka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूल</span> <i>mūla</i> <a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">n.</a> racine; pied (d'une arbre); racine comestible, radis <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> base, fondement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> origine, cause; principe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ville principale, capitale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. texte original, source; œuvre primaire (opp. <a href="#entry-v_rtti" class="skt-link">vṛtti</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<a href="#entry-muulabarha_nii" class="skt-link">mūlabarhaṇī</a>] astr. np. du <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a> Mūla «Racine» — ifc. fondé sur, provenant de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulaadhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūlādhāra</i> [<a href="#entry-aadhaara" class="skt-link">ādhāra</a>] <a href="#entry-muulaadhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [«le support du fondement»] 1⟨sup⟩er⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a> du <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a> tantr., situé au fondement; il est représenté par un lotus de 4 pétales rouges; un carré jaune y symbolise la terre, et porte le <a href="#entry-biijamantra" class="skt-link">bījamantra</a> ‘<i>laṃ</i>’; la <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> <a href="#entry-Uku_n_dalinii" class="skt-link">Kuṇḍalinī</a> y sommeille, lovée trois fois et demi autour du <a href="#entry-svayambhuulifga" class="skt-link">svayaṃbhūliṅga</a>; ce <a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a> contrôle le sens de l'odorat; il est le centre psychique de l'Éveil Incitateur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulaaraadhanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūlārādhanā</i> [<a href="#entry-aaraadhanaa" class="skt-link">ārādhanā</a>] <a href="#entry-muulaaraadhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> jn. np. de la Mūlārādhanā, texte doctrinaire jn. sur le choix de sa fin de vie; aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Ubhagavatii" class="skt-link">Bhagavatī</a> <a href="#entry-aaraadhanaa" class="skt-link">ārādhanā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulabandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūlabandha</i> [<a href="#entry-bandha" class="skt-link">bandha</a>] <a href="#entry-muulabandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] contraction des muscles du périnée visant à faire monter l'<a href="#entry-apaanavaayu" class="skt-link">apānavāyu</a> dans le canal [<a href="#entry-maarga" class="skt-link">mārga</a>] de la <a href="#entry-Usu_sum_naa" class="skt-link">Suṣumṇā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulabarha_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūlabarhaṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-barha_nii" class="skt-link">barhaṇī</a>] <a href="#entry-muulabarha_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> astr. np. de Mūlabarhaṇī «celle qui déracine», 17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à l'étoile ε du Scorpion; les mânes [<a href="#entry-pitaras" class="skt-link">pitaras</a>] y président; son symbole est une queue de lion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulagranthakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūlagranthakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-granthakaara" class="skt-link">granthakāra</a>] <a href="#entry-muulagranthakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> auteur fondateur (de ⟨iic.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूलक</span> <i>mūlaka</i> [<a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muulaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muulaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muulikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūlikā</i> ifc. issu de; enraciné en — <a href="#entry-muulaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muulaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> racine; radis, navet, rave — <a href="#entry-muulaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [BhP.] np. du roi Mūlaka «Issu (du Soleil)», fils d'<a href="#entry-Uazmaka_1" class="skt-link">Aśmaka_1</a> de la lignée solaire [<a href="#entry-suuryava_mza" class="skt-link">sūryavaṃśa</a>]; il est père de <a href="#entry-Ukha_tvaafga" class="skt-link">Khaṭvāṅga</a>-<a href="#entry-Udiliipa_2" class="skt-link">Dilīpa_2</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Unaariikavaca" class="skt-link">Nārīkavaca</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulamadhyamaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūlamadhyamaka</i> [<a href="#entry-madhyamaka" class="skt-link">madhyamaka</a>] <a href="#entry-muulamadhyamaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. du Mūlamadhyamaka «Origine de la Voie du Milieu», ouvrage en strophes [<a href="#entry-kaarikaas" class="skt-link">kārikās</a>] de <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Umadhyamakazaastra" class="skt-link">Madhyamakaśāstra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulamantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūlamantra</i> [<a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>] <a href="#entry-muulamantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a>-racine, dédié à la Déesse Suprême; on le prononce en introduction aux rites d'offrande <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. <a href="#entry-mantra" class="skt-link">mantra</a> principal d'invocation d'une divinité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> forme originale d'un mantra avant son enrobage par des syllabes germes [<a href="#entry-biija" class="skt-link">bīja</a>] et autres incantations.<br> <i>hrīṃ</i> <i>śrīṃ</i> <i>krīṃ</i> <i>parameśvari</i> <i>svāhā</i> [<a href="#entry-muulamantra" class="skt-link">mūlamantra</a>] Hommage à la Déesse suprême.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulaprak_rti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūlaprakṛti</i> [<a href="#entry-prak_rti" class="skt-link">prakṛti</a>] <a href="#entry-muulaprak_rti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] la Nature Primordiale, cause originelle des phénomènes du monde matériel; cf. <a href="#entry-avyakta" class="skt-link">avyakta</a>, <a href="#entry-pradhaana" class="skt-link">pradhāna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulasa_mhitaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūlasaṃhitā</i> [<a href="#entry-sa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">saṃhitā</a>] <a href="#entry-muulasa_mhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Mūlasaṃhitā, présumée collection d'origine des livres mythologiques [<a href="#entry-puraa_na" class="skt-link">purāṇa</a>], attribuée à <a href="#entry-Ulomahar_sa_na" class="skt-link">Lomaharṣaṇa</a>; elle serait proche du <a href="#entry-Uvaayupuraa_na" class="skt-link">Vāyupurāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulavibhuja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūlavibhuja</i> [<a href="#entry-vibhuja" class="skt-link">vibhuja</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muulavibhuja" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muulavibhuja" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muulavibhujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūlavibhujā</i> qui ploie les racines — <a href="#entry-muulavibhuja" class="skt-link">m.</a> chariot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muulya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूल्य</span> <i>mūlya</i> [<a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muulya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muulya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muulyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūlyā</i> à la racine, à l'origine — <a href="#entry-muulya" class="skt-link">n.</a> valeur marchande, coût, prix d'achat; prix <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rémunération; gain; capital.<br> <i>amūlya</i> sans prix.<br> <i>mūlyahīna</i> sans valeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurcch">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्च्छ्</span> <i>mūrcch</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(mūrcchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mūrcchita</i><i>)</i> cf. <a href="#entry-muurch" class="skt-link">mūrch</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurcchana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्च्छन</span> <i>mūrcchana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muurcch" class="skt-link">mūrcch</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muurcchana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muurcchana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muurcchanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrcchanā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-muurchana" class="skt-link">mūrchana</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurch">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्छ्</span> <i>mūrch</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(mūrchati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mūrchita</i>, <i>mūrta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>sam</i><i>)</i> se solidifier, durcir, se raidir; se former, épaissir; grumeler, coaguler, cristalliser; devenir une masse homogène <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> grossir, s'accroître; prendre de la force, devenir violent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pénétrer, s'étendre sur; avoir effet — ca. <i>(mūrchayati)</i> façonner; renforcer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurchaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्छा</span> <i>mūrchā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-muurch" class="skt-link">mūrch</a>] <a href="#entry-muurchaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> évanouissement; égarement, hallucination.<br> <i>mūrchāṃ</i> <i>bhaj</i> s'évanouir.<br> <i>mūrchāṃ</i> <i>kal</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurchana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्छन</span> <i>mūrchana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-muurch" class="skt-link">mūrch</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muurchana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muurchana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muurchanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrchanā</i> qui stupéfie; qui anesthésie (se dit d'une flèche de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>) — <a href="#entry-muurchana" class="skt-link">n.</a> évanouissement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (mus.) modulation mélodique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véhémence; intensification — <a href="#entry-muurchanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrchanā</i> évanouissement; stupeur; hallucination <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mélodie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurchita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्छित</span> <i>mūrchita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-muurch" class="skt-link">mūrch</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muurchita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muurchita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muurchitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrchitā</i> stupéfié; solidifié, congelé; accru, grossi — ifc. plein de, rempli de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्ध</span> <i>mūrdha</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-muurdhan" class="skt-link">mūrdhan</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurdhaabhi_sikta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrdhābhiṣikta</i> [<a href="#entry-abhi_sikta" class="skt-link">abhiṣikta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muurdhaabhi_sikta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muurdhaabhi_sikta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muurdhaabhi_siktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrdhābhiṣiktā</i> consacré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurdhaavasikta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrdhāvasikta</i> [<a href="#entry-avasikta" class="skt-link">avasikta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muurdhaavasikta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muurdhaavasikta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muurdhaavasiktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrdhāvasiktā</i> soc. consacré par l'onction royale.<br> <i>rājño</i> <i>mūrdhāvasiktasya</i> <i>vadho</i> <i>brahmavadhād</i> <i>guruḥ</i> [BhP.] Le meurtre d'un roi consacré est pire que celui d'un brâhmane.<br> <a href="#entry-muurdha" class="skt-link">mūrdha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurdhaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrdhaga</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_1" class="skt-link">ga_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muurdhaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muurdhaga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muurdhagaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrdhagā</i> qui est sur la tête de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurdhaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrdhaja</i> [<a href="#entry-ja" class="skt-link">ja</a>] <a href="#entry-muurdhaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>mūrdhajās</i> chevelure, cheveux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurdhan">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्धन्</span> <i>mūrdhan</i> <a href="#entry-muurdhan" class="skt-link">m.</a> tête, crâne; sommet, cime; commencement, origine; chose capitale — loc. <i>mūrdhni</i> adv. en tête.<br> <i>sa</i> <i>sarveṣāṃ</i> <i>mūrdhni</i> <i>tiṣṭhati</i> il est au-dessus de tous.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurdhanya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrdhanya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muurdhanya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muurdhanya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muurdhanyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrdhanyā</i> issu de la tête <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. (consonne) rétroflexe ou apicale — <a href="#entry-muurdhanya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phonème apical (apico-alvéolaire ou rétroflexe).<br> <a href="#entry-muurdhan" class="skt-link">mūrdhan</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurkha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्ख</span> <i>mūrkha</i> [<a href="#entry-muurch" class="skt-link">mūrch</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muurkha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muurkha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muurkhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrkhā</i> sot, niais; buté, obtus; stupide, imbécile — <a href="#entry-muurkha" class="skt-link">m.</a> imbécile, crétin; fou.<br> <i>tāvacchobhate</i> <i>mūrkho</i> <i>yāvat</i> <i>kiṃcin</i> <i>na</i> <i>bhāṣate</i> [<a href="#entry-Uhitopadeza" class="skt-link">Hitopadeśa</a>] Un imbécile ne brille qu'aussi longtemps qu'il n'ouvre pas la bouche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurkhalak_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrkhalakṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-lak_sa_na" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-muurkhalak_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> signe de stupidité.<br> <a href="#entry-muurkha" class="skt-link">mūrkha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurkhataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrkhatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-muurkhataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> sottise, bêtise, stupidité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurkhatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrkhatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-muurkhatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> sottise, bêtise, stupidité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्त</span> <i>mūrta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-muurch" class="skt-link">mūrch</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muurta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muurta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muurtaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrtā</i> corporel, incarné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurtatara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrtatara</i> [<a href="#entry--tara" class="skt-link">-tara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muurtatara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muurtatara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muurtataraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrtatarā</i> substantiel, concrétisé.<br> <a href="#entry-muurta" class="skt-link">mūrta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्ति</span> <i>mūrti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-muurch" class="skt-link">mūrch</a>] <a href="#entry-muurti" class="skt-link">f.</a> corps solide; matière; forme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> manifestation; individu; représentation, statue, image, idole <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 3 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Mūrti «Matière», fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, une des épouses de <a href="#entry-Udharma" class="skt-link">Dharma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurtikaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrtikāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-muurtikaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> sculpteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurtimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrtimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muurtimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muurtimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muurtimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūrtimatī</i> ayant une forme; incarné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurtipuujaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrtipūjā</i> [<a href="#entry-puujaa" class="skt-link">pūjā</a>] <a href="#entry-muurtipuujaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. culte au moyen d'idoles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurtizaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūrtiśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-muurtizaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> sculpture.<br> <a href="#entry-muurti" class="skt-link">mūrti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muurvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूर्वा</span> <i>mūrvā</i> <a href="#entry-muurvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Sansevieria roxburghiana</i>, plante aux feuilles charnues, sorte d'agave; on en fait des cordes d'arc et des ceintures [<a href="#entry-maurvii" class="skt-link">maurvī</a>]; son rhizome est utilisé en médecine contre la toux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muutra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूत्र</span> <i>mūtra</i> <a href="#entry-muutra" class="skt-link">n.</a> urine — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(mūtrayati)</i> pr. md. <i>(mūtrayate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>mūtrita</i><i>)</i> uriner; uriner contre ⟨acc.⟩.<br> <i>mūtraṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> uriner.<br> <i>tiṣṭhan</i> <i>mūtrayati</i> <i>gauḥ</i> le bœuf pisse debout.<br> <i>tiṣṭhan</i> <i>mūtrayati</i> <i>yavanaḥ</i> le grec pisse debout.<br> <i>abrāhmano'yaṃ</i> <i>yas</i> <i>tiṣṭhan</i> <i>mūtrayati</i> [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] N'est pas un brâhmane celui qui urine debout.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muutraazaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>mūtrāśaya</i> [<a href="#entry-aazaya" class="skt-link">āśaya</a>] <a href="#entry-muutraazaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. vessie.<br> <a href="#entry-muutra" class="skt-link">mūtra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muutrita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूत्रित</span> <i>mūtrita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-muutra" class="skt-link">mūtra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muutrita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muutrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muutritaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūtritā</i> qui a uriné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> uriné; souillé d'urine — <a href="#entry-muutrita" class="skt-link">n.</a> miction.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-muutrya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मूत्र्य</span> <i>mūtrya</i> [<a href="#entry-muutra" class="skt-link">mūtra</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-muutrya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-muutrya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-muutryaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>mūtryā</i> urinaire; concernant l'urine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-mu_nja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुञ्ज</span> <i>muñja</i> <a href="#entry-mu_nja" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Saccharum arundinaceum</i>, véd. roseau utilisé pour la divination et pour tresser des corbeilles et des ceintures [<a href="#entry-mekhalaa" class="skt-link">mekhalā</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Muñja, roi <a href="#entry-Uparamaara" class="skt-link">Paramāra</a> qui régnait à <a href="#entry-Udhaaraa" class="skt-link">Dhārā</a> au 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; ce roi-poète avait le titre de <a href="#entry-Uvaakpati" class="skt-link">Vākpati</a>; il fut décapité par le roi <a href="#entry-Ukalyaa_na" class="skt-link">Kalyāṇa</a> Tailapa; son frère cadet <a href="#entry-Usindhuraaja" class="skt-link">Sindhurāja</a> lui succéda.<br> <i>muñjīkṛ</i> [«réduire en roseaux»] déchiqueter.
  </div>
</div>
